### [1] [FlyingDrones: A Dataset and Benchmark for Optical Flow Estimation from UAV motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07705)
*Laurenz Ruzicka,Roman Pflugfelder,Daniel Cremers*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FlyingDrones基准（基于物理渲染生成，含稠密光流真值与分割掩码）及分辨率归一化指标EPE Norm，用于评估小尺寸无人机目标的光流估计；评测16类模型族并结合真实UAV航拍验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机航拍监视与远程目标跟踪中光流是重要线索，但远处小目标像素少、对比度低、背景动态复杂，光流估计困难；现有标准光流基准以大型、以物体为中心的运动为主，无法很好刻画远距离空中目标。

Method: 构建FlyingDrones生成式基准，利用physically based rendering生成稠密光流真值与分割掩码；定义按图像尺寸归一化的EPE Norm指标；在运动与尺寸分层的999对光流、三种分辨率子集上评测16个SOTA模型族；另开展两项补充研究：21台相机真实UAV素材上的无真值运动对齐评估（41个checkpoint，Spearman ρ=0.63），以及用800对FlyingDrones数据微调。

Result: 所有模型族均优于零光流参考，且差距随分辨率增大；1920x1080下全局匹配、循环、Transformer与短窗口时序模型明显优于金字塔、经典、自监督、轻量及扩散模型；族内checkpoint差异与族间差异相当或更大；合成排名对真实性能有中等且显著的预测信号但精确名次不同；微调使留出无人机区域误差最多降低37%，并在四种架构上均改善真实素材中的无人机定位。

Conclusion: 小目标空中光流的评测需专门基准与归一化指标；训练课程（curriculum）与架构同等重要；合成基准可对真实性能提供有意义的排序信号，且少量本域数据微调即可显著迁移。

Abstract: Optical flow is an important cue for drone analysis in aerial surveillance, airspace monitoring, and long-range target tracking, where motion remains informative when appearance is weak. Estimating the motion of small Unmanned Aerial Vehicles, however, is difficult because distant targets occupy few pixels, have low contrast, and move against complex dynamic backgrounds. Standard optical-flow benchmarks emphasize large, object-centric motions and therefore do not characterize distant aerial targets well. We introduce FlyingDrones, a generated benchmark with dense ground-truth optical flow and segmentation masks derived from physically based rendering, and define resolution-normalized metrics (EPE Norm) that normalize flow by image dimensions. We evaluate 16 state-of-the-art model families on a motion- and size-stratified subset of 999 flow pairs at three resolutions. Every family recovers drone motion better than the zero-flow reference, but the gap between families widens with resolution: at 1920x1080, global-matching, recurrent, transformer, and short-window temporal models separate clearly from pyramid, classical, self-supervised, lightweight, and diffusion models. Within-family checkpoint spread is comparable to or larger than between-family spread, so the training curriculum is as important as the architecture. Two complementary studies test the benchmark beyond synthetic accuracy: a ground-truth-free motion-alignment evaluation on 21 cameras of real UAV footage shows that the synthetic ranking carries a moderate, significant signal about real-world performance (Spearman \r{ho} = 0.63 over 41 checkpoints) while the exact top ranks differ, and a fine-tuning study shows that 800 FlyingDrones pairs reduce held-out drone-region error by up to 37% and improve drone localization on the real footage for all four architectures tested.

</details>


### [2] [InfMasking: A Unified Variational Framework for Synergistic Multimodal Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07702)
*Liangjian Wen,Qun Dai,Jiang Duan,Yong Dai,Jianzhuang Liu,Dongkai Wang,Jun Wang,Fuyuan Cao,Zenglin Xu,Zhao Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出InfMasking：融合前对每个模态特征随机掩码，要求所有掩码融合表示与参考目标（无监督时为未掩码融合视图，有监督时为类代理）保持一致，以高斯建模+闭式变分界优化无限掩码期望，迫使融合模块学习跨模态协同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态模型本应利用协同信息（仅多模态共同观测时才可得），但融合模块往往只需依赖主导模态或模态间共享信息即可拟合训练目标，导致协同信息未被利用。

Method: 随机掩码各模态特征后再融合，每个掩码产生不同的部分跨模态证据组合与相应融合表示；所有掩码融合表示须与参考目标一致（无监督：未掩码融合视图；有监督：任务标签的类代理）。将掩码融合表示建模为高斯分布，优化闭式变分界以处理无限多掩码上的期望目标，其总体矩差距由该模型下掩码诱导的协方差控制。

Result: 在评测基准上提升预测性能；学到的模态特定表示中任务标签协同的PID均值估计更高；训练后的预测器InterSHAP交互归因更高。

Conclusion: 跨多样部分组合的一致性约束可抑制对主导模态或共享信息的依赖，驱动融合模块学习在部分观测下仍有意义的跨模态交互模式。

Abstract: Multimodal models are expected to exploit synergistic information, that is, information available only when multiple modalities are observed together. In practice, however, a fusion module can fit the training objective by relying on a dominant modality or on information that modalities share, and the synergy between modalities is often left unused. We propose InfMasking, a unified multimodal learning framework based on Infinite Masking. Before fusion, InfMasking randomly masks the features of each modality; each sampled mask produces a different partial combination of cross-modal evidence and a fused representation of the corresponding modality interactions. All masked fused representations are required to remain consistent with a reference target: the unmasked fused view when training is unsupervised, and the class proxy of the task label when labels are available. Maintaining this consistency across diverse partial combinations discourages reliance on a dominant modality or on shared information alone, and drives the fusion module to learn cross-modal interaction patterns that remain meaningful under partial observations. Since the objective is an expectation over infinitely many masks, we model the masked fused representations as Gaussian and optimize closed-form variational bounds, whose population-moment gaps are controlled by the masking-induced covariance under this model. On the evaluated benchmarks, InfMasking improves predictive performance and yields higher mean PID estimates of task-label synergy in learned modality-specific representations, alongside higher InterSHAP interaction attribution in the trained predictors. Code is available at https://github.com/brightest66/InfMasking.

</details>


### [3] [CoGenRec: A Unified Generative Framework for Complementary Vision Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07665)
*Yapeng Meng,Yuguo Chen,Xiangru Chen,Shaojun Xu,Xuezhen Wang,Lijian Wang,Zheyu Yang,Yihan Lin,Luping Shi,Rong Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CoGenRec——基于互补视觉传感器(CVS)双通路互补测量的生成式重建框架，统一视频去模糊、高帧率插帧与HDR融合，在SportsSloMo-CVS上较最强通用基线PSNR分别提升4.40/3.89/5.04 dB，并在129个真实场景取得良好感知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端运动与高动态范围场景下，时域分辨率、空间细节、信噪比与动态范围之间存在固有权衡（受带宽、功耗、量子效率限制），传统帧式相机因此出现时域欠采样、曝光导致运动模糊与传感器饱和。CVS通过"空间稠密/时域稀疏的长曝光RGB通路"与"时域稠密/空间稀疏的短曝光时空差分通路"互补分解视觉信息，其互补测量跨越宽动态范围提供外观、结构与运动线索，具备超越常规成像的重建潜力，但缺乏统一利用这些互补信息的重建方法。

Method: CoGenRec是互补性驱动的生成式重建框架，将互补测量编码为多尺度时空特征，采用曝光感知外观建模与双向时序传播；这些特征用于约束视频扩散模型，并注入VAE解码器以恢复潜空间压缩中丢失的精细结构，从而结合物理测量的场景保真度与学习型视频先验的外观恢复能力。

Result: 在SportsSloMo-CVS数据集上，相较最强通用基线，PSNR在去模糊上提升4.40 dB、插帧上提升3.89 dB、HDR融合上提升5.04 dB；在129个真实CVS采集的未见场景上亦取得较强的感知质量。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了在困难运动与光照条件下进行高质量重建的统一范式：以硬件层面的互补采样为物理基础，以扩散先验与解码器级特征注入为生成手段，单一框架同时解决去模糊、高时域插帧与HDR融合，并具备良好的真实场景泛化能力。

Abstract: Capturing scenes with extreme motion and high dynamic range is fundamentally constrained by trade-offs among temporal resolution, spatial detail, signal-to-noise ratio, and dynamic range under practical bandwidth, power, and quantum-efficiency limits. Conventional frame-based cameras therefore suffer from temporal undersampling, exposure-induced motion blur, and sensor saturation. The Complementary Vision Sensor (CVS) addresses these limitations by decomposing visual information across two complementary pathways, a spatially dense, temporally sparse, long-exposure RGB pathway and a temporally dense, spatially sparse, short-exposure patiotemporal difference pathway. Their complementary measurements provide appearance, structural, and motion cues across a wide dynamic range that enable reconstruction beyond conventional imaging. We present CoGenRec, a complementarity-driven generative reconstruction framework that unifies video deblurring, high-temporal-resolution frame interpolation, and HDR fusion. CoGenRec encodes complementary measurements into multi-scale spatiotemporal features using exposure-aware appearance modeling and bidirectional temporal propagation. These features constrain a video diffusion model and are injected into the VAE decoder to recover fine structures lost during latent-space compression, combining the scene fidelity of physical measurements with the appearance recovery capabilities of learned video priors. On SportsSloMo-CVS, CoGenRec improves PSNR over the strongest general-purpose baseline by 4.40 dB for deblurring, 3.89 dB for frame interpolation, and 5.04 dB for HDR fusion, respectively. It also achieves strong perceptual quality on 129 unseen scenes captured with real CVS. These results establish a unified approach to advance high-quality reconstruction under challenging motion and illumination conditions. Project page: https://cogenrec.github.io/.

</details>


### [4] [TRACEMARK: Editor-Aware Watermarking for User Attribution in Text-Guided Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07632)
*Xiaodong Wu,Xiangman Li,Jianbing Ni,Rongxing Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出编辑器感知水印框架 Tracemark：在编辑前嵌入用户专属信号，经共享检测器与参考向量比对实现编辑后图像的用户归属，500用户平均识别准确率87.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像编辑服务虽记录请求用户，但生成图像本身不含账户信息；图像被下载分享后无法仅凭图像溯源请求者。直接嵌入用户水印可能被语义编辑抹除，因此需要一种能抵抗编辑破坏的归属方案。

Method: 框架由共享编码器与共享检测器组成：编码器在编辑前嵌入用户特定信号，检测器通过比对编辑后图像特征与学习到的参考向量恢复用户。二者借助固定参数的编辑器联合训练，用户识别误差损失驱动水印抗编辑，保真损失约束对输入图像与预期编辑的改动。每个图像域与编辑指令训练独立模型并在注册用户间共享。

Result: 在三个图像域上，编辑器感知训练提升了参考向量匹配与二值水印解码两种方式的用户恢复效果；独立训练的模型在500用户中达到87.5%平均识别准确率；在未重训练情况下换用不同编辑器，水印模块仍能恢复用户信息。

Conclusion: 通过在编辑过程中学习水印，可在不修改编辑器的前提下实现编辑后图像的用户归属，验证了编辑器感知水印训练的有效性与一定跨编辑器泛化性。

Abstract: An image editing service records which user requests each edit, but the edited image itself does not contain this account information. Once the image is downloaded and shared, the service cannot identify the requesting user from the image alone. Embedding a user-specific watermark could enable such attribution, but semantic editing may erase the signal. We propose Tracemark, an editor-aware watermarking framework for identifying users from edited images. A shared encoder embeds a user-specific signal before editing, and a shared detector recovers the user by comparing features of the edited image with learned reference vectors. Both components are trained jointly through an editor with fixed parameters. User identification errors encourage the encoder to produce watermarks that survive editing, while fidelity losses limit changes to the input image and intended edit. Each image domain and editing instruction uses a separately trained model shared across registered users. Experiments on three image domains show that editor-aware training improves user recovery with both reference-vector matching and binary watermark decoding. Separately trained models reach 87.5% mean identification accuracy among 500 users. When tested with a different editor without retraining, the watermark modules still recover user information. These results demonstrate that learning watermarks through the editing process enables user attribution without modifying the editor.

</details>


### [5] [FisherDistill: Diffusion Distribution Distillation via Closed-Form Fisher Critics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07604)
*Mahesh Bhosale,Yuanhao Zhai,Siwei Lyu,Junsong Yuan,David Doermann,Defang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FisherDistill 用基于冻结教师中间特征的 Fisher 线性判别分析替代对抗判别器，为扩散蒸馏提供轻量精炼信号，缩小与多步教师的分布差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激进蒸馏后的生成器只需 1~几次 NFE，但与多步教师之间存在残余分布差距；现有方法靠引入可学习对抗判别器来精炼，作者质疑这一精炼是否必须依赖对抗判别器。

Method: 利用冻结教师的中间表示，估计真实样本与生成样本的一阶、二阶统计量，推导出使类间分离相对类内变异最大化的闭式 Fisher 方向；该方向作为轻量精炼信号与原始蒸馏目标互补，无需额外判别器及其优化循环。

Result: 在多个蒸馏框架上一致优于各自蒸馏基线，并显著缩小与对抗精炼版本的差距。

Conclusion: 对抗精炼的有效性未必源自对抗机制本身，Fisher 判别方向即可提供等价且更廉价的精炼；该方法通用、即插即用、无额外判别器开销。

Abstract: Recent advances in diffusion distillation have enabled high-quality image generation in only one or a few network function evaluations (NFEs). Despite their efficiency, aggressively distilled generators often retain a residual distribution gap from their multi-step teachers, motivating recent methods to introduce adversarial refinement with a learned discriminator. In this work, we ask whether such refinement necessarily requires a learned adversarial discriminator. We introduce \textbf{FisherDistill}, a simple alternative based on Fisher linear discriminant analysis. Using intermediate representations from the frozen teacher, we estimate the first- and second-order statistics of real and generated samples and derive a closed-form Fisher direction that maximally separates their feature distributions relative to within-class variation. This direction provides a lightweight refinement signal that complements the original distillation objective without requiring an additional adversarial discriminator or its optimization loop. Across multiple distillation frameworks, our approach consistently improves generation quality over their respective distilled baselines and substantially closes the gap to adversarially refined counterparts. Code: https://bhosalems.github.io/FisherDistill/

</details>


### [6] [MetricTrail: Revisiting the Power of Metric Depth and Local Optimization for Long-Sequence Streaming 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07555)
*Jingnan Gao,Jiahui Huang,Sven Elflein,Jianhao Zheng,Bingxin Ke,Xiaokang Yang,Yichao Yan,Laura Leal-Taixé,Qunjie Zhou,Jiawei Ren,Shengyu Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MetricTrail 是一个面向长单目视频的流式相机运动估计与稠密重建系统，在 DPVO 稀疏局部估计器基础上引入单图像度量深度作为初始化与软约束，并用冷冻 DINOv3 特征训练的适配器增强匹配，在不重训长序列的前提下取得更低轨迹误差并保持实时效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长序列单目视频的流式位姿估计与稠密重建需在精度与推理效率间折中；DPVO 类稀疏局部优化窗口受限，纯几何对应难以在长时间上维持精度，而端到端长序列训练代价高、且深度与内参/立体等外部信息难以灵活替换。

Method: 复用 DPVO 稀疏局部估计器并改造：用单图像度量深度初始化 patch 深度，并在优化中作为软约束（不重训对应网络、不扩大优化窗口）；引入学习式适配器，将冷冻 DINOv3 表征注入匹配特征与循环上下文以改进对应估计；仅在多样短片段上训练对应估计器与适配器；模块化设计使深度提供者可独立升级，并支持外部相机内参与标定立体观测而无需重训。

Result: 在纯 RGB 协议下，于 KITTI、Oxford Spires、VBR 上取得所评估方法中最低的平均轨迹误差；以估计位姿结合单图像深度预测，在室内外基准上获得强稠密重建，并对野外视频产生连贯重建；提供内参时，KITTI-02 上达 12.70 FPS，峰值 GPU 显存 3.21 GB。

Conclusion: 以单图像度量深度作为软约束并配合冷冻 DINOv3 适配器，可在不重训长序列、不扩大优化窗口的条件下显著提升流式单目 SLAM 的轨迹精度，同时保持模块化、可替换深度源与实时资源效率，兼顾精度、泛化与工程可用性。

Abstract: We present MetricTrail, a streaming system for accurate camera motion estimation and dense reconstruction from long monocular videos. To keep inference efficient over long sequences, we revisit DPVO's sparse local estimator. We find that single-image metric depth substantially improves its trajectory accuracy, without retraining the correspondence network or enlarging the optimization window. Metric predictions initialize patch depths and remain as soft constraints during optimization. We further improve correspondence estimation through learned adapters that augment matching features and recurrent context with frozen DINOv3 representations. The correspondence estimator and adapters are trained on diverse short clips without additional long-sequence training. This modular design also allows the depth provider to be independently upgraded and supports supplied camera intrinsics and calibrated stereo observations without retraining. Under the reported RGB-only protocols, MetricTrail achieves the lowest average trajectory error among evaluated methods on KITTI, Oxford Spires, and VBR. Pairing the estimated poses with single-image depth predictions also produces strong dense reconstruction on indoor and outdoor benchmarks and coherent reconstructions of in-the-wild videos. With supplied intrinsics, the system processes KITTI-02 at 12.70 FPS with 3.21 GB peak GPU memory.

</details>


### [7] [Concertina: Embodying Manipulation and Cognition in One Diffusion Vision-Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07513)
*Chengcheng Wang,Wei He,Wenhao Li,Hongguang Li,Kai Han,Jianyuan Guo,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Concertina，一种将连续轨迹表示、分块生成与物理坐标监督直接嵌入共享扩散VLM主干计算与参数路径的Embodied Vision-Language Model（eVLM），在LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus与CALVIN上取得强劲性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA策略将操作与认知解耦：要么把动作量化为离散token（误差与物理位移无稳定对应），要么依赖独立的动作专家生成连续轨迹（梯度与VLM主干竞争或被阻断）。作者主张操作与认知应体现在同一模型中，即从解耦VLA转向eVLM。

Method: 在共享扩散VLM主干的隐藏空间中，将每个控制时间步编码为一个连续隐动作token，由同一Transformer通过多模态预训练所用的"破坏-重建"算子重建时序有序的动作块，并施加物理坐标监督；活跃动作投影仅占4.46B模型参数的0.21%，无独立动作生成层。

Result: 对比代表性连续VLA需16.5%–35.1%专用动作专家容量；提出的Action Interface Audit表明跨时间轨迹耦合涌现于共享主干的中后层而非动作接口；LIBERO平均成功率98.15%，10,030任务LIBERO-Plus达85.9%，CALVIN ABC-D平均完成链长4.142。

Conclusion: 通过将连续动作表示与生成完全置于共享VLM主干的参数与计算路径上，可在极低额外参数开销下实现操作与认知的统一，并保持或超越专用动作专家的性能，支持eVLM范式。

Abstract: Most vision-language-action (VLA) policies decouple manipulation from cognition by bolting an external action mechanism onto a vision-language model (VLM)--either quantizing actions into discrete tokens whose errors bear no stable correspondence to physical displacements, or delegating continuous trajectory generation to a separate action expert whose gradients compete with or are blocked from updating the VLM backbone. We argue that manipulation and cognition should instead be embodied in the same model, shifting from a decoupled VLA to an Embodied Vision-Language Model (eVLM). We introduce Concertina, an eVLM that places continuous trajectory representation, blockwise generation, and physical-coordinate supervision directly on the computation and parameter path of a shared diffusion VLM backbone. Each control timestep is encoded as one continuous latent action token in the backbone hidden space, and the shared Transformer reconstructs temporally ordered action blocks via the same corrupt-and-reconstruct operator used for multimodal pretraining. Active action projections account for only 0.21% of parameters in the 4.46B model with zero separate action-generator layers, compared with 16.5%-35.1% dedicated action-expert capacity in representative continuous VLAs. Using our proposed Action Interface Audit, we confirm that cross-time trajectory coupling emerges in the middle and late layers of the shared backbone rather than at the action interfaces. On standard benchmarks, Concertina achieves 98.15% average success on regular LIBERO, 85.9% on the 10,030-task LIBERO-Plus benchmark, and 4.142 average completed chain length on CALVIN ABC-D.
  Code: https://github.com/lose4578/Concertina Hugging Face: https://huggingface.co/collections/Traly/concertina

</details>


### [8] [Perceptual Distortion for Overfitted Video Coding: Sliced Matching in Space, Error Alignment in Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07148)
*Tianyi Yang,Ge Gao,Fan Zhang,David Bull*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将Wasserstein失真扩展到视频编码：空间上用局部切片Wasserstein失真匹配特征分布，时间上用流残差约束运动轨迹上的编码误差，在COOL-CHIC上相比逐帧WD取得DISTS 9.2%、LPIPS 3.0%的BD-rate降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经视频编码在固定码率下日益需要在感知质量与重建保真度之间权衡。WD通过放松特征匹配的空间对应关系来平衡二者，使轻量过拟合图像编解码器可与计算密集的生成式解码器竞争。但将原始WD直接扩展到视频存在两个问题：独立高斯近似限制了特征分布匹配的表达能力；跨帧无约束的纹理重采样引入时间不一致。

Method: 区分可放松与必须保持的对应关系。空间维度：提出局部切片Wasserstein失真（local SWD），通过一维最优传输匹配经验特征分布，捕捉跨通道依赖而无需精确空间对齐。时间维度：采用新颖的流残差，沿运动轨迹约束编码误差，在保持时间一致性的同时不额外施加重建保真度约束。

Result: 在UVG与HEVC Class B数据集上、基于过拟合编解码器COOL-CHIC评估：相对逐帧WD，测试序列平均BD-rate在DISTS上降低9.2%、LPIPS降低3.0%；在匹配码率下tOF降低10.2%。

Conclusion: 通过解耦空间与时间的对应关系约束，WD可有效扩展至视频编码，在感知质量与时间一致性上均优于逐帧WD基线。

Abstract: Recent works in neural video coding have increasingly been trading off perceptual quality against reconstruction fidelity at a fixed bitrate. Wasserstein Distortion (WD) offers a principled approach to balancing these objectives by relaxing spatial correspondences in feature matching, enabling lightweight overfitted image codecs to compete with computationally intensive generative decoders. Extending vanilla WD to video, however, presents two challenges: its independent Gaussian approximation limits the expressiveness of feature distribution matching, while unconstrained texture resampling across frames introduces temporal inconsistencies. We address these challenges by distinguishing between the correspondences that can be relaxed and those that must be preserved. Spatially, we introduce a local sliced Wasserstein distortion (SWD) that matches empirical feature distributions through one-dimensional optimal transport, capturing cross-channel dependencies without enforcing exact spatial alignment. Temporally, we employ a novel flow residual that constrains coding errors along motion trajectories, preserving temporal consistency without imposing additional reconstruction fidelity constraints. Evaluated on UVG and HEVC Class B with the overfitted codec COOL-CHIC, our framework achieves BD-rate reductions of 9.2% in DISTS and 3.0% in LPIPS relative to frame-wise WD, averaged over test sequences, while lowering tOF by 10.2% at matched rate.

</details>


### [9] [Segment What It Can Draw: Querying a Text-to-Image Generator with Its Own Images for Skin Lesion Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07061)
*Hongyu Cao,Guangfu Guo,Jinghan Zhang,Dakshak Keerthi Chandra,Ying Zhang,Kunpeng Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的 GENIQ 方法，利用生成图像构建皮肤/病变原型方向，在无需标注掩码与额外模型的条件下实现皮肤镜病变分割，三个测试集 Dice 0.85–0.88。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无标注掩码的皮肤病变分割需借助其他线索指定目标。现有方法依赖皮肤镜先验规则、标注导出的模型先验或用户框选区域；通用文生图扩散 Transformer 虽提供文本接口，但其概念词注意力在皮肤镜图像上不可靠：在注意力输出空间中，"词导出的皮肤—病变方向"与"真实皮肤—真实病变方向"对齐较差。

Method: 发现由生成的健康皮肤图与病变图导出的方向对齐显著更优。据此提出 GENIQ：(1) 用生成图像构建初始皮肤原型与病变原型；(2) 用无标注皮肤镜图像中最像皮肤、最像病变的 patch 精炼原型；(3) 沿精炼后的方向对测试 patch 打分；(4) 采用门控阈值抑制小病变周围上下文溢出。全程免训练、无推理期标注、无额外模型。

Result: 在三个皮肤镜测试集上 Dice 达 0.85–0.88；三个集上均优于零样本方法与基于词注意力的方法；在其中两个集上 Dice 与 IoU 均优于手工设计的皮肤镜流水线。

Conclusion: 生成图像所导出的语义方向比文本词注意力更可靠，可作为无标注皮肤病变分割的有效目标线索；GENIQ 以轻量、免训练的方式取得有竞争力的分割性能。

Abstract: Skin lesion segmentation without annotated masks requires the target to be specified through other cues. Existing approaches rely on dermoscopy-specific rules, annotation-derived model priors, or user-selected regions. A general text-to-image diffusion transformer offers a text interface to the target, but its concept-word attention is unreliable on dermoscopy images: in the model's attention-output space, the word-derived skin-to-lesion direction is poorly aligned with the direction separating real skin from real lesions. We show that the direction derived from generated healthy-skin and lesion images is substantially better aligned. Based on this finding, we propose GENIQ, a training-free method that constructs initial skin and lesion prototypes from generated images and refines them using the most skin-like and lesion-like patches from unlabelled dermoscopy images. It scores test patches along the refined direction and applies a gated threshold to suppress contextual spill-over around small lesions. Without inference-time annotations or additional models, GENIQ achieves Dice scores of 0.85-0.88 on three dermoscopy test sets. It outperforms zero-shot and word-based attention methods on all three sets, and a hand-designed dermoscopy pipeline in Dice and IoU on two.

</details>


### [10] [Expanding the Quality-Coverage Frontier with Competitive Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07055)
*Wenxuan Peng,Richard Zhang,Haitian Zheng,Jaskirat Singh,Tsai-Shien Chen,Eli Shechtman,Bharath Hariharan,Hadar Averbuch-Elor,Zongze Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出训练时"赢者通吃"竞争的流匹配目标：以随机低维条件码 z 采样 K 个候选速度，仅对最匹配目标的候选计算损失，从而缓解质量-覆盖权衡，并衍生出辅助条件缩放式引导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配生成器存在质量与覆盖的权衡，无分类器引导（CFG）提升质量但牺牲覆盖。作者认为其根源在目标函数本身：一个中间噪声图像可能对应多个干净图像，而模型只能预测单一速度，最多对这些可能性取平均，导致模式塌缩/折中。

Method: 在预测器中加入随机采样的低维条件码 z；对每个训练样本采样 K 个 z 生成 K 个候选速度，让它们竞争解释目标，只对最匹配的候选施加损失（winner-take-all），使不同模式塑造不同预测而非被平均。该目标还自然引出新的引导方式——辅助条件缩放（auxiliary conditioning scaling），可与传统引导互补。

Result: 在 ImageNet-256 上以 SiT 和 RAEv2 为测试平台验证。使用不到原 SiT 预训练算力 3% 进行后训练：在 67% Recall 点，FD_{r,6} 从 19.22 降至 5.91；RAEv2 上在 70% Recall 点，FD_{r,6} 从 16.83 降至 3.18。

Conclusion: 将多候选竞争引入流匹配训练可让不同模式各自塑造预测，配合辅助条件缩放与常规引导，显著改善质量-覆盖前沿，且后训练成本极低，具有实用性。

Abstract: Flow matching (FM) generators often face a quality-coverage tradeoff. Inference-time techniques such as classifier-free guidance raise quality, but at the cost of coverage. We hypothesize this limitation stems from a basic property of the objective: an intermediate noisy image could correspond to many clean images, yet the model can only predict a single velocity, at best averaging over the multiple possibilities. To mitigate this, we introduce competition among predictions during training. We achieve this by augmenting the predictor with a randomly drawn low-dimensional conditioning code $z$. For each training example, we sample $K$ values of $z$ to produce $K$ candidate velocities that compete to explain the target, and apply the loss only to the best-matching candidate. This winner-take-all objective allows different modes to shape different predictions, rather than being averaged into a single compromise. Furthermore, this term naturally enables a new form of guidance, auxiliary conditioning scaling, which complements traditional guidance methods. Together, these two guidance mechanisms show that our method substantially improves the quality-coverage frontier. We validate on ImageNet-256, using two different testbeds, SiT and RAEv2. Given less than 3% of the original SiT pretraining compute for post-training, at the 67% Recall point, our method reduces $\mathrm{FD}_{r,6}$ from 19.22 to 5.91. On RAEv2, at the 70% Recall point, it reduces $\mathrm{FD}_{r,6}$ from 16.83 to 3.18.

</details>


### [11] [TRiST: Topology-Reinforced Swin Transformer for Medical Image Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07431)
*Pengfei Gu,Huimin Li,Guangyu Meng,Hao Zheng,Haoteng Tang,Bin Fu,Danny Z. Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TRiST将2D持续同调（H0/H1）拓扑信息嵌入Swin Transformer的多尺度特征构建、精化与注意力中，在五个医学图像数据集上优于Swin基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分层ViT在医学图像分析中表现优异，但未在其多尺度表示中显式建模拓扑结构（连通分量、环等），而这类拓扑模式在从微小病灶到器官边界的不同尺度上普遍存在。

Method: 提出Topology-Reinforced Swin Transformer（TRiST），含三个组件：(1) 分层多尺度拓扑表示算法，在四个Swin原生阶段分别计算2D持续同调（H0与H1），生成与各阶段token网格对齐的描述子，而非下采样细尺度拓扑；(2) Band-Aware H0/H1残差精化模块，用门控残差网络分别处理短、中、长持续性信息；(3) Persistence-Band渐进式注意力偏置，将阶段特定描述子转为可学习的key显著性分数，直接修正移位窗口注意力logits。实例化为分类模型Topo-SwinV2-B与分割模型Topo-Swin-UNet，保持原有Swin骨干。

Result: 在五个医学图像数据集上，相较Swin基线取得一致提升，并与现有拓扑增强方法相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 将分层持续同调拓扑描述子以阶段对齐方式注入Swin的特征与注意力机制，是一种有效且骨干兼容的医学图像分析增强方案。

Abstract: Medical images contain topological patterns, such as connected components and loops, at scales ranging from small lesions to organ boundaries. Although hierarchical Vision Transformers have advanced medical image analysis, they do not explicitly incorporate topology throughout their multiscale representations. We propose Topology-Reinforced Swin Transformer (TRiST), a framework that integrates topological information into feature construction, refinement, and attention. First, a Hierarchical Multi-Scale Topology Representation algorithm computes 2D persistent homology (H0 and H1) independently at four native Swin stages, producing descriptors aligned with each stage's token grid rather than downsampling fine-scale topology. Second, a Band-Aware H0/H1 Residual Refinement module uses gated residual networks to process short-, medium-, and long-persistence information separately. Third, a Persistence-Band Progressive Attention Bias converts stage-specific descriptors into learned key-saliency scores that directly modify shifted-window attention logits. We instantiate TRiST as Topo-SwinV2-B for classification and Topo-Swin-UNet for segmentation, preserving the underlying Swin-based backbones. Experiments on five medical image datasets show consistent improvements over Swin-based baselines and competitive performance relative to existing topology-augmented methods.

</details>


### [12] [Where Do Reported Long-Video QA Gains Come From? A Controlled Audit of Frame Selection, Evidence Injection, and Expert Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07329)
*Shih-Yao Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Long-video QA is typically framed as frame selection; a controlled audit (VideoRouteAudit) holds backbone, budget, decoding, prompt and grader fixed and varies one decision at a time, showing that no tested selector significantly beats uniform sampling on the full Video-MME long bucket, that the same contrasts are unstable at small subsets, that naive ASR gains were artifacts, and that the largest observed gain from model routing is mostly a model swap rather than audio-visual evidence use.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频问答的主流范式是"选帧"，但选帧的收益常与两类替代方案混淆：注入非视觉证据（如ASR文本）和路由到更强的回答模型。并且小样本子集上的结果易被放大或反转。作者希望用一个严格控制变量的审计框架，把"选帧"这一决策与另外两类决策分离，量化各自真实贡献。

Method: 提出 VideoRouteAudit 审计流程：固定骨干模型、帧预算、解码策略、提示词与评估器（grader），每次只改变一个决策变量。实验在 Video-MME full long bucket（n=900，K=8）上进行，与多种选择器对比；同时考察级联（cascade）路由与换用回答模型；ASR 文本按"置于问题前/后"两种位置注入；并做重采样 200 项子集的稳定性分析（sign-reversal 频率统计）。

Result: 在 n=900 full bucket 上，没有任何选择器显著优于均匀采样：最佳 selection-only 估计为 +1.9 pt（95% CI [-1.2,+5.0]），级联为 +1.2 pt [-1.7,+4.1]，均在 ±5 pt 内与均匀采样等价。在常用的 n=200 子集尺度上，四个对比中三个效应放大至 3.8 倍，一个发生符号反转，重采样 200 项子集在 35.8% 的抽样中反转全桶效应的符号。ASR 方面：XD-Violence 上文本追加在问题之后使准确率下降 10.0 pt，置于之前无影响；UCF-Crime 上无论位置均下降 13.0 pt；Video-MME 上置于最前约提升 4 pt（Qwen2-VL 为 7 pt），对均匀采样与级联相同（事后、未校正）。换用回答模型给出最大对比（MUSIC-AVQA +15.1 pt，最差类型 +33.3 pt），但同一音视频专家在静音音轨下仍保留 92% 增益（音轨不匹配时 69%），说明该对比主要源于模型替换而非模态证据利用。

Conclusion: 在严格控制变量的审计下，帧选择相对均匀采样无显著收益，小样本子集的结论不可靠；此前观察到的 ASR 增益部分是选择效应叠加开发前缀造成的伪影，且 ASR 的效果对插入位置高度敏感、方向不一致；模型路由的最大增益主要来自模型本身能力而非多模态证据。作者主张以全桶样本、单变量控制、公开逐项预测与可复算脚本作为长视频问答评估的标准做法。

Abstract: Long-video question answering is usually studied as frame selection: which few frames should a vision-language model see? We introduce VideoRouteAudit, a controlled audit that separates this choice from two alternatives, injecting non-visual evidence and routing to another answering model. It holds the backbone, frame budget, decoding, prompt and grader fixed while varying one decision at a time. On the full Video-MME long bucket (n=900, K=8), no tested selector significantly improves over uniform sampling. The largest selection-only estimate is +1.9 pt (95% CI [-1.2,+5.0]) and our cascade is +1.2 pt [-1.7,+4.1]; all are equivalent to uniform within +/-5 pt. The same contrasts are unstable at the n=200 subset scale commonly used: three of four are up to 3.8x larger, one reverses sign, and resampled 200-item subsets reverse the full-bucket effect's sign in 35.8% of draws. The audit also corrects our own first results. The anomaly-domain "+ASR" arms injected no text, so the pooled +4.7 pt gain was selection plus a development prefix. With transcripts, ASR lowers XD-Violence accuracy by 10.0 pt when appended after the question and not at all when placed before it, and lowers UCF-Crime by 13.0 pt either way; on Video-MME, placing it first adds about 4 pt (7 on Qwen2-VL) to uniform sampling and to the cascade alike (post hoc, unadjusted). Swapping the answering model gives the largest contrast (+15.1 pt on MUSIC-AVQA, +33.3 pt on its worst type), but the same audio-visual expert with its soundtrack silenced keeps 92% of the gain (69% with a mismatched soundtrack), so the contrast is mostly a model swap. Code, per-item predictions and scripts that recompute every statistic are available at https://github.com/mikeslin-ir/long-video-qa-audit.

</details>


### [13] [Self-Supervised Diffusion Models for Handwritten Signature Verification via Ruin Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07810)
*Peirong Zhang,Zhenhua Yang,Jiaxin Zhang,Moises Diaz,Miguel A. Ferrer,Umapada Pal,Wei Zhou,Lianwen Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SigDiffuser 提出基于扩散模型的自监督手写签名表征框架，通过"从废墟重建（RFR）"预训练任务对签名进行严重褶皱破坏后重建，在多语言数据上显著优于现有自监督与签名验证方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手写签名验证（HSV）的性能取决于表征判别性，但现有方法几乎全部依赖监督学习，自监督学习在该领域尚未被充分探索；作者希望验证极端破坏性预训练任务能否带来更强的签名表征。

Method: 提出 SigDiffuser 扩散式自监督框架。核心是预训练任务 Reconstruction from Ruins (RFR)：对签名施加严重褶皱，破坏其结构完整性与书写风格；视觉编码器从完整签名区域提取笔迹模式，引导去噪器重建被"摧毁"的区域，从而学习有意义的表征。另引入金字塔特征聚合模块（PFAM），通过分层聚合与调制连接编码器与去噪器以提升表征质量。实验在多语言签名数据集上开展。

Result: 在多语言签名数据集上，SigDiffuser 显著优于当前最先进的自监督方法与 HSV 模型；且增加褶皱严重程度可有效提升表征质量。

Conclusion: 极端破坏结合自监督扩散模型，可产生优于传统轻微形变（mild deformation）的签名表征，为 HSV 及其他生物特征识别领域开辟新路径。代码将公开。

Abstract: Handwritten signature verification (HSV) is a critical biometric technique in legal and forensic authentication, whose efficacy hinges on the discriminability of signature representations. Current HSV methods predominantly adopt supervised learning to enhance representation quality, leaving self-supervised learning largely unexplored. We propose \emph{SigDiffuser}, a diffusion-based self-supervised framework for signature representation and verification. At its core, \emph{SigDiffuser} introduces a novel pretext task termed \emph{Reconstruction from Ruins (RFR)}, which deliberately ``ruins'' signatures through severe crumpling, obliterating their structural integrity and writing styles. A visual encoder is employed to capture crucial handwriting patterns from intact signature regions, guiding the denoiser to reconstruct the ``ruined'' portions and learning meaningful representations. Additionally, we introduce a \emph{pyramidal feature aggregation module (PFAM)} to bridge the visual encoder and denoiser, enhancing representation quality via hierarchical aggregation and modulation. Extensive experiments on multilingual signature datasets demonstrate that \emph{SigDiffuser} significantly outperforms both state-of-the-art SSL methods and HSV models. We further show that increasing the crumpling severity on signatures effectively improves representation quality. Notably, our work demonstrates that extreme destruction, coupled with self-supervised diffusion models, can yield superior signature representation compared to traditional mild deformations, paving new avenues for HSV and other biometric domains. Code will be publicly available.

</details>


### [14] [BiGrounder: Bidirectional Training Unlocks the Potential of GUI Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07765)
*Dengyao Shi,Tianyu Fu,Fei Hu,Anyang Su,Hanning Wang,Shuo Wang,Minghui Wu,Chenxu Zhao,Zhen Lei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出双向奖励范式（BiGrounder）：SFT阶段训练坐标→指令的逆向生成能力，RL阶段用策略采样语义一致的指令变体并回grounding到原目标，以循环一致性门控耦合语义质量与定位成功，实现自增强，4B模型在五个基准上取得有竞争力精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI grounding将任务形式化为从指令预测动作坐标对，端到端仅由最终坐标监督；训练集对同一目标坐标通常只提供少量描述，而真实场景中同一目标的表述变体极多，导致对语言变化的鲁棒性不足。近期引入强化学习的范式虽提升定位精度，但模型在RL前探索能力有限，即便训练数据相同，增益也受限。

Method: 核心为双向奖励的自强化框架。(1) SFT阶段进行双向训练，使模型兼具正向（指令→坐标）与逆向（坐标→动作描述）生成能力；(2) RL阶段在策略上（on-policy）采样语义一致的指令变体，并将每个变体重新grounding回其目标；(3) 设计循环一致性门控，将语义质量与定位成功绑定，把模型自身的指令策略转化为持续刷新的强化轨迹来源。

Result: 在五个grounding基准上评估，4B参数模型取得有竞争力的准确率，验证了所提训练范式的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过逆向生成+循环一致性自增强，可在不依赖大规模人工指令变体标注的情况下缓解数据描述稀疏与RL探索不足的问题；4B规模的模型即可在多个基准上达到有竞争力表现，说明训练范式设计比单纯扩大数据或模型更关键。

Abstract: Graphical User Interface (GUI) grounding aims to map natural language instructions to precise operational coordinates, serving as a critical task for autonomous agent interaction. Current approaches formalize GUI grounding as predicting action-coordinate pairs from instructions, with end-to-end training supervised solely by final coordinates. Training datasets typically provide only a small number of descriptions per target coordinate, whereas real-world scenarios involve a much larger set of varied descriptions for the same target, which limits robustness to linguistic variation. While recent GUI grounding paradigms have begun employing reinforcement learning to enhance localization accuracy, models with limited exploratory capability before reinforcement yield constrained improvements even with identical training data during the reinforcement phase. To overcome these limitations, we propose a bidirectional reward paradigm that enables model self-reinforcement. During SFT, bidirectional training equips the model to generate action descriptions from target coordinates. During RL, the model samples semantically consistent instruction variations on-policy and grounds each variation back to its target. A cycle-consistency gate couples semantic quality to grounding success, turning the model's own instruction policy into a source of continually refreshed reinforcement trajectories. Our 4B-parameter model achieves competitive accuracy across five grounding benchmarks in our evaluation, indicating the effectiveness of the proposed training paradigm. https://github.com/Spell-shi/BiGrounder

</details>


### [15] [Interactive Visual Gym: Evaluating Visual Interaction Models with Simulated Users in Interactive Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08286)
*Chenhao Zheng,Zixian Ma,Kai Li,Kanchana Ranasinghe,Jieyu Zhang,Artemis Panagopoulou,Weikai Huang,Jianxu Shangguan,Yuanyuan Tan,Silvio Savarese,Juan Carlos Niebles,Ranjay Krishna,Ran Xu,Honglu Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Interactive Visual Gym 闭环评估与训练框架，构建 IVG-Bench（315 个交互场景），揭示当前顶尖 VLM 作为视觉交互模型的显著不足，并验证闭环训练可带来提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 支持用户执行持续性物理任务的视觉交互模型，其回复会改变用户后续行为与任务结果，因此必须闭环评估；而现有基准多依赖离线回放，无法捕捉这种交互反馈效应。

Method: 联合模拟可执行任务环境与人类用户：贡献覆盖烹饪、家务操作、移动界面三个领域的模拟器及人机对齐的动作空间，并配备 persona 条件化的用户模拟框架（含受控执行错误与偏离计划行为）；实例化为含 315 个场景的 IVG-Bench；以 SOTA VLM 进行评估，辅以人类研究验证评测协议，并用 GRPO 做闭环训练概念验证。

Result: 最佳模型 Gemini 4 Argon 在时限内仅完成 53.4% 任务；在事实依据与可操作指导方面存在明显弱点；考虑响应延迟时常因过慢而无法阻止错误。人类研究显示交互质量指标与参与者偏好的一致率为 78.5%。GRPO 实验中，Qwen3.5-4B 在烹饪环境下任务成功率与交互质量均提升。

Conclusion: 闭环仿真框架既可提供更真实的视觉交互模型评测，也能作为有效的训练信号来源，弥补离线回放基准的不足。

Abstract: Visual interaction models that support users during ongoing physical tasks require evaluation in closed loop, where assistant responses can change subsequent user behavior and task outcomes. Existing benchmarks largely rely on offline replay and therefore cannot capture these effects. We introduce Interactive Visual Gym, a framework for closed-loop evaluation and training of visual interaction models through joint simulation of executable task environments and human users. We contribute three simulators that recreate everyday tasks and human-aligned action spaces across cooking, household manipulation, and mobile-interface domains, together with a persona-conditioned user simulation harness that collaborates with the assistant while incorporating controlled execution errors and other out-of-plan behaviors. We instantiate the framework as IVG-Bench, a benchmark with 315 interaction scenarios. Evaluating state-of-the-art VLMs as visual interaction models reveals substantial limitations: the best-performing model, Gemini 4 Argon, completes only 53.4% of tasks within the time limit, exhibits significant weaknesses in factual grounding and actionable guidance, and is often too slow to prevent errors when response latency is considered. We verify our evaluation protocol with a human study which finds 78.5% agreement between our interaction-quality metric and participant preferences. We further show that the environments provide useful learning signals: in a proof-of-concept GRPO experiment, closed-loop training improves both task success and interaction quality for Qwen3.5-4B model in our cooking environment.

</details>


### [16] [DART: Domain-Agnostic Residual Transfer for Generalist Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08271)
*Muhammad Aqeel,Maham Nazir,Marco Cristani,Francesco Setti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DART 是一个即插即用框架，将 normal-abnormal 引导的 GAD 中的残差分解为"异常相关"与"域特定"两部分，以缓解残差的域纠缠并提升跨域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 normal-abnormal 引导的 GAD 方法虽利用异常参考提升性能，但其残差中混杂了可迁移的异常线索与纹理、几何、采集条件等域特定外观因素。这种残差的域纠缠会限制跨域迁移，并在新域上产生虚假激活。

Method: DART 由两部分组成：(1) Residual Disentanglement Module（残差解耦模块），在保留异常判别信息的同时抑制域信息；(2) Cross-Domain Meta-Training（跨域元训练），在源域间构造 episode 以使解耦真正生效。方法为架构无关设计，可插入现有 GAD 流水线中残差计算与检测头之间。

Result: 在 MVTec AD、VisA 与 BraTS 上的实验表明，DART 在多种基线上均带来一致提升，且域偏移越强，增益越大。

Conclusion: 将残差显式解耦为异常相关与域特定成分，并结合跨域元训练，是提升 GAD 跨域迁移性的有效且通用的策略。

Abstract: Generalist Anomaly Detection (GAD) aims to detect anomalies in unseen domains using only a few reference samples. Recent normal-abnormal guided methods improve this setting by exploiting abnormal references, but their residuals often mix transferable anomaly cues with domain-specific appearance factors such as texture, geometry, and acquisition conditions. This residual domain entanglement limits cross-domain transfer and may produce spurious activations on novel domains. We propose DART, a plug-in framework for normal-abnormal guided GAD that learns to separate residuals into anomaly-relevant and domain-specific components. DART combines a Residual Disentanglement Module, which preserves anomaly-discriminative information while suppressing domain information, with Cross-Domain Meta-Training, which forms episodes across source domains to make the separation effective. The method is architecture-agnostic and can be inserted between residual computation and the detection head of existing GAD pipelines. Experiments on MVTec AD, VisA, and BraTS show consistent improvements over multiple base methods, with the largest gains under stronger domain shifts. https://github.com/aqeeelmirza/DART

</details>


### [17] [Towards Real-Time Full-Waveform LiDAR Transformers via Intensity-Guided Token Reduction and Physics-Aware Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07613)
*Kotaro Oishi,Kazuma Ikeda,Ryosei Hara,Ryo Yoshida,Mariko Isogawa,Kentaro Yoshioka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文针对全波形LiDAR（FWL）Transformer的计算冗余与泛化瓶颈，提出基于强度分布的token剪枝合并机制FWL-ToPM及保持波形结构的正则化数据增强FWLAug，在Ghost-FWL上实现8倍以上加速与近9点F1提升，并成为首个实时且精度同时提升的FWL Transformer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiDAR在雾、雨、透明物体、低反射表面等恶劣条件下脆弱，全波形LiDAR通过直方图形式保留完整回波（多径回波、弱信号）以缓解该问题。但现有基于Transformer的FWL方法存在两大瓶颈：低强度背景区域冗余token造成高计算开销；标注数据稀缺导致泛化能力受限。

Method: 利用FWL数据固有的物理结构：1）FWL-ToPM——依据强度分布进行token剪枝与合并，选择性减少token同时保留关键波形内容；2）FWLAug——作为正则化方法的数据增强框架，保持波形结构，保留每条射线的ToF波形与视锥几何，以提升对未见场景的泛化。

Result: 在Ghost-FWL基准上，FWL-ToPM在实时吞吐下实现超过8倍加速，同时相较此前SOTA取得近9点F1提升；FWLAug在标准基准与未见场景的OOD测试集上均进一步提升性能。代码与OOD数据集已公开。

Conclusion: 通过挖掘FWL物理结构同时解决效率与泛化两大瓶颈，首次实现实时且精度提升的FWL Transformer，推动FWL感知走向实际部署。

Abstract: LiDAR is a critical sensor for autonomous driving and robotics, yet it remains vulnerable to adverse conditions such as fog, rain, transparent objects, and low-reflectance surfaces. Full-waveform LiDAR (FWL) addresses these limitations by capturing the complete return waveform as a histogram, preserving rich reflection characteristics including multi-path echoes and weak signals. While recent Transformer-based approaches have demonstrated promise on FWL data, two fundamental bottlenecks remain: high computational cost from redundant tokens in low-intensity background regions, and limited generalization due to scarce annotated training data. We address both bottlenecks by exploiting the physical structure inherent to FWL data. First, we propose FWL-ToPM, a token pruning and merging mechanism that leverages intensity distributions to selectively reduce tokens while retaining critical waveform content. Second, we introduce FWLAug, a data augmentation framework designed as a regularization method that preserves waveform structure, retaining the Time-of-Flight waveform of each ray and the frustum geometry, to improve generalization to unseen scenes. Evaluated on the Ghost-FWL benchmark, FWL-ToPM achieves over 8 times speedup at real-time throughput alongside a nearly 9-point F1 gain over the prior state of the art, establishing the first real-time FWL Transformer that simultaneously improves accuracy. FWLAug further improves performance on both the standard benchmark and an out-of-distribution test set of unseen scenes. Together, these methods push FWL-based perception toward practical deployment. The code and the OOD dataset are publicly available and can be accessed via the project page: https://keio-csg.github.io/FWL-ToPM

</details>


### [18] [Multimodal Information Interactions Bottleneck for Multimodal Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07718)
*Liangjian Wen,Jiangtao Zheng,Jiang Duan,Qun Dai,Yong Dai,Jianzhuang Liu,Jun Wang,Shu-Dong Huang,Xinwang Liu,Zenglin Xu,Zhao Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多模态信息交互瓶颈MIIB，基于部分信息分解显式编码冗余、独特与协同信息，实现多模态自监督表示学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态对比学习方法依赖强多视图冗余假设，仅对齐共享语义，忽略了模态特有信息与协同信息，导致表示次优。

Method: 以部分信息分解为理论基础，设计双路径架构（模态特有编码+多模态融合），用基于熵的可处理代理目标强制最小充分表示，结合变分界与基于分数的熵估计，无需显式互信息估计。

Result: 在多个多样化基准上相较当前最先进的多模态自监督方法取得一致性提升。

Conclusion: 显式建模冗余、独特与协同信息可有效提升多模态自监督表示质量，并抑制冗余信息。

Abstract: Multimodal self-supervised learning is challenged by heterogeneity, unequal informativeness, and complex information interactions across multimodal data sources such as images, text, audio, and tabular data. While contrastive methods effectively align shared semantics, they typically rely on strong multi-view redundancy assumptions, overlooking modality-specific and synergistic information, thus leading to suboptimal representations. We propose the Multimodal Information Interactions Bottleneck (MIIB), a principled framework grounded in Partial Information Decomposition that explicitly encodes redundant, unique, and synergistic information within multimodal interactions. MIIB employs a dual-path model architecture for modality-specific encoding and multimodal fusion, optimized using surrogate tractable entropy-based objectives that enforce minimal sufficient representations without requiring explicit mutual information estimation. By leveraging variational bounds and score-based entropy estimation, MIIB efficiently captures complex cross-modal interactions while suppressing superfluous information. Experiments across diverse benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements over state-of-the-art multimodal self-supervised methods. Code is available at https://github.com/wanglingzaishi/MIIB.

</details>


### [19] [Fast and Accurate Hand Estimation in Pixels, Interaction and World Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07711)
*Jinkun Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FAH单目手部网格估计模型族，通过关键点/表面点联合预测与网格拟合降低误差，兼顾速度与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单目手部网格估计器通常预测骨骼参数再投影得到2D关键点，这种方式与2D预测的一致性不足、网格误差偏大。作者发现直接预测的3D关节与2D预测更吻合，且将网格拟合到预测点可降低网格误差。

Method: 基于SAM 3D Body手部分支，经微调与特征蒸馏构建FAH模型族；预测对应的2D与3D关节及表面点，拟合MANO或MHR-Hand参数化手模型；可选地在推理时估计解剖部位与接触（无需物体几何）；采用因果细化降低加速度误差，并进行相机估计将手置于世界坐标。

Result: 在图像空间对齐、手部几何、接触估计及世界坐标跟踪方面均优于所评估基线。默认ViT-L模型在RTX 4070 Ti SUPER上含网格拟合达65 fps，开启部位与接触估计为49 fps；最小ViT-S模型在不含网格拟合、部位与接触时达125 fps。

Conclusion: FAH以点预测加拟合的范式替代传统的参数预测范式，在手部网格估计的精度与实时性上取得良好平衡，并提供部位、接触与全局跟踪等扩展能力。

Abstract: Monocular hand mesh estimators commonly predict rig parameters and project the mesh to obtain 2D keypoints. We find that directly predicted 3D joints agree better with 2D predictions, and fitting a mesh to predicted points reduces mesh error. We present FAH (Fast and Accurate Hand Estimation), a family of models built on SAM 3D Body's hand branch through fine-tuning and feature distillation. The models predict corresponding 2D and 3D joints and surface points, fit MANO or MHR-Hand, and optionally estimate anatomical parts and contact without object geometry at inference. Causal refinement reduces acceleration error, and camera estimation places the hands in world space. Experiments show improvements in image-space alignment, hand geometry, contact estimation and world-space tracking over the evaluated baselines. The default ViT-L model runs at 65 fps with mesh fitting on an RTX 4070 Ti SUPER, or 49 fps with parts and contact enabled. The smallest ViT-S model reaches 125 fps without mesh fitting, parts or contact. Code: https://github.com/noahcao/FAH.

</details>


### [20] [ReferClaw: Disambiguating User Intent for Egocentric Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07683)
*Hangyu Qin,Junbin Xiao,Angela Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ReferClaw——一个将自我中心流式视频问答建模为交互式澄清过程的智能体框架，通过三个协作智能体先澄清用户意图再作答，在增强基准上提升答案准确率与答案-定位联合性能，并具零样本跨数据集迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我中心 AI 助手的用户提问常简短且指代歧义（如"我把它放哪了？"可能指多个物体），仅凭视频与对话历史无法唯一确定指代对象。现有 VideoQA 系统倾向于直接给出单一解释，容易产生"貌似合理但与用户意图不符"的答案，因此需要显式消解用户意图。

Method: 将自我中心流式 QA 形式化为交互过程：(1) 三个协作智能体分别负责候选解释识别、发起澄清提问、基于已消解意图与视觉证据生成答案；(2) 设计有界、成本感知的策略，权衡期望信息增益与用户负担，并限制澄清轮数与问题复杂度；(3) 通过意图特定技能更新以及带智能体级信用分配的过程奖励引导策略优化，从交互中学习。

Result: 在增强的自我中心 QA 基准上，答案准确率以及"答案+定位"联合性能均优于现有基线；同时实现跨数据集的零样本迁移。

Conclusion: 交互式澄清是解决自我中心流式辅助中在线用户意图消解问题的可行且实用途径。

Abstract: Users of egocentric AI assistants often ask short, ambiguous questions whose intended referents cannot be uniquely resolved from video and dialogue history. For example, "Where did I leave it?" may refer to several previously handled objects. Existing VideoQA systems typically commit to a single interpretation, risking plausible but intention-misaligned answers. We introduce ReferClaw, an agentic framework that formulates egocentric streaming QA as an interactive process: the assistant asks clarifying questions to resolve user intent before answering. Three cooperating agents identify candidate interpretations, request clarification, and generate answers grounded in the resolved intent and visual evidence. A bounded, cost-aware policy balances expected information gain against user effort while limiting clarification turns and question complexity. Referclaw learns from interaction through intent-specific skill updates and process-reward-guided policy optimization with agent-level credit assignment. Evaluations on augmented egocentric QA benchmarks demonstrate improved answer accuracy and joint answer-and-grounding performance over existing baselines, alongside zero-shot transfer across datasets. These findings support interactive clarification as a practical approach to resolving online user intent in egocentric streaming assistance.

</details>


### [21] [Neural Image Compression with Internal-External Prior Fusion and Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07644)
*Tiancheng Si,Wenlong Xu,Dahua Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出IE-PFA上下文模型，以编解码器共享的可学习字典作为外部先验，与内部先验融合对齐，实现单网络64档变码率编码，在七个基准上取得SOTA率失真性能，相对VTM-22.0-Intra平均BD-Rate节省21.65%，仅29.9M参数，编码61ms/解码52ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习图像压缩的上下文模型主要依赖当前图像内部先验，忽略了自然图像间共享的共性模式与典型纹理，限制建模能力。

Method: 基于四叉树空间-通道联合建模构建Internal-External Prior Fusion-Alignment (IE-PFA)上下文模型：以编解码器共享的可学习字典作为外部先验；提出RWKV-based Spatial-Channel Prior Fusion (RSCPF)模块，以线性复杂度沿空间与通道维度融合内外先验；引入仅在训练阶段使用的Self-Distilled Prior Alignment (SDPA)机制对齐二者，不增加推理开销；支持单网络64个质量等级的变码率编码，并使用定制CUDA算子加速。

Result: 在七个基准数据集上取得SOTA率失真性能，相对VTM-22.0-Intra平均BD-Rate节省21.65%，参数量仅29.9M，在先进神经编解码器中速度最快（编码61 ms，解码52 ms）。

Conclusion: 将外部字典先验与内部先验在空间-通道维度高效融合并对齐，可在低参数、低延迟条件下显著提升学习图像压缩的率失真性能，且训练期蒸馏对齐与CUDA加速兼顾了性能与实用性。

Abstract: Existing context models for learned image com- pression rely primarily on internal priors from the current image, overlooking common patterns and typical textures shared across natural images. To address this limitation, we propose an Internal-External Prior Fusion-Alignment (IE-PFA) context model built upon quadtree spatial-channel joint modeling, where a learnable dictionary shared by the encoder and decoder serves as an external prior. Specifically, an RWKV-based Spatial- Channel Prior Fusion (RSCPF) module efficiently fuses inter- nal and external priors along spatial and channel dimensions with linear complexity, while a training-only Self-Distilled Prior Alignment (SDPA) mechanism aligns them without inference overhead. Moreover, our model supports variable-rate coding over 64 quality levels within a single network and is accelerated by custom CUDA operators. Experiments on seven benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art rate-distortion performance, achiev- ing an average BD-Rate saving of 21.65% over VTM-22.0-Intra, with only 29.9M parameters and the fastest coding speed among advanced neural codecs (61 ms encoding and 52 ms decoding). Code is available at https://github.com/stcnjtech/DCVC-X-Intra.

</details>


### [22] [Improving Long-Tail Detection via Layout-Instruction Conditioned Diffusion Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07553)
*Xinyu Zhang,Xiaodi Wang,Yanpeng Sun,Lingfeng Yang,Jingdong Wang,Lingqiao Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Tail-SD，一种以布局指令为条件、支持文本/布局/混合输入的可控扩散合成框架，用于提升长尾目标检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型合成数据可改进目标检测，但长尾性能受限于稀有类别样本稀缺，且条件控制方式僵化（仅支持文本或仅支持布局），难以充分覆盖长尾物体。

Method: 构建统一的条件扩散框架，接受boxes、masks和/或prompts的任意组合作为布局指令输入；为在异构条件下训练单一模型，引入多任务交叉注意力模块与多任务嵌入token，以提升生成质量与多样性。

Result: 在COCO与LVIS上的大量实验表明，混合条件扩散合成能持续提升检测性能；在LVIS val上取得+4.89% / +5.68%的稀有类别Box/Mask AP提升。

Conclusion: 混合条件可控扩散合成在缓解长尾目标检测问题上具有较强潜力。

Abstract: Synthetic data from diffusion models can improve object detection, but long-tail performance is still limited by scarce rare classes and inflexible conditioning (e.g., text-only or layout-only inputs). In this paper, we propose Tail-SD, a layout-instruction conditioned stable diffusion framework that supports text, layouts, or their hybrid, enabling controllable synthesis from boxes, masks, and/or prompts to better cover the long-tail objects. To train a single model across heterogeneous conditions, we introduce a multi-task cross-attention module and multi-task embedding tokens, improving generation quality and diversity. Extensive experiments on COCO and LVIS demonstrate that diffusion synthesis with hybrid conditioning consistently benefits detection, e.g., on LVIS val, it achieves +4.89% / +5.68% rare Box/Mask AP, highlighting strong potential for addressing long-tail challenges.

</details>


### [23] [OptIMerge: Optimization-Based Merging of Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07543)
*Viraj Shah,Ashwini Pokle,Peyman Milanfar,Mauricio Delbracio,Hossein Talebi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OptIMerge：一种基于梯度优化的I2I扩散模型合并框架，通过学习参数高效、架构感知的合并系数避免破坏性参数干扰，以<0.4%的额外参数实现接近联合训练的密集像素任务多任务能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 朴素权重平均（model souping）在多任务模型合并中常因特征表示结构差异导致严重性能退化。现有合并方法多面向LLM和T2I扩散模型，而面向图像修复等密集像素级I2I扩散模型的合并研究几乎空白：I2I专家在卷积核中编码相互冲突的空间先验（如各向异性运动模糊与各向同性离焦模糊），朴素soup和LLM式逐元素合成都产生可见伪影。

Method: 梯度驱动的优化框架，而非启发式标量。在小规模图像集（100–500张）上最小化多任务扩散损失来学习高表达力且参数高效的合并系数；采用架构感知参数化：注意力层使用带显式通道偏置的结构化低秩特征对齐，卷积层使用逐核合并系数及低秩Cayley变换（以正交旋转对齐通道而不扭曲滤波器）。专家模型保持冻结，仅优化轻量合并参数。

Result: 合并参数仅占网络规模<0.4%，即在少量图像上即可完成优化；所得模型具备同时多任务能力，性能可媲美计算昂贵的联合训练，并显著优于零样本model soup。

Conclusion: 针对I2I扩散模型密集任务合并中空间先验冲突问题，架构感知、低秩约束的梯度式合并参数化解法在极低参数开销下有效缓解破坏性干扰，为像素级多任务模型合并提供了可行路径。

Abstract: The arithmetic merging of weights from multiple task-specific models fine-tuned from a shared base model, commonly known as model souping, has emerged as a popular strategy for multi-task learning without the computational overhead of joint retraining. However, naive scalar weight averaging frequently leads to severe performance degradation when the constituent models learn structurally divergent feature representations, leading to destructive parameter interference. We study this problem in a largely unexplored setting: merging image-to-image (I2I) diffusion models fine-tuned for dense, pixel-accurate tasks such as image restoration. Unlike the large language models (LLMs) and text-to-image (T2I) diffusion models for which most merging methods were developed, I2I experts encode conflicting spatial priors (e.g., anisotropic motion-blur vs. isotropic defocus-blur) in their convolutional filters, where both naive soup and LLM-style element-wise merging introduce visible artifacts. We introduce OptIMerge, a gradient-based optimization framework: instead of relying on heuristic scalars, OptIMerge learns highly expressive, yet parameter-efficient merger coefficients by minimizing a multi-task diffusion loss over a small set of 100-500 images. OptIMerge parameterizes the merge with architecture-aware transformations: for attention layers, a structured low-rank feature alignment with explicit channel biases; for convolutional layers, per-kernel merger coefficients, and a low-rank Cayley transform that aligns channels by orthogonal rotation without distorting the filters. By keeping the experts frozen and optimizing only these lightweight merger parameters (equivalent to <0.4% of the network footprint), OptIMerge achieves simultaneous multi-task capabilities that rival computationally expensive joint training and decisively outperform zero-shot model soups.

</details>


### [24] [Reviving the Discarded High-Resolution Feature for Transformer-Based Tiny Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07994)
*Zehao Wang,Si Wu,Guiping Cao,Xin Li,Yaowei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ReDHF框架，通过"复活"被DETR类检测器丢弃的最高分辨率特征来提升微小目标检测性能，包含CSDF、EGSS、FSQI三个组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微小目标仅占少量像素，细粒度信息在下采样中易丢失；最高分辨率特征虽保留丰富局部细节，但DETR类检测器因计算成本高而弃用，直接引入又会带来密集背景噪声。

Method: 三个协同组件：(1) Cross-Scale Deformable Fusion (CSDF)：以适中代价构建语义增强的最高分辨率特征，并将其细粒度细节注入浅层特征；(2) Ellipse-Guided Shallow Supervision (EGSS)：通过几何自适应辅助监督，引导增强特征学习更可靠的分类与定位先验；(3) Full-Scale Query Interaction (FSQI)：使解码器查询同时访问低层细节与高层语义，聚合更丰富的微小目标线索。

Result: 在多种DETR变体与多个基线上一致提升，并在评估基准上达到SOTA：最佳变体在AI-TOD-v2上+1.4 AP、VisDrone上+1.6 AP、SODA-D上+1.5 AP，在tiny与small目标上增益尤为显著。

Conclusion: 有效复用被丢弃的高分辨率特征可在可控成本下显著改善DETR类检测器的微小目标检测能力，且具备跨模型、跨数据集的通用性。

Abstract: Tiny object detection remains challenging because tiny instances occupy only a few pixels, and their fine-grained details are easily lost during feature downsampling. Although the highest-resolution feature preserves rich local details for tiny objects, DETR-like detectors often discard it due to its large computational cost, while directly reintroducing this feature also brings dense background noise. To address this problem, we propose ReDHF, a novel framework that Revives the Discarded High-resolution Feature for tiny object detection. ReDHF has three cooperative components. Cross-Scale Deformable Fusion (CSDF) first constructs a semantically enhanced highest-resolution feature and injects its fine-grained details into the shallow feature under a moderate cost. Ellipse-Guided Shallow Supervision (EGSS) then guides the enhanced feature to learn more reliable classification and localization priors by applying geometry-adaptive auxiliary supervision. Full-Scale Query Interaction (FSQI) further enables decoder queries to access both low-level details and high-level semantic information, allowing them to aggregate richer cues for tiny objects. Extensive experiments show that ReDHF consistently improves multiple baselines across different DETR variants and achieves state-of-the-art performance on the evaluated benchmarks. The best ReDHF variants surpass their baselines by +1.4 AP on AI-TOD-v2, +1.6 AP on VisDrone, and +1.5 AP on SODA-D, with especially notable gains on tiny and small objects.

</details>


### [25] [StitchEdit: Stitching Depth Priors into Image Editors via Per-Layer Gradient Probing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07922)
*Qin Guo,Dan Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 现有扩散模型文本图像编辑在依赖3D结构理解的空间编辑上表现不佳。作者通过逐层梯度探测发现编辑与深度目标在浅层兼容、深层冲突，提出StitchEdit：先用轻量深度去噪适配器激活骨干几何先验，再以探测引导的逐层学习率微调适配器，在新建的StitchEditBench上同时超越指令式与深度条件基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本引导的扩散图像编辑难以处理需要3D场景结构理解的空间编辑（物体操控、视角变换、遮挡操控）。已有工作（如Marigold）表明预训练扩散骨干本身蕴含强几何先验，可被激活用于深度估计，但如何将这些先验"改道"服务于编辑仍无解；朴素地用深度条件做编辑要么忽略几何、要么损害画质，原因不明。

Method: 1) 逐层梯度探测（per-layer gradient probing），分析编辑目标与深度目标在各Transformer层的梯度关系；2) 用轻量深度去噪适配器（depth-denoising adapter）激活骨干的几何能力；3) 以探测引导的逐层学习率调度对适配器进行选择性微调：在深度知识有益的层保留、在冲突的层剪除（prune）；4) 构建StitchEditBench基准，含150个几何依赖编辑，覆盖空间变换、遮挡操控与视角变化三类。

Result: 梯度探测显示：编辑与深度目标在早期Transformer层相互兼容，而在后期层明显冲突，这解释了朴素深度条件编辑失效的机理。在StitchEditBench上，StitchEdit在感知质量与几何保真度两方面均稳定优于基于指令的基线和深度条件基线。

Conclusion: 深度先验与编辑目标并非全局冲突，而是层间可分：通过适配器激活加逐层学习率调度即可兼顾两者。该"探测—定位冲突—选择性微调"的范式为复用预训练扩散模型的几何先验进行空间编辑提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Text-guided image editing with diffusion models struggles with spatial edits (e.g. manipulating objects, changing viewpoints, manipulating occlusions) that depend on the 3D structure understanding of a scene. Marigold and follow-up work demonstrate that a pretrained diffusion backbone already harbors strong geometric priors that can be activated for depth estimation, yet how to redirect these priors toward editing remains unresolved. Through per-layer gradient probing, we discover that the editing and depth objectives are compatible in early transformer layers but actively conflict in late layers, explaining why naive depth-conditioned editing either ignores geometry or degrades visual quality. We present StitchEdit, which first activates the backbone's geometric capability via a lightweight depth-denoising adapter, then selectively fine-tunes this adapter for editing using a probe-guided per-layer learning rate schedule that preserves depth knowledge where it helps and prunes it where it conflicts. On the proposed StitchEditBench, a benchmark of 150 geometry-dependent edits spanning spatial transformation, occlusion manipulation, and viewpoint change, StitchEdit consistently surpasses both instruction-based and depth-conditioned baselines on perceptual quality and geometric fidelity.

</details>


### [26] [PlayGen2: Learning Real-Time Playable Worlds with Explicit 3D State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07867)
*Jihai Zhang,Yuhan Zhou,Junyou Li,Deheng Ye,Qi Dou,Yu Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PlayGen2 是一个实时交互式世界模型，将学习到的 3D 状态演化与神经渲染解耦，通过持久化世界记忆（体素占据栅格 + 实体身份索引 3D 状态）实现可响应控制、空间持久性和一致交互的生成式游戏世界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅以生成帧作为循环世界表示，会把控制响应、空间结构与动态实体交互等需求绑定到视角相关的表观合成上，导致长期空间与时间一致性难以维持，交互能力受限。因此需要将世界状态演化与图像生成分离。

Method: 维护持久世界记忆：静态几何用体积占据栅格表示，玩家与其它实体用身份索引的 3D 状态表示。在固定大小局部区域内，世界状态模型联合学习动作条件实体动力学、几何补全与实体发现，并将更新提交至持久记忆；独立神经渲染器从世界状态的深度与语义投影合成第一人称观测，且不将生成图像回馈到状态预测。以 Doom 为测试平台。

Result: 成功学习含几何感知玩家运动与玩家–NPC 交互的世界动力学；在 MacBook Pro M4 Pro 上整条模拟与渲染管线后端延迟约 160ms；实现未见 Doom 地图上的零样本部署、通过单玩家模型组合实现多人交互，以及基于规则的状态干预实现可编程玩法。

Conclusion: 将 3D 状态演化与神经渲染分离的持久世界记忆架构，可在实时约束下维持长期一致性并支持交互、多人、可编程与跨地图泛化，体现了所学游戏世界模型的可迁移性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Building a playable world requires more than generating plausible video: it demands responsive control, persistent spatial structure, and coherent interactions with dynamic entities. Using generated frames as the sole recurrent world representation ties these requirements to view-dependent appearance synthesis, making it difficult to maintain long-term spatial and temporal consistency in generation and interaction. We present PlayGen2, a real-time interactive world model that separates learned 3D state evolution from neural rendering. PlayGen2 maintains a persistent world memory comprising static geometry represented by a volumetric occupancy grid and identity-indexed 3D states for the player and other entities. Within a fixed-size local region, its world-state model jointly learns action-conditioned entity dynamics, geometry completion, and entity discovery, committing updates to a persistent world memory. A separate neural renderer synthesizes first-person observations from depth and semantic projections of the world state, without feeding generated images back into state prediction. Using Doom as a testbed, we demonstrate that the model successfully learns world dynamics including geometry-aware player motion and player--NPC interactions. The framework is computationally efficient, enabling the complete simulation and rendering pipeline to achieve approximately 160ms backend latency on a MacBook Pro with an M4 Pro chip. Beyond single-player interaction, we demonstrate zero-shot deployment on unseen Doom maps, multiplayer interaction through the composition of single-player models, and programmable gameplay through rule-based state interventions. These capabilities demonstrate the transferability and extensibility of the learned game-world model. Code and pretrained model weights are publicly available.

</details>


### [27] [STAG-Net: Structure-Aware Attention over Landmark Groups for Lightweight Head Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07828)
*Asiri Gawesha Lindamulage,Sanka Mohottala,Dharshana Kasthurirathna,Nuwan Kodagoda*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出STAG-Net，一种基于关键点分组结构感知注意力的轻量头部姿态估计方法，在保持领先精度的同时将计算与训练/推理成本降低数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 头姿估计对人机交互与驾驶员监控很重要，但许多基于图像的方法成本过高，难以在资源受限场景部署。因此需要兼顾精度与低计算/低训练成本的方案。

Method: 从面部关键点回归头姿：采用运动感知选择、空间聚类、紧凑DeepSet编码器、自注意力和全局几何描述子构建STAG-Net；分别基于FaceMesh与FAN(Face Alignment Network)关键点开发两种变体。在300W-LP、AFLW2000、BIWI上按跨数据集与受试者独立两种协议评估。

Result: 在所有评估设置中，STAG-Net在基于关键点的方法中取得最低MAE；BIWI有效检测帧上FAN变体总体误差最低，跨数据集3.51°，受试者独立2.39°。回归器仅0.012–0.043M参数、24.1–83.6K FLOPs，FLOPs约为FSA-Net的千分之一。含关键点提取的FaceMesh流程在Tesla T4上达322.9 FPS（约为最快图像基线164.5 FPS的两倍），CPU上111.0 FPS。按数据集规模归一化后，训练样本处理速度比FSA-Net快7–8倍，比HopeNet与TokenHPE快百倍以上，完整训练在28分钟内完成。

Conclusion: STAG-Net在基于关键点的精度上达到领先水平，同时显著降低训练与推理成本，适合资源受限部署。

Abstract: Head pose estimation is important for human computer interaction and driver monitoring, but many image based methods remain too costly for resource constrained deployment. We propose STAG-Net, a structure-aware attention over landmark groups for lightweight head Pose estimation that estimates head pose from facial landmarks using movement aware selection, spatial clustering, compact DeepSet encoders, self attention, and a global geometry descriptor. We develop variants using FaceMesh and Face Alignment Network landmarks and evaluate them under cross dataset and subject independent protocols on 300W-LP, AFLW2000, and BIWI. STAG-Net achieves the lowest mean absolute error among all compared landmark based methods in every evaluated setting. On BIWI frames with valid detections, the FAN variant also records the lowest overall error among all compared methods, reaching $3.51^\circ$ under cross dataset evaluation and $2.39^\circ$ under subject independent evaluation. The regressors use only 0.012 to 0.043 million parameters and 24.1 to 83.6 thousand FLOPs, about three orders of magnitude fewer FLOPs than FSA-Net. Despite including landmark extraction and regression, the FaceMesh pipeline reaches 322.9 frames per second on a Tesla T4 GPU, nearly twice the 164.5 frames per second of the fastest measured image based baseline, and 111.0 frames per second on CPU. After normalization by dataset size, STAG-Net processes training samples seven to eight times faster than FSA-Net and more than one hundred times faster than HopeNet and TokenHPE, while complete training finishes within 28 minutes. These results show that STAG-Net combines leading landmark based accuracy with substantially lower training and inference cost.

</details>


### [28] [CMDiff: Left Atrial Scar Segmentation from 3D LGE MRI Using Conditional Mask Diffusion Model: A Feasibility Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07814)
*Jing Zhang,Bastien Bergere,Jonas Leite,Emilie Bollache,Mikaël Laredo,Alban Redheuil,Nadjia Kachenoura*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出条件掩膜扩散模型CMDiff用于左心房瘢痕分割，并在LASCARQS数据集上与确定性基线对比，结果显示方法可行但性能不及基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LGE心脏MRI可无创评估左心房瘢痕，该影像标志物与房颤消融预后相关。但瘢痕分割面临低对比度、心房壁薄、标注稀缺和极端类别不平衡等问题。条件扩散模型具备迭代式掩膜生成能力，但其在稀疏3D左心房瘢痕分割任务中的适用性尚不明确。

Method: 提出条件掩膜扩散模型CMDiff，使用动态编码器条件化的3D正弦UNet直接预测干净掩膜（clean-mask prediction）。在60例公开LASCARQS受试者上进行五折交叉验证，每折48例训练、12例留出，与确定性基线在相同样本上对比；同时在同一实现下比较clean-mask预测与噪声预测配置。

Result: CMDiff的瘢痕DSC为0.47±0.08，HD95为12.89±6.39 mm，ASD为1.91±0.96 mm；确定性基线分别为0.51±0.08、10.86±5.39 mm、1.58±1.03 mm。同一实现下，clean-mask预测显著优于本文评估的噪声预测配置。

Conclusion: CMDiff技术可行但未提升分割性能：DSC更低，HD95与ASD更高。直接掩膜扩散为该任务提供了分析性替代方案，但不构成有竞争力的性能改进。代码已开源。

Abstract: Late gadolinium enhancement (LGE) cardiac MRI enables non-invasive assessment of left atrial (LA) scar, an imaging marker associated with atrial fibrillation ablation outcome. However, automatic scar segmentation is hindered by low contrast, the thin atrial wall, scarce labels, and extreme class imbalance. Conditional diffusion offers iterative mask generation, but its suitability for sparse 3D LA scar segmentation remains unclear. To address this gap, we propose Conditional Mask Diffusion (CMDiff), which predicts clean masks using a dynamic-encoder-conditioned 3D sinusoidal UNet. We compared CMDiff with a deterministic baseline on identical cases in five-fold cross-validation of 60 scar-labelled public LASCARQS subjects, with 48 training and 12 held-out cases per fold. CMDiff achieved a scar DSC of 0.47 $\pm$ 0.08, HD95 of 12.89 $\pm$ 6.39 mm, and ASD of 1.91 $\pm$ 0.96 mm, compared with deterministic baseline values of 0.51 $\pm$ 0.08, 10.86 $\pm$ 5.39 mm, and 1.58 $\pm$ 1.03 mm, respectively. Under the same implementation, clean-mask prediction performed substantially better than the noise-prediction configuration evaluated in this study. Thus, CMDiff was technically feasible but did not improve segmentation performance: DSC was lower, and both HD95 and ASD were higher. Direct mask diffusion therefore provides an analytical alternative, but not a competitive performance advance for this task. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/jizhang02/CMDiff.

</details>


### [29] [MIKU: Material Invariance and Knowledge Unification for PBR Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07354)
*Yajie Bao,Jiaye Fu,Youtian Lin,Shuang Wu,Jiachen Qian,Feihu Zhang,Xun Cao,Yao Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MIKU：面向PBR材质的统一标准化表示与物理一致材料生成框架，通过统一处理管线与归一化方案协调异构材质参数化，并以两阶段多视角扩散模型分别生成几何感知反照率与金属度-粗糙度贴图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PBR材质数据集与合成管线缺乏标准化表示，材质定义、渲染约定与参数化方式不一致，导致生成结果不稳定且物理上不合理，阻碍跨数据集训练与泛化。

Method: 构建统一处理管线，标准化从数据预处理到纹理合成的全流程，保持一致几何与纹理表示；提出归一化方案，将异构材质参数化映射到公共表示空间，支持跨数据集训练推理；在此基础上训练物理感知扩散模型，采用两阶段多视角合成框架：先以显式几何引导生成多视角反照率图以保证跨视角外观一致，再以反照率与统一表示为条件，用扩散模型预测物理合理的粗糙度与金属度图。

Result: 在标准化数据上端到端训练后，模型学到符合类别级材质先验与语义部件边界的材质；实验显示纹理质量与部件语义一致性达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: 将表示标准化与外观合成解耦，可显著提升材质生成的稳定性与物理一致性，推动3D内容创建的自动化材质生成。

Abstract: High-quality material generation for physically based rendering (PBR) remains challenging because datasets and synthesis pipelines lack standardized representations. Differences in material definitions, rendering conventions, and parameterizations lead to unstable and physically implausible results. We propose MIKU, a unified framework for standardized PBR asset representation and physically consistent material generation across diverse 3D content domains. Its core is a unified processing pipeline that standardizes the workflow from data preprocessing to final texture synthesis, maintaining consistent geometry and texture representations. A normalization scheme harmonizes heterogeneous material parameterizations in a common representation space, enabling robust cross-dataset training and inference. Building on this formulation, we train a novel physically aware diffusion model for PBR texture generation within a two-stage multi-view synthesis framework. First, geometry-aware albedo generation synthesizes multi-view albedo maps under explicit geometric guidance to ensure cross-view appearance consistency. Second, metallic-roughness generation uses the diffusion model to predict physically plausible roughness and metallic maps conditioned on the generated albedo and unified representation. Trained end-to-end on standardized data, the model learns to generate materials that respect category-level material priors and semantic part boundaries. By decoupling representation standardization from appearance synthesis, MIKU substantially improves material generation stability and physical consistency. Extensive experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art texture quality and semantic consistency across object parts, advancing automated material generation for 3D content creation.

</details>


### [30] [SD-LoRA: Training-Time Structural Distillation into LoRA for Few-Shot Vision-Language Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07063)
*Mohammed Rahman Sherif Khan Mohammad,Ardhendu Behera,Sandip Pradhan,Swagat Kumar,Yonghuai Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SD-LoRA 是一种仅训练时使用 token 级异构图教师的结构蒸馏框架，将结构偏置隐式迁移到轻量 CLIP-LoRA 主干，推理时无需图计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少样本适配视觉语言模型需兼顾细粒度视觉区分与部署效率。现有轻量方法（prompt tuning、LoRA、cache adapter）仅监督全局图像嵌入，浪费 token 级结构；结构感知方法虽利用局部证据，但带来额外推理开销。

Method: 训练阶段构建异构教师图，建模 patch-patch、全局-局部、视觉-文本三类关系于高分辨率 ViT token 上；通过隐式蒸馏与原型预测对齐（Prototype Predictive Alignment，将图条件表示与支持集类原型对齐）把结构偏置注入共享 CLIP-LoRA 主干。推理时丢弃图教师，仅需单次 CLIP-LoRA 前向与 cache 分类器。对比基线为强 CLIP-LoRA。

Result: 11 个少样本基准上，1/2/4/8/16-shot 分别较 CLIP-LoRA 基线提升 2.4/1.8/3.0/2.4/2.0 个百分点，且测试期无额外图计算；在 base-to-novel 泛化、跨数据集迁移、OOD 鲁棒性与校准上均显示训练期结构监督提升精度与迁移可靠性。

Conclusion: 结构监督可作为纯训练期正则/蒸馏信号，在不增加推理成本前提下显著提升少样本适配性能与可靠性，为结构与效率的权衡提供可行范式。

Abstract: Few-shot adaptation of vision-language models must balance two competing goals: learning fine-grained visual distinctions from very limited supervision and preserving the efficiency that makes frozen CLIP-like models attractive for deployment. Existing lightweight adaptation methods, including prompt tuning, LoRA, and cache-based adapters, typically supervise global image embeddings and therefore underuse token-level structure. Conversely, structure-aware methods exploit local evidence but often introduce additional inference-time computation. We propose SD-LoRA, a structural distillation framework that uses token-level graph reasoning only during training and transfers its effects into a lightweight LoRA-adapted CLIP model. During training, a heterogeneous graph teacher models patch-patch, global-local, and visual-text relations over high-resolution ViT tokens. Rather than retaining this graph at test time, SD-LoRA transfers its structural bias into the shared CLIP-LoRA backbone through implicit distillation and Prototype Predictive Alignment, which aligns graph-conditioned representations with support-derived class prototypes. At inference, the graph teacher is discarded; prediction requires only a single CLIP-LoRA forward pass and a cache-based classifier. Across 11 few-shot benchmarks, SD-LoRA improves over a strong CLIP-LoRA baseline by 2.4/1.8/3.0/2.4/2.0 percentage points in the 1/2/4/8/16-shot settings, respectively, without additional graph computation at test time. Results on base-to-novel generalization, cross-dataset transfer, out-of-distribution robustness, and calibration further show that training-time structural supervision improves both accuracy and transfer reliability. Code is available at https://github.com/MR-Sherif/SD-LoRA.git.

</details>


### [31] [USB Gym: A Gymnasium Testbed for Closed-Loop Ultrasound View Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08352)
*Thanapong Intharah,Zhichao Gao,Yiliang Liu,Hao Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 构建 USB Gym 超声引导测试平台，将20种已发表方法组合进三槽位引导流程，在60个episode上评估；组合后仅达13.7 mm（为oracle进展的16%），说明该任务仍未被充分探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超声引导（移动探头使图像匹配目标视图）的各子模块此前均在独立仿真器中评估，缺乏"组合后是否够用"的统一验证。

Method: 搭建USB Gym：标准引擎基于公开患者CT仿真超声，网络从CT标签生成器官纹理；引导含三个可替换槽位（器官分割器、控制器、奖励），接入20种已发表方法，均在6名留出患者的相同60个episode上运行；槽位内仅在可检测更优时替换原方法，组合与原创引导中较优者作为benchmark基线，pose oracle（仅仿真可得的精确位姿）作为参照。

Result: 目标视图初始平均距离16.0 mm，pose oracle可达1.5 mm；仅SSIM图像相似度奖励略优且可检测，故组合为原引导+该奖励，最终13.7 mm，仅为oracle进展的16%。

Conclusion: 组合未调优的已发表方法与自研方法无法将探头引导至目标视图附近，任务仍属欠探索；测试平台可接纳新方法，且未在硬件上验证。

Abstract: Background and Objective: Our main goal is ultrasound guidance: moving a probe until its image matches a target view. Published methods for each part of a guide are evaluated in separate simulators. We asked whether these methods, combined into one guide, are good enough.
  Methods: We built USB Gym, a testbed whose standard engine simulates ultrasound from public patient CT, with a network drawing organ textures from CT labels. Its guide has three replaceable parts (slots), an organ segmenter, a controller and a reward, which received 20 published methods, each run on the same 60 episodes of six held-out patients. In each slot, a published method replaced ours only if detectably better on these patients. The better of this combination and our original guide on 20 new CTs is the benchmark baseline. A pose oracle, reading the exact probe pose available only in simulation, set the reference. The results below are exploratory.
  Results: On these CTs, the probe started on average 16.0 mm from the target view; the pose oracle brought it to 1.5 mm. Of the results known when the combination was fixed, only an image-similarity reward (SSIM) was slightly but detectably better, so the combination was our guide with that reward. This combination was the benchmark baseline, ending at 13.7 mm (16% of the oracle's progress).
  Conclusions: Combining untuned published methods with ours did not guide the probe near the target view; the task remains under-explored. The testbed accepts new methods; no guide was tested on hardware.

</details>


### [32] [SplatAnything: Gaussian Spacing Normalization for Feed-forward 3DGS across Arbitrary Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08264)
*Hao Yu,Zhenwei Wang,Yongzhen Hu,Yuning Gong,Junta Wu,Xiaowei Zhou,Hujun Bao,Tengfei Wang,Chunchao Guo,Sida Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文指出前馈3DGS方法中高斯属性在径向距离归一化空间预测与高斯中心间距实际变化之间的错配导致监督歧义，提出高斯间距归一化（GSNorm）构建SplatAnything，并建立系统性基准，取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈3DGS方法通常在径向距离归一化空间中预测高斯属性，该空间只对重建几何的全局径向统计做规范化，而相邻高斯中心间距仍随场景尺度与相机配置大幅变化。这一错配导致高斯尺度目标出现系统性偏移，产生歧义性监督。

Method: 提出SplatAnything与高斯间距归一化（GSNorm）：将高斯间距的鲁棒场景级统计量规范化，为高斯预测提供更一致的空间参考；并构建覆盖多数据集、不同输入稀疏度与挑战性外推视角的系统性基准。

Result: 大量实验表明SplatAnything达到SOTA性能，尤其在场景尺度与视角显著变化时增益明显。

Conclusion: 以高斯间距归一化替代/补充径向距离归一化可有效缓解监督歧义，提升前馈3DGS在尺度与视角变化下的鲁棒性；所建基准为系统评估提供了规范。

Abstract: We identify a key limitation of existing feed-forward 3DGS methods: Gaussian attributes are typically predicted in a radial-distance-normalized space that canonicalizes a global radial statistic of the reconstructed geometry, while the spacing between neighboring Gaussian centers can still vary substantially across scene scales and camera configurations. This mismatch induces systematic shifts in Gaussian-scale targets, leading to ambiguous supervision. To address this, we propose SplatAnything with Gaussian Spacing Normalization (GSNorm), which canonicalizes a robust scene-level statistic of Gaussian spacing to provide a more consistent spatial reference for Gaussian prediction. To rigorously assess our method, we further establish a systematic benchmark spanning diverse datasets, input sparsity levels, and challenging extrapolated viewpoints. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SplatAnything achieves state-of-the-art performance, with particularly strong gains under substantial variations in scene scale and viewpoint.

</details>


### [33] [ProQuant: Progressive Quantization-aware Training for Edge MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08216)
*Yufei Xue,Yushi Huang,Jiawei Shao,Pingcheng Dong,Yonghao Tan,Shiyao Li,Kwang-Ting Cheng,Jun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ProQuant是一个面向低比特多模态大模型（MLLM）的权值-激活量化感知训练（QAT）框架，通过自重标注（SRL）与渐进块激活（PBA）机制，在W4A8下显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM计算与内存开销大，难以部署于资源受限的边缘设备；PTQ在中等比特（W8A8）可行，但在低比特（W4A8）下性能严重退化，小规模模型尤甚。

Method: 提出ProQuant低比特QAT框架：1）自重标注（SRL）方案重采样开源多模态数据集的回复，使有限微调数据分布与预训练分布对齐；2）首次将多模态理解能力与语言建模能力解耦，提出渐进块激活（PBA）机制优先恢复MM理解能力；3）引入视觉敏感因子τ以识别MM关键层。

Result: 在2B–8B多模型、多基准实验中显著优于现有方法；W4A8的Qwen3-VL-2B-Instruct平均精度提升6.6%，相较全精度模型仅约1%下降。

Conclusion: ProQuant为边缘端低比特MLLM部署提供了有效的QAT方案，兼顾压缩效率与多模态能力保持，代码已开源。

Abstract: Multi-modal large language models (MLLMs) with impressive perception and understanding capabilities will enable a range of downstream applications. However, their substantial computational and memory requirements hinder the real-world implementation, particularly for resource-constrained edge devices. Quantization has emerged as an effective solution for deploying large models. While post-training quantization (PTQ) successfully retains the performance at moderate bit-width (e.g., W8A8), it suffers from severe performance degradation under low-bit quantization (e.g., W4A8), especially for small-scale models that are more suitable for edge deployment. In this paper, we propose ProQuant, a novel weight-activation quantization-aware training (QAT) framework tailored for low-bit MLLMs. First, to align the limited finetuning dataset distribution with the pretrained dataset distribution, we propose a self-relabeling (SRL) scheme to resample the responses of the open-source multimodal dataset. Second, we are the first to take a decoupled view of multi-modal (MM) understanding and language modeling abilities. We propose a progressive block activation (PBA) mechanism to prioritize the MM understanding recovery. We also introduce a vision-sensitive factor $\vecτ$ that effectively identifies the MM-critical layers. Extensive experiments across various benchmarks, covering models with 2B-8B parameters, show that ProQuant significantly outperforms existing methods.
