For example, our W4A8 Qwen3-VL-2B-Instruct improves average accuracy by impressively 6.6% and achieves performance comparable to full precision counterparts with only approximately 1% performance drop. Code is available at https://github.com/yufeixue-ai/ProQuant/.

</details>


### [34] [Improving Context-Shift Robustness of Convolutional Networks via Context-Regularized Cross-Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08157)
*Jinen Setpal,Chaoyue Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ContrastiveCAMs 归因方法与 Context-Regularized Cross-Entropy (CR-CE) 损失，抑制模型对上下文区域的依赖，提升 CNN 在 context shift 下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像分类模型在测试时遇到与训练分布不同的、多样且不可预测的上下文（context shift），会因依赖非因果的上下文线索而显著性能下降，需要提升此情形下的鲁棒性。

Method: 先指出现有特征归因方法的理论局限，提出更忠实的归因变体 ContrastiveCAMs；再基于它构造 CR-CE，即在标准交叉熵上加入正则项，利用空间特征归因抑制上下文区域对预测的影响、引导模型关注物体相关特征。

Result: 在 Waterbirds、Spawrious、ImageNet/ImageNet-BG、Hard-ImageNet 等中大规模数据集上评测，context-shift 鲁棒性均取得一致提升。

Conclusion: 以归因为先导的注意力正则可有效缓解上下文偏移带来的脆弱性，是一种通用且有效的训练框架增强。

Abstract: Image classification models often encounter objects in diverse and unpredictable contexts at test time, which may differ substantially from those seen during training. This phenomenon, termed context shift, exposes a key fragility in neural networks: when models rely on non-causal contextual cues for prediction, shifts in context can lead to significant performance degradation. In this paper, we propose a novel training framework to improve the robustness of convolutional neural networks under context shift. Our approach leverages spatial feature attribution to guide models toward making predictions that rely less on contextual regions and more on object-relevant features. As a first step, recognizing a theoretical limitation of existing feature attribution methods we introduce a new variant, ContrastiveCAMs, which produces more faithful attribution maps of model predictions. Building on ContrastiveCAMs, we further propose Context-Regularized Cross-Entropy (CR-CE), a modification of the standard cross-entropy loss that regularizes the model's attention by suppressing the influence of contextual regions, thereby improving robustness to context shift. We evaluate the effectiveness of our approach on several medium-to-large scale datasets (Waterbirds, Spawrious, ImageNet/ImageNet-BG, Hard-ImageNet), and report consistent improvements in context-shift robustness.

</details>


### [35] [MCUFlowNet: Hardware-Aware Neural Architecture Search for Optical Flow on Microcontrollers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08147)
*Enmin Lin,Jona Beysens*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PACE-NAS宏观-微观神经架构搜索框架，在Ethos-U55 MCU上实现峰值SRAM与延迟解耦，得到MCUFlowNet稠密光流网络，最快变体比基线快1.76倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集光流可用于微型无人机避障，但面向带神经加速器的微控制器（MCU）的密集光流网络很少，其片上SRAM限制了输入分辨率。作者希望在不增加峰值SRAM的前提下优化精度-延迟权衡。

Method: 提出PACE-NAS框架：宏观设计固定主干网络及其峰值SRAM（通过profiling定位峰值在输出head，用双线性上采样+卷积替换转置卷积，峰值SRAM降18%，并加两个轻量上下文模块降低EPE不增峰值）；微观设计在共享峰值SRAM下搜索算子与深度，以编译器估计的延迟换取精度。应用于Grove Vision AI V2（Ethos-U55）上的EdgeFlowNet。

Result: 所有搜索子网共享同一峰值SRAM，搜索无需内存约束。MCUFlowNet-S比再训练的EdgeFlowNet快1.76倍而EPE仅高1.6%；2 m/s模拟森林飞行中无碰撞到达目标比例为51.5%对41.8%。在语义分割上，PIDNet-S变体速度提升1.17倍且精度相近。

Conclusion: 通过宏观设计将峰值SRAM固定、微观设计仅优化延迟-精度权衡，可在MCU上高效获得帕累托最优密集光流网络，框架具有跨任务通用性（如语义分割）。

Abstract: Dense optical flow allows small drones to avoid obstacles. However, few dense optical flow networks are designed for microcontrollers (MCUs) with neural accelerators, whose on-chip SRAM limits the input resolution. We present PACE-NAS, a macro-to-micro neural architecture search framework that decouples memory from latency: a macro design fixes the backbone and its peak SRAM, and a micro design searches operators and depths to trade accuracy against compiler-estimated latency. We apply it to EdgeFlowNet on a Grove Vision AI V2 board (Ethos-U55), where profiling locates the peak SRAM at the full-resolution output head. Replacing transposed convolutions with bilinear upsampling and convolutions lowers the peak SRAM by 18%, and two lightweight context modules lower the endpoint error (EPE) without raising the peak. All searched subnets share this peak, so the search needs no memory constraint. We name the Pareto-optimal models MCUFlowNet; the fastest variant, MCUFlowNet-S, runs 1.76x faster than a retrained EdgeFlowNet at a 1.6% higher EPE and, in simulated forest flights at 2 m/s, reaches the goal without collision in 51.5% of trials, against 41.8%. PACE-NAS also applies to semantic segmentation, where a searched PIDNet-S variant runs 1.17x faster at similar accuracy. Code is available at https://gitlab.kuleuven.be/emedia-research-lab/esat-stadius/mcuflownet/mcuflownet.

</details>


### [36] [ATHAAR: A Medieval Scientific Arabic Manuscript Dataset for Handwritten Text Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08091)
*Ismail El Jamiy,Khadija Msalek,Guillaume Loizelet,Farah Benamara,Nabil Hathout*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文针对中世纪阿拉伯科学手稿识别难题，提出首个基于文本校勘学的转写标注指南，并构建人工标注数据集ATHAAR，通过一系列实验揭示当前先进VLM在古代科学文本上的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中世纪阿拉伯科学手稿因连笔合字、变音符不固定以及训练数据匮乏，对当前视觉语言模型（VLM）仍构成开放挑战。现有资源缺少针对此类文本的统一转写规范与多样化标注数据，限制了模型训练与评估。

Method: 首先借鉴历史文本编辑中成熟的"文本校勘学"（textual criticism），提出首个面向阿拉伯科学手稿转写的标注指南；随后依据该指南人工标注构建新数据集ATHAAR，其在同一文本的不同抄本（witnesses）、书法风格和内容（文本与数字）上均具有多样性；最后基于先进VLM设计系列实验，考察模型在书体、章节与论著之间的迁移能力。

Result: 跨书体、跨章节、跨论著的迁移实验表明，现有先进VLM在面对古代科学文本的特定特征时表现受限，暴露出其在真实手稿场景中的不足。

Conclusion: 该文通过标注规范、数据集与系统性评测三方面工作填补了阿拉伯科学手稿研究的空白，为后续古代科学文本的VLM研究提供了基准与方向。

Abstract: Despite recent progress in handwritten text recognition, medieval Arabic scientific manuscripts remain an open challenge for current vision-language models (VLMs) due to cursive ligatures, variable diacritization, and more importantly the lack of training data. This paper aims to address this gap, by proposing the first annotation guidelines dedicated to the transcription of scientific Arabic manuscripts inspired by textual criticism, a well-established approach to historical text editing. We then propose, ATHAAR, a new dataset manually annotated following this guideline. Unlike previous resources in the field, ATHAAR varies both witnesses of the same text, calligraphic styles, and content to deal with texts but also numerals. Relying on state-of-the-art VLMs, we finally design a set of experiments to study models transfer across scripts, chapters, and treatises. Our findings highlight the limitations of current systems when faced with the specificities of ancient scientific texts.

</details>


### [37] [Subspace Learning with Outlier Exposure for Out-of-Distribution Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07580)
*Hongxu Shen,Kun Fang,Jiahui Zheng,Jian Sun,Qinghua Tao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于Outlier Exposure的特征子空间检测框架RecOE，通过学习InD与OoD各自的主导特征子空间并利用重构误差差异作为OoD评分，在多个基准上显著优于强基线且开销极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于OE的OoD检测方法多为logit-centric，即生成多样outliers或在预测输出层面进行正则化，未能充分利用特征空间中的丰富信息。

Method: 从InD数据与辅助outliers中学习特征子空间：通过最小化各自子空间内的重构误差以捕获主导变化；引入样本级目标抑制在对方子空间中的重构；并加入正交正则器减少子空间重叠以增强判别性。推理时以两子空间重构误差之差作为OoD评分。

Result: 在多个基准上取得优于近期强基线的显著性能提升，且仅需两个小尺寸子空间基矩阵带来的少量额外开销。

Conclusion: 将OoD检测从logit层面拓展到特征子空间层面是有效方向，RecOE以轻量结构实现更优的InD-OoD区分能力。

Abstract: Out-of-Distribution (OoD) detection is pivotal for reliable deployments of Deep Neural Networks (DNNs), aiming to distinguish anomalous samples from the In-Distribution (InD) data in training. By introducing outliers as surrogate OoD data to finetune DNNs, Outlier Exposure (OE) has greatly advanced OoD detection in recent years. Existing OE-based research remains predominantly logit-centric, either by generating diverse outliers or by regularizing them with objectives operating at the prediction-output level. Nonetheless, these approaches leave much of the rich information in feature spaces untapped. In this work, we propose an OE-based detection framework, named RecOE, that learns appropriate feature subspaces from InD data and auxiliary outliers, such that the InD-OoD discrepancy can be effectively characterized through the learned feature subspaces. Specifically, the feature subspaces are optimized to capture the dominant variations of InD and outliers by minimizing reconstruction errors therein. Besides, a sample-level objective discourages reconstruction in the opposing subspace, while an orthogonality regularizer reduces subspace overlap, further promoting discriminative subspaces. At inference, the difference in reconstruction errors between the learned subspaces serves as the OoD detection score. Extensive experiments show the superiority of RecOE on multiple benchmarks, in which significant performance gains are achieved over recent strong baselines with only slight extra overhead from two small-size matrices of subspace bases. The source code is available at https://github.com/shenhongxu123/RecOE.

</details>


### [38] [TraCE: Trajectory-Aware Concepts for Explainable Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07009)
*Wooil Lee,Junpyo Hong,Jongseo Lee,Tuomas Oikarinen,Tsui-Wei Weng,Jinwoo Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TraCE——基于光流轨迹聚类的概念式视频理解可解释框架，通过类型化的轨迹/物体/场景概念接口同时支持动作识别与视频问答，在保持竞争力的同时提升可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频XAI存在"运动来源欠指定"问题：以区域为中心的解释存在来源歧义，而局限于单人姿态序列的运动词汇表则来源不完整，无法说明究竟哪类运动来源支撑了预测。

Method: 从训练视频的光流轨迹中聚类，发现"来源灵活"的运动概念（人、物体、交互等动态证据），并辅以物体概念与场景概念刻画静态上下文；构建类型化的轨迹/物体/场景概念接口，用于动作识别的类别预测与VQA的条件化概念匹配。

Result: 在六个基准（NTU RGB+D Mutual、Chiral SSv2、UCF-101、KTH、TOMATO、STAR）上以五个随机种子评估：相比不可解释基线，动作识别平均仅下降1.0分，VQA名义上提升0.4分；运动扰动与概念移除实验证明方向性语义对齐与决策忠实性，样本级干预可实现针对性模型调试。

Conclusion: TraCE通过来源灵活的运动概念解决了运动来源欠指定问题，在几乎不损失任务性能的前提下提供忠实且可干预的视频模型解释。

Abstract: Video understanding models often rely on motion, yet their explanations can obscure which motion sources support a prediction. We identify motion-source under-specification as a limitation of existing video XAI: region-centric explanations can be source-ambiguous, while motion vocabularies restricted to single-person pose sequences are source-incomplete. We propose TraCE (Trajectory-Aware Concepts for Explainable Video Understanding), a concept-based framework that discovers source-flexible motion concepts by clustering optical-flow trajectories from training videos. These concepts represent dynamic evidence from people, objects, and interactions, while object and scene concepts account for static context. The resulting typed trajectory/object/scene interface supports class prediction for action recognition and question-conditioned concept matching for video question answering (VQA). We evaluate TraCE across five random seeds on six benchmarks: NTU RGB+D Mutual, Chiral SSv2, UCF-101, KTH, TOMATO, and STAR. Relative to non-interpretable baselines, TraCE maintains competitive task performance, with an average accuracy drop of 1.0 points for action recognition and a nominal gain of 0.4 points for VQA. Motion perturbation and concept-removal tests provide complementary evidence of directional semantic alignment and decision faithfulness, while sample-level interventions illustrate targeted model debugging. Our project page is available at https://leewooil.github.io/TraCE_project_page/.

</details>


### [39] [VINO: Revisiting 3D Representation Learning with Volume Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07176)
*Adrian Kruse,Kadir Yilmaz,Daan de Geus,Francis Engelmann,Bastian Leibe*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VINO：重新设计3D场景自监督表示学习为核心的Volume Transformer骨干与改进的学生-教师目标，实现无标签的语义3D表示学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2D视觉基础模型已能学习通用可迁移的图像表示，但3D场景的表示学习尚未取得同等成功。作者因此重新审视当前3D表示学习方法的核心设计选择，识别其关键局限。

Method: 1）用Volume Transformer（类ViT的3D架构）替换常用的分层PTv3骨干；2）重新设计学生-教师网络的两大目标：跨视图匹配中分离教师与学生角色（教师提供干净一致的目标，学生学习对更强扰动的不变性），掩码内容预测中禁止学生访问被掩码几何、仅从可见上下文推断；3）重设计2D→3D知识蒸馏，用视图一致监督替代对视图相关图像特征的直接回归。

Result: VINO在室内与室外基准的密集线性探测（dense linear probing）上均有显著提升，并在域外场景中表现出强迁移能力。

Conclusion: 通过系统性重构骨干架构与核心训练目标，可在无标签条件下学到高质量、可迁移的3D场景语义表示。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning has given rise to vision foundation models that learn general-purpose image representations transferable across downstream tasks, yet representation learning for 3D scenes has not achieved comparable success. Motivated by this, we identify key limitations of current 3D representation learning approaches and revisit their core design choices. We first replace the commonly used hierarchical PTv3 backbone with the Volume Transformer, a ViT-like architecture for 3D scenes. We then redesign two core objectives of modern approaches built around student-teacher networks: matching representations across views and predicting masked content from visible context. For the first objective, we separate the roles of teacher and student: the teacher provides clean, consistent targets while the student learns invariance to stronger perturbations. For the second objective, we prevent the student from accessing the masked geometry and instead require it to infer missing representations from visible context only. Finally, we redesign 2D-to-3D knowledge distillation, replacing direct regression of view-dependent image features with view-consistent supervision. Together, these changes form VINO, a framework that learns semantic representations of 3D scenes without labels. VINO substantially improves dense linear probing across indoor and outdoor benchmarks and transfers strongly to out-of-domain settings.

</details>


### [40] [Gaussians Can Subtract: Neural Dissolve Fields for Hybrid Scene Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07049)
*Yiyan Wang,Haohan Liu,Tetsuo Shibuya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将混合Mesh-Gaussian渲染重构为多视角不一致性问题，提出"先溶解、再填补"的Gaussolve框架：用神经溶解场选择性溶解不一致的网格内容，再由残差高斯填充，实现更高保真、更少基元、更快渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Mesh-Gaussian混合表示多采用加性形式，用高斯残差补偿网格误差；但网格-高斯冲突并非都源于缺失内容——残差几何误差、遮挡不匹配、纹理投影伪影与视角相关外观会导致同一网格内容在某一视角一致、在另一视角不一致。因此需要从多视角不一致角度重新建模。

Method: 提出Gaussolve减法-重填框架。核心是轻量级神经溶解场（Neural Dissolve Field），以标量球谐高斯参数化，学习网格内容应在何处、沿哪些视角方向被溶解；并设计连续透射率融合规则，逐步显露溶解网格背后的残差高斯，从而保留其重建梯度并避免硬性可见性跳变。

Result: 在结构化与半结构化场景上，相比现有方法取得更高视觉保真度，同时使用更少高斯基元并实现更快渲染。

Conclusion: 将混合渲染视为多视角不一致问题并通过选择性溶解网格、残差高斯重填，是一种有效的混合表示范式，可在保持视觉质量的同时提升紧凑性与渲染效率。

Abstract: Hybrid Mesh-Gaussian rendering combines compact textured surfaces with flexible 3D Gaussian primitives, offering an efficient alternative to purely Gaussian scene representations for structured environments. Existing scene-reconstruction hybrids commonly follow an additive formulation, where Gaussian residuals compensate for errors left by the mesh. However, not every Mesh-Gaussian conflict is caused by missing content: residual geometry errors, occlusion mismatch, texture-projection artifacts, and view-dependent appearance can make the same mesh content consistent in one view but inconsistent in another. We therefore formulate hybrid rendering as a multi-view inconsistency problem and present Gaussolve, a subtract-and-refill framework that selectively dissolves inconsistent mesh content before residual Gaussians refill the revealed regions. We introduce a lightweight Neural Dissolve Field, parameterized by scalar spherical-harmonic Gaussians, to learn where and from which viewing directions mesh content should be dissolved. A continuous transmittance fusion rule progressively exposes residual Gaussians behind the dissolved mesh, preserving their reconstruction gradients and avoiding hard visibility discontinuities. Experiments on structured and semi-structured scenes demonstrate higher visual fidelity with fewer Gaussian primitives and faster rendering. Project page: https://ooosn.github.io/gaussians-can-subtract/

</details>


### [41] [T$^2$-Splat: Adaptive Topology Mesh Splatting with Texture Residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06974)
*Zhihao Tang,Youjia Zhang,Mingbo Zhao,Wei Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: T²-Splat将拓扑自适应与外观细化解耦：用Safe RDT初始化网格连通性，用动态拓扑演化(DTE)通过边折叠/顶点分裂调整三角形分配，再用逐面残差Mip纹理做尺度自适应外观细化，从而在紧凑网格下取得竞争性新视角合成效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于三角形的splatting把新视角合成与常规图形管线连接起来，但为保留外观细节往往需要过度细分（excessive tessellation）。

Method: 解耦拓扑自适应与外观细化：(1) Safe Restricted Delaunay Triangulation (Safe RDT) 初始化网格连通性；(2) Dynamic Topology Evolution (DTE) 通过边折叠与顶点分裂操作自适应分配三角形；(3) 在固定网格上用逐面残差Mip纹理、结合尺度自适应滤波细化外观。

Result: 在Mip-NeRF 360、Tanks & Temples、DTU上以紧凑网格取得竞争性结果；Mip-NeRF 360上PSNR较MeshSplatting提升0.71dB；压缩实验表明网格复杂度可灵活控制，面数预算可低至原始的10%。

Conclusion: 通过将拓扑自适应与外观细化分离，T²-Splat以更紧凑的网格实现高质量新视角合成，并提供灵活的网格复杂度—质量权衡。

Abstract: Triangle-based splatting connects novel view synthesis with conventional graphics pipelines, but often requires excessive tessellation to preserve appearance details. We present T$^2$-Splat, which decouples topology adaptation from appearance refinement. Safe Restricted Delaunay Triangulation (Safe RDT) initializes mesh connectivity. Dynamic Topology Evolution (DTE) then adapts triangle allocation through edge collapse and vertex split operations. Residual per-face Mip-textures then refine appearance on the fixed mesh using scale-adaptive filtering. Experiments on Mip-NeRF 360, Tanks & Temples, and DTU demonstrate competitive results with compact meshes. T$^2$-Splat improves PSNR by 0.71dB over MeshSplatting on Mip-NeRF 360. Additional compression experiments demonstrate flexible control of mesh complexity, supporting face budgets as low as 10% of the original face count. Project page: https://t2-splat.github.io/.

</details>


### [42] [UniGADD: Universal Generator-Agnostic Deepfake Detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07647)
*Bernard Tomczyk,Agil Aghasanli,Plamen Angelov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出通用、与生成器无关的深度伪造检测方法 UniGADD，通过两阶段监督优化，在已知生成器上保持高精度，同时显著提升对未见生成器的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度伪造检测方法在已知生成器上精度高，但面对未见过的生成技术时泛化性差，难以应对实际对抗性内容的快速演化，因此需要兼顾已知与未知生成器的通用检测器。

Method: 两阶段优化：第一阶段用对比损失从真实与已知伪造图像中学习判别性特征嵌入，保证训练域内性能；第二阶段仅对真实样本进行嵌入空间精炼，通过促进簇间分离与簇内紧凑来提升泛化性。

Result: 在已知生成器上精度与当前最优方法相当，在未见案例上显著优于现有方法，体现出可扩展性与实际适用性。

Conclusion: UniGADD 通过两阶段监督式嵌入学习，在保持已知伪造检测精度的同时增强了跨生成器泛化能力，为对抗性内容检测提供了可行的通用方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce Universal Generator-Agnostic Deepfake Detector (UniGADD), a novel method that employs supervised learning to achieve high accuracy on known deepfake generators, while maintaining robust performance on previously unseen ones. The proposed approach follows a two-stage optimisation process. In the first stage, a contrastive loss encourages the model to learn discriminative feature embeddings from real and known fake images, resulting in strong performance within the training domain. In the second stage, the embedding space is refined by promoting inter-cluster separation and intra-cluster compactness, applied exclusively to real samples. This refinement enhances generalisability, enabling the method to exhibit improved robustness against unseen deepfake generation techniques. UniGADD achieves accuracy on par with state-of-the-art methods for known generators, while significantly outperforming them on unseen cases, demonstrating its scalability and practical applicability for adversarial content detection.

</details>


### [43] [TACT-KV: Tri-Axis Cosine Transform for Compressing Volumetric KV Caches in Medical VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07593)
*Chongyu Qu,Ritchie Zhao,Yufan He,Dong Yang,Zhengyi Lu,Junchao Zhu,Tianyuan Yao,Juming Xiong,Junlin Guo,Yanfan Zhu,Yuechen Yang,Daguang Xu,Bennett Allan Landman,Yucheng Tang,Yuankai Huo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TACT-KV，一种免校准的KV缓存压缩方法，对3D医学扫描的视觉KV缓存沿三条解剖轴应用可分离3D离散余弦变换，结合反向注水式逐层比特分配与固定标量码本量化，在等HBM下将解码批大小最多提升8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D医学扫描（CT/MRI）输入VLM时，单个体数据产生数万视觉token，KV缓存随批大小与上下文线性膨胀，形成"结构性的显存墙"。现有KV压缩方法仅基于特征或token统计量，或仅用1D/2D变换，未利用缓存中三轴空间结构。

Method: TACT-KV（Tri-Axis Cosine Transform）：1) 利用视觉KV状态沿三个解剖轴的强空间相关性，使能量在频域集中；2) 对KV缓存施加可分离3D离散余弦变换；3) 通过反向注水在高能量频带上分配更多比特，实现逐层比特预算分配；4) 各频带用固定标量码本量化。方法免校准，并给出失真降低量由变换后缓存谱集中度主导的闭式关系。

Result: 在6个医学VLM与2个3D CT基准上，12个模型-数据集组合中有11个取得最低1-bit预测翻转率；在4个通用VLM的视频任务上同样复现该优势，说明三轴结构利用可泛化至医学影像之外；在等HBM条件下，单GPU解码批大小最多提升8倍。

Conclusion: 三维空间结构的频域集中是KV缓存压缩中被忽视的有效信号，可分离3D DCT配合自适应比特分配能在几乎无损（以1-bit预测翻转率衡量）前提下大幅提升3D医学VLM的显存效率，且该原理具有跨模态通用性。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) applied to 3D medical scans face a structural memory wall. A single CT or MRI volume produces tens of thousands of vision tokens, and the resulting key-value (KV) cache scales linearly with both batch and context. Existing KV compression methods operate on the cache's feature or token statistics, or apply 1D and 2D transforms, leaving the three-axis spatial structure in the cache unexploited. We propose TACT-KV (Tri-Axis Cosine Transform), a calibration-free KV compression method that exploits strong spatial correlations in visual KV states along all three anatomical axes of 3D scans. This correlation concentrates the KV cache energy under a frequency-domain transform. TACT-KV applies a separable 3D discrete cosine transform to the KV cache, allocates a per-layer bit budget across frequency bins via reverse water-filling (more bits to high-energy components), and quantizes each bin with a fixed scalar codebook. We derive a close-form relation: the distortion reduction from adaptive bit allocation is dominated by the spectral concentration of the transformed cache. Across six medical VLMs and two 3D CT benchmarks, TACT-KV achieves the lowest 1-bit prediction-flip rate on eleven of twelve model and dataset pairs in our main comparison. The same advantage reproduces on four general-purpose VLMs evaluated on video tasks, indicating that the benefit of exploiting three-axis structure generalizes beyond medical imaging. At equal high-bandwidth memory (HBM), TACT-KV scales the decoding batch size on a single GPU by up to $8\times$.

</details>


### [44] [Guidance Is a Field, Not a Scalar: Interval-Integrated Classifier-Free Guidance for One-Step Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07566)
*Zijie Meng,Xixin Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AGAVE 通过 interval-integrated CFG identity 将任意引导场吸收进 MeanFlow 目标，实现单步生成器中的无场级调度引导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 MeanFlow 的单步视频生成器把 CFG 尺度冻结或仅作为单一标量，与"最优引导随噪声水平、空间内容及帧位置变化的场"这一认识冲突；而现有多步采样器重新调度引导的方案在单步模型上不成立。

Method: 提出 interval-integrated CFG identity：因 MeanFlow 目标为噪声区间上的积分，任意引导场可被吸收进目标本身并编译为单次函数评估。据此构建 AGAVE，为平均速度场加入可学习时间调度、自由运动显著性空间调制与帧位置非对称项，仅新增不足十个标量。

Result: 编译后的引导不含调度离散化误差，理论上可匹配或超越任意有限步多步采样器；在 VBench、UCF-101 与 MSR-VTT 上超过所有单步基线，并达到多步引导采样水平，且零额外推理开销。

Conclusion: 单步生成器无需多步采样即可实现场级引导调度；AGAVE 以极低参数代价统一了单步效率与多步引导质量。

Abstract: One-step generators built on MeanFlow synthesize a video in a single function evaluation, yet inherit a rigid treatment of classifier-free guidance (CFG): the guidance scale is frozen during training or, at best, exposed as a single scalar. This conflicts with mounting evidence that the optimal guidance is not a constant but a field that varies with the noise level, with spatial content, and, for video, with frame position. Every existing remedy presupposes a multi-step sampler along which guidance can be re-scheduled--an assumption one-step models violate by construction. We resolve this tension through a single observation: because the MeanFlow target is an integral over a noise interval, an arbitrary guidance field can be absorbed into the target itself and compiled into one evaluation. We formalize this as the interval-integrated CFG identity, prove that the compiled guidance carries no schedule-discretization error and can thus match or exceed any finite multi-step sampler, and instantiate it as AGAVE, which equips the average-velocity field with a learnable temporal schedule, a free motion-saliency spatial modulation, and a frame-position asymmetry. Adding fewer than ten scalars at zero inference cost, AGAVE surpasses all one-step baselines on VBench, UCF-101 and MSR-VTT while matching multi-step guided sampling. Project repository: https://github.com/YMlinfeng/AGAVE.

</details>


### [45] [Action-Conditioned VLM Monitoring for Autonomous Vehicle Semantic Anomalies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07540)
*Daniel Adejumo,Omkar Ankalkope,Kunal Runwal,Aliasghar Arab*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出动作条件下的自动驾驶行为监控任务：用VLM预测场景所需动作，再与实际自车轨迹比对判定是否异常，并构建配对场景数据集与两阶段监控器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义异常驾驶场景中，单靠视觉异常无法判定行为是否恰当——同一场景既可对应正常行为也可对应异常行为，监控标签取决于动作与场景的匹配性，因此需要动作条件化的监控框架。

Method: (1) 定义任务：视觉异常≠行为异常，标签由"执行动作是否适配场景"决定；(2) 两阶段监控：VLM在隐藏结果信息的视频窗口内预测场景所需动作，再依据显式兼容性规则将该期望与实际自车运动轨迹比对；(3) 构建六字段分类法的112个配对场景（含42个源自已记录事件、覆盖两种失败极性）、带估计轨迹的生成视频语料。

Result: 在15个场景族、235个片段上，固定T-2.5 s窗口的两阶段协议取得83.0%准确率、异常召回0.773，单阶段纯视频基线为74.5%与0.536；特异性分别为0.880与0.928。

Conclusion: 动作条件化的两阶段监控显著提升异常召回与整体准确率，代价是特异性略降；显式轨迹兼容性规则结合VLM动作预期是可行的监控范式。工作属探索性，语料为生成视频，规模有限。

Abstract: We study action-conditioned monitoring of autonomous-vehicle behavior in semantically unusual driving scenes. The target is whether the executed ego action is appropriate for the scene: the same scene can admit both normal and anomalous behavior, so visual unusualness alone does not determine the monitoring label. A Vision-Language Model (VLM) first predicts the scene-required action from an outcome-hidden video window; the monitor then compares that expectation with the realized ego-motion trajectory under an explicit compatibility rule. To study this monitoring task, we contribute a six-field taxonomy of 112 paired scenarios spanning both failure polarities (42 derived from documented incidents), a generated video corpus with estimated trajectories, and an exploratory two-stage monitor on that corpus. On 235 clips from 15 scenario families, the two-stage protocol with a fixed T-2.5 s window obtains 83.0% accuracy and anomaly recall 0.773, versus 74.5% and 0.536 for a single-stage video-only baseline; specificity is 0.880 versus 0.928.

</details>


### [46] [ArtiVidRM: Verifiable Artifact-as-Rubric Reward Modeling for Explicit Quality Articulation in Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07301)
*Caorui Li,Yangguang Ji,Shuxia Lin,Yu Chen,YiCheng Xiao,Yongliang Wu,Zhenxiang Jiang,Licheng Tang,Deng Li,Zhuxuanzi Wang,Jiahao Sun,Chonghan Liu,Lirian Su,Jay Wu,Xu Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ArtiVidRM——一种"先观察再打分"的可验证、以伪影为评分细则的视频奖励模型，并配合多奖励强化学习联合优化伪影识别与质量评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习优化视频生成高度依赖可靠奖励模型，但现有方法或将视频直接映射为不可解释的标量分数，或生成自由形式的理由却未将其作为判断依据进行显式优化，导致可解释性与优化信号不足。

Method: ArtiVidRM先识别视频中可见的生成伪影并组织为结构化伪影列表，再以该列表作为显式评分细则进行质量评估，使分数基于具体视频级证据；同时引入多奖励强化学习策略，联合优化伪影预测与质量评估，奖励分解为语义覆盖度、预测效率等伪影预测质量目标，以及通过分差监督排序的成对偏好目标。

Result: 在视频偏好基准上达到SOTA，为视频扩散模型优化提供有效信号，并支持对闭源视频生成器进行伪影引导的精细化改进。

Conclusion: 将伪影作为可验证的显式评分中间表示，并与下游偏好预测联合优化，可同时提升奖励的可解释性与质量评估准确性，具有较强的实用价值。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has become an effective approach for improving video generation, but its success depends heavily on reliable reward models. Existing methods either map videos directly to opaque scalar scores or generate free-form rationales that are not explicitly optimized as the basis of the final judgment. We propose ArtiVidRM, a verifiable, artifact-as-rubrics video reward model that follows an observe-then-score paradigm. It first identifies visible generation artifacts and organizes them into a structured artifact list, then uses these artifacts as explicit rubrics for quality assessment. This intermediate representation grounds each score in concrete video-level evidence, making the reward more interpretable and informative for optimization. We further introduce a multi-reward reinforcement learning strategy to jointly optimize artifact prediction and quality assessment. The reward decomposes into complementary objectives that evaluate artifact prediction quality, including semantic coverage and prediction efficiency, together with a pairwise preference objective that supervises quality ranking through score differences. By jointly optimizing the intermediate artifact representation and the downstream preference prediction, the model learns to produce informative artifact lists while improving the accuracy of video quality assessment. Experiments show that ArtiVidRM achieves state-of-the-art performance on video preference benchmarks, provides effective signals for video diffusion optimization, and enables artifact-guided refinement of closed-source video generators.

</details>


### [47] [Householder Motion Embedding: Noise Modulation for Motion-Controllable Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07294)
*Tao Jun Lin,Jinhong Ni,Ruyi Zha,Yifu Wang,Xibin Song,Pan Ji,Hongdong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Householder Motion Embedding (HME)，用一系列 Householder 反射构成的与用户运动信号相关的正交变换作用于每个位置通道向量，从而把运动编码进噪声：既保持帧内空间高斯结构，又引入跨帧相关性，实现运动可控视频生成，速度较噪声弯折快 2.7 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散模型在显式用户引导下的精确运动控制仍具挑战。现有噪声弯折（noise warping）方法依据运动信号弯折噪声以引入时间相关性，但标准空间弯折会破坏噪声的 i.i.d. 高斯结构，需分布校正（如基于 Jacobian 的校正），计算开销显著。

Method: 将运动信号编码为作用于每个位置通道向量的一系列 Householder 反射，构成正交变换。由于正交变换保持高斯分布在通道空间的分布形态，HME 在帧内精确保持空间高斯结构，同时通过由运动决定的变换在不同帧间引入跨帧相关性，从而促进时间一致性。该方法与网络架构无关，可直接嵌入标准视频扩散微调流程。

Result: 在多种场景实验中，HME 达到或超过当前最优方法，且相对噪声弯折实现 2.7 倍加速。

Conclusion: 通道空间中的 Householder 变换为运动可控视频生成提供了一种有原理依据且高效的替代噪声弯折的方案。

Abstract: Diffusion models for video generation have achieved remarkable success, yet precise motion control under explicit user guidance remains challenging. Recent noise-warping methods introduce temporal correlations by warping the noise according to the user-provided motion signal. However, standard spatial warping generally disrupts the noise's i.i.d. Gaussian structure, requiring distribution correction, such as Jacobian-based correction, but at a substantial computational overhead. We propose Householder Motion Embedding (HME), which encodes the motion signal into the noise through a sequence of Householder reflections that together form an orthogonal transformation applied to each location's channel vector. HME exactly preserves the spatial Gaussian structure within each frame while introducing cross-frame correlations that promote temporally consistent video. HME is agnostic to the network architecture and integrates directly into standard video diffusion fine-tuning pipelines. Experiments on different scenarios show that HME matches or outperforms the state of the art, while achieving a 2.7x speedup over noise warping. These results demonstrate that channel-space Householder transformations offer a principled and efficient alternative to noise warping for motion-controllable video generation.

</details>


### [48] [Vis2State: Visual Reasoning through Executable State Abstractions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07397)
*Sheng Jin,Wentao Liu,Chen Qian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练智能体框架 Vis2State，将视觉谜题抽象为显式、可修订的问题状态，解耦视觉理解与程序化求解，在四个基准上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将视觉假设与任务约束直接嵌入求解代码，导致所表征的问题难以独立检查和修改，视觉解释与计算过程耦合过紧。

Method: 免训练的智能化框架：由视觉检查指导状态的构建与精炼，生成程序求解所表征的问题；模型无需预定义谜题模式或求解流程，自行构建状态与程序，并借助检查反馈和执行反馈分别独立修订问题表示与求解过程。

Result: 在四个基准上达到SOTA；跨模型评估显示一致性提升；消融实验、错误分析及固定程序的状态干预实验支持以状态为中心设计的有效性。

Conclusion: 将视觉谜题显式抽象为可修订状态、并将表示与求解解耦，是提升视觉谜题求解可靠性与可解释性的有效范式。

Abstract: Solving visual puzzles requires both accurate interpretation of visual evidence and reliable computation under task-specific rules. Embedding visual assumptions and task constraints directly in solving code makes the represented problem difficult to inspect and revise independently. We introduce Vis2State, a training-free agentic framework that abstracts visual puzzles into explicit, revisable problem states linking visual inspection and execution. Visual inspection guides state construction and refinement, while generated programs solve the represented problem. The model constructs both the state and the program without predefined puzzle schemas or solving procedures. Inspection and execution feedback allow it to revise the problem representation and solving procedure independently. Vis2State achieves the state-of-the-art performance on four benchmarks. Cross-model evaluation further demonstrates consistent gains. Ablations, error analyses, and fixed-program state interventions further support the effectiveness of the state-centered design. The code will be made publicly available to facilitate future research.

</details>


### [49] [WeLM-Image: A Unified Image Generation Model towards Visual Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07392)
*Canmiao Fu,Chen Li,Guohui Zhang,Hao Lu,Hongyang Wei,Huadong Wu,Huaisong Zhang,Hubery Yin,Liyuan Cui,Qixin Yan,Tong Shao,Xiaoqiang Zhou,Xinhang Leng,Ying Zhang,Zhenhai Liu,Zhipeng Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WeLM-Image 是一个统一图像基础模型，将多模态理解融入文生图、指令编辑与多参考合成，可在单卡部署，1536 分辨率下在 PRISM-Bench、Qwen-Image-Bench、UnicBench 上排名开源第一，Turbo 版单卡 4.98s 生成 1024×1024 图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向微信大规模消费级应用的单 GPU 部署需求，需要统一模型同时具备多模态理解、生成、编辑与多参考合成能力，同时避免理解与生成任务之间的优化冲突。

Method: 将文本、参考图像与目标 latent 组织为单一交错序列；采用 Mixture-of-Transformers 骨干（共享注意力 + 独立的理解/生成参数）并在各路径加入 MoE（总参数 31.7B，每目标 latent token 激活约 5.4B 生成参数）；构建生成导向数据引擎（知识驱动视觉词表 + 大规模视觉聚类 + 训练期评估迭代数据精炼），配合双语标注与已验证编辑监督进行渐进训练至原生 1.5K 分辨率；分别以 rubric 与 checklist 奖励优化生成和编辑，再通过噪声感知 on-policy 蒸馏整合策略；MeanFlow 少步蒸馏得到 Turbo 版本（8 步无 CFG）。

Result: 1536 分辨率模型在 PRISM-Bench、Qwen-Image-Bench、UnicBench 总体对比中位列所评估开源模型第一；WeLM-Image-Turbo 在单张 NVIDIA H20 上 8 步无 guidance 生成 1024×1024 图像耗时 4.98s。

Conclusion: 通过 MoT+MoE 架构、生成导向数据引擎与分阶段奖励优化/策略蒸馏，可在单卡条件下实现理解与生成统一且性能领先的高分辨率图像基础模型。

Abstract: We present \textbf{WeLM-Image}, a unified image foundation model that integrates multimodal understanding into text-to-image generation, instruction-guided editing, and multi-reference synthesis. Designed for single-GPU deployment in WeChat's large-scale consumer applications, the model processes text, reference images, and target latents as one interleaved sequence. A Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) backbone combines shared attention with independent understanding and generation parameters to limit optimization interference. Equipping each path with Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) modules provides sparse capacity, with 31.7B stored parameters and approximately 5.4B generation parameters activated per target latent token. A generation-oriented data engine combines a knowledge-grounded visual vocabulary with large-scale visual clustering to balance semantic coverage and editing tasks, while training-time evaluation guides iterative data refinement. Bilingual annotations and verified editing supervision support progressive training up to native 1.5K resolution. We optimize generation and editing separately with rubric- and checklist-based rewards, then consolidate the specialized policies through noise-aware on-policy distillation. In the reported overall comparisons, the 1536-resolution model ranks first among the evaluated open-source models on PRISM-Bench, Qwen-Image-Bench, and UnicBench. MeanFlow-based few-step distillation yields WeLM-Image-Turbo, which generates a $1024\times1024$ image in 4.98\,s on a single NVIDIA H20 GPU using eight guidance-free steps.

</details>


### [50] [Fault-Guided Cross-Layer Prediction for Unsupervised Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08140)
*Tao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出单类异常检测器：将冻结 ViT 的深度视为离散转移序列，用有向预测器学习 block-to-block 隐状态转移，以相邻层余弦相似度揭示的"断层"确定源/目标角色，并结合有界前景先验抑制背景；MVTec-AD/VisA/Real-IAD 上 I-AUROC 达 99.75/99.42/94.27。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有特征重构式单类工业异常检测缺乏对"哪些信息可由一层传递到另一层"的机理分析；深层保留语义却丢失局部细节，简单重构难以区分可传输与不可传输信息，且背景证据容易干扰缺陷定位。

Method: 1) 将冻结 ViT（DINOv2 ViT-L）的深度建模为离散转移序列；2) 训练有向预测器，逐 token 学习 block-to-block 隐状态转移；3) 用相邻层余弦相似度识别四条明显"断层线"，将深度划为稳定片段，据此固定源/目标角色（深/浅源 → 敏感目标）；4) 该规则可迁移至 ViT-B 与 RADIO-B；5) 采用仅在训练图像上标定的有界前景先验，衰减背景证据而不抹除估计前景之外的缺陷；6) 统一预测器架构与仅用训练集的标定协议。

Result: I-AUROC：MVTec-AD 99.75、VisA 99.42、Real-IAD 94.27，Real-IAD 物体级 AUROC 96.26；256×256 像素分辨率下 P-AUROC/P-AUPRO 分别为 MVTec-AD 98.46/95.97、VisA 99.21/96.12、Real-IAD 99.26/96.59。

Conclusion: 跨层信息传输的"断层"结构为单类异常检测提供了无需额外训练的原则性源-目标选择依据；单一预测器+训练集标定即可在多个基准上取得领先的分割与检测性能，且具备跨骨干泛化性。

Abstract: Feature reconstruction is widely used for unsupervised industrial anomaly detection in the single-class setting, where only normal images from one product category are available for training. We propose a single-class detector that asks what information one block can pass to another. We treat the depth of a frozen vision transformer as a sequence of discrete transitions. A directed predictor learns a block-to-block hidden-state transition pointwise over tokens. Adjacent-layer cosine similarity exposes four sharp fault lines in DINOv2 ViT-L, separating stable depth segments. The deepest blocks retain semantic context but lose local detail; a prediction from deep or shallow sources to sensitive targets therefore exposes the information that cannot be transported. The fault structure fixes the source and target roles, and the same rule transfers to ViT-B and RADIO-B. We use a bounded foreground prior calibrated solely on training images: it attenuates background evidence without also erasing defects outside the estimated foreground. Using one predictor architecture and a common training-only calibration protocol, image AUROC is 99.75 on MVTec-AD, 99.42 on VisA and 94.27 on Real-IAD. On Real-IAD the object-level AUROC is 96.26. At 256x256 pixel resolution, P-AUROC/P-AUPRO are 98.46/95.97 on MVTec-AD, 99.21/96.12 on VisA and 99.26/96.59 on Real-IAD.

</details>


### [51] [When GeoFMs Meet VHR: Benchmarking Foundation Models on Very High Resolution Earth Observation Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08003)
*Kshitij Raj Sharma,Pierre Adorni,Minh-Tan Pham,Dirk Tiede,Sébastien Lefèvre*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VHR-Bench基准（10个≤1.5m GSD数据集、5类任务、8个应用域），系统评测12个编码器发现自然图像预训练与VHR专门预训练互补；据此在102万VHR瓦片上继续自监督预训练DINOv3 ViT-L，在标签稀缺时显著提升、全量标签时小幅提升，且不增加推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流地理空间基础模型多在Sentinel-1/2与Landsat的10m及更粗GSD上预训练，但众多应用依赖可分辨建筑、道路与设施的甚高分辨率（VHR）影像，何种预训练范式能有效迁移到该尺度尚缺乏系统研究。

Method: 构建VHR-Bench：10个数据集、GSD≤1.5m、覆盖5类任务、8个应用域、卫星与航空双平台；在冻结骨干、两种训练数据比例下评测三个预训练层级共12个编码器；随后在1.02M预留VHR瓦片上对DINOv3 ViT-L继续自监督预训练，保持下游协议不变。

Result: 大规模自然图像预训练与VHR专门预训练均能有效迁移且优势互补：标签稀缺时SkySense领先，全量标签时Web预训练的DINOv3 ViT-L略优、两者接近；仅用Sentinel尺度影像预训练的编码器在RGB输入下迁移最差；VHR续训模型在标签稀缺时明显优于原发布骨干，全量时略有提升，推理参数与算力不增。

Conclusion: 支持"先泛化、后专门化"策略：已具备强泛化能力的自然图像预训练可通过短程VHR续训进一步提升，同时保留其通用视觉特征。

Abstract: Geospatial foundation models are a widely adopted starting point for remote sensing tasks. Most are pretrained on Sentinel-1/2 and Landsat imagery at a ground sampling distance (GSD) of 10m or coarser. Many applications instead rely on very high resolution (VHR) imagery, where individual buildings, roads, and amenities are resolved. We study which pretraining regimes transfer well to this scale. We introduce VHR-Bench, a benchmark suite of ten datasets at 1.5m GSD or finer spanning five task families, eight application domains, and both satellite and aerial platforms. We evaluate twelve encoders in three pretraining tiers with frozen backbones at two training-data fractions. We find that both pretraining on large natural-image collections and VHR-specific pretraining transfer effectively to VHR tasks, with complementary strengths. SkySense leads when labels are scarce; at the full label fraction the web-pretrained DINOv3 ViT-L edges ahead, with the two close together there. Encoders pretrained only on Sentinel-scale imagery transfer least well under RGB input. Guided by these complementary results, we continue the self-supervised pretraining of DINOv3 ViT-L on 1.02 million held-out VHR tiles and evaluate the resulting encoder without changing the downstream protocol. The continued model gives a clear improvement over the released backbone when labels are scarce and a modest one at the full fraction, at no increase in inference parameters or compute. These findings support a generalize-then-specialize strategy: already strong, broad natural-image pretraining can be further improved by a short VHR continuation stage while preserving its general visual features.

</details>


### [52] [Bri3D: Bridging the Object-Scene Gap in Single-Image 3D Generation via Scene-Anchor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07961)
*Hongye Hou,Shuai Zhang,Hongjie Li,Zhuoxiao Li,Haiping Wang,Yuan Liu,Wufan Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Bri3D 通过"实例感知体素场景锚点"桥接场景级结构推理与物体级生成先验，从单图生成组合式3D场景：先预测颜色编码的场景骨架，再为每个实例提取锚点并细化为保持位姿、兼容先验的体素支撑，最后用冻结的物体生成器合成细节几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单图生成组合式3D场景需同时恢复细节几何与一致空间布局。现有物体到场景方法（SAM3D、MIDI）依赖图像上下文推理或实例间建模，在场景级监督有限时难以协调多物体；直接把物体中心生成器扩展到整场景会损伤几何，且无法保持实例的尺度、朝向与位姿。

Method: 提出粗到细框架 Bri3D：(1) 预测颜色编码的场景脚手架，编码粗略物体几何与空间布局；(2) 将每个实例提取为场景锚点，并细化为保持位姿、与预训练先验兼容的体素支撑；(3) 冻结的物体生成器在锚点定义的空间参考系中合成带纹理的细节几何。该设计将场景组织与物体级细节合成解耦，使场景级训练专注于空间组织与锚点细化，同时复用预训练物体先验。

Result: 在场景布局、物体几何、视觉质量与泛化性上均取得提升；Bri3D 能高效预测场景布局，完整场景生成速度优于 SceneGen 与 SAM3D。

Conclusion: 以体素场景锚点为接口、解耦场景组织与细节合成，是复用物体生成先验、实现高质量单图组合式3D场景生成的有效途径，并兼具效率优势。

Abstract: Generating compositional 3D scenes from a single image requires recovering detailed object geometry and coherent spatial arrangements. Existing object-to-scene methods, including SAM3D and MIDI, extend object generation through image-context reasoning or inter-instance modeling. Coordinating objects remains challenging with limited scene-level supervision. Directly extending object-centric generators to entire scenes can degrade geometry and fail to preserve instance scale, orientation, and pose. We propose Bri3D, a framework bridging scene-level structural reasoning and object-level generative priors through instance-aware voxel scene anchors. Bri3D predicts a color-coded scene scaffold encoding coarse object geometry and spatial arrangements. Each instance is extracted as a scene anchor and refined into a pose-preserving, prior-compatible voxel support, enabling a frozen object generator to synthesize detailed textured geometry in the anchor-defined spatial reference. This coarse-to-fine design separates scene organization from object-scale detail synthesis, focusing scene-level training on spatial organization and anchor refinement while reusing pretrained object priors. Experiments demonstrate improvements in scene layout, object geometry, visual quality, and generalization. Bri3D predicts scene layouts efficiently; full scene generation is faster than SceneGen and SAM3D.

</details>


### [53] [Rotation Is Token-Blind: A Structural Criterion for Post-Training Quantization of Feed-Forward Geometry Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07955)
*Huanfei He,Bohao Zhang,Jiaxin Zhang,Wei Sui,John See,Zeyd Boukhers,Cong Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出结构感知PTQ框架QMapAnything，将旋转/平滑建模为token-blind重参数化群，并引入统计量Γ判定是否需差异化偏置校正，实现前馈3D模型的W4A8/ W8A8高效部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈3D重建模型（Geometry Transformers）序列长、注意力深，计算与内存开销大，需PTQ部署；而现有PTQ主要针对通道级离群值做平滑或正交旋转，无法处理异构token类型共享同一激活量化网格导致的“按token类型差异化退化”问题。

Method: 将保浮点旋转与平滑统一刻画为token-blind重参数化群，证明其仅带来共模增益、不改变相对token SNR；引入统计量Γ，融合特殊token的量化劣势与输出杠杆度，以预测旋转之外是否需要差异化偏置校正。

Result: 几何基准上W4A8接近全精度性能；W8A8下峰值内存较FP32降低2.2–3.1×，真实硬件上最高3.1×加速。

Conclusion: 为资源受限边缘端部署前馈3D重建模型提供了有理论依据且高效的PTQ方案。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D models use Transformers to predict camera poses, depth, and structure in one pass, avoiding iterative multi-view optimization. Long sequences and deep attention incur high compute and memory costs, motivating Post-Training Quantization (PTQ) for deployment. Existing PTQ methods mainly suppress channel-wise outliers by smoothing or orthogonal rotation. Geometry Transformers introduce a different challenge: heterogeneous token types share one activation quantization grid. A few special tokens may exert high leverage over global outputs and be updated by only a subset of blocks. Token-blind transforms, which apply the same transformation to every token, reduce overall quantization error but cannot remove differentiated degradation across token types. We propose QMapAnything, a structure-aware PTQ framework. We cast floating-point-preserving rotation and smoothing as a token-blind reparameterization group, and prove that they yield common-mode gains while leaving relative token SNR unchanged. We introduce a statistic $Γ$ that combines a special token's quantization disadvantage and output leverage to predict whether differentiated bias correction is needed beyond rotation. Experiments on geometric benchmarks show that W4A8 closely matches full-precision performance. Under W8A8, peak memory falls by $2.2$--$3.1\times$ versus FP32, with up to $3.1\times$ speedup on real hardware. These results provide a principled and efficient approach to deploying feed-forward 3D reconstruction models in resource-constrained edges.

</details>


### [54] [WAM-Sim0.1: World Anchored Memory Meets Online RL for Consistent and Realistic Driving Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07939)
*Jiahao Cui,Jiaye Li,Wenhao Zhang,Yaoyi Li,Shan Luan,Quanhui Tang,Jianhua Han,Lin Cheng,Hang Xu,Siyu Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WAM-Sim：以持久层次化记忆为锚的驾驶世界模型，并用在线 flow-matching RL 优化自生成 rollout，缓解长时域漂移与质量衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归驾驶世界模型依赖瞬态条件信号、缺乏持久状态，导致长 rollout 中出现结构漂移、身份漂移与质量衰减。

Method: 三层时间/语义尺度的持久上下文：位姿对齐结构 token（持久道路拓扑）、只读场景 token（地理定位背景外观）、一次写入实例 token（跨遮挡与分块边界保持交通参与者身份）；并提出在线后训练框架，以 flow-matching RL 在自生成 rollout 上优化，奖励由层级对齐视觉奖励与 VLM 物理评判器构成。

Result: nuScenes 上 FID=3.47、FVD=25.72，下游感知 24.16 mAP、道路 mIoU 71.23；自采数据分钟级 rollout 显著缓解结构与身份漂移，保持高场景相似度与主体身份一致性。

Conclusion: 不变记忆将策略优化约束在锚定上下文内，抑制语义坍缩与 reward hacking；在线 RL 系统性地强化物理常识与细粒度视觉保真度。

Abstract: Autoregressive driving world models systematically suffer from structural drift, identity drift, and quality decay over long rollout horizons due to transient conditioning signals that lack persistent state. We present WAM-Sim, a driving world model anchored by a persistent hierarchical memory and optimized via online flow-matching RL. WAM-Sim structures persistent context across three temporal and semantic scales: pose-aligned structural tokens encoding persistent road topology, read-only scene tokens supplying geolocated background appearance, and write-once instance tokens preserving individual traffic participant identities across occlusions and chunk boundaries. To restore visual sharpness and physical plausibility degraded by multi-scale conditioning, we introduce an online post-training framework that optimizes self-generated rollouts via flow-matching RL guided by hierarchy-aligned visual rewards and a vision-language model physical judge. The invariant memory empirically confines policy optimization within the anchored context, helping suppress semantic collapse and reward hacking, while online RL systematically enforces physical commonsense and fine-grained visual fidelity. On the nuScenes benchmark, WAM-Sim achieves a superior generation fidelity (FID=3.47, FVD=25.72) and strong downstream perception performance (24.16 mAP, 71.23 road mIoU). On minute-scale rollouts of self-collected data, WAM-Sim substantially mitigates structural and identity drift, preserving high scene similarity and subject identity coherence across extended horizons. Project page: https://fudan-generative-vision.github.io/WAM-Sim.

</details>


### [55] [Structure from Collision via Hypothesis-Space Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07900)
*Takuhiro Kaneko*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将 Structure from Collision (SfC) 重构为假设空间推理：用显式结构假设表示隐藏空腔，先以无梯度的全局阶段（前向 MPM）搜索假设，再用基于可微 MPM 的局部阶段精化，性能优于密度基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物体内部隐藏空腔（存在性与位置）对预测仿真、机器人操作与无损检测至关重要，但静态可见表面无直接线索，只能依赖碰撞引起的形变间接推断。密度基 SfC 基线存在两难：表面匹配梯度不直接指向空腔位置，且空洞对应材料缺失，使密度梯度在空洞内或其附近微弱甚至无信息。

Method: 把 SfC 形式化为假设空间推理，将隐藏空腔表示为显式结构假设（变量编码结构先验假设）而非隐式密度更新。全局阶段在该假设空间上用仅前向的 MPM 仿真做无梯度优化；局部阶段将被选假设精化为拉格朗日粒子系统，用可微 MPM 仿真做基于梯度的优化。

Result: 在 SfC 基准上，隐藏结构重建与未来预测均优于密度基 SfC 基线；并通过内部拓扑压力测试与鲁棒性分析刻画方法的适用范围与局限。

Conclusion: 以显式假设替代隐式密度可规避空洞区域梯度失效问题，全局–局部两级优化兼顾搜索鲁棒性与精度，并给出明确的能力边界分析。

Abstract: Knowledge of the hidden interior structure of an object, including the presence and location of cavities, is essential for predictive simulation, robotic manipulation, and nondestructive inspection. However, such cavities leave no direct signature on the static visible surface, making collision-induced deformation one of the few cues used to infer their presence. Structure from Collision (SfC) addresses this problem by recovering the hidden interior structure of an object from multiview observations of a collision event. SfC is challenging for two reasons: hidden cavities are only indirectly observable through surface motion, and voids correspond to the absence of material, making direct optimization using standard density representations difficult. The density-based SfC baseline inherits both difficulties. Surface-matching gradients do not directly indicate the cavity location, while density gradients can be weak or uninformative inside or near void regions. Therefore, we reformulate SfC as hypothesis-space reasoning, representing hidden cavities as explicit structural hypotheses whose variables encode available structural assumptions rather than relying on implicit density updates. A global stage performs gradient-free optimization over this hypothesis space using forward-only material point method (MPM) simulation. A local stage then refines the selected hypothesis as a Lagrangian particle system through gradient-based optimization using differentiable MPM simulation. On the SfC benchmark, our method improves hidden-structure reconstruction and future prediction over the density-based SfC baseline. Interior-topology stress tests and robustness analyses characterize its scope and limitations. The project page is available at https://www.kecl.ntt.co.jp/people/kaneko.takuhiro/projects/sfc-hypothesis/.

</details>


### [56] [PVDepth: Panoramic Video Depth Estimation via Geometry-Aware Spatiotemporal Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07873)
*Chuanxin Song,Peixi Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出全景视频深度数据集PanoCARLA与端到端框架PVDepth，通过渐进球面噪声初始化与立方体校正时序建模解决ERP畸变与非线性时序问题，实现几何准确且时序一致的深度估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全景视频深度估计对VR与世界模型重要，但受两方面阻碍：大规模训练数据稀缺；ERP投影特有的时空挑战使透视模型难以直接迁移。

Method: 构建大规模合成RGB-D全景视频数据集PanoCARLA（自然运动轨迹、无人机式漫游视角）；基于透视视频深度模型改造为端到端框架PVDepth，引入两机制：(1)渐进球面感知噪声初始化，将噪声分布从平面退火至球面以适配非均匀信息密度；(2)立方体校正时序建模，用辅助立方体图时序分支校正ERP域非线性时序动态。

Result: 大量实验表明PVDepth性能优越，生成几何准确、时序一致的深度序列；代码与数据将开源于GitHub。

Conclusion: PanoCARLA与PVDepth为全景视频深度估计提供数据与方法基础，验证了球面感知噪声初始化与立方体时序校正在ERP域的有效性。

Abstract: Panoramic video depth estimation is pivotal for applications such as Virtual Reality and World Models. However, advancements in this field are impeded by two primary obstacles: the scarcity of large-scale training data and the unique spatiotemporal challenges of Equirectangular Projection (ERP), which hinder the direct transfer of perspective models. In this paper, we first present PanoCARLA, a large-scale synthetic RGB-D panoramic video dataset, featuring natural motion trajectories and drone-like roaming perspectives. Building on this foundation, we propose PVDepth, an end-to-end framework adapted from perspective video depth models. To tackle ERP-specific geometric distortions and consequent non-linear temporal dynamics, we introduce two core mechanisms: (1) A Progressive Sphere-aware Noise Initialization strategy that anneals the noise distribution from planar to spherical, guiding the model to adapt to non-uniform information density; and (2) A Cube-rectified Temporal Modeling module that incorporates an auxiliary cubemap temporal branch to rectify non-linear temporal dynamics in the ERP domain. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PVDepth achieves superior performance, generating geometrically accurate and temporally consistent depth sequences. Code and data will be released at https:// github.com/ChuanxinSong/PVDepth.

</details>


### [57] [MV-Sign: Multi-View 3D Motion Integration and Enhanced Text-to-Motion Conditioning for Sign Language Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07811)
*Singyu Muk,Kaustubh Kundu,Kunyuan Xie,Weijie Wang,Kalin Stefanov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多视角3D运动数据与文本条件联合优化的手语生成(SLP)框架，并发布How2Sign-MV3D等新数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D手语生成依赖单视角正面视频拟合的伪3D数据，存在自遮挡与手部细节丢失的噪声监督，且文本条件建模较弱，导致生成质量下降。

Method: (1) 构建How2Sign-MV3D数据集，采用跨视角数据策展管线融合多视角估计以消除遮挡并过滤非手语片段；(2) 设计鲁棒SLP模型，包含手语感知时空VQ-VAE(ST-VQVAE)实现身体-手部联合token化、伪gloss适配器及关键帧感知检索以增强文本-动作对齐。

Result: 在How2Sign基准上，相较SOTA，身体与手部DTW-PA-JPE分别降低32.8%与20.9%。

Conclusion: 多视角3D数据与文本条件路径的联合优化可有效缓解噪声监督与弱文本条件问题；并发布ASLCitizen-3D、MM-WAuslan-MV3D数据集及SGNify-MV多视角视频基准以推动社区研究。

Abstract: Sign language production (SLP) aims to generate appropriate sign language motion from spoken language text. However, current 3D SLP methods suffer from noisy supervision and weak text conditioning. Specifically, these methods typically rely on pseudo-3D motion data fitted from single-view frontal videos, which introduces severe self-occlusion and loses fine-grained hand details, ultimately degrading downstream production. To overcome these limitations, we propose a comprehensive framework that jointly integrates multi-view 3D motion data and optimizes the text-conditioning pathway. First, we introduce How2Sign-MV3D, a high-fidelity dataset constructed via a novel cross-view curation pipeline that fuses multi-view estimates to resolve occlusions and filter non-signing segments. Second, we design a robust SLP model incorporating a sign-aware spatio-temporal VQ-VAE (ST-VQVAE) for joint body-hand tokenization, a pseudo-gloss adapter, and keyframe-aware retrieval to enhance text-to-motion alignment. Evaluated on the How2Sign benchmark, our framework reduces body and hand DTW-PA-JPE by 32.8% and 20.9% respectively compared to the state-of-the-art. Finally, we release two new high-quality 3D datasets, ASLCitizen-3D and MM-WAuslan-MV3D, alongside SGNify-MV as a multi-view video benchmark to support broader community research.

</details>


### [58] [Rain Beyond Streaks: Cascaded Realistic Rain and Wet Surface Synthesis via Scene Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07793)
*Melissa Tijink,Kirill Tolstobrov,Shunxin Wang,Estefania Talavera,Duc V. Le,Luuk Spreeuwers,Shaodi You,Nicola Strisciuglio*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CascadeRS：利用深度与分割先验，以零样本、可控的物理启发生成管线同时合成雨丝、反射与积水等表面效应，并发布Cityscapes-CascadeRS数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有雨天合成方法多只关注雨丝，忽略反射、积水等表面效应，而这些对感知真实性与下游视觉任务鲁棒性至关重要；真实雨天条件下的评估数据也较为缺乏。

Method: 提出CascadeRS框架，以深度和分割先验为依据，在零样本、可控的生成式流程中渲染与场景语义一致的雨丝及反射、积水表面效应，保持场景一致性与可控性。

Result: 生成照片级真实感的雨天城市场景；通过大规模用户研究进行定性验证并结合定量评估证实高感知真实度；构建Cityscapes-CascadeRS数据集用于研究真实雨天条件下的下游任务表现。

Conclusion: CascadeRS可作为可扩展且真实的雨景生成框架，用于评估与提升恶劣雨天条件下视觉系统的鲁棒性；代码与数据集已开源。

Abstract: Realistic rain synthesis is a persistent challenge for vision systems operating in adverse weather. Existing methods primarily focus on generating rain streaks, often neglecting consequent surface effects such as reflections and puddles, which are critical for perceptual realism and robustness of downstream vision tasks. We introduce CascadeRS, a rain synthesis framework that leverages depth and segmentation priors to render scene-consistent rain and surface effects. In addition to rain streaks, CascadeRS produces reflections and puddles aligned with scene semantics through a physics-inspired zero-shot, controllable generative pipeline. It produces photorealistic rainy urban scenes, with high perceptual realism, which we validate qualitatively with an extensive user study and quantitatively. We produce the Cityscapes-CascadeRS dataset, to study downstream task performance under real rainy conditions. Our results establish CascadeRS as a scalable and realistic rain-generation framework for evaluating and enhancing the robustness of vision in adverse rainy weather. Code and dataset are available via https://github.com/nis-research/CascadeRainSynthesis

</details>


### [59] [Coverage Hides Churn: Auditing and Preserving Conformal Decisions as VLM Label Registries Grow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07502)
*Jai Kumar Sharma,Peeyush Tapadiya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文揭示视觉-语言分类器的标签扩展会通过softmax归一化项"静默地"改变旧标签的conformal决策，且覆盖率指标无法察觉；提出生命周期审计方法及PrefixCP修复方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言分类器可从文本直接学习新标签，标签清单持续增长。常用基于softmax的conformal评分将每个标签与全体标签的均值比较，因此新增标签会改变该均值，进而改变所有旧标签的评分——即使模型、图像与旧校准数据均未变化。而覆盖率保证只检验真实标签，无法发现这类对旧标签决策的偏移。

Method: (1) 形式化刻画标签增长导致的归一化项漂移及其对旧标签评分的影响；(2) 提出生命周期审计，将旧决策变化率与覆盖率、预测集大小并列度量；(3) 提出PrefixCP：每个类别固定其被加入时的标签注册表（归一化前缀），使旧决策永不改变，并在可交换性下保持精确的类条件覆盖率，用一次前缀扫描同时计算所有历史归一化项。

Result: 在11种设置中，更新后重校准虽维持覆盖率，但仍有53%–90%的新图像至少有一个旧标签决策发生改变。在5个仅改变归一化项、保留旧校准的受控设置中，旧类覆盖率变化不超过0.01，而60%–67%的图像决策发生改变。PrefixCP可恢复"永不更新参考"与"重校准"之间集合大小差距的40.9%–58.5%。

Conclusion: 标签清单增长是conformal预测中被忽视的生命周期问题：覆盖率保证对旧标签决策漂移完全不敏感。PrefixCP以极低计算开销实现旧决策稳定与类条件覆盖率保障，并在集合效率上优于不更新参考的方案，为持续演化的开放词汇分类系统提供可审计的可靠预测框架。

Abstract: Vision-language classifiers can learn new labels from text alone, so the label lists they serve keep growing. We show that this growth silently changes conformal decisions about old labels. Common softmax-based conformal scores compare each label with an average over all labels; adding labels changes that average, and with it the score of every old label, even when the model, the image, and the old calibration data stay the same. Recalibrating after the update keeps the coverage guarantee, yet across eleven settings it changes at least one old decision on 53-90% of new images. Coverage cannot reveal this, because it checks only the true label: in five controlled settings that keep the old calibration, so that only the normalizer changes, old-class coverage moves by at most 0.01 while 60-67% of images change. We propose a lifecycle audit that measures these changes alongside coverage and set size, and a simple fix, PrefixCP: each class keeps the label registry that existed when it was added. Its old decisions then never change, each class keeps exact class-conditional coverage under exchangeability, and one prefix scan computes all historical normalizers at once. PrefixCP recovers 40.9-58.5% of the set-size gap between never updating the reference and recalibrating, which gives smaller sets but changes old decisions.

</details>


### [60] [Degree-Equalized View-Graph Selection under Tight Pair Budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07498)
*Minhyeok Im,Jongseong Brad Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的度均衡分配规则DEAL，通过对已充分覆盖图像对指数降权，解决一次性图像对选择器度分布集中问题，提升SfM匹配覆盖率与配准率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Structure-from-Motion中对全部图像对进行完全匹配的代价随图像数量呈二次增长，因此需将预算内的候选对子集送入后端。现有一次性选择器仅依据图像检索、廉价几何探测或学习先验打分，并在完全匹配前定下B对，其优先级不考虑每张图像已分配的度数，易使边集中于少数图像：在九个1DSfM大规模网络照片集上（B=2N），覆盖率top-B选择器使54%的已覆盖图像实现度数不超过2，导致即使所选图近似连通且几乎完全验证，重建仍可能失败。

Method: 提出免训练的贪心规则Degree-Equalized Allocation（DEAL）：对端点已被充分覆盖的图像对施加指数级降权，在贪心选择过程中动态均衡各图像的度数分配。

Result: 在九个1DSfM集合上，DEAL将基于同一先验的一次性选择器从0.776提升至0.929；在共享后端下，其平均配准图像比例与SfM监督边先验相当（0.929 vs 0.930），且无需训练。在基本饱和的38场景标准基准上，DEAL在评估的免训练选择器中取得最高均值（0.875 vs 一次性方法0.858）。

Conclusion: 度数均衡是图像对选择中被忽视但关键的因素；简单的免训练指数降权贪心规则即可显著改善覆盖质量，并在不依赖监督的情况下达到与SfM监督先验相当的配准效果。

Abstract: Fully matching all image pairs in Structure-from-Motion is quadratic in the number of images, so practical pipelines pass a budgeted subset to the final matching and reconstruction backend. One-shot selectors score candidates with image retrieval, cheap geometric probes, or a learned prior and finalize B pairs before full matching. Their priorities do not account for the degree already allocated to each image, which can concentrate edges on a few images. Across nine large 1DSfM internet photo collections at B=2N, the coverage top-B selector leaves 54% of covered images with realized degree at most two. Reconstruction can then fail even when the selected graph is nearly connected and almost fully verified. Our training-free greedy rule, Degree-Equalized Allocation (DEAL), exponentially downweights pairs whose endpoints are already well covered. On these collections, DEAL improves the one-shot selector built on the same prior (from 0.776 to 0.929) and matches the mean registered-image ratio of an SfM-supervised edge prior under the shared backend (0.929 vs 0.930), without training. On the largely saturated 38-scene standard benchmark suite, DEAL gives the highest mean among the evaluated training-free selectors (0.875 vs 0.858 one-shot).

</details>


### [61] [National-scale mapping of tree species composition in Switzerland from high-resolution aerial imagery and multimodal data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08095)
*Yixin Zhou,Luca Ferrari,Verena C. Griess,Nataliia Rehush,Konrad Schindler,Mirela Beloiu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究在瑞士30 m分辨率下绘制了10个主要树种及一个"其他"类别的断面积占比图，用深度学习融合航空RGB影像、ALS、AlphaEarth卫星嵌入与气候变量，并在NFI与森林保护区数据上验证（像素级macro-R² 0.552、样地级0.733）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全国森林地图多采用"单像素单一优势树种"的表示方式，无法反映伴生树种的比例，而树种组成决定了森林生产力及对干旱、风倒、虫害等扰动的响应能力。

Method: 以3,992个瑞士国家森林调查（NFI）样地的断面积观测为标签，构建深度学习模型，融合0.1 m航空RGB影像、机载激光扫描（ALS）数据、年度AlphaEarth卫星嵌入及气候变量，对11个类别（10个树种+Other）逐像素预测断面积占比；采用空间交叉验证与独立森林保护区数据集评估。

Result: 空间交叉验证macro-R²为0.485；独立森林保护区数据上像素级macro-R²为0.552、样地级为0.733，优势类识别准确率分别为73.2%（像素）与81.1%（样地）。制图结果揭示了优势类地图所遗漏的槭树与白蜡作为广泛伴生种，并重现了从低地阔叶林经云杉至林线附近落叶松与瑞士石松的海拔序列。

Conclusion: 通过解析30 m像素内部的树种混交结构，该地图补充了样地清查统计，提供空间连续信息，可支持森林监测、混交林经营与气候适应规划。

Abstract: Tree-species composition shapes forest productivity and how forests respond to climate disturbances such as drought, windthrow and insect outbreaks. Yet most national forest maps assign each pixel a single dominant species and cannot show the share of accompanying species. We mapped the basal-area shares of ten main tree species and a residual Other class across Switzerland at 30 m resolution. A deep-learning model combining 0.1 m aerial RGB imagery, airborne laser scanning (ALS), annual AlphaEarth satellite embeddings, and climate variables was trained on basal-area observations from 3,992 Swiss National Forest Inventory (NFI) sites. In spatial cross-validation, the model reached a macro-$R^2$ of 0.485 across the 11 classes. On an independent forest-reserve dataset, the map reached a macro-$R^2$ of 0.552 for individual 30 m pixels and 0.733 for plots, and identified the dominant class in 73.2% of pixels and 81.1% of plots. The map shows maple and ash as widespread minor components that dominant-class maps omit. It also reproduces the elevational sequence from broadleaved lowland stands through spruce to larch and Arolla pine near the treeline. By resolving the species mixture within each 30 m pixel, the map complements field inventory statistics with spatially continuous information that can support forest monitoring, the management of mixed stands, and climate-adaptation planning across Switzerland.

</details>


### [62] [TerraMesh-Masks: Open-Vocabulary Segmentation for Earth Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08081)
*Benedikt Blumenstiel,Hugues Devimeux,Johannes Jakubik,Konrad Schindler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出面向地球观测（EO）的大规模开放词汇分割数据集TerraMesh-Masks及其专家审核评测集，并基于TerraMind构建开放词汇版本，显著优于通用SOTA模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇分割（OVS）方法在自然图像上表现良好，但由于语义、尺度、成像模态与地理背景差异，迁移到地球观测数据时效果很差；同时缺乏大规模、EO专用的OVS数据集与基准。

Method: 构建TerraMesh-Masks数据集：含1600万个二值掩码，与900万个多模态TerraMesh样本对齐，标注语义来自OpenStreetMap与Overture；另提供523个样本、覆盖200+类别的专家审核评测集。并基于TerraMind构建开放词汇版本进行验证。

Result: 开放词汇版TerraMind达到35.18% IoU，显著优于DINOv3.txt（16.29%）与SAM-3（11.33%）等通用SOTA模型。

Conclusion: TerraMesh-Masks填补了EO领域OVS数据与基准的空白，验证了EO专用数据对开放词汇分割迁移能力的关键作用；数据集以宽松许可公开发布。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary segmentation (OVS) enables models to segment a variety of concepts specified in natural language, removing the need for fixed label sets and fine-tuning. While recent OVS approaches have shown strong performance on natural images, they transfer poorly to Earth observation (EO) data due to differing semantics, scales, sensing modality, and geographic context. To date, large-scale EO-specific datasets and benchmarks for OVS remain scarce. We, therefore, introduce TerraMesh-Masks, a large-scale open-vocabulary segmentation dataset for EO, comprising 16 million binary masks aligned with nine million multimodal TerraMesh samples and annotated using OpenStreetMap and Overture-derived semantics. In addition, we provide an expert-reviewed evaluation set of 523 samples covering over 200 classes to enable robust evaluation. To demonstrate the impact of TerraMesh-Masks, we build an open-vocabulary version of TerraMind. Our results demonstrate significantly improved performance over general-purpose state-of-the-art models reaching 35.18% IoU versus 16.29% for DINOv3.txt and 11.33% for SAM-3. We release the dataset publicly under a permissive license at https://huggingface.co/datasets/ibm-esa-geospatial/TerraMesh-Masks.

</details>


### [63] [PSGAgent: Enhancing MLLMs' Dynamic Visual Perception for Panoptic Scene Graph Generation via Agentic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07408)
*Qingyun Liu,Yuankun Liu,Jiaye Feng,Jie Huang,Zonghong Dai,Haochen Li,Jingkun Wang,Weiping Li,Tong Mo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PSGAgent智能体框架，通过"思考-行动-观察"循环与工具调用突破MLLM在PSG任务中的固定分辨率感知瓶颈，并结合两阶段训练实现SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有应用于全景场景图生成（PSG）的多模态大模型存在感知瓶颈：作为静态观察者，其视野被固定分辨率所限，导致容易忽略涉及小型或边缘物体的关键关系，且在复杂场景中难以区分语义模糊的谓词。

Method: 构建以"think-act-observe"循环为核心的智能体框架，赋予MLLM两个工具：用于小型/边界关系局部探索的Region Zoom-In Tool，以及用于关系聚焦分割、过滤无关上下文噪声的Visual Highlight Tool。训练上采用两阶段流程：先构建高质量、错误感知的多模态思维链（M-CoT）数据集进行冷启动监督；再设计分层奖励机制并结合Group Sequence Policy Optimization（GSPO）实现策略化、自适应的工具使用。

Result: 在PSG基准上的大量实验表明，PSGAgent达到SOTA性能，在全景与细粒度场景理解两方面均展现强能力。

Conclusion: 通过智能体化的主动视觉感知与阶段化训练优化，PSGAgent有效缓解固定分辨率感知瓶颈，验证了工具增强感知范式的有效性。

Abstract: Panoptic Scene Graph generation (PSG) requires holistic yet fine-grained reasoning over complex relations among objects. However, current Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) applied to PSG suffer from a perception bottleneck: they function as static observers limited by fixed-resolution views. As a result, models often overlook critical relations involving small or peripheral objects and struggle to differentiate ambiguous predicates in complex scenes. To address these limitations, we propose PSGAgent, an agentic framework that enhances visual perception for PSG through a think-act-observe loop. PSGAgent allows MLLMs to dynamically refine their visual perception using two tools: a Region Zoom-In Tool for local exploration of small or boundary relations and a Visual Highlight Tool for relation-focused segmentation that filters out irrelevant contextual noise. To optimize decision-making, we construct a two-stage training pipeline. First, we create a high-quality, error-aware Multimodal Chain-of-Thought (M-CoT) dataset that provides robust supervision for cold-start learning. Then, we design a novel hierarchical reward mechanism integrated with Group Sequence Policy Optimization (GSPO) to enable strategic and adaptive tool usage. Extensive experiments on the PSG benchmark show that PSGAgent achieves SOTA performance, demonstrating strong capability in both holistic and fine-grained scene understanding.

</details>


### [64] [FreePath: Neural Mean Free Path for Image Dehazing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07398)
*Rongrong Gao,Jingxuan Feng,Wenyu Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FreePath：将图像去雾表示为特征空间中的路径状态恢复，用平均自由程概念替代传统大气参数估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有去雾方法分两类：一是反演显式散射变量，在复杂场景下不稳定；二是学习直接恢复映射，往往丢弃了雾形成的物理组织性。

Method: 受视觉响应在与雾交互前可传播的期望距离启发，将去雾建模为特征空间路径状态恢复。在残差编码器-解码器内，Mean Free Path Residual Block 学习路径场与碰撞场以引导雾约束修正；FreePath State Conservation Integration 在深层特征聚合中保持已恢复的路径状态。

Result: 在成对 CDD-11 雾图与真实 RTTS 场景上，相比代表性去雾基线，在全参考保真度、无参考感知质量与视觉可信度上均一致提升。

Conclusion: 以平均自由程为物理启发的特征空间状态恢复范式，可在不显式估计大气参数的前提下兼顾稳定性与物理组织性，取得优于基线的去雾效果。

Abstract: Single image dehazing requires recovering scene radiance from visual responses that are weakened and veiled by participating media. Existing methods either invert explicit scattering variables, which can be unstable under complex scenes, or learn direct restoration mappings, which often discard the physical organization of haze formation. We present \emph{FreePath}, a neural mean free path framework for image dehazing. Instead of estimating atmospheric parameters, FreePath represents dehazing as feature space path state restoration, inspired by the expected distance a visual response can propagate before interacting with haze. Within a residual encoder and decoder, the Mean Free Path Residual Block learns path and collision fields to guide haze constrained correction, while FreePath State Conservation Integration preserves restored path states during deep feature aggregation. Experiments on paired CDD-11 haze images and real RTTS scenes show that FreePath consistently improves full reference fidelity, no reference perceptual quality, and visual faithfulness over representative dehazing baselines.

</details>


### [65] [A Proxy-Based Method for Measuring Racial Disparities in~Text-to-Image~Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07132)
*Nia Mucher,Ngan Pham,Jason Chin,Aaron Brown,David Evans*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于美国反歧视法中"种族代理变量"的自动化审计方法，对文本到图像生成模型进行细粒度种族与肤色表征偏差比较分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像生成器可能存在针对人口统计基准的表征偏差，但缺乏细粒度、可比、且具备法律依据的审计手段；同时现有方法难以捕捉不体现在人口比例比较中的隐性偏差（如不同种族/肤色间图像质量差异）。

Method: 将美国反歧视法中的种族代理变量（geographic、occupational、surname 三类，每类50个变体）迁移到审计流程；对每张生成图像进行种族分类，并与公开人口统计基准数据对比以识别过度/不足表征模式；另外用CIELAB值度量肤色，并计算图像组间余弦相似度，以刻画人口比较之外的质量与同质性偏差。实验测试 Imagen 4 Fast、Stable Diffusion 3.5 Large Turbo、Flux Schnell 三个模型。

Result: 模型间及不同代理类别间差异显著：Imagen 4 Fast 相比其他模型更过度表征被判为白人的图像，肤色更浅且更同质，并在姓氏与地理提示下对黑人群体呈现最大表征差距；Stable Diffusion 3.5 Large Turbo 整体上偏离普查数据最多，但肤色范围最多样。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一个可推广、具备法律依据的审计工具，可在不依赖隐含规范性公平定义的前提下，揭示模型输出的细粒度信息。

Abstract: We present an automated method for auditing text-to-image generators for representational bias against ground-truth demographic benchmarks. Our approach adapts the racial proxy variables used in U.S. antidiscrimination law to provide a fine-grained comparative analysis of variations in racial representation in generated images. We classify each generated image by race and then compare the model's distribution to publicly available demographic benchmark data to identify patterns of over- and under-representation. We also measure skin tone using CIELAB values and calculate cosine similarity amongst groups of images in order to capture biases that don't show up in demographic comparisons such as differences in image quality across races and skin tones. We report on experiments testing three models (Imagen 4 Fast, Stable Diffusion 3.5 Large Turbo, and Flux Schnell) using three categories of proxies (geographic, occupational, and surname) and 50 variants within each category. We find notable variation across the models as well as by proxy category. For example, Imagen 4 Fast overrepresents white classified images more than the other models tested and produces images with lighter, more homogeneous skin tones. Imagen 4 Fast also had the largest representational disparity for Black demographics among surname and geographical prompts. Stable Diffusion 3.5 Large Turbo produces results that overall diverge most from census data but yield the most diverse range of skin tones. Our method yields a generalizable and legally-grounded auditing tool that surfaces detailed information about model outputs without relying on implicit normative definitions of fairness.

</details>


### [66] [Wide View Synthesis from Overlapping Perspective Cameras in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07081)
*Kaixuan Zhang,Zhipeng Xiong,Minxian Li,Mingwu Ren,Xiatian Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出广视角合成（WVS）任务及免逐场景优化的前馈框架WV-GS，用分片透视渲染与空间仿射光度建模解决边缘拉伸和跨相机光度不一致，并构建WideDrive基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多透视相机系统在自动驾驶中广泛部署，需要从空间分离的观测合成统一广视角。传统图像拼接无法处理因相机中心分离带来的深度相关视差与遮挡，需要三维几何与可见性推理；而原生3DGS依赖逐场景优化，难以用于驾驶场景，且WVS还面临透视投影下边缘拉伸和跨相机光度不一致的问题。

Method: 将WVS形式化为在指定虚拟视点合成几何一致的广视角；提出WV-GS前馈高斯泼溅框架，无需逐场景优化。核心设计包括：Piecewise Perspective Rendering（PPR），通过共享同一相机中心的局部透视投影缓解边缘拉伸；Spatial Affine Photometric Modeling（SAPM），在训练中建模相机相关的光度差异。同时构建合成基准WideDrive（提供密集真值广视角），并在真实数据集上采用LiDAR投影的稀疏参考协议。

Result: 在多个数据集上的大量实验验证了WV-GS对WVS任务的有效性。

Conclusion: WV-GS以几何一致、免优化的方式实现多相机广视角合成，PPR与SAPM分别有效应对边缘拉伸与光度不一致；WideDrive基准与LiDAR稀疏参考协议为该任务提供评测基础，数据集与代码将公开。

Abstract: Multi-perspective-camera systems are widely adopted since they provide broad visual coverage for autonomous driving, motivating the synthesis of a unified wide view from spatially separated observations. In this work, we formulate wide view synthesis (WVS), which aims to synthesize a geometrically consistent wide view at a specified virtual viewpoint from multiple perspective cameras. Unlike conventional image stitching, WVS requires reasoning about 3D geometry and visibility to handle depth-dependent parallax and occlusions arising from spatially separated camera centers. While vanilla 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables high-fidelity view synthesis, its reliance on per-scene optimization limits its applicability to driving scenes. Furthermore, WVS introduces additional challenges, *e.g.*, peripheral stretching under perspective projection and photometric inconsistencies across cameras. To address these challenges, we propose **Wide-View Gaussian Splatting (WV-GS)**, a feed-forward Gaussian-splatting framework that synthesizes wide views without per-scene optimization. Specifically, we introduce Piecewise Perspective Rendering (PPR) to mitigate peripheral stretching through local perspective projections sharing a common camera center, and Spatial Affine Photometric Modeling (SAPM) to account for camera-dependent photometric variations during training. We further introduce **WideDrive**, a synthetic benchmark providing dense ground-truth wide views and adapt a LiDAR-projected sparse-reference protocol to real-world datasets. Extensive experiments on multiple datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of WV-GS for WVS. Dataset and code will be publicly released.

</details>


### [67] [Batch-Conditioned Semantic Anchors for Robust Transductive Adaptation of Vision--Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06993)
*Mohammed Rahman Sherif Khan Mohammad,Ardhendu Behera,Sandip Pradhan,Swagat Kumar,Amr Ahmed*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出JPTA，将直推式VLM自适应重构为批条件化的语义参考估计，在批先验偏移与稀疏场景下兼顾利用批结构与保持CLIP语义，超越TransCLIP与StatA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直推式自适应存在两难：激进方法利用批结构但易在稀疏、先验偏移或标签覆盖不足的批次上漂移；保守方法保留CLIP文本语义，但固定语义参考在批内证据充分时自适应不足。

Method: JPTA（Joint Prior and Transport Adaptation）将CLIP文本原型视为可校准迁移的语义参考而非固定先验或可自由替换项：估计批先验与图像侧软原型，构造"传输后的语义参考"，其影响强度由批可靠性缩放；仅在校准证据充分时向图像侧批结构偏移。

Result: 在标准基准、低有效类别场景、全类别评估及在线流上一致优于TransCLIP与StatA，在稀疏与先验偏移批次上增益最大；anchor-drift、active-support、absent-class-mass诊断显示其修复有效类别失配且无无约束直推漂移。

Conclusion: 稳健的直推式VLM自适应应以可靠批证据重新校准语义参考，而非在无约束直推与固定原型之间二选一。

Abstract: Transductive adaptation improves vision-language models at test time by refining predictions on an unlabeled batch, but current methods face a key trade-off. Aggressive approaches exploit batch structure but can drift when batches are sparse, have prior shift, or cover little of the label space. Conservative methods better preserve CLIP text semantics, but fixed semantic references can under-adapt even when the batch provides strong corrective evidence. We propose JPTA (Joint Prior and Transport Adaptation), a prior-aware framework that casts transductive VLM adaptation as batch-conditioned semantic reference estimation. Instead of treating CLIP text prototypes as fixed priors or freely replaceable, JPTA views them as semantic references that shift toward image-side batch structure only under calibrated evidence. It estimates batch priors and soft image-side prototypes to form transported semantic references whose impact is scaled by batch reliability. Across standard benchmarks, low effective-class regimes, all-class evaluation, and online streams, JPTA consistently outperforms TransCLIP and StatA, with the largest gains on sparse and prior-shifted batches. Anchor-drift, active-support, and absent-class-mass diagnostics show that JPTA fixes effective-class mismatch without unconstrained transductive drift, indicating that robust transductive VLM adaptation should recalibrate semantic references using reliable batch evidence rather than choose between unrestricted transduction and fixed prototypes. Code is available at https://github.com/MR-Sherif/JPTA.git.

</details>


### [68] [Deformable Contact-Aware 3D Object Placement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08005)
*Songhe Wang,David Miller,Charles A. Kamhoua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DCAP 将语言引导的 3D 物体摆放重构为“投放到平衡”过程，用语义释放状态代替静态 6-DoF 位姿预测，并由刚柔耦合仿真器在重力与摩擦下求解最终位置，从而显式分离语义意图与物理实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言引导 3D 物体摆放方法通常直接预测刚体 6-DoF 位姿，忽略了室内摆放本质上由接触决定这一物理现实，导致预测位姿悬空、穿模或无法使软表面发生形变。

Method: 提出 Deformable Contact-Aware Placement（DCAP）框架：不预测静态位姿，而是构造“语义释放状态”，指定投放位置、物体尺度与可形变部件；再由刚体-形变体耦合仿真器在重力与摩擦作用下使物体沉降到平衡态。该设计将语义意图与物理实现显式解耦。

Result: 在包含 2,063 个 prompt-asset-scene 案例的基准上显著优于静态基线：标准摆放场景下，相较最强原始刚体基线，VLM 物理合理性/自然度提升 21.2 分、人类偏好提升 26.5 分，平均穿透深度降低 72.3%；相较仿真 rollout 基线，残余速度降低 72.1%。

Conclusion: 结果证明，实现物理准确的 3D 摆放需要把语义意图与物理实现作为两个严格分离的过程来建模。

Abstract: Current language-guided 3D object placement typically predicts a rigid 6-DoF pose, ignoring the physical reality that indoor placements are dictated by contact. Consequently, predicted poses often float, interpenetrate supports, or fail to deform soft surfaces. We propose Deformable Contact-Aware Placement (DCAP), a framework that reframes placement as a drop-to-equilibrium process. Rather than predicting a static pose, DCAP constructs a semantic release state that specifies the drop location, object scale, and deformable components, allowing a coupled rigid-deformable simulator to settle the object under gravity and friction. This explicitly separates semantic intent from physical realization. Evaluated on a benchmark of 2,063 prompt-asset-scene cases, DCAP significantly outperforms static baselines. On standard placements, it improves VLM Physics & Naturalness by 21.2 points and human preference by 26.5 points over the strongest raw rigid baseline, while reducing mean penetration depth by 72.3%. Compared to simulated rollout baselines, DCAP reduces residual speed by 72.1%. Ultimately, our results prove that physically accurate 3D placement requires modeling semantic intent and physical realization as strictly separate processes.

</details>


### [69] [XM4D: 4D Reconstruction through Certifiable Geometric Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07902)
*Haoyu Han,Jack Benarroch Jedlicki,Jiahui Lei,Heng Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 XM4D，一个基于可认证全局最优几何优化的 4D 重建框架，将相机运动估计与非刚性形变解耦，分别建模为仅含旋转的 QCQP 并求解其 SDP 松弛，配合 GPU 加速的 Burer-Monteiro 黎曼优化求解器，进而初始化动态高斯泼溅做光度精修。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4D 重建需联合估计相机运动、场景几何与非刚性形变，属于高度非凸优化问题，全局最优性难以保证，现有方法精度与可扩展性受限。

Method: 将相机运动与非刚性形变分离估计：(1) 相机运动——用基础深度模型把静态点轨迹提升为 3D，构建联合估计位姿、深度尺度与静态路标的 scaled bundle adjustment，解析消去平移与路标，化为关于缩放旋转的 QCQP；(2) 非刚性重建——采用嵌入式形变图（EDG）建模动态运动，其跟踪、刚性、时序正则项同样化为仅含旋转的 QCQP。两问题均取 SDP 松弛，用 GPU 加速、基于黎曼优化的 Burer-Monteiro 求解器高效求解，并用恢复几何初始化动态高斯泼溅进行光度精修。

Result: 在七个基准上的实验表明：静态与动态场景下相机运动估计准确，优化可扩展至数千帧，新视角渲染质量达到 SOTA。

Conclusion: 将结构化 QCQP/SDP 松弛与可验证全局最优性证书引入 4D 重建，可在保证可认证最优性的同时实现高效、可扩展的高质量重建。

Abstract: Accurate 4D reconstruction remains challenging: jointly estimating camera motion, scene geometry, and non-rigid deformation entails highly nonconvex optimization, making global optimality difficult to guarantee. We present XM4D, a framework for accurate and efficient 4D reconstruction built upon certifiably globally optimal geometric optimization. By separating the estimation of camera motion from non-rigid deformation, we formulate both stages as structured quadratically constrained quadratic programs (QCQP) that admit semidefinite programming (SDP) relaxations and verifiable global optimality certificates.
