For camera motion estimation, we lift static point tracks into 3D using foundational depth prediction models and formulate a scaled bundle adjustment problem that jointly estimates camera poses, depth scales, and static landmarks. By analytically eliminating translations and landmarks, we reduce the problem to a QCQP over scaled rotations. For non-rigid reconstruction, we represent dynamic motion using an embedded deformation graph (EDG) and show that its tracking, rigidity, and temporal regularization objectives similarly reduce to a rotation-only QCQP. Both problems admit SDP relaxations that we solve efficiently with a GPU-accelerated Burer-Monteiro solver based on Riemannian optimization. The recovered geometry further initializes dynamic Gaussian splatting for photometric refinement. Extensive experiments across seven benchmarks demonstrate accurate camera motion estimation on both static and dynamic scenes, efficient optimization that scales to thousands of frames, and state-of-the-art novel-view rendering quality.

</details>


### [70] [Compact Motion Embeddings for Feed-forward Unified Human Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07481)
*Seonwook Park,Hyunsoo Cha,Amrita Mazumdar,Shengze Wang,Tianye Li,Hanbyul Joo,Koki Nagano,Shalini De Mello*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FUHA：一个可实时运行的前馈Transformer，学习整个人体的紧凑运动嵌入，用于面部、上半身与全身动画，无需外部姿态估计器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全身动画方法多依赖外部姿态估计器并需多步去噪渲染，而紧凑的学得运动表征通常仅覆盖面部，缺少兼顾全身且与外观解耦的紧凑运动表征。

Method: 用Q-Former将冻结视觉编码器的驱动特征压缩为紧凑运动嵌入；引入新对比损失，使该嵌入靠近驱动者渲染关键点图像的运动嵌入、远离同一人在另一姿态下的嵌入，从而实现运动与外观解耦。推理时无需外部姿态估计器、关键点或参数化人体模型。

Result: 作为检索键，其运动嵌入对身体姿态的匹配优于十个视觉编码器的全局嵌入；同人检索率0.1%（其DINOv3骨干为9.5%）。在上半身基准上，所有自重现指标及跨重现身份相似度均优于基线；面部与全身具竞争力；单张RTX 4090上512×512可达38.9 FPS。

Conclusion: 紧凑、外观解耦的全身运动嵌入可驱动前馈式实时人像动画，并在姿态检索与上半身动画质量上优于现有方案。

Abstract: Animating a reference image with a driver's motion requires a motion representation that captures face, hand, and body articulation but excludes the driver's appearance. Most body animation methods take motion from external pose estimators and render it with many denoising steps; compact learned motion representations mostly cover the face. We present FUHA, a real-time-capable feed-forward transformer that learns a compact motion embedding of the whole human and uses it for face, upper-body, and full-body animation. A Q-Former compresses the driver's features from a frozen visual encoder into this embedding. To disentangle motion from appearance, a novel contrastive loss pulls it toward the motion embedding of the driver's rendered keypoint image and away from that of the same person in another pose. At inference time, FUHA needs no external pose estimators, keypoints, or parametric body models. As a retrieval key, the motion embedding matches body pose better than the learned global embeddings of ten vision encoders. Its same-person retrieval rate is 0.1%, against 9.5% for its own DINOv3 backbone. On our upper-body benchmark, FUHA outperforms all baselines on every self-reenactment metric and on cross-reenactment identity similarity. It is competitive on face and full-body and runs at up to 38.9 FPS at 512x512 on a single RTX 4090.

</details>


### [71] [Multimodal Hierarchical Mixture-of-Experts for 3D Medical Vision-Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08185)
*Gizem Mert,Szymon Płotka,Pedro R. A. S. Bassi,Wenxuan Li,Zongwei Zhou,Jarosław B. Ćwikła,Arkadiusz Sitek,Ewa Szczurek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MedHiVE：以解剖尺度组织的两级Soft MoE实现视觉-文本token的层级路由，并结合解剖级与全局对比学习，统一建模3D CT的细粒度解剖与全局上下文，在6个数据集3项任务上超越11个SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学3D扫描与报告具有层级结构，诊断线索集中于特定解剖区域且跨多个组织层级；但多数医学视觉-语言模型忽略该结构，无法聚焦临床相关解剖。既有工作存在两点局限：静态池化特征做直接的区域-文本对齐，以及仅在视觉端做层级路由。

Method: 提出MedHiVE：1）两级可学习的token路由机制——局部图像与文本特征先经局部SMoE路由，再由全局SMoE融合为统一多模态表示，视觉与文本token同时参与层级；2）按解剖尺度组织两级SMoE层级，实现基于slot的动态跨模态信息流；3）报告派生的解剖级对比对齐，在统一空间同时对齐解剖级与全局视觉-语言表示；4）在25,000+例CT扫描上以解剖监督预训练。

Result: 在6个数据集、分割/分类/检测3项任务上一致超越11个SOTA方法，验证层级建模的重要性。

Conclusion: 按解剖尺度进行跨模态层级路由与多粒度对比对齐，是提升3D医学视觉-语言表征临床相关性的有效途径；代码已开源。

Abstract: Medical 3D scans and reports are inherently hierarchical, with diagnostic cues concentrated in specific anatomical regions across multiple levels of organization. Yet, most medical vision-language models ignore this structure and fail to focus on clinically relevant anatomy. We introduce MedHiVE, which combines hierarchical vision-language Soft Mixture-of-Experts (SMoE) with contrastive learning to jointly model fine-grained anatomy and global scan context. To our knowledge, MedHiVE is the first framework to route both vision and text tokens through a two-level SMoE hierarchy organized by anatomical scale, coupling this with report-derived anatomy-level contrastive alignment for 3D medical imaging. This replaces the direct region-text alignment of statically pooled features and vision-only hierarchical routing of prior work with dynamic, slot-based cross-modal information flow across anatomical scales. MedHiVE operates via a two-level, learnable token-routing mechanism, where local image and text features are first routed through a local SMoE and then fused by a global SMoE into unified multimodal representations. Contrastive learning aligns both anatomy-level and global vision-language representations in a unified space. Pre-trained on 25,000+ CT scans with anatomical supervision, MedHiVE consistently surpasses 11 state-of-the-art methods across 6 datasets on 3 tasks: segmentation, classification, and detection, demonstrating the importance of hierarchical modeling. The code for MedHiVE is at github.com/szczurek-lab/MedHiVE.

</details>


### [72] [ProtoGestalt: Interpretable Prototypes for Facial Phenotyping and Diagnosis of Rare Genetic Disorders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08125)
*Ranga Baminiwatte,Alexander Hustinx,Fabio Hellmann,Aaron J Masino*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出可解释的基于原型的面部表型分析框架 ProtoGestalt，用于罕见遗传病筛查与综合征识别，预测可通过局部化、可加的面部区域证据解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 罕见遗传病种类多、临床特征重叠，诊断困难；面部形态可提供线索，但现有诊断模型可解释性有限，且并非面向未确诊疾病的广泛筛查设计。

Method: 扩展原型学习（prototype learning），约束学习到的视觉特征选择面部区域，使预测由局部化、可加的证据解释；在多个对照数据集与不同类别患病率下评估综合征与未患病面部的区分能力，并评估训练中见过的综合征的诊断性能。

Result: 学习到的原型表征在综合征与病例匹配对照间产生显著分离，随对照比例上升仍保持可用性能；框架亦能区分不同综合征。

Conclusion: 尽管已有较准确的罕见病面部分类专用模型，ProtoGestalt 提供了可解释替代方案，将预测与解剖学意义明确的面部区域关联，有望支持临床决策及表型-基因关联发现。

Abstract: Rare genetic disorders are difficult to diagnose due to their diversity and overlapping clinical features. Facial morphology can provide diagnostic clues, yet most diagnostic models offer limited interpretability and are not designed to support broad screening for undiagnosed disease. We present ProtoGestalt, an interpretable prototype-based framework for facial phenotyping, rare-disease screening, and syndrome identification. The method extends prototype learning by constraining learned visual features to select facial regions, allowing predictions to be explained through localized and additive evidence. We assess ProtoGestalt's ability to distinguish syndromic from unaffected faces on multiple control datasets and class prevalence values. We further evaluate it's diagnostic performance on syndromes observed during training. The learned prototype-based representations provide significant separation between syndromic and case-matched control images and retain useful performance as the proportion of controls increases. The framework also demonstrates the ability to differentiate among syndromes. Although reasonably accurate specialized models for facial classification of rare disorders already exist, ProtoGestalt offers an interpretable alternative that links predictions to anatomically meaningful facial regions, potentially supporting clinical decision-making and the discovery of phenotype-gene associations.

</details>


### [73] [Towards Practical 20 kbps Generative Video Compression via Adaptive Tokenization and Entropy Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08099)
*Jiancheng Zhao,Jixiang Luo,Haibin Huang,Chi Zhang,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出适配预训练视频表征的压缩导向视频编解码框架，将MiniMax-H3因果视频VAE转为离散tokenizer并配轻量自回归熵模型，通过可微码率代理联合优化，在超低码率下显著提升感知率失真性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超低码率视频压缩中感知重要细节易丢失；现有生成式视频压缩多为"先压缩后生成"范式，生成先验仅在重建阶段使用，且生成/重建导向的表征并非为熵编码优化，编码效率受限。

Method: 1) 将预训练MiniMax-H3因果视频VAE适配为离散视频tokenizer，保留其重建能力；2) 设计小型自回归熵模型，建模局部与全局token依赖，采用分组预测实现并行解码；3) 以可微码率代理联合优化tokenizer与熵模型，使码率梯度引导token分配趋向更低编码代价，同时保持重建质量。

Result: 在UVG、MCL-JCV、HEVC Class B上取得更优感知率失真性能；相对GLC-Video，BD-LPIPS节省82.2%–87.5%，BD-DISTS节省78.3%–85.0%。

Conclusion: 将预训练生成视频表征与压缩目标端到端对齐（离散tokenizer+自回归熵模型+可微码率联合优化）可有效提升超低码率视频编码的感知质量与编码效率，突破"先压缩后生成"范式的局限。

Abstract: Video compression at ultra-low bitrates remains challenging due to the loss of perceptually important details under limited bit budgets. Recent generative video compression methods leverage pretrained video priors to improve perceptual quality and temporal coherence, but many follow a compress-then-generate paradigm, where generative priors are primarily exploited during reconstruction. Moreover, representations learned for generation and reconstruction are not inherently optimized for entropy coding.
  In this work, we propose \method, which adapts pretrained video representations into compression-oriented representations while preserving their reconstruction capability. Specifically, we adapt the pretrained causal video VAE of MiniMax-H3 into a discrete video tokenizer and develop a small autoregressive entropy model that captures local and global token dependencies, with grouped prediction for parallel decoding. To further improve coding efficiency, we jointly optimize the tokenizer and entropy model using a differentiable rate surrogate, allowing rate gradients to adapt token assignments toward lower coding costs while preserving reconstruction quality. Experiments on UVG, MCL-JCV, and HEVC Class B demonstrate superior perceptual rate--distortion performance, achieving BD-LPIPS savings of 82.2\%--87.5\% and BD-DISTS savings of 78.3\%--85.0\% relative to GLC-Video.

</details>


### [74] [NutriVisRAG: Multimodal Exemplar Grounding for Nutrient Estimation with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08054)
*Lubnaa Abdur Rahman,Ioannis Papathanail,Maria Panagiotou,Nefeli Dimitropoulou,Margret Harðardóttir,Elisabeth J. den Brok,Bastiaan E. de Galan,Stavroula Georgia Mougiakakou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NutriVisRAG：一种基于视觉相似餐食检索增强的VLM营养估算框架，无需任务微调即可在多配置下降低估算误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 饮食是健康的重要决定因素，但从自由生活场景的餐食图像进行准确的膳食评估仍具挑战。现有基于检索的视觉语言模型（VLM）营养估算方法多依赖文本化食物表征，而据作者所知，尚无研究探索直接以视觉相似且带标注的餐食作为接地依据。

Method: 提出NutriVisRAG检索增强生成框架：用视觉编码器检索具有已知营养标注的相似餐食，将其作为多模态上下文示例（图像+标注）在推理时提供给VLM进行营养估算，且不进行任务特定微调。在4个数据集、4种VLM、3种检索编码器、2种提示策略（整餐级 vs 逐项级）共96种配置上评估，并纳入胰岛素治疗成人糖尿病患者的自由生活餐食，以两名营养师的标注作为碳水估算参照。

Result: 全部96种配置中，NutriVisRAG在56种配置下降低了主要估算误差；整餐级提示（35/48）优于逐项级提示（21/48）。16个VLM–数据集组合中有13个的最低NutriVisRAG误差低于最佳Vanilla误差。对GPT-5.4-mini在整餐级提示下，每个数据集的最佳检索编码器均在全部四个数据集上相对Vanilla降低主要误差，降幅为1.7%–35.7%。

Conclusion: 以图像及对应标注构成的多模态示例作为接地依据，可在推理阶段、无任务特定微调的情况下改善营养估算；但增益依赖具体配置。

Abstract: Diet is a major determinant of health, yet accurate dietary assessment from free-living meal images remains challenging. Existing retrieval-based Vision-Language Model (VLM) approaches to nutrient estimation typically rely on textual food representations, while, to our knowledge, direct grounding on visually similar annotated meals has not been investigated. We present NutriVisRAG, a retrieval-augmented generation framework that uses a vision encoder to retrieve similar meals with known nutritional annotations and provide them as multimodal in-context exemplars for VLM nutrient estimation at inference time, without task-specific fine-tuning. We evaluate NutriVisRAG across four datasets, four VLMs, three retrieval encoders, and two prompting strategies, including free-living meals from adults with insulin-treated diabetes, where carbohydrate estimates are compared against two dietitians' annotations. Across all 96 configurations (4 datasets x 4 VLMs x 3 retrieval encoders x 2 prompting strategies), NutriVisRAG reduced the primary estimation error in 56, with improvements more frequent under whole-meal (35/48) than per-item prompting (21/48). Across the evaluated configurations, the lowest NutriVisRAG error was below the best Vanilla error in 13 of 16 VLM--dataset pairs. For GPT-5.4-mini, under whole-meal prompting, the best-performing retrieval encoder for each dataset reduced the primary error on all four datasets relative to Vanilla, with reductions of 1.7--35.7%. Overall, our results show that grounding in multimodal exemplars comprising images and respective annotations can improve nutrient estimation at inference time without task-specific fine-tuning, although the benefits remain configuration-dependent.

</details>


### [75] [Hierarchical Incremental Beta Splatting for Online Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08288)
*Linus Franke*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HIBS 将 beta 核引入在线增量式 3D Gaussian Splatting：用可控"软硬度"形状参数兼作几何置信度开关，配合置信度感知的生成策略、误差驱动的重定位与闭式形状保持合并构成的 LOD 层次，在 Mip-NeRF 360 在线协议下 PSNR 提升最多 0.8 dB、优化时间减少最多 40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线 3DGS 在采集过程中即重建场景，每个关键帧仅生成一次基元并只做数十次迭代细化，初始化差或细节缺失难以补救；同时高斯基元本质偏"软"，需大量重叠才能表现实心表面，导致效率与几何质量受限。作者希望让基元同时具备"可修正的软性"与"可验证的实心性"，并支持高效渲染。

Method: 1) 用 beta 核替代高斯核，其形状参数在类高斯软轮廓与平顶近不透明 surfel 之间插值，充当几何置信度旋钮；2) 置信度感知生成：依据几何可观测性与多视角深度一致性同时初始化不透明度与形状，几何可验证处生成实心基元，否则生成柔软可纠正基元；3) 在线致密化中以误差驱动重定位，把陈旧基元回收至光度误差热点；4) beta 核有界支撑实现精确 tile 剔除与更快光栅化；5) 提出闭式、保形的合并公式构建 LOD 层次，加速优化并支持实时渲染。

Result: 在 Mip-NeRF 360 数据集的 on-the-fly 协议下，PSNR 最高提升 0.8 dB，同时优化时间最多减少 40%。

Conclusion: 将形状可控的 beta 核与置信度驱动的增量式建元、重定位和 LOD 合并结合，可在在线重建场景中同时改善重建质量与优化/渲染效率，说明基元"软硬度"可作为有效的几何不确定性表达手段。

Abstract: Online 3D Gaussian Splatting reconstructs a scene during capture: each keyframe spawns primitives once and refines them for only a few dozen iterations, leaving little chance to fix poor initialization or missing detail. Gaussians are also inherently soft, needing many overlaps for solid surfaces. We present Hierarchical Incremental Beta Splatting (HIBS), which brings beta kernels to incremental reconstruction. Their shape parameter interpolates between a soft, Gaussian-like profile and a flat-topped, near-opaque surfel, acting as a confidence dial for geometry. Our confidence-aware spawning uses geometric observability and multi-view depth agreement to initialize both opacity and shape, producing solid primitives where geometry is verifiable and soft, correctable primitives where it is not. As online densification, error-driven relocation recycles stale primitives into photometric-error hotspots. Bounded beta kernel support enables exact tile culling and faster rasterization, and a closed-form, shape-preserving merging formulation provides a level-of-detail (LOD) hierarchy for fast optimization and real-time rendering. On Mip-NeRF 360 in the on-the-fly protocol, HIBS improves PSNR by up to 0.8 dB while reducing optimization time by up to 40%. Project page: https://linus-franke.com/hibs/

</details>


### [76] [Selective Event Evidence and Persistent Temporal Priors for Event-Based Point Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07713)
*Xiaokai Song,Zhang Li,Leqi Liu,Yibin Ye,Kun Wang,Xichao Teng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SEPT：仅用事件相机的长序列任意点跟踪框架，通过任务自适应事件证据调制（TEEM）与持久轨迹先验（PTP）两个模块，实现选择性利用有效事件证据并跨时间窗传递轨迹约束，在5个数据集上优于重训练的ETAP基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机可在快速运动和复杂光照下跟踪，但长时点跟踪仍困难：事件活动稀疏且随场景内容与运动变化，导致对应关系难以建立；同时轨迹估计在长序列中易漂移。因此需要一个能选择性利用有效事件证据、并在时间窗间保持轨迹约束的跟踪器。

Method: 提出SEPT，基于ETAP架构的事件单模态TAP框架。模块一TEEM：仅用跟踪监督学习一个有界可靠性场，在特征提取前按空间、时间、通道对事件张量加权。模块二PTP：通过Schur补对固定的局部二次轨迹模型进行边缘化，在重叠窗口间传递几何信息；其可见性感知校正用于精化重叠区坐标，并以截断终端速度初始化新暴露时间戳的坐标。

Result: 仅用合成EventKubric数据训练，在5个数据集的TAP与特征跟踪任务上评测：TAP平均Jaccard优于重训练的ETAP基线；特征跟踪的特征年龄与期望特征年龄指标更优；在EC与EDS上超过若干融合事件与帧输入的方法。

Conclusion: 选择性事件证据调制结合持久时序先验，可有效缓解事件稀疏性与长序列漂移问题，验证了纯事件模态长时点跟踪的可行性；代码已开源。

Abstract: Event cameras enable tracking under fast motion and challenging illumination. However, stable long-term point tracking remains challenging: event activity is sparse and varies with scene content and motion, complicating correspondence, while trajectory estimates can drift over long sequences. Together, these challenges motivate a tracker that selectively uses informative event evidence and retains trajectory constraints across temporal windows. We propose Selective Event Evidence and Persistent Temporal Priors (SEPT), an event-only framework for tracking any point (TAP) over long sequences. SEPT introduces two complementary modules: Task-Adaptive Event Evidence Modulation (TEEM) and Persistent Trajectory Prior (PTP). Using only tracking supervision, TEEM learns a bounded reliability field that weights the event tensor across space, time, and channels before feature extraction. PTP carries geometric information across overlapping windows by marginalizing a fixed local quadratic trajectory model with a Schur complement. Its visibility-aware correction refines overlap coordinates, while a clipped terminal velocity initializes coordinates at newly exposed timestamps. SEPT builds on the ETAP architecture. SEPT is trained exclusively on synthetic EventKubric data and evaluated on five datasets across TAP and feature tracking. Results show that SEPT outperforms the retrained ETAP baseline in average Jaccard for TAP and in feature age and expected feature age for feature tracking; it also surpasses several methods combining event and frame inputs on EC and EDS. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/sssxxxkkkk/SEPT.

</details>


### [77] [AffineGS: Unlocking Accurate Geometry in 3DGS via Spatially Varying Affine Rectification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07709)
*Tze Ho Elden Tse,Jizong Peng,Richard Chen,Angela Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 现有深度先验引导的3DGS方法依赖全局scale-and-shift对齐，而单目深度网络实际存在区域相关的分段线性畸变；本文提出空间变化的仿射校正模型，将单目视差校正为一致的度量深度，并推导法向感知正则项，在无需传感器的情况下同时保持光度质量并显著提升几何精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS视觉质量优异但几何精度不足。已有工作用单目深度先验正则化优化，但通常假设全局单一的scale-and-shift对齐关系。作者发现该假设存在根本缺陷：现代单目深度网络系统性地表现出区域相关、分段线性的畸变，全局对齐会扭曲场景几何。

Method: 提出空间变化的仿射校正（spatially varying affine rectification）模型，利用区域平滑且边缘感知的场，将单目视差校正为一致的度量深度；并推导法向感知正则项（normal-aware regularizer），利用空间梯度将校正后的视差与3DGS深度及法向耦合。

Result: 在多种实验设置下，该无需传感器的方法在保持3DGS强光度质量的同时显著提升几何精度，并优于当前面向几何的最新方法。

Conclusion: 全局统一对齐的深度先验正则是错误建模；采用空间变化仿射校正加法向感知耦合正则，可在纯图像监督下有效改善3DGS几何精度，且不牺牲渲染质量。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has become the leading approach for photorealistic novel view synthesis, yet its geometric accuracy lags behind its visual quality. Existing depth-guided methods attempt to resolve this by regularizing the optimization with monocular depth priors, typically using a single global scale-and-shift alignment. Crucially, we discover a fundamental flaw in this assumption: modern monocular depth networks systematically exhibit region-dependent, piecewise-linear distortions. This discovery invalidates standard global alignment strategies, which warp scene geometry by enforcing a single transform. To address this, we introduce a spatially varying affine rectification model that corrects monocular disparity into consistent metric depth using regionally smooth, edge-aware fields. We further derive a normal-aware regularizer that uses spatial gradients to couple the rectified disparity with 3DGS depth and normals. Across diverse experimental settings, our sensor-free method shows strong photometric quality of Gaussian Splatting while substantially improving geometric accuracy and outperforming state-of-the art geometry-focused methods.

</details>


### [78] [DEQ-Det: Dual Evidence Queries for Few-Shot Analog IC Device Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07682)
*Zidong Zhao,Rong Huang,Chengxing Fan,Jie Hu,Malik Haris,Ghazal A. Fahmy,Morteza Mousazadeh,Guoqiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DEQ-Det，用同一批支持图像构造互补的前景与背景参考，联合引导查询选择与区域验证，在小样本模拟IC器件版图检测中取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 版图图像中的模拟IC器件检测可用于电路分析与检查，但仅有少量标注样本；器件几何形状变化大，且易与dummy结构、布线结构在视觉上相似，仅依赖前景相似度会突出似是而非的目标，难以与竞争性版图图案区分。

Method: 构建双证据查询：从同一支持图像（无需额外标注）同时导出前景参考（刻画目标形态）与精选背景参考（刻画周边版图图案）；两类参考共同参与查询选择与区域验证，将目标外观与版图上下文结合；并通过RoI背景证据进行消融验证。

Result: 在五个留出器件类别、三个随机种子上，10-shot与30-shot的macro AP50分别为39.41与53.03，较最强基线分别提升3.97与9.71个点；匹配的30-shot消融显示，在召回率相当的情况下，RoI背景证据降低了汇总误检数。

Conclusion: 背景证据是抑制干扰的关键补充信号，前景+背景双证据框架能有效提升少样本模拟IC器件版图检测的可靠性。

Abstract: Detecting analog integrated circuit (IC) devices in layout images supports circuit analysis and inspection. With only a few annotated examples, variation in device geometry and visual similarity to dummy or routing structures make reliable detection difficult. Foreground similarity alone can highlight plausible targets without distinguishing them from competing layout patterns. We propose Dual Evidence Queries for Detection (DEQ-Det), which constructs complementary foreground and background references from the same support images without additional annotations. Foreground references capture target morphology, while selected background references characterize surrounding layout patterns. Both sources guide query selection and region verification, combining target appearance with layout context. Across five held-out device categories and three random seeds, DEQ-Det achieves macro AP50 of 39.41 and 53.03 at 10 and 30 shots, exceeding the strongest listed baselines by 3.97 and 9.71 points, respectively. A matched 30-shot ablation further shows that RoI background evidence reduces pooled false positives at comparable recall, supporting its role in interference suppression.

</details>


### [79] [Egocentric Action Recognition with Objects and Vision-Language Affordances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07673)
*Raúl Boix-Aldeguer,Manuel Benavent-Lledo,David Ortiz-Perez,Jose Garcia-Rodriguez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出将冻结的SlowFast视频专家与基于注意力的物体专家晚期融合，用视觉语言模型零样本生成可供性表示物体，并生成交互可供性过滤非活动检测，在MECCANO上提升自我中心动作识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我中心视频中动作很大程度上由所涉物体定义，但现有基于RGB视频或深度等视觉模态的方法很少对物体建模；同时数据集中仅标注参与动作的物体，而实际检测会引入大量非活动物体检测。

Method: 冻结的SlowFast视频专家与注意力机制物体专家晚期融合；用视觉语言模型零样本为每个物体生成可供性（affordance）表示；再用同一模型生成交互可供性，判断手是否正在操作某检测物体以剔除干扰检测；训练用标注框，评估用官方活动物体检测器，并以标注框作为上界。

Result: 无供能时融合在标注框下Top-1为68.4%，检测框下为49.0%，下降约一半来自非活动检测；加入交互可供性过滤后，MECCANO上物体使视频专家Top-1从44.9%升至49.0%，两种可供性共同提至49.9%，超过SlowFast与物体感知的RGB基线。

Conclusion: 显式建模物体及可供性、并剔除非活动检测，可有效提升自我中心动作识别，结果优于视频与物体感知RGB基线。

Abstract: Action recognition, and more specifically egocentric action recognition, is a core task in computer vision, with applications such as monitoring the daily life activities of elderly people and task assistance. Recent methods rely on visual modalities such as RGB video or depth. However, in egocentric video, an action is largely defined by its objects, which these methods rarely model. We propose a method that combines a frozen SlowFast video expert with an attention-based object expert by late fusion. Each object is represented with its affordances, that is, what can be done with it, generated zero-shot by a vision-language model. Since datasets such as MECCANO annotate only the objects involved in each action, evaluating with these boxes tells the model which objects are active. We therefore train on annotated boxes and evaluate the method with detections from the official active-object detector, and with annotated boxes as an upper bound. Without affordances, our fusion reaches 68.4% Top-1 with annotated boxes but 49.0% with detections. About half of this drop comes from non-active detections. To remove them, the same vision-language model generates an interaction affordance that decides whether a hand is manipulating each detected object. On MECCANO, objects raise the Top-1 of the video expert from 44.9% to 49.0%, and the two affordances raise it to 49.9%, above SlowFast and object-aware RGB baselines. Code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [80] [ArtCrafter: Feed-Forward Generation of Articulated 3D Object with Analytic Joint Derivation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07658)
*Minh Canh Tu,Quang-Binh Nguyen,Khoi Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ArtCrafter 是一个前馈生成模型，从“静止态+打开态”一对图像联合生成部件级网格，并以 Triplet Attention 与排斥损失保证跨状态几何一致与部件不穿插，再由刚性变换解析导出 URDF 关节参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铰接 3D 资产对机器人仿真与训练至关重要，但从日常图像生成时，部件几何与运动学参数需相互一致，这一双约束是核心难点；现有方法在几何质量与关节精度上难以兼顾。

Method: 基于 DiT 的前馈生成模型，单次前向同时输出两个关节状态下的部件级网格；借鉴 Slot Attention 采用 1+2N 槽位布局（1 个共享 base 槽 + N 组 rest/open 配对槽），使配对槽绑定同一运动部件。引入 Triplet Attention 经共享 base 槽锚定，强制配对 rest/open 槽间的跨状态几何一致性；并在解码点云上施加排斥损失 L_rep 防止部件互相穿透。关节参数（类型、轴、原点、范围）由每对 rest/open 网格的刚性变换解析求得，替代可学习的关节参数回归。

Result: 在分布内与分布外数据集上，相同双状态输入设定下，ArtCrafter 在几何质量与关节精度上均优于现有方法，并与采用不同输入设定或范式的方法保持可比竞争力。

Conclusion: 联合生成双状态部件网格并解析求解关节参数，是一种可解释且有效的铰接资产生成路径；槽位配对 + 跨状态一致性约束 + 排斥正则可同时兼顾几何一致性与运动学准确性。

Abstract: Articulated 3D assets are essential for robotic simulation and training, yet their generation from casual images poses a key challenge: recovering part geometry and kinematics that remain mutually consistent. We present ArtCrafter, a novel feed-forward generative model for controllable generation of articulated 3D objects from a pair of images, one depicting the object in the rest state and the other in the open state. At the core of ArtCrafter is a DiT-based generative model that jointly produces part-level meshes for both articulation states in a single forward pass. Inspired by Slot Attention, its denoising process operates on a $1+2N$ slot layout comprising one shared base slot and $N$ paired rest/open slots, encouraging each pair to bind to the same kinematic part. To ensure geometrically consistent and disjoint part generation, we introduce Triplet Attention to enforce cross-state geometric consistency between paired rest/open slots anchored through the shared base slot, and a repulsion loss $\mathcal{L}_{\mathrm{rep}}$ on decoded part point clouds to prevent inter-part penetration throughout denoising. Given the resulting paired meshes, URDF joint parameters---type, axis, origin, and range---are derived analytically from the rigid transformation between each rest/open pair, providing an interpretable alternative to learned joint-parameter regression. Extensive experiments on both in-distribution and out-of-distribution datasets demonstrate that ArtCrafter outperforms existing method under the same dual-state setting in geometry quality and articulation precision, while remaining competitive with methods operating under different input settings and paradigms.

</details>


### [81] [Generator-Aligned Reward Modeling with Target-Aware Bounded Optimization for Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07648)
*Ziqiao Weng,Yuanzhi Liang,Haibing Huang,Weidong Cai,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出结合GARM（生成器对齐奖励建模）与TABO（目标感知有界优化）的奖励引导后训练框架，并引入TA-GRPO，用于视觉生成模型的目标属性增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉生成模型后训练需增强目标属性同时保持通用生成能力，但奖励引导后训练存在三方面问题：真实训练数据与生成器输出分布不匹配；属性监督被无关内容混淆；无界奖励最大化导致过度优化并损害生成质量。

Method: 1) GARM：通过生成器支撑集匹配缓解分布不匹配，并利用内容匹配的真实对比减少内容混淆，得到与生成器相关、聚焦属性的奖励监督；2) TABO：将由真实目标正例校准的奖励映射为平滑且上有界函数，与优化算法无关，兼容直接奖励梯度与策略优化；3) TA-GRPO：基于Flow-GRPO改造，采用固定分阶段优势缩放、生成器到目标的课程学习以及时间步均衡的信用分配。

Result: GARM提升目标属性表达并保持有竞争力的生成质量；TABO相较无约束奖励最大化更好地平衡属性增强与视觉质量；TA-GRPO相较原始Flow-GRPO提升目标增强效果，并减少中性提示下的非预期属性泄漏。

Conclusion: 结果凸显了生成器对齐的奖励监督与受控、数据校准的优化在定向视觉生成中的重要性。

Abstract: Post-training aims to enhance desired attributes in visual generative models while preserving their general generation capabilities. Reward-guided post-training faces two challenges: real training data may mismatch the generator's output distribution, and attribute supervision may be confounded by unrelated content. Unbounded reward maximization can also cause overoptimization and degrade generation quality. We propose a framework combining Generator-Aligned Reward Modeling (GARM) and Target-Aware Bounded Optimization (TABO). GARM mitigates distribution mismatch through generator-support matching and reduces content confounding using content-matched real contrasts, yielding generator-relevant, attribute-focused reward supervision. TABO maps the learned reward to a smooth, upper-bounded function calibrated from real target-positive examples. This transformation is independent of the optimization algorithm and supports both direct reward-gradient and policy-based optimization. We further introduce Target-Aware GRPO (TA-GRPO), an adaptation of Flow-GRPO that combines fixed stage-wise advantage scaling, a generator-to-target curriculum, and timestep-balanced credit assignment for flow-based video optimization. Experiments show that GARM improves target-attribute expression while maintaining competitive generation quality. TABO better balances attribute enhancement and visual quality than unconstrained reward maximization, while TA-GRPO improves target enhancement over vanilla Flow-GRPO and reduces unintended attribute leakage on neutral prompts. These results highlight the importance of generator-aligned reward supervision and controlled, data-calibrated optimization for targeted visual generation.

</details>


### [82] [Georeferenced Cross-View 3D Reconstruction from Ground and Satellite Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07575)
*David G. Shatwell,Mubarak Shah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: X-VGGT 是一个前馈式跨视角几何 Transformer，联合处理地面图像与带地理坐标的卫星图像，统一输出地面相机位姿、稠密 3D 点图以及场景级对齐变换，实现含地理配准的 3D 重建，并提出了相应评测协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨视角地理定位方法只能估计单台地面相机的 GPS 坐标，无法重建稠密 3D 几何；而近期前馈式多视角 3D 重建模型仅在局部坐标系中预测点图与相机位姿。两类任务各自为政，缺乏将局部重建整体对齐到地理坐标系（卫星平面）的统一方案。

Method: 提出前馈式跨视角几何 Transformer：同时输入一组地面图像与一张带地理参考的卫星图像，联合预测（1）地面视角相机位姿、（2）稠密 3D 点图、（3）场景级变换参数（重力、尺度、偏航角、平移）。该场景变换将局部重建投影至卫星平面，从而把全部地面图像与卫星图像完成地理配准。此外设计了针对该联合任务的评测协议。

Result: 在多个跨视角数据集上，X-VGGT 能生成准确的地理配准重建结果，并在性能上优于先前的跨视角地理定位方法与多视角 3D 重建模型。

Conclusion: 将跨视角地理定位与前馈多视角稠密重建统一到单一 Transformer 框架中，通过可解释的场景级变换参数（重力/尺度/偏航/平移）实现局部重建的地理配准，为带地理坐标的稠密 3D 重建提供可行范式与配套评测基准。

Abstract: Georeferenced 3D reconstruction from ground images requires recovering not only the local scene geometry, but also localizing every camera center and 3D point in a geographic coordinate frame. Existing cross-view geo-localization methods are able to estimate the GPS coordinate of a single ground camera, but do not reconstruct dense 3D geometry, while recent feed-forward multi-view 3D reconstruction models predict point maps and camera poses only in a local coordinate frame. We introduce X-VGGT, a feed-forward cross-view geometry transformer that unifies these tasks by jointly processing a set of ground-level images and a georeferenced satellite image to predict ground-view camera poses, dense 3D point maps, and a scene-level transform parameterized by gravity, scale, yaw and translation. This transform projects the local reconstruction into the satellite plane, resulting in the geo-registration of all ground images with the georeferenced satellite image. We further propose an evaluation protocol for this joint task and show that, across multiple cross-view datasets, X-VGGT produces accurate georeferenced reconstructions and outperforms prior cross-view geo-localization and multi-view 3D reconstruction models.

</details>


### [83] [Integrating Learned Priors through Prior-Space Reparameterization for Physically Based Inverse Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07573)
*Hun Hwangbo,Minjun Park,Yongsung Kim,Wooseok Song,Jungmin Ko,Junghyun Hwang,Sungroh Yoon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出先验引导的材质重参数化：在预测特征空间中用permutohedral lattice聚类相似表面点共享材质变量，保持纯光度目标不变，改善逆渲染材质分解与重光照。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理逆渲染中材质与光照分解本质欠定，学习型材质预测可提供先验；但现有方法多以辅助正则损失引入先验，会改变优化目标并需与光度拟合项权衡权重。

Method: 将多视角材质预测融合到网格上，在所得先验特征空间构建permutohedral lattice；预测相似的表面点共享材质变量，与几何、光照在纯光度目标下联合优化，从而实现限制优化空间而不改变目标函数。

Result: 在两个学习预测器、三个合成基准及Stanford-ORB上实验：Synthetic4Relight与TensoIR-Synthetic上重光照PSNR较最强网格基线提升1.5–2.5 dB；对照实验优于使用相同先验的正则损失；重参数化汇聚噪声光度梯度，使跨视角更新更一致，联合优化调度缩短至一分钟内且重光照质量仍优于所有基线。

Conclusion: 以重参数化而非正则损失引入材质先验，可在不改变目标函数的前提下提升材质分解与重光照质量，并显著加速优化，为逆渲染中先验利用提供新范式。

Abstract: Physically based inverse rendering decomposes appearance into materials and illumination, an ambiguous problem for which learned material predictions provide useful prior information. Most existing methods incorporate these cues through auxiliary regularization losses, which alter the optimization objective and require balancing their influence against photometric fitting. We propose a prior-guided material reparameterization that uses the predicted feature relationships to restrict the optimization space while leaving the objective unchanged. We fuse multi-view material predictions onto a mesh and construct a permutohedral lattice in the resulting prior feature space. Surface points with similar predictions share material variables, which are optimized jointly with geometry and illumination using a purely photometric objective. Experiments with two learned predictors on three synthetic benchmarks and Stanford-ORB demonstrate improved material decomposition and relighting. Our method improves relighting PSNR by 1.5-2.5 dB over the strongest evaluated mesh-based baseline on Synthetic4Relight and TensoIR-Synthetic, and outperforms a regularization loss using the same prior in controlled comparisons. We further show that our reparameterization pools noisy photometric gradients, producing updates that are more consistent across sampled views. This consistency enables a shortened joint optimization schedule that takes less than a minute while retaining relighting quality above all evaluated baselines on TensoIR-Synthetic.

</details>


### [84] [VIPA: View-Invariant Projector-Based Adversarial Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07443)
*Jiyu Han,Qingyue Deng,Bingyao Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VIPA，通过视图不变投影-相机仿真与多视图联合优化实现物理世界中视图不变、分类器无关的投影对抗攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐蔽式投影对抗攻击在单一静态视角下建模"投影-拍摄"过程，缺乏视角不变性，难以适用于动态环境与多视角场景，实用性受限。

Method: 1) 构建视图不变的投影-相机系统仿真方法，建模投影图案与真实表面间的物理交互；2) 聚合多个视角下的模拟攻击损失，对对抗投影进行联合优化，以兼顾多视角鲁棒性与隐蔽性；3) 将优化图案物理投影到真实场景，实现对不同分类器的攻击。

Result: 在有目标与无目标攻击实验中，VIPA在各测试视角下的攻击成功率均持续高于现有方法，同时提升隐蔽性，并保持最小的感知退化。

Conclusion: 多视角联合优化可有效赋予投影对抗攻击视图不变性与分类器无关性，使物理攻击在动态、多视角场景下更鲁棒、更实用。

Abstract: Projector-based adversarial attack aims to physically manipulate real-world scenes by projecting adversarial patterns, thereby causing deep image classifiers to produce incorrect predictions. However, existing stealthy projector-based adversarial attack methods model the project-and-capture process in a single and static view, limiting their view-invariant capability and practical applicability in dynamic environments. In this paper, we introduce View-Invariant Projector-Based Adversarial Attack (VIPA), a novel method designed to overcome these limitations by achieving both view-invariant and classifier-agnostic adversarial attacks in the physical world. We first leverage a view-invariant projector-camera system simulation method to model the physical interactions between projected patterns and real-world surfaces. To ensure robustness and stealthiness of the attack across different views, VIPA optimizes adversarial projections by aggregating simulated attack losses from multiple views. This joint optimization across diverse views helps maintain robustness regardless of the viewpoints. Finally, the optimized patterns are physically projected into real-world scenes, where they successfully fool various classifiers from different viewpoints, thereby enabling robust and practical view-invariant adversarial attacks. Our experiments in both targeted and untargeted attacks demonstrate that VIPA consistently achieves higher attack success rates than existing methods, while also enhancing stealthiness and ensuring minimal perceptual degradation across all tested views.

</details>


### [85] [OcTreeMamba: Topology-Aware Mamba for 3D Medical Segmentation via Octree Sparsification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07865)
*Kaixiang Mai,Jiaqi Yu,Yuzhu Ji,Yiqun Zhang,Zhijie Wang,Haijun Zhang,Yiu-ming Cheung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OcTreeMamba，将八叉树结构引入状态空间模型（SSM），实现三维拓扑感知的长程上下文特征传播与融合，用于3D医学解剖结构分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Mamba的分割方法采用启发式选择性扫描策略，破坏内在空间层次结构、增加计算冗余，且忽视3D解剖结构固有的稀疏性。

Method: 包含三个核心模块：(1) Structure Learner通过层次化门控与Gumbel-Softmax采样进行解剖自适应结构化稀疏化，实现可微的解剖区域选择；(2) Dynamic Octree Builder将选中区域组织为保留层次连通性的多层拓扑结构；(3) 双向Tree-SSM沿"叶-父-根"与"根-父-叶"的连通性感知轨迹传播并融合上下文特征。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在拓扑连续性与计算效率方面具有竞争力，尤其在血管数据集的复杂结构恢复上表现突出。

Conclusion: 通过将八叉树层次拓扑与SSM结合，OcTreeMamba以线性复杂度实现三维拓扑感知的长程建模，有效利用解剖稀疏性并提升复杂结构分割的连续性与效率。

Abstract: Accurate segmentation of 3D anatomical structures is crucial to medical image analysis. Recently, Mamba-based approaches have achieved promising segmentation performance in capturing long-range dependencies with linear-time computational complexity. However, heuristic selective scanning strategies inherently disrupt the intrinsic spatial hierarchy and increase computational redundancy, ignoring the inherent sparsity of 3D anatomical structures. To this end, we propose OcTreeMamba, a hierarchical framework that incorporates Octree structures into SSM, achieving 3D topology-aware long-range context feature propagation and integration. Specifically, a Structure Learner performs anatomy-adaptive structured sparsification through hierarchical gating and Gumbel-Softmax sampling, enabling differentiable selection of anatomical regions. The selected regions are subsequently organized by a Dynamic Octree Builder into a multi-level topology that preserves their hierarchical connectivity. Based on the constructed Octree, a bidirectional Tree-SSM propagates and integrates contextual features along connectivity-aware leaf--parent--root and root--parent--leaf trajectories. Extensive experiments on four benchmark datasets show that our proposed OcTreeMamba achieves competitive performance \wrt topological continuity and computational efficiency, particularly for recovering the complex structure on vascular datasets. The code is available at https://github.com/MikeMak123/OcTreeMamba.

</details>


### [86] [4D Point Splatting for mmWave Radar Micro-Doppler Differentiable Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08342)
*Adnan Armouti,Guanyue Qian,Rajalakshmi Nandakumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 4DPS：基于物理的可微渲染器，将显式运动场景拟合到真实毫米波雷达采集，生成原始复数回波，实现数据集规模的逆渲染并显著缩小sim2real差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫米波雷达的微多普勒可用于步态、手势、姿态、生命体征等感知，但雷达数据集规模远落后于视觉与语言领域。现有合成方法（正向仿真器、学习式生成器、静态场景逆渲染器）无法拟合含运动人体的真实采集，存在sim2real差距。

Method: 提出4D Point Splatting（4DPS）：用同步相机初始化房间与人体，将场景表示为携带材质、法向与速度的有向点，并把每个点投影（splat）到距离与多普勒bin中，输出与真实雷达一致的原始复数回波；在渲染数据上施加相同信号处理，得到任意表示以训练下游SOTA模型。

Result: 在HuPR拟合235条序列、RT-Pose拟合100条序列，重建达SOTA：距离-方位中位相关0.940/0.830，距离-多普勒0.829/0.695，微多普勒0.988/0.966。RT-Pose上仅用渲染数据训练的位姿模型在真实数据达21.9 cm MPJPE（真实训练16.7 cm，RF-Genesis基线127.6 cm），且渲染训练与真实训练学到近乎相同的特征。单张H100上拟合一条序列耗时5.7分钟（HuPR）与12.9分钟（RT-Pose）。

Conclusion: 首次实现毫米波雷达数据集规模的可微逆渲染，可支撑任意以人为中心的下游任务的合成数据生成及场景编辑数据增强。

Abstract: Millimeter-wave radar resolves the micrometer displacements of a moving body as micro-Doppler, enabling gait, gesture, activity, pose and vital-sign sensing. Each task relies on a downstream model trained on real data, yet radar datasets lag orders of magnitude behind vision and language. Brute-force collection will not close that gap, and existing synthesis methods (forward-only simulators, learned generators and static-scene inverse renderers) cannot fit a real capture of a moving body, leaving a sim2real gap. We present 4D Point Splatting (4DPS), a physics-based differentiable renderer that fits an explicit moving scene to a real capture. Using the synchronized camera to initialize the room and body, we represent the scene as oriented points carrying material, normal and velocity, and splat each point into range and Doppler bins. Our output is the raw complex return a real radar records, so we apply the same signal processing on the rendered data to obtain any representation for training any SOTA downstream model. We fit 235 sequences on HuPR and 100 on RT-Pose, two datasets with different radars. Our reconstructions set the state of the art, with median correlations of 0.940 and 0.830 on range-azimuth, 0.829 and 0.695 on range-Doppler, and 0.988 and 0.966 on micro-Doppler. Our renders also set the state of the art in downstream sim2real gap. On RT-Pose, a pose model trained on them reaches 21.9 cm MPJPE on real data, against 16.7 cm for real training and 127.6 cm for the RF-Genesis baseline, where both the rendered- and real-trained models learn nearly the same features. Our custom CUDA kernels fit a sequence in 5.7 minutes on HuPR and 12.9 on RT-Pose on one H100, making dataset-scale inverse rendering of mmWave radar possible for the first time. This work can enable synthetic data generation for any human-centered downstream task and model, and scene editing for data augmentation.

</details>


### [87] [InstantHand: Feed-Forward 3D Gaussian Splatting for Animatable Hand Avatar Reconstruction from a Single Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08330)
*Chanho Kim,Muhammad Salman Ali,Kyeonghwan Gwak,Muhammad Shaheryar,Incheol Park,Jeongwan On,Seungryul Baek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InstantHand 提出一种前馈式框架，仅凭单张图像与现成的 MANO、相机、掩码估计，无需测试时优化即可在 2.84 秒内构建可动画的 3D 高斯手部化身，并以 161 FPS 渲染；通过 Soft Alpha Band 正则与 Reliability-gated Appearance Transfer 解决轮廓alpha泄漏与颜色漂移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手部化身重建方法依赖多视角采集或逐受试者优化，可扩展性差；单图前馈重建又面临两个问题：图像平均损失对alpha泄漏监督不足，导致细手指轮廓变粗；由图像特征回归的颜色即使在观测区域也易漂移向学习到的先验。

Method: 基于现成 MANO、相机与掩码估计，用前馈网络从单张图像构建可动画 3D 高斯手部化身，无测试时优化。提出 Soft Alpha Band 正则器，将监督集中于轮廓边界以缓解alpha泄漏；提出 Reliability-gated Appearance Transfer，依据每个高斯在渲染中的贡献转移源图像颜色以保留观测外观，并以全局源身份为条件、用学习先验补全未观测区域。

Result: 构建化身仅需 2.84 秒，渲染速度 161 FPS；在 InterHand2.6M 上匹配逐受试者优化基线，在 HanCo 的零样本评测中保持竞争力。

Conclusion: InstantHand 以单图前馈方式实现了高效、可动画的3D高斯手部化身重建，兼顾速度与质量，验证了边界聚焦监督与可靠性门控外观迁移在细粒度轮廓保持与外观保真上的有效性，具备良好可扩展性。

Abstract: Hand avatar reconstruction typically relies on multi-view capture or per-subject optimization, limiting its scalability. We introduce InstantHand, a feed-forward framework that constructs an animatable 3D Gaussian hand avatar from a single image using off-the-shelf MANO, camera, and mask estimates, without test-time optimization. Image-averaged losses provide insufficient supervision for alpha leakage, which thickens thin finger contours. To address this issue, we propose a Soft Alpha Band regularizer that concentrates supervision on silhouette boundaries. Furthermore, colors regressed from image features can drift toward a learned prior even in observed regions. Our Reliability-gated Appearance Transfer preserves observed appearance by transferring source colors according to each Gaussian's rendering contribution, while completing unobserved regions using the learned prior conditioned on global source identity. InstantHand constructs an avatar in 2.84 seconds and renders at 161 FPS, matching per-subject-optimized baselines on InterHand2.6M and remaining competitive in zero-shot evaluation on HanCo.

</details>


### [88] [ManimCode: Learning Visual Self-Correction for Educational Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08294)
*Zhengyang Liang,Lizi Liao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ManimData数据构建流程与ManimCode模型（4B），通过渲染引导的多轮生成-执行-评审-修订轨迹，结合角色条件SFT与强化学习训练修订器，显著提升Manim教育动画的渲染成功率与视频质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Manim可编程生成教育动画，但"渲染成功"不等于视觉正确、讲解完整或内容无误；渲染结果无法直接指示所需代码修改，需反馈驱动的迭代修订。现有agent框架依赖大型（常为闭源）模型，且现有Manim数据集仅含最终程序，缺乏质量校验与"反馈—修订"轨迹，无法学习该精修过程。

Method: 构建ManimData：采用渲染引导流水线，结合检索到的API文档，迭代执行生成、运行、评审、修订，保留中间程序、执行错误、视觉批评与修正版本，得到24,034条经渲染验证与质量过滤的记录。基于这些轨迹对4B模型做角色条件监督微调，训练其编写、评审、修订动画程序；随后在自生成状态上用强化学习训练Reviser，奖励来自渲染结果与无参考质量反馈，以增强对分布外错误的纠正能力。

Result: ManimCode在ManimData-test与ManimBench上渲染成功率达96.6%与97.0%，同推理设置下未训练4B模型仅为52.6%与82.0%；视频质量分由2.48升至2.94、由3.00升至3.44。接入两个长视频教育生成系统后，完整生成视频数分别由19/117升至107/117、由53/239升至239/239，教育有效性与视觉质量同步提升。

Conclusion: 以反馈-修订轨迹为核心的数据构建与角色条件训练，可使小规模（4B）开源模型胜任Manim教育动画的编写、评审与修订，在渲染成功率、视频质量和长视频系统端到端完成率上取得显著提升，验证了轨迹级数据与强化学习修订策略的有效性。

Abstract: Manim supports precise, programmatic video generation, but a successfully rendered educational animation can still have visual defects, missing explanations, or incorrect content. Rendering does not directly identify the code changes needed to correct these visual failures, so feedback-driven revision is needed. Existing agentic frameworks use iterative rendering and revision but typically rely on large models, often proprietary. Existing Manim datasets primarily contain final programs and often lack quality verification and the trajectories of feedback and revisions needed to learn this refinement process. We introduce ManimData and ManimCode, a data and training framework for feedback-driven educational animation generation. We construct ManimData through a render-guided pipeline that iteratively generates, executes, reviews, and revises Manim programs with retrieved API documentation. Its 24,034 render-verified, quality-filtered records preserve intermediate programs, execution errors, visual critiques, and corrective revisions. We use role-conditioned supervised fine-tuning on these trajectories to train ManimCode, a single 4B model, to write, review, and revise animation programs. To improve correction of errors that differ from those in the collected trajectories, we then train the Reviser with reinforcement learning on self-generated states, using rendering outcomes and reference-free quality feedback. ManimCode achieves 96.6% and 97.0% render success on ManimData-test and ManimBench, compared with 52.6% and 82.0% for the untrained 4B model under the same inference setup, while also improving video quality scores from 2.48 to 2.94 and from 3.00 to 3.44. Integrating ManimCode into two long-form educational video generation systems increases completed videos from 19/117 to 107/117 and from 53/239 to 239/239, alongside improvements in educational effectiveness and visual quality.

</details>


### [89] [MammoSeek: A Mammography Agent with selective zooming for grounded reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07188)
*Haoyan Ding,Qian Wang,Lixia Wang,Debiao Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出乳腺摄影读片智能体MammoSeek，模仿放射科医生的"整体印象+选择性放大"流程，通过让多模态大模型自主决定对哪些候选病灶区域进行全分辨率查看，并结合BI-RADS评估技能与决策层实现检查级召回判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型（MLLM）以固定、降低的分辨率查看整幅图像，导致亚毫米级钙化不可见，在乳腺摄影上表现接近随机水平；而放射科医生的真实读片流程是先形成四视图的整体印象，再对可疑区域选择性放大并给出BI-RADS评估。

Method: 构建乳腺摄影智能体MammoSeek：先对每侧乳腺形成整体印象，随后执行选择性放大——由智能体自主判断哪些候选病灶值得细看，仅对入选候选在全分辨率视图上检查；在BI-RADS评估技能引导下给出类别并生成定位化、词典结构化的报告；其评估结果与图像模型分数在决策层融合以决定召回。在内部EMBED与外部VinDr-Mammo数据集上验证。

Result: 检查级AUC分别达0.824（EMBED）与0.818（VinDr-Mammo），显著优于其他MLLM、乳腺摄影智能体与CNN分类器；选择性放大在定位、结构化报告F1以及低成本的召回决策上均优于不放大与强制放大两种策略。

Conclusion: 将高分辨率信息获取交由智能体按需、有选择地完成，可在可控成本下显著提升MLLM在乳腺摄影筛查中的诊断与报告能力，并验证了模仿人类读片流程的有效性。

Abstract: Radiologists read a screening mammogram by first forming a global impression of the four views and then selectively magnifying suspicious regions before assigning a BI-RADS assessment. MLLMs, in contrast, see the whole image at a fixed, reduced resolution, which makes sub-millimeter calcifications invisible, and they perform close to chance on mammography. Therefore, we present MammoSeek, a mammography agent that reproduces this reading process. After forming an impression of each breast, the agent performs selective zooming: it decides by itself which detector candidates deserve a closer look and inspects only those on full-resolution views. Guided by a BI-RADS assessment skill, it then assigns a category and generates a localized, lexicon-structured report for each breast; its assessment is combined with image-model scores in a decision layer to decide recall. On an internal (EMBED) and an external (VinDr-Mammo) dataset, \ours reaches exam-level AUCs of 0.824 and 0.818, far above other MLLMs, mammography agent and CNN classifiers. Selective zooming also outperforms both no zooming and forced zooming in localization, structured-report F1 and recall decisions at low cost.

</details>


### [90] [IDPrior: Decoupling Identity Generation from Facial Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07941)
*Fadi Boutros,Meriç Demirörs,Darian Tomašević,Vitomir Štruc,Naser Damer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出IDPrior两阶段框架，将身份生成与图像合成解耦：先用扩散先验在预训练人脸识别模型嵌入空间中建模身份分布并生成新身份嵌入，再用其条件化预训练的ID条件扩散模型合成同身份多张图像，生成数据训练的人脸识别模型在多个验证基准上达到与现有合成数据方法相当或更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模身份标注人脸数据集采集与标注成本高，且涉及隐私与伦理问题。现有合成人脸生成方法的身份来源于预定义类别标签、无条件生成或预定义潜变量采样策略，未显式建模人脸身份的真实分布，导致生成身份的多样性与分布真实性受限。

Method: IDPrior为两阶段模块化框架：（1）在预训练人脸识别模型的嵌入空间中学习扩散先验，建模身份分布并采样生成新的身份嵌入；（2）将生成的身份嵌入作为条件输入预训练的ID条件扩散模型，为同一身份合成多张图像。该设计使身份生成与图像合成解耦，可与现有及未来的ID条件扩散模型兼容。

Result: 实验表明IDPrior能生成多样且分离良好的身份，且与真实身份分布更接近；使用IDPrior生成身份所构建数据集训练的人脸识别模型，在多个face verification基准上相较现有合成数据方法取得有竞争力或更优的表现。

Conclusion: 显式建模身份嵌入分布并解耦身份生成与图像合成，是一种可扩展、可复用且更贴近真实身份分布的合成人脸数据方案，可有效替代或补充真实身份标注数据用于人脸识别训练。

Abstract: Large-scale identity-labeled face datasets are essential for training modern face recognition models, yet collecting and annotating such data is costly and raises privacy and ethical concerns. Existing synthetic face generation methods typically derive identities from predefined class labels, unconditional image generation, or predefined latent sampling strategies, without explicitly modeling the underlying distribution of facial identities. In this paper, we propose IDPrior, a two-stage framework that decouples identity generation from image synthesis. We first learn a diffusion prior in the embedding space of a pretrained face recognition model to model the distribution of facial identities and generate novel identity embeddings. These embeddings are then used to condition a pretrained identity-conditioned diffusion model (DM), enabling the synthesis of multiple images of the same identity. This modular design provides a scalable approach to identity generation while remaining compatible with existing and future identity-conditioned DMs. Experiments indicate that IDPrior generates diverse and well-separated identities while maintaining a closer correspondence to the distribution of authentic identities. Face recognition models trained on datasets generated using IDPrior-generated identities achieve competitive or improved performance compared with existing synthetic-data-based methods across multiple face verification benchmarks. Code and generated datasets are publicly available at https://github.com/fdbtrs/IDperturb.

</details>


### [91] [Towards Scalable Diagnostic-Driven Whole-Slide Image Retrieval for Pathology Archives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07935)
*Jingsong Liu,Youssef Wally Han Li,Weiwei Zhou,Jing Dai,Elisabeth Wetzer,Peter J. Schüffler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CLEAR-WSI 是一个可扩展、诊断驱动的全切片图像检索框架，用固定长度切片嵌入实现无需手工索引管线的相似病例检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字病理普及使 H&E 全切片图像归档激增，但元数据常不一致或缺失，难以高效导航；现有基于内容的检索系统依赖手工索引管线与预定义采样策略，限制可扩展性与泛化性。

Method: 提出 CLEAR-WSI（Constant-Length Embedding for Automatic Retrieval of Whole-Slide Images）：生成固定长度切片嵌入；根据检索设定，用预测诊断标签或真实标签对候选进行精炼；新处理的病例增量加入可扩展的记忆库，使检索档案随时间增长。

Result: 在两个公开数据集和一个私有临床数据集、四项检索任务的基准上，Precision@5 均优于最强基线：CAMELYON16 +3.10，BRACS-1 +2.75，BRACS-2 +1.95，X-HER2 +3.75；消融与延迟实验进一步验证各组件贡献。

Conclusion: CLEAR-WSI 通过固定长度嵌入与诊断标签精炼及可增量扩展的记忆库，实现了兼具检索精度与可扩展性的切片级检索方案，具备实际部署潜力。

Abstract: The growing adoption of digital pathology has rapidly expanded collections of hematoxylin-and-eosin whole-slide images, yet efficient navigation through these archives remains challenging, as metadata is often inconsistent or unavailable. Content-based image retrieval addresses this challenge by identifying similar cases directly from visual representations instead of manual annotations or metadata. Existing retrieval systems have demonstrated promising performance but largely rely on handcrafted indexing pipelines and predefined sampling strategies, constraining scalability and generalization. Here, we present CLEAR-WSI (Constant-Length Embedding for Automatic Retrieval of Whole-Slide Images) as a scalable, diagnostic-driven slide-retrieval framework, validated on both public and clinical data. Using a fixed-length slide embedding, CLEAR-WSI refines retrieved candidates with either a predicted diagnostic label or the ground-truth label, depending on the retrieval setting. Newly processed cases are subsequently added to the expanding memory bank, allowing the retrieval archive to grow over time. On a comprehensive benchmark consisting of two public and one private clinical datasets representing four retrieval tasks, CLEAR-WSI improves Precision@5 over the strongest evaluated baseline, with gains on CAMELYON16 (3.10), BRACS-1 (2.75), BRACS-2 (1.95), and X-HER2 (3.75). Additional ablation and latency studies further examine the contribution of each component and highlight its potential for scalable deployment.

</details>


### [92] [InnerLogic-Surg: An Open Surgical Foundation Model via Continual Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07883)
*Kun Yuan,Hao Ding,Yiqing Shen,Gasper Podobnik,Benjamin Killeen,Tito Porras,Axel Krieger,Mathias Unberath*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出InnerLogic-Surg系列手术视觉编码器（21M–300M），通过在约2900小时、11965个网络手术视频上持续预训练，取得20项手术任务全部最优的线性探测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手术视觉系统多依赖自然图像预训练编码器，或在较小数据集上从头训练的专用模型，缺乏大规模手术领域预训练的通用骨干网络。

Method: 沿用DINOv3预训练流程对Transformer图像编码器进行手术数据持续预训练，以保留表征能力；并引入可学习的特征上采样模块，在低token开销下实现高分辨率密集预测。数据覆盖15种以上术式。

Result: 在涵盖工作流、器械与解剖识别、分割、检测、单目深度的基准上，相同架构下最大模型在全部20项手术任务中线性探测性能最优。

Conclusion: 手术持续预训练可获得跨术式、跨解剖区域、跨任务类型迁移的表征，是替代传统术式专用表征学习范式的可扩展方案。

Abstract: Vision foundation models are the backbone of modern surgical image analysis, yet most surgical systems still rely on encoders pretrained on natural images or on specialist models trained from scratch on comparatively small datasets. Pretraining encoders on large amounts of surgery-specific data has the potential to generally enhance the performance of surgical image analysis methods simply by providing stronger backbones. Here we present InnerLogic-Surg, an open family of surgical vision encoders (21M--300M parameters) obtained by continually pretraining a transformer-based image encoder on millions of surgical images curated from 11,965 internet-sourced surgical videos spanning ~2,900 hours and more than fifteen procedure types. Our training recipe inherits DINOv3's pretraining stage to preserve representation ability after surgical adaptation, and additionally applies a learned feature-upsampling module for high-resolution dense prediction at low token cost. Across a benchmark covering workflow, instrument and anatomy recognition, segmentation, detection, and monocular depth, under the same architecture, the largest InnerLogic-Surg model achieves the best linear-probe performance in all 20 surgical tasks. We demonstrate that surgical continual pretraining yields transferable representations that generalize across procedures, anatomical regions, and task types, showing our surgical image encoders as a promising and scalable alternative to the procedure-specific representation learning approaches that have traditionally dominated surgical computer vision.

</details>


### [93] [Enhanced MRI brain tumor detection using deep learning in conjunction with explainable AI SHAP based diverse and multi feature analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07843)
*Asif Rahman,Maqsood Hayat,Nadeem Iqbal,Fawaz Khaled Alarfaj,Salem Alkhalaf,Fahad Alturise*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究系统比较LBP、Gabor、DWT、FFT、CNN、GLRLM等多种特征表示与KNN、随机森林、SVC、PNN、CNN等学习算法的组合，发现LBP+SVC/CNN在MRI脑肿瘤识别中表现最优，并用卡方检验、p值及SHAP验证LBP特征空间的显著贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统脑肿瘤诊断方法存在分辨率低、辐射暴露、对比度有限等缺陷；MRI虽具高分辨率、非侵入优势，但需高效的特征表示与分类方案以支撑精准的自动化肿瘤诊断，因此有必要探究多种特征表示与学习算法之间的协同效应。

Method: 构建多特征表示（LBP、Gabor滤波、离散小波变换、快速傅里叶变换、CNN、灰度游程矩阵GLRLM）与五类学习算法（KNN、随机森林、SVC、概率神经网络PNN、CNN）的组合评估框架；采用卡方检验与p值统计检验验证LBP特征空间的显著性，并用SHAP分析识别最具判别力的特征；在小数据集与大型基准数据集上分别验证性能与泛化能力。

Result: 小数据集上CNN准确率97.8%、SVC达98.06%，LBP结合SVC与CNN获得高特异度与准确率；统计检验证实LBP特征空间对脑肿瘤识别具有显著影响。大型基准数据集上CNN最高达98.9%，SVC为96.7%，表明CNN具备较强的泛化能力。

Conclusion: LBP特征与SVC/CNN的组合是MRI脑肿瘤诊断的有前景方法；CNN在自动化高精度诊断中效果最佳，SHAP与统计检验为特征有效性提供了可解释性支撑。研究代码已开源，具备可复现性。

Abstract: Recent innovations in medical imaging have markedly improved brain tumor identification, surpassing conventional diagnostic approaches that suffer from low resolution, radiation exposure, and limited contrast. Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI) is pivotal in precise and accurate tumor characterization owing to its high-resolution, non-invasive nature. This study investigates the synergy among multiple feature representation schemes such as local Binary Patterns (LBP), Gabor filters, Discrete Wavelet Transform, Fast Fourier Transform, Convolutional Neural Networks (CNN), and Gray-Level Run Length Matrix alongside five learning algorithms namely: k-nearest Neighbor, Random Forest, Support Vector Classifier (SVC), and probabilistic neural network (PNN), and CNN. Empirical findings indicate that LBP in conjunction with SVC and CNN obtained high specificity and accuracy, rendering it a promising method for MRI-based tumor diagnosis. Further to investigate the contribution of LBP, Statistical analysis chi-square and p-value tests are used to confirm the significant impact of LBP feature space for identification of brain Tumor. In addition, The SHAP analysis was used to identify the most important features in classification. In a small dataset, CNN obtained 97.8% accuracy while SVC yielded 98.06% accuracy. In subsequent analysis, a large benchmark dataset is also utilized to evaluate the performance of learning algorithms in order to investigate the generalization power of the proposed model. CNN achieves the highest accuracy of 98.9%, followed by SVC at 96.7%. These results highlight CNN's effectiveness in automated, high-precision tumor diagnosis. Code: https://github.com/asifrahman557/BrainTumorDetection

</details>


### [94] [THG-Omni: Traceable Human-Centric Graph Supervision for Video Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07836)
*Jisheng Dang,Kai Wei,Ronghao Lin,Bimei Wang,Hong Peng,Wei-Shi Zheng,Bin Hu,Qi Tian,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出THG-Omni：以人中心图结构监督全模态视频推理，把SAM3参与者轨迹与时间对齐的Qwen3-VL标注连成图，并据此构造训练问题；推理时用预测图输入，在IntentBench与NExT-QA上均优于未适配骨干。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全模态模型能回答视频问题，但预测结果缺乏可解释的证据支撑（哪个参与者、事件、时间区间），且仅用答案级监督无法教会模型解读结构化视频证据。

Method: 构建人中心图结构监督框架：用Segment Anything Model 3获得参与者轨迹，与时间锚定的Qwen3-VL标注关联；候选语义主张先经源视频与音频核验再用于生成训练问题；轨迹几何提供显式时空监督。训练问题覆盖参与者属性、动作、情感、交互及其时序。推理阶段使用由评测视频预测得到的图。

Result: IntentBench上在社会推理、情绪理解、欺骗感知三方面持续优于未适配骨干；NExT-QA上匹配图输入达89.54%，无图输入为85.10%；紧凑图输入78.22%，完整图78.02%，无图77.58%；诊断实验显示预测图在缺少源视频与音频时仍保留可用于答题的证据。

Conclusion: THG-Omni提升了人中心视频推理性能，并将证据锚定到可识别的参与者与事件上，实现更可解释的全模态视频理解。

Abstract: Omni-modal models can answer questions about video, but their predictions rarely show which participant, event, or interval supports an answer. Moreover, answer-level supervision alone does not teach a model to interpret structured video evidence. To systematically mitigate this issue, we present THG-Omni, a human-centric graph-supervision framework that links Segment Anything Model~3 participant tracks with temporally grounded Qwen3-VL annotations. Candidate semantic claims are checked against the source video and available audio before they are used to construct training questions, while track geometry provides explicit spatial and temporal supervision. The resulting questions teach an omni-modal reasoning model to interpret participant attributes, actions, affect, interactions, and their timing from a structured graph. At inference, THG-Omni uses a predicted graph constructed from the evaluation video. Experiments on IntentBench show consistent improvements over the unadapted backbone across social reasoning, emotion understanding, and deception perception. On NExT-QA, THG-Omni achieves 89.54\% accuracy with the matched graph, compared with 85.10\% without graph input. Further validation experiments show that compact graph input achieves 78.22\% accuracy, compared with 78.02\% using the full graph and 77.58\% without graph input. A complementary diagnostic demonstrates that the predicted graph preserves evidence useful for answering questions even without the source video and audio. Together, these findings show that THG-Omni improves human-centric video reasoning while grounding its evidence in identifiable participants and events. Our codes are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/review-n94hhfcwp5666/

</details>


### [95] [GEKey: Learning Geodesic Eccentric 3D Keypoints via Self-Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07677)
*Pierre Onghena,Santiago Velasco-Forero,Beatriz Marcotegui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GEKey是一个自监督三维点云关键点检测框架，以测地偏心度变换（geodesic eccentricity transform）生成伪标签训练模型，在多个数据集上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点云关键点检测中，有监督方法依赖人工标注，无监督方法仅利用内在几何属性定位关键点，二者各有局限。作者希望用简单高效的自监督方式识别实例特异性显著点，同时保持类内差异。

Method: 提出GEKey：利用SE(3)不变的测地偏心度变换定位实例特异性显著点，将其作为伪标签训练网络，使预测与物体内在结构对齐；通过平衡实例级与类别级结构来保留类内变化。

Result: 在ShapeNetCoreV2、KeypointNet、CAPE、ITOP、DeformingThings4D上，关键点检测与对齐均达到SOTA，并对噪声、下采样、形变及真实场景条件具有强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: GEKey以简单、高效、鲁棒的自监督方案实现高质量关键点检测与对齐，代码与预训练模型已开源。

Abstract: Point clouds represent 3D object geometry as sets of spatial coordinates. A fundamental task in their analysis is keypoint detection, i.e., identifying a sparse subset of points that captures the most salient features. While supervised methods rely on manual annotations, unsupervised approaches leverage intrinsic geometric properties to localize keypoints without human input. We introduce GEKey, a self-supervised framework that uses the geodesic eccentricity transform, an SE(3)-invariant geometric measure, to identify instance-specific salient points. These points serve as pseudo-labels to train a model that aligns its predictions with the object's intrinsic structure. GEKey is simple, efficient, and robust, preserving intra-class variation by balancing instance-specific and category-level structure. Extensive experiments on ShapeNetCoreV2, KeypointNet, CAPE, ITOP, and DeformingThings4D show state-of-the-art performance in both keypoint detection and alignment, with strong resilience to noise, downsampling, deformations, and real-world conditions. The code and pretrained models are available at https://github.com/Pierreoo/GEKey.

</details>


### [96] [Which Objects Stay? Learning Hierarchical Persistence Beliefs for Lifelong Open-Vocabulary Object Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07662)
*Thanh Nguyen Canh,Haolan Zhang,Nak Young Chong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 HiPer，一种不依赖语义类别的分层持续性滤波器：通过检索视觉与几何相似的训练实例、由其观察历史估计初始存活率，以共轭 Beta 信念初始化并按 Rosen 持续性滤波框架闭式更新，实现开放词汇场景下更可靠的终身物体地图持续性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 终身物体地图需要判断物体离开视野后是否仍在原位，但现有持续性滤波器依赖基于类别的存活先验，在开放词汇场景下难以可靠迁移。

Method: HiPer 从数据而非语义类别推导持续性信念：对新物体检索视觉与几何相似的训练实例，用其观测历史估计初始存活率（无需类标签）；该估计初始化关于存活概率的共轭 Beta 信念，并在 Rosen 持续性滤波框架下用检测与漏检以闭式形式更新；物体在新位置被重识别时，其持续性信念随物体迁移，从而随时间学习物体特异的移动性。

Result: 在 120 条留出仿真序列上，HiPer 在对比方法中持续取得最佳性能；检索先验优于由 CLIP 导出的标签先验，物体特异的在线学习带来进一步提升。在 47 个留出 3RScan 环境中，检索式先验在真实场景下同样优于当前最优基线。

Conclusion: 以检索式数据驱动先验替代类别先验，并结合信念随物体迁移的在线学习，可在开放词汇与真实场景中有效提升物体持续性估计的准确性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Lifelong object maps must estimate whether an object remains at its previously observed location after it leaves view. Existing persistence filters rely on class-based survival priors, making them difficult to apply reliably in open-vocabulary settings. We introduce HiPer, a hierarchical persistence filter that derives persistence beliefs directly from data rather than semantic categories. For a new object, HiPer retrieves visually and geometrically similar training instances and estimates an initial survival rate from their observed histories, without requiring class labels. This estimate initializes a conjugate Beta belief over the object's survival probability, which is updated in closed form using detections and missed observations under Rosen's persistence-filter framework. When an object is re-identified at a new location, its persistence belief is transferred with the object, enabling the map to learn object-specific mobility over time. Experiments on 120 held-out simulated sequences show that HiPer consistently achieves the best performance among competing methods. Retrieval-based priors outperform CLIP-derived label priors, and object-specific online learning provides further gains. Results on 47 held-out 3RScan environments demonstrate that the proposed retrieval-based prior also outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in real-world settings.

</details>


### [97] [Training-Free Predictive Spatio-Temporal Token Pruning for Few-Step Autoregressive Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07551)
*Jia Li,Xurui Peng,Xing Wang,Hayden Kwok-Hay So*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的预测性时空Token剪枝框架PreSTP，利用早步干净估计作为在线信号构建统一的token级冗余掩码，在少步自回归视频扩散模型上实现1.52x–3.20x加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少步自回归视频扩散模型已使高效视频生成实用化，但少步蒸馏削弱了跨步冗余，现有免训练缓存方法难以进一步加速至实时生成。

Method: 识别两类token级冗余：相邻帧静态内容导致的跨帧冗余与去噪中早期收敛的跨步冗余；以模型自身去噪轨迹上的早期干净估计作为在线信号，结合跨帧相似度与跨步收敛性构造统一token级冗余掩码，冗余token跳过后续计算并采用一致的"最后计算值恢复"规则，另引入内容自适应掩码复用策略降低密集探测开销。

Result: 5秒生成获得1.52x–3.20x加速，效率-保真权衡优于TeaCache、FlowCache等粗粒度缓存基线；30秒生成最高1.96x加速，同时提升VBench-Long并抑制长程漂移；在跨步冗余完全消失的一步极端情形下仍能降低推理成本。

Conclusion: token级、免训练、无需重训练或辅助预测器的时空剪枝可突破少步蒸馏下缓存方法失效的瓶颈，并在极端一步生成场景保持有效性。

Abstract: Few-step autoregressive (AR) video diffusion models generate videos chunk by chunk and have made efficient video generation practical. However, further accelerating these models toward real-time generation remains challenging, as few-step distillation leaves little cross-step redundancy for existing training-free caching methods to exploit. We revisit this regime and identify two token-level redundancy patterns: cross-frame redundancy from static content shared across adjacent frames, and cross-step redundancy from tokens that converge early during denoising. Crucially, we find that early clean estimates along the model's own denoising trajectory provide an effective online signal for locating both patterns, requiring no retraining, auxiliary predictor, or reference video. Based on these findings, we propose PreSTP (Predictive Spatio-Temporal Token Pruning), a training-free framework that constructs a unified token-wise redundancy mask from cross-frame similarity and cross-step convergence. Tokens marked as redundant skip subsequent computation under a consistent last-computed-value restoration rule, and a content-adaptive mask reuse strategy further reduces dense probing cost. On 5-second generation, PreSTP achieves 1.52x-3.20x speedups and a better efficiency-fidelity trade-off than coarse-grained caching baselines such as TeaCache and FlowCache. On 30-second generation, it reaches up to 1.96x speedup while improving VBench-Long and suppressing long-horizon drift. Notably, it continues to reduce inference cost even in the extreme one-step regime, where cross-step redundancy vanishes entirely and coarse step-wise caching is no longer applicable.

</details>


### [98] [Describe Your Facial Expressions by Linking Image Encoders and Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08138)
*Yujian Yuan,Jiabei Zeng,Shiguang Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出"面部表情描述"新任务，并构建三阶段训练框架，借助合成图文对与BLIP-2预训练技术，实现用自然语言描述面部动作细节与情绪状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统表情研究局限于离散情绪类别或面部动作单元（AU）标注，难以刻画面部动作的细微差异及其与情绪状态之间的推断关系，因此需要一种更丰富的自然语言描述形式；同时该任务缺乏训练所需的图像-文本配对数据。

Method: 提出三阶段训练框架，基于BLIP-2预训练技术训练视觉-语言模型；针对图文对缺失问题，利用GPT-3.5结合现有情绪类别或AU标注合成并组合描述文本，构造合成图文对用于模型训练。

Result: 实验表明该方法能生成描述面部动作细节、情绪状态及二者推断关系的caption，即使训练数据中未出现相应情绪亦可泛化；同时该视觉-语言任务提升了中间视觉特征在AU检测与情绪分类任务上的性能。

Conclusion: 面部表情描述任务可行且有效，合成图文对策略可缓解数据稀缺问题，且该任务对AU检测与情绪分类具有正向的辅助/正则化作用。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel task of describing human facial expressions of a facial image in natural language, which captures the nuances of facial actions and emotional states beyond traditional emotion categories or facial action units (AUs). To achieve the facial expression captioning model, we propose a three-stage training framework that trains a vision-to-language model using synthesized image-text pairs and the BLIP-2 pre-training techniques. To overcome the challenge of missing training image-text pairs for facial expression captioning, we propose a strategy that involves synthesizing and combining captions using GPT-3.5 and existing annotations on either emotion categories or AUs. Experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our method in generating captions that describe details of facial actions and emotions, as well as the inferential relationship between them, even when those emotions are not present in the training data. It is also demonstrated that the vision-to-language task enhances the performance of the intermediate visual features on both AU detection and emotion classification tasks.

</details>


### [99] [FreeOcc: Decoupling Ego-Motion for Efficient 4D Occupancy Forecasting via Continuous Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08128)
*Zeping Zhang,Zhuoya Zhao,Samy Metari,Robert Laganière*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FreeOcc 通过显式 SE(2) 几何解耦与条件流匹配构建轻量连续时间 4D 占据世界模型，实现无注意力的时序融合与不确定性感知的混合解码，以 28.3M 参数达到 SOTA 预测性能并保持高鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 4D 占据世界模型依赖重型交叉注意力或深度递归来解耦自车运动与场景动态，计算开销大；而自车未来运动已由下游运动规划器提供，这部分建模努力在很大程度上是冗余的。

Method: 提出 FreeOcc：在条件流匹配框架中引入显式 SE(2) 几何解耦，通过数学上分解自车运动构造纯动态特征空间；该空间支持无注意力时序融合，并使物理约束 ODE 仅作用于残余的智能体运动学。同时引入不确定性感知混合机制：将高置信观测体素路由至单次前向解码器，仅对高不确定区域启用 ODE 求解器；并提供集成模式 FreeOcc-E。

Result: 模型仅需 28.3M 参数（约为同类模型的 40%），最优集成模式 FreeOcc-E 运行速度 27 FPS。在 nuScenes 上取得 SOTA 预测性能，近场 Chamfer 距离（NFCD）0.47 m、mIoU 32.1%；在 SemanticKITTI 上实现零样本迁移，并在严重传感器丢包与时间戳抖动下仍保持理想 mIoU 的 92.8%。

Conclusion: 将自车运动从世界模型建模中显式剔除可显著降低 4D 占据预测的计算冗余，配合按不确定性分级计算的设计，可在参数量与推理速度上取得优势的同时保持预测精度、跨数据集迁移能力与对传感器异常的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Current 4D occupancy world models use heavy cross-attention or deep recurrence to disentangle ego-motion from scene dynamics. This requires significant computational capacity. However, this effort is largely redundant since the ego-vehicle's intended future motion is provided by the downstream motion planner. We present FreeOcc, a lightweight continuous-time world model that introduces explicit $SE(2)$ geometric decoupling into a conditional flow-matching framework. By mathematically factoring out self-motion, we create a purely dynamic feature space. This space supports attention-free temporal fusion and allows physics-constrained ODEs to operate strictly on residual agent kinematics. To maximize deployment efficiency, FreeOcc introduces an uncertainty-aware blending mechanism. This routes well-observed voxels to a single-pass decoder, reserving the ODE solver exclusively for ambiguous, high-uncertainty regions. Requiring only 28.3M parameters-roughly 40% of comparable models-our optimal ensemble mode (FreeOcc-E) runs at 27 FPS. On nuScenes, FreeOcc achieves state-of-the-art forecasting performance with a 0.47 m Near Field Chamfer Distance (NFCD) and 32.1% mIoU. Furthermore, the model transfers zero-shot to SemanticKITTI. It also proves highly robust, retaining 92.8% of its ideal mIoU under significant sensor packet loss and timestamp jitter.

</details>


### [100] [Spatiotemporal Dynamics-Aware Industrial Anomaly Detection and Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08080)
*S-Wei Chen,Siangyu Yang,Yi-Chen Jan,Chun-Yi Lee,Shao-Yuan Lo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个基于多模态大模型、面向"动态型"工业异常检测的框架MUSTARD，通过光流引导关键帧选择与时空模态增强，实现无需模型更新的少样本、可解释检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工业异常检测（IAD）多依赖静态视觉模式，而真实制造场景中存在大量基于动态的异常（如不规则运动），仅凭静态线索无法感知；即便是近期基于MLLM的方法，也因时序建模能力有限而难以识别此类动态异常。

Method: MUSTARD框架为MLLM注入细粒度时空动态感知能力：(1) 光流引导的关键帧选择，捕捉关键运动不规则性；(2) 增强模态，融合局部运动模式与全局动态演化；(3) 基于正常模式的推理流程，仅需少量正常参考样本作为检测基准，且无需任何模型更新。

Result: 实验表明，MUSTARD在检测时空异常方面性能优越，并能给出合理的解释说明；同时因无需模型更新，可高效适配多种产品并具备高决策可解释性。

Conclusion: MUSTARD是首个面向动态型工业异常检测的MLLM框架，验证了时空建模与少样本正常模式推理在动态缺陷检测中的有效性，代码将公开。

Abstract: Industrial Anomaly Detection (IAD) is a long-established problem that aims to detect defects in industrial products during manufacturing for quality control. Most existing studies focus on static visual patterns as the basis for detection. However, in real manufacturing environments, many anomalies are dynamics-based, such as irregular movements that cannot be perceived from static cues alone. We observe that even recent Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM)-based approaches fall short in identifying such dynamic anomalies due to limited temporal modeling capability. To address this, we introduce MUSTARD: MUltimodal SpatioTemporal Anomaly Reasoning and Detection, a framework that equips MLLMs with fine-grained spatiotemporal dynamics awareness for detecting and reasoning about dynamics-based defects. MUSTARD introduces optical-flow-guided keyframe selection and augmented modalities to capture critical motion irregularities through local motion patterns and global dynamic evolution. Its normal pattern-based reasoning pipeline requires only a few normal reference samples as the detection basis, avoiding any model updates. This enables efficient adaptation across various products and provides high decision interpretability. MUSTARD is the first MLLM-based framework for dynamics-based IAD. Experiments demonstrate its superior performance in detecting spatiotemporal anomalies and providing plausible explanations. The code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [101] [Do You Miss the Nights We Spent Crafting Figures? Rethinking Teaser and Framework Figures in the Age of Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08486)
*Xiaoqi Zhao,Youwei Pang,Kailai Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文以371幅"动机图/框架图"为语料，用多模态大模型评审六维度沟通与设计质量并尝试源约束修订，发现修订显著提升清晰度但略降美学一致性，主张AI辅助探索、研究者主导设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学图通过抽象、构图与强调的选择将研究思想视觉化；生成式AI为这些选择提供新实现途径，由此引出"解释清晰性"与"有目的的视觉表达"两方面的问题，作者意图以实证方式审视AI介入科学制图的效果。

Method: 构建371幅动机图与框架图的视觉回溯语料：主样本160篇论文（配额抽样，跨四个时间期）加40篇目的性风格对照样本；用单一多模态评审模型对六个沟通与设计维度打分，每图重复判定三次；对全部371幅图尝试"源约束修订"，其中369幅通过保真度校验；辅以案例分析。

Result: 原始图评分在各时间期未呈现一致下降；修订使清晰度均分提升0.428分（五分制），美学一致性下降0.111分；六维平均增益在主样本中动机图大于框架图；案例分析显示增补解释可澄清对比关系，但排版层级与视觉经济性未被解决；全部371幅中369幅通过保真度检查。

Conclusion: 基于模型的发现支持"研究者主导设计"：AI用于探索与修订，作者保留对解释目的、构图与科学关系的责任；随文开放图集与评估记录。

Abstract: Scientific figures give research ideas a visual form through choices of abstraction, composition, and emphasis. Generative AI offers new ways to realize these choices, raising questions about explanatory clarity and purposeful visual expression. We examine these questions through a visual retrospective of 371 motivation and framework figures from 200 papers. The corpus comprises a quota-based main sample of 160 papers spanning four temporal periods and a separate purposive style-contrast sample of 40 papers. A single multimodal judge evaluates six dimensions of communication and design, with three repeated judgments per figure. We attempt source-constrained revisions of all 371 figures, of which 369 pass the recorded fidelity checks. Original-figure ratings show no uniform decline across periods. Revisions increase mean clarity ratings by 0.428 points and decrease aesthetic coherence ratings by 0.111 points on a five-point scale. Gains in the six-dimension average are larger for main-sample motivation figures than for frameworks. Case analyses show that added explanation can clarify comparisons while leaving typographic hierarchy and visual economy unresolved. These model-based findings support researcher-led design, with AI assisting exploration and authors retaining responsibility for explanatory purpose, composition, and scientific relationships. The accompanying atlas and evaluation records are available at https://ntu-ai4x.github.io/Rethinking_Figures/index_en.html.

</details>


### [102] [Where and Why is Grounded Reasoning Useful?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08485)
*David Yan,Le Xue,Forrest Huang,Qiuyi Zhang,Dustin Tran,Andrew M. Dai,Yinfei Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文系统研究"带定位的推理"（grounded reasoning，即模型在思维链中输出相关位置的文本坐标）何时有效、为何有效，并提出诊断方法、消融实验与新基准 BrickVision。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Grounded reasoning 被广泛用于复杂视觉推理，但其收益与局限缺乏系统研究；作者希望回答"在何处、为何"grounded reasoning 最有用。

Method: 提出一种思维链分析方法，量化模型在推理轨迹中对其输出的 grounding 的实际使用程度；对 grounded reasoning 训练配方进行细粒度消融实验；并在多个空间推理与感知基准以及若干合成任务上评估，最后构建需"不可描述引用"的新基准 BrickVision。

Result: Grounded reasoning 可提升计数能力，但在广泛的空间推理与感知基准上相较无坐标 grounded 的基线无显著增益；合成任务表明其价值主要在任务需要"不可描述引用"（难以用语言描述视觉概念）时体现；现有许多视觉推理基准仅用可描述文本引用即可解决。

Conclusion: Grounded reasoning 的收益是条件性的，取决于任务是否需要不可描述的视觉引用；为弥补现有基准的缺口，作者提出 BrickVision 基准以强制模型依赖不可描述视觉引用。

Abstract: Grounded reasoning, where a model outputs text coordinates of relevant locations in chain-of-thought reasoning, is a common strategy for solving complex visual reasoning problems. However, the benefits and limitations of grounded reasoning are understudied. We study where and why grounded reasoning is most useful. To do so, we introduce a chain-of-thought analysis method to measure how much a model uses its outputted grounding in its thinking trace and perform a detailed ablation study of a grounded reasoning training recipe. We find that grounded reasoning improves counting but shows no significant gain over baselines without coordinate grounding across a wide range of spatial reasoning and perception benchmarks. Using several synthetic tasks, we show that grounded reasoning is most useful when a task requires undescribable references, which are visual concepts that are difficult to describe in language. However, we observe that many existing visual reasoning benchmarks are solvable with only describable text references. To close this gap, we introduce BrickVision, a new benchmark that requires undescribable visual references to solve.

</details>


### [103] [Adaptive Low-Light Enhancement for Road Anomaly Detection: A Multi-Architecture Benchmark and Edge Deployment Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08393)
*Obed Honour Eje,Oluwatosin Kolade,Glory Bagai,Taliha Abiodun Folorunso*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文在黑夜、降雨与沙尘雾等退化视觉条件下基准测试四种目标检测器（YOLOv8n、YOLOv12n、RT-DETR、Faster R-CNN），发现统一施加SCI低光增强在黄昏级光照下反而有害，进而提出一种免训练、基于亮度的自适应路由器，在边缘设备上验证完整流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶道路异常检测需在黑暗、降雨和沙尘雾等退化视觉条件下可靠运行；而现有低光增强方法（如SCI）常被无条件统一应用于所有帧，缺乏对"增强是否总是有益"的检验。

Method: 构建物理合理的照明与天气退化基准，对比四种检测架构；逐帧分析SCI增强在不同亮度下的影响；提出轻量、免训练的亮度自适应路由器，依据Rec. 601亮度在"不增强/中等SCI/去噪+强SCI"三档间切换；在NVIDIA Jetson Nano上验证。

Result: 统一应用SCI在黄昏级光照下使mAP@50下降16.1%–30.7%；亮度自适应路由可挽回大部分黄昏性能损失，同时保留低光条件下的增强收益。

Conclusion: 低光增强不应被统一施加，按亮度条件自适应选择增强策略更为有效；所提路由器免训练、轻量，适合部署于边缘硬件。

Abstract: Road anomaly detection systems on autonomous vehicles must function reliably under degraded visual conditions including darkness, rain, and Harmattan dust haze. This paper benchmarks four object detection architectures, YOLOv8n, YOLOv12n, RT-DETR, and Faster R-CNN, under physically grounded lighting and weather degradation, and shows that uniform application of the SCI low-light enhancement network is actively harmful at dusk-level lighting (-16.1% to -30.7% mAP@50). We propose a lightweight, training-free luminance-adaptive router that selects between no enhancement, moderate SCI, and denoising plus strong SCI based on per-frame Rec. 601 luminance, recovering most of the dusk penalty while preserving low-light gains, and we validate the full pipeline on NVIDIA Jetson Nano edge hardware.

</details>


### [104] [Edge-Assisted Crowdsensing for Explainable Cyclist Safety Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08441)
*Hongsen Cao,Chia-Yen Chiang,Mona Jaber,Meihui Wang,Yasmin Fathy*

Main category: cs.CV
