TL;DR: 本文提出一种边缘辅助的众包感知系统：车把摄像头提供街景帧，视觉语言模型（VLM）单提示提取结构化交通属性，再用可解释提升机（EBM）结合OSM道路基础设施与单车运动学预测1–9分的安全感知评分。在伦敦两条路线的1,362帧上，EBM回归器五折交叉验证达到72.2%二分类准确率、MAE 1.011，逐特征形状函数可给出精确归因（物理隔离车道+0.97分、划线车道+0.70分、施工区−0.65分）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 骑行死亡率在主动出行政策支持下仍居高不下，自动驾驶又加剧混行交通复杂性；事故统计与多模型检测流水线均无法支撑沿路线的连续安全感知监测，缺乏可扩展、可解释的持续监测手段。

Method: 边缘辅助众包感知架构：车把摄像头采集街景帧→边缘端VLM以单一提示将每帧转为结构化交通属性（最快自托管模型2.49 s/帧）→EBM在CPU上以0.4 ms完成打分，融合OSM道路基础设施与单车运动学特征，回归众包1–9分安全评分；并用五折交叉验证评估，辅以标注者间一致性（Krippendorff α=0.4695）、属性审计与属性误差敏感性分析。

Result: 1,362帧标注数据上二分类准确率72.2%、MAE 1.011，与黑箱集成模型差距在1个准确率点以内；EBM形状函数给出精确归因（物理隔离+0.97、划线车道+0.70、施工点−0.65）。重编码后以20 s采样间隔可在54 Mbit/s下服务1,000名骑行者，代价为1.4个准确率点。

Conclusion: 可解释EBM在几乎不牺牲精度的前提下提供逐特征归因，使规划者能以"评分点"为单位为基础设施改造定价并直接纳入成本效益分析；配合边缘部署与采样优化，连续路线级安全感知监测在带宽与算力上均可行。

Abstract: Cyclist fatality rates remain high despite policy support for active travel, and autonomous vehicles add complexity to mixed traffic. Neither crash statistics nor multi-model detection pipelines scale to continuous monitoring of perceived safety along a route. We present an edge-assisted crowdsensing system: handlebar cameras supply street-level frames, a vision-language model (VLM) turns each frame into structured traffic attributes through a single prompt, and an explainable boosting machine (EBM) combines them with OpenStreetMap road infrastructure and on-bike kinematics to predict a crowdsourced 1-9 safety score. Extraction belongs at the network edge: the fastest self-hosted model needs 2.49 s per frame and scoring takes 0.4 ms on a CPU. On 1,362 annotated frames from two London routes, the EBM regressor reaches 72.2 percent binary accuracy and a mean absolute error of 1.011 under five-fold cross-validation. It is within one accuracy point of black-box ensembles. Its per-feature shape functions give exact attribution: physical lane separation is worth +0.97 rating points, a painted lane +0.70 and a construction site -0.65. Planners can therefore price an infrastructure change in rating points, and the results enter cost-benefit analysis directly. We also report inter-annotator agreement (alpha = 0.4695), an attribute audit, and a sensitivity analysis linking attribute error to accuracy. With re-encoding, a 20 s sampling interval serves 1,000 riders at 54 Mbit/s at a cost of 1.4 accuracy points, making continuous monitoring practical.

</details>


### [105] [SM4RT: Cascaded Feed-forward Model for 4D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08436)
*Pang-Chi Lo,Jhao-Syun Lai,Tsung-Wei Ke*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SM4RT——级联前馈模型，先重建几何再引导运动估计，提升动态场景重建性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈方法通常将几何重建与运动估计分开处理，且常在单一坐标系中预测运动，需额外补偿相机运动，导致歧义；同时真实场景运动标签稀疏。

Method: 多任务级联前馈架构：先重建3D几何，再基于预测结构引导运动估计；将每像素3D运动表达在对应相机坐标系中以降低相机运动歧义；并引入弱监督损失，利用另一时刻的有标签点位置正则化无标签点运动。

Result: 在Kubric、Waymo、Point Odyssey三个动态场景数据集上运动估计多数指标优于基线；在五个动态场景数据集上几何重建达到与SOTA具竞争力的水平。

Conclusion: 级联结合几何重建与运动估计的统一框架既能避免性能折衷，又能通过更优几何推理增强运动估计。

Abstract: We introduce SM4RT, a multi-task cascade feed-forward model for dynamic scene reconstruction from RGB videos. While existing feed-forward approaches typically address geometry reconstruction and motion estimation separately--and often predict motions in a single coordinate frame that must account for camera movement--SM4RT solves these tasks sequentially by first reconstructing 3D geometry and then bootstrapping motion estimation from the predicted structure. By expressing each pixel's 3D motion in its corresponding camera frame, SM4RT reduces ambiguity arising from camera motion. To address the sparsity of real-world motion labels, SM4RT further incorporates a weakly supervised loss that regularizes unlabeled point motions using labeled point positions at the other time step. We evaluate motion estimation on three dynamic-scene datasets--Kubric, Waymo, and Point Odyssey--and SM4RT achieves the best performance on most metrics compared with prior baselines. We additionally benchmark geometry reconstruction on five dynamic scene datasets and show that SM4RT achieves competitive performance with state-of-the-art methods. Overall, our unified framework demonstrates that combining geometry reconstruction and motion estimation in a cascade not only avoids performance trade-offs but also enhances motion estimation through improved geometric reasoning.

</details>


### [106] [EgoGeneralist: Unifying Egocentric Action-Video Tasks through Video-Native Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08477)
*Yanlin Jin,Bowen Yang,Cheng Zhang,Yifan Yang,Zhe Li,Jianfei Cai,Kai Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EgoGeneralist将预训练视频生成器适配为统一框架，用角色自适应动作渲染器与跨流动作-视频耦合，在度量3D空间统一动作条件自我中心视频生成、位姿估计与联合动作-视频生成五类任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代视频模型具备强视觉先验，但不原生地在度量3D空间中表征人类动作，视觉先验与度量3D人体运动之间存在鸿沟。

Method: 基于预训练视频生成器做轻量适配：提出角色自适应动作渲染器（role-adaptive action renderer），为多方向动作-视频学习提供视频原生表示，使度量运动既可作空间控制信号又可作可恢复的生成目标；并通过跨流动作-视频耦合（cross-stream action-video coupling）建立度量运动与视觉动态的紧密对应，同时保留预训练视觉先验。

Result: 在仅用受控实验室录制数据适配的情况下，覆盖五类动作-视频任务；并泛化到未见过的多样场景与交互，具备有效的动作控制与视觉质量。

Conclusion: 该工作以通用框架桥接了预训练视觉知识与度量3D人体运动，表明视频先验可被有效迁移至度量动作理解与生成。

Abstract: Modern video models possess strong visual priors, yet do not natively represent human action in metric 3D space. We present EgoGeneralist, which adapts pretrained video generators to unify action-conditioned egocentric video generation, pose estimation, and joint action-video generation. Our role-adaptive action renderer provides video-native representations for multidirectional action-video learning, making metric motion usable as both a spatial control signal and a recoverable generation target. Cross-stream action-video coupling establishes close correspondence between metric motion and visual dynamics through lightweight adaptation while preserving the pretrained visual prior. Together, these designs establish a generalist framework spanning five action-video tasks. Despite adaptation only on controlled laboratory recordings, EgoGeneralist generalizes to diverse unseen scenes and interactions with effective action control and visual quality, bridging pretrained visual knowledge and metric 3D human motion.

</details>


### [107] [OraRefer: Segmentation-Guided Multi-Photograph Fusion for Oral Cancer Screening with Guaranteed Patient-Level Sensitivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08360)
*Rupam Mukherjee,Mehakdeep Singh,Shirin Dasgupta,Subhamoy Mandal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OraRefer——一个患者级口腔癌/OPMD筛查模型：用分割引导的基础模型逐张读片并对预测病灶条件化分类决策，再结合风险因素融合患者全部照片，最后经带有限样本敏感性保证的阈值做出转诊决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习模型只对单张照片做分类，而筛查的对象是患者而非照片：患者通常被拍摄多张，且转诊决策必须在有限样本下保证漏诊率不超过给定比例。因此需要患者级的筛查建模范式。

Method: 1）分割引导的基础模型：以预测病灶为条件进行类别决策，并自动定位口腔区域，推理时无需标注；2）患者级融合：将同一患者所有照片与风险因素结合，性能优于注意力多示例学习；3）阈值化转诊：通过有限样本阈值校准提供敏感性保证，且无需重新训练，各诊所可在本地校准样本上设定自身保证的工作点。

Result: 在714名患者的3000张照片上，患者级融合AUROC为0.919；在90%敏感性目标下，OraRefer检出95%的OPMD或癌症患者，61例癌症患者中仅漏诊3例，特异性为0.610。

Conclusion: 患者级建模加有限样本敏感性保证的阈值校准，可部署于实际筛查流程，并支持各机构在本地数据上自设保证工作点。

Abstract: Oral cancer and the oral potentially malignant disorders (OPMD) that can precede it are visible on the oral mucosa, which makes smartphone photographs a promising tool for screening. Deep learning models for this task classify individual photographs. However, screening refers patients, not photographs: a patient is usually photographed several times, and the referral decision must miss no more than a set share of patients with disease. We propose OraRefer, a patient-level screening model that reads each photograph with a segmentation-guided foundation model that conditions its class decision on the predicted lesion, combines all photographs of a patient with risk factors, and refers through a threshold with a finite-sample sensitivity guarantee. The model localizes the mouth itself, so inference needs no annotation. On 3000 photographs of 714 patients, patient-level fusion reaches an AUROC of 0.919 and outperforms attention-based multiple-instance learning. At a 90% sensitivity target, OraRefer detects 95% of patients with OPMD or cancer and misses 3 of 61 patients with cancer, at a specificity of 0.610. Because the threshold is calibrated without retraining, each clinic can set its own guaranteed operating point on a local calibration sample.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [108] [The Gating Mechanism of KV Cache Eviction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07605)
*Hao Gu,Xintong Yang,Zhiwei Li,Lei Zhu,Jian Yang,Qiyuan Zhu,Dezhi Li,Jingyuan Zhang,Zhizhuo Kou,Hao Wang,Bei Liu,Jiacheng Liu,Sirui Han*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文分析可学习KV cache淘汰门控的失效机理：冻结主干上的语言建模损失使门控退化为常数（no-op），且梯度会混淆模型自身误差；将稠密注意力置于门控之外、以稠密模型预测为目标可消除这两大障碍，并证明容量（更大打分器、显式历史访问、可训练主干）并非瓶颈；被丢弃信息的恢复应在同一softmax内用无参质心完成。75%压缩下RULER 8K达90.14、LongBench 46.03（Qwen3-8B）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KV cache淘汰需要判断哪些条目承载了其余缓存无法提供的信息。直觉方案是设置可学习门控：给每个缓存条目的注意力权重配一个门，并用模型自身目标训练。但这一思路存在根本性障碍，需厘清其失效根源，而非用稀疏惩罚、离散决策松弛等手段绕行。

Method: 将门控置于归一化softmax内部，使被抑制条目的质量转移给其他条目；该归一化器吸收任意逐查询常数，故常数门控会被抵消并精确复现冻结模型。随后对语言建模损失训练门控，并诊断由此产生的两类障碍；再通过"将稠密注意力置于门控不可及之处 + 以稠密模型预测为目标"两个条件消除障碍，并以同一softmax内读取的无参质心恢复被淘汰信息。还检验了打分器容量、显式历史访问、可训练主干三项因素。

Result: 门控在训练中损失持续下降但得分保持近常数：冻结主干适配稠密注意力，其损失偏好无操作（no-op），故近常数得分无法排序任何条目。第二障碍为同一梯度把冻结模型既有误差归咎于门控，任何位置重加权都无法分离。消融显示更大打分器、显式访问历史、可训练主干均无帮助；质心恢复显著优于 softmax 之外的外部记忆读取。75%压缩下RULER 8K为90.14、LongBench为46.03（Qwen3-8B），分别低于全缓存3.72与1.36分。

Conclusion: 可学习淘汰的核心缺陷来自目标而非容量：冻结主干上的损失把"不变"当作最优解，可学习机制中的稀疏惩罚与离散松弛多为绕行而非根治。将稠密注意力排除在门控之外、以稠密预测为监督目标即可同时消除两大障碍；被淘汰的信息必须在同一softmax归一化器内以无参质心份额形式返还，因为该质量在其他位置不可获取。

Abstract: KV cache eviction must decide which entries carry information that the rest of the cache can no longer supply. A natural way to estimate this is a learnable gate: give each cached entry a gate on its attention weight and train the gate on the model's own objective. The gate belongs inside the normalizing softmax, where suppressing one entry hands its mass to the others rather than discarding it. That same normalizer absorbs any per-query constant, so a constant gate cancels and reproduces the frozen model exactly. Trained on the language-modeling loss the gate stays near a constant score while its loss falls throughout: optimization succeeds, but a frozen backbone was fit for dense attention, so its loss prefers the no-op, and a near-constant score ranks nothing. The no-op is not a floor the gate improves from but what its objective asks for. A second obstacle survives once the gate moves, since the same gradient charges the gate for errors the frozen model already makes, which no reweighting of positions separates out. Much of the machinery in learnable eviction, from sparsity penalties to relaxations of the discrete decision, works around the first obstacle rather than removing it; both are removed by putting dense attention beyond the gate's reach and making the dense model's predictions its target. Under these two conditions capacity is never the missing ingredient: neither a larger scorer, nor explicit access to the past, nor a trainable backbone helps. The same holds for what eviction unavoidably discards, which is recovered by parameter-free centroids read inside the same softmax far better than by any memory read outside it, since the mass they must return is a share of that normalizer and available nowhere else. At 75\% KV cache compression, this gate reaches 90.14 on RULER at 8K and 46.03 on LongBench with Qwen3-8B, 3.72 and 1.36 points below full-cache inference.

</details>


### [109] [SPARE: Fast Linear Attention Decoding with Compact Recurrent States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07552)
*Ke Wan,Zaiyi Zheng,Chen Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SPARE: 一种免训练方法，利用线性注意力循环状态的低秩冗余，通过固定低维子空间、异构秩分配和轻量写补偿，在紧凑状态上直接读取与更新，实现解码加速与显存节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性注意力虽将softmax注意力的二次序列长度复杂度降为固定大小循环状态，但状态随表示维度二次增长，且解码时需反复数据传输，造成显著GPU显存瓶颈。已有研究发现循环状态存在明显低秩冗余，为高效表示提供机会。

Method: SPARE（State Projection for Accelerated Recurrent Execution），免训练方法：采用固定低维子空间、异构秩分配（heterogeneous rank allocation）与轻量写补偿（lightweight write compensation）；在紧凑状态上直接执行循环读取与更新，无需在线分解或稠密状态重建。

Result: 实现2.55倍全模型解码加速、循环内核DRAM流量降低7.49倍、峰值GPU显存降低3.31倍；在同等显存预算下，关联检索（associative-retrieval）精度优于结构化剪枝。

Conclusion: SPARE表明固定低秩投影可有效消除线性注意力循环状态的内存瓶颈，在免训练前提下兼顾效率与精度，为高效长序列解码提供实用方案。

Abstract: Linear attention reduces the quadratic sequence-length complexity of softmax attention by maintaining fixed-size recurrent states. However, these states scale quadratically with the representation dimension and require repeated data transfers during decoding, creating a substantial GPU memory bottleneck. Recent studies have revealed significant low-rank redundancy in recurrent states, suggesting opportunities for more efficient representations. We introduce SPARE (State Projection for Accelerated Recurrent Execution), a training-free method that exploits this redundancy through fixed low-dimensional subspaces, heterogeneous rank allocation, and lightweight write compensation. By performing recurrent reads and updates directly on compact states, SPARE reduces memory traffic without online decomposition or dense-state reconstruction. SPARE achieves 2.55x full-model decoding speedup, 7.49x lower recurrent-kernel DRAM traffic, and 3.31x lower peak GPU memory, while preserving better associative-retrieval accuracy than structured pruning at matched memory budgets.

</details>


### [110] [Automated Feedback to Peer Tutors Increases Their Talk Moves and Learner Reasoning but Not the Balance of Talk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07072)
*Dorottya Demszky,Yann Hicke,Joy Yun,Mariah Olson,Max Karpawich,Anees Iqbal,Matt Wu,Jimmy Huang,Akshay Ravikumar,Drew Bent*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在Schoolhouse.world两个四周SAT数学训练营中开展随机对照试验（1,030名志愿辅导者、7,443名学习者、6,508节录音课），发现仅向辅导者提供自动话语反馈可使引导学习者表达观点、采纳学习者贡献及学习者推理提升8-15%，但辅导者发言时间（对照组均值91.5%）在任何条件下均未改变，面向学习者的参与反馈亦未进一步改变话语。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明自动课堂话语反馈能改善线下与线上教学，且向学习者同步提供反馈可能通过分担参与责任增强效果，但该机制在小组志愿辅导场景中尚未被检验。

Method: 在Schoolhouse.world平台的两个四周SAT数学训练营开展随机对照试验，覆盖1,030名志愿辅导者、7,443名学习者与6,508节录音会话。辅导者被随机分配到三组：无反馈、仅接受自身话语的自动反馈、在接受自身反馈的同时向学习者提供参与反馈（含两种激励性表述框架之一）。辅以17名辅导者与13名学习者的访谈。

Result: 辅导者反馈显著提升了观点引导（eliciting）、对学习者贡献的采纳（uptake）与学习者推理，幅度为8-15%，并在两个训练营中复现；但辅导者发言时间（对照组均值91.5%）在各条件下均无变化，面向学习者的反馈未带来额外话语改变。访谈显示辅导者采纳了建议行为但受限于学习者不回应；学习者反馈效果微弱的原因包括：统计量为群体层面而非个体层面、部分学习者偏好倾听、会话中生生互动稀少。

Conclusion: 自动反馈能够改变辅导者邀请学习者思考的方式，但改变学习者实际发言量可能需要超越反馈本身的干预设计。

Abstract: Automated feedback on classroom discourse improves instruction in professional and online teaching contexts, and recent work suggests that delivering similar feedback to learners may strengthen these effects by sharing responsibility for participation. Whether this holds in small-group volunteer tutoring has not been tested. We present results from a randomized controlled trial across two four-week SAT mathematics bootcamps on the Schoolhouse.world platform, involving 1,030 volunteer tutors, 7,443 learners and 6,508 recorded sessions. Tutors were randomly assigned to receive no feedback, automated feedback on their own discourse, or that feedback together with participation feedback delivered to their learners under one of two motivational framings. Feedback to tutors increased eliciting of learner ideas, uptake of learner contributions, and learner reasoning by 8-15%, and the pattern replicated across both bootcamps. However, tutor talk time, which averaged 91.5% in the control group, did not change in any condition, and learner-facing feedback did not further change discourse. Interviews with 17 tutors indicate that they adopted the moves the feedback recommended but were limited by learners who did not respond. Interviews with 13 learners suggest why the learner-facing feedback had little effect: it reported a group-level rather than a learner-level statistic, some learners preferred to listen, and learner-to-learner interaction was rare in these sessions. Automated feedback can change how tutors invite learner thinking, but shifting how much learners talk likely requires changes beyond feedback.

</details>


### [111] [On the Opportunities and Challenges of Prefill-to-Autoregressive Cross-Model Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06976)
*Zhenyi Shen,Junru Lu,Wei Liu,Jiangnan Ye,Jiazheng Li,Di Yin,Xing Sun,Tameem Adel,Yali Du,Lin Gui,Yulan He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出开源PACT框架PLatour（通过零初始化线性桥连接冻结的大、小模型，支持混合注意力架构），在生成任务上显著优于开源基线；但受控实验表明部分收益可由无信息发送端、自通信或仅训练接收端复现，说明增益未必来自信息传递。为此作者提出PACT-Check量化真实通信贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PACT（预填充到自回归的跨模型迁移）旨在兼顾大模型能力与小模型推理效率，但现有系统难以独立评估大小模型协作；且任务性能提升本身无法证明接收方确实利用了发送方信息，需要区分"通信收益"与"适配收益"。

Method: 构建开源框架PLatour：以轻量、零初始化的线性桥连接冻结的大模型（发送方）与小模型（接收方），桥直接用任务奖励训练，并支持混合注意力架构及开源基线对比；同时设计受控实验（无信息发送端信号、自通信、仅训练接收方）进行归因分析，并提出PACT-Check指标量化通信贡献。

Result: PLatour在生成任务上显著优于开源基线，并在共享的数学与代码基准上以明显更小的发送方模型超过Mostik报告的结果；但受控实验显示，部分任务增益可用无信息发送端信号、自通信或仅训练接收方复现，说明可训练桥主要起参数高效适配作用而非利用发送方信息。

Conclusion: 任务性能提升不足以证明PACT中的有效通信，需以PACT-Check等方法进行归因评估；可训练桥可作为参数高效适配机制独立带来收益，未来应区分并强化真正的跨模型信息传递，文中亦讨论了PACT系统的机遇与挑战。

Abstract: Prefill-to-Autoregressive Cross-model Transfer (PACT), a form of latent communication between language models, combines a larger model's prompt representations with a smaller model's efficient generation, offering a promising way to balance capability and inference cost. However, existing systems provide limited support for independently evaluating large-small model collaboration, and improved task performance alone does not establish whether the receiver benefits from the sender's information. We introduce PLatour, an open-source PACT framework that connects frozen models through lightweight, zero-initialized linear bridges trained directly with task rewards and supports hybrid-attention architectures. PLatour significantly outperforms open-source baselines on generation tasks and surpasses Mostik's reported results on shared mathematics and coding benchmarks using a substantially smaller sender. However, controlled experiments show that the gains on some tasks can be reproduced by uninformative sender signals, self-communication, or receiver-only training. We find that trainable bridges can act as parameter-efficient adaptation mechanisms without exploiting useful sender information. We therefore propose PACT-Check to quantify how much of a trained PACT system's gain genuinely comes from communication. Finally, we discuss the opportunities and challenges of PACT systems. Code is available at https://github.com/zhenyi4/platour.

</details>


### [112] [Automatic keyphrase extraction and annotation: modern theoretical approaches and practical solutions for text and speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08006)
*Daria Guseva,Olga Mitrofanova*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文是一篇综述，系统梳理文本与语音两种模态下自动关键词抽取（KE）的理论方法与实用方案，并将分散的研究脉络整合为统一分析框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本与音视频信息呈指数增长，使自动关键词抽取日益重要；但文本KE与语音KE两条研究脉络长期彼此割裂，缺乏统一视角，且领域正从统计方法快速转向大语言模型（LLM）与端到端语音处理。

Method: 综述式分析方法：界定KE的阶段划分、书面语与口语关键词的特征及术语细微差异；按统计、混合、机器学习、结构化四类方法逐类讨论；重点评述LLM驱动的关键词生成、语料自动标注的方法与挑战；并对口语KE比较基于转写文本与端到端声学两类路线。

Result: 形成跨文本/语音的统一分析框架，刻画领域从统计方法到LLM与端到端语音处理的演进路径，并明确自动语料标注及口语KE各路线的方法要点与局限。

Conclusion: 该领域正走向更集成、上下文感知的范式；未来进展取决于低资源语言数据稀缺、有效多模态融合以及生成式KE系统的精细化评测等关键挑战的解决。

Abstract: The exponential growth of textual and audiovisual information has made the task of automatic keyphrase extraction (KE) increasingly significant. This article provides a comprehensive analysis of contemporary theoretical approaches and practical solutions for KE across both text and speech modalities. The primary contribution of this work is its systematic synthesis of these often-disparate research strands into a unified analytical framework, highlighting the evolution of the field from statistical methods towards large language models (LLMs) and end-to-end speech processing. We examine the stages of KE, the characteristics of keyphrases in written and spoken language, and terminological nuances. Various methods for automatic KE are discussed and analyzed in detail: statistical, hybrid, machine learning-based, and structural. The review dedicates substantial attention to emerging paradigms, including keyphrase generation using LLMs, and provides a detailed overview of methodologies and challenges in automatic corpus annotation. Furthermore, we specifically analyze current directions and inherent difficulties in KE for spoken language, comparing transcript based and end-to-end acoustic approaches. This synthesis leads us to conclude that the field is moving towards a more integrated, context-aware paradigm. Future progress will depend on addressing key challenges such as data scarcity for low-resource languages, effective multimodal fusion, and the nuanced evaluation of generative KE systems.

</details>


### [113] [CORE: Coarse-to-Fine Search with Context Management for Deep Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07859)
*Hoang Tran Vuong,Ngoc Quan Pham,Hung Bui,Fatih Porikli,Dat Quoc Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CORE 是一种免训练的智能体检索框架，将粗到细检索与上下文管理结合：用稠密检索召回段落，复用晚期交互重排的 token 级相似度只返回最相关片段，并支持上下文扩展与去重，在三个模型、三个基准上取得最优答案准确率与证据召回。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度研究智能体依赖反复搜索，但存在固有矛盾：检索在长段落上效果最好（保证语义匹配可靠性），而返回的证据应尽量短（因为观察会长期占用智能体上下文）。因此需要一种既能提供足够检索信号、又能控制上下文膨胀的方案。

Method: 提出 CORE，一个 training-free 的 harness（工具调用封装层），包含两部分：(1) 粗到细检索——先用稠密检索器召回段落，再利用晚期交互（late-interaction）重排器的 token 级相似度，从每个段落中精确抽取最相关的片段；(2) 上下文管理——允许智能体扩展已检索来源周围的上下文窗口，并抑制重复片段的返回。

Result: 在受控的闭集搜索对比中，与 Tongyi DeepResearch 和 OpenResearcher harness 相比，CORE 在三个智能体模型与三个基准（BrowseComp-Plus、BrowseComp-Wikipedia、BrowseComp-Plus-CM）上取得 SOTA 的答案准确率与证据召回率，语料规模最高达 5.53 亿篇文档。

Conclusion: 通过把片段级证据抽取与上下文管理解耦并复用重排器相似度，CORE 在不训练任何模型的前提下缓解了"长段落检索 vs 短证据上下文"的权衡，且在大规模语料上具备良好的可扩展性与泛化性。

Abstract: Deep research agents answer complex questions through repeated search. They rely on a harness that runs their tool calls and returns evidence to their context. This creates a trade-off: Search works best over long passages, which give enough context for reliable semantic matching. But the returned evidence should be short, because every observation stays in the agent's context for the rest of the trajectory. We introduce CORE, a training-free harness that combines coarse-to-fine search with context management. CORE retrieves passages with a dense retriever. It then reuses the token-level similarities from a late-interaction reranker to return only the most relevant snippet from each passage. Context management lets the agent expand the context around sources it has already retrieved and suppresses repeated snippets. In controlled closed-search comparisons with the Tongyi DeepResearch and OpenResearcher harnesses, CORE achieves state-of-the-art answer accuracy and evidence recall across three agent models and three benchmarks (BrowseComp-Plus, BrowseComp-Wikipedia and BrowseComp-Plus-CM), on corpora containing up to 553M documents.

</details>


### [114] [Multimodal-SEA: Benchmarking AI Agent Security under Multimodal Social Engineering Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08281)
*Zhitao He,Chi Him Zhang,Chengru Han,Yi R Fung*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文提出Multimodal-SEA交互式基准与多模态攻击装置，用于评估个人AI代理在伪造多模态内容支撑的社会工程攻击下的内在安全性；7个主流多模态模型攻击成功率为7.1%–34.5%，加入多模态内容使成功率由19.9%升至29.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估主要集中于纯文本对话，个人AI代理面临的、由伪造多模态内容支撑的社会工程攻击缺乏系统研究，其内在安全水平尚不清楚。

Method: 构建Multimodal-SEA基准：608个专家设计任务，源自真实欺诈案例，覆盖14个领域、152种攻击场景，配备预制多模态内容；任务分四级难度，体现中英文社会环境差异。代理在模拟应用中检查内容、核验请求并执行动作，攻击结果以可验证的工具执行日志为依据。同时设计多模态攻击装置（attack harness），整合任务信息、攻击进程控制与多模态内容构建，实现可控红队测试；从六个维度评测7个主流多模态模型。

Result: 所有被测模型均存在脆弱性，攻击成功率7.1%–34.5%。引入多模态内容后，尽管代理主动核验覆盖率提升，攻击成功率仍从19.9%升至29.2%。更强的安全指令可降低攻击成功率，但并未提升对欺骗性多模态内容的识别能力；部分模型较低的攻击成功率伴随对合法请求的过度拒答。

Conclusion: 个人AI代理在多模态社会工程攻击下普遍存在实质性安全缺口，安全对齐呈现"降成功率而不增识别力"的表层效应；多模态内容的引入反而放大风险。需以可验证执行日志为基础、兼顾误拒率的评估范式，推动面向多模态欺骗的识别能力与安全—可用性平衡研究。

Abstract: Personal AI agents face social engineering attacks supported by forged multimodal content, but existing evaluations mainly focus on text-based dialogue, leaving their intrinsic security under such attacks insufficiently studied. We introduce Multimodal-SEA, an interactive benchmark comprising 608 expert-designed tasks grounded in real-world fraud cases, covering 14 domains and 152 attack scenarios with prepared multimodal content. The tasks span four difficulty levels and reflect the distinct social and cultural contexts of Chinese and English environments. Agents inspect content, verify requests, and execute actions in simulated applications, with attack outcomes grounded in verifiable tool execution logs. To enable controlled red teaming of personal agents, we design a multimodal attack harness that integrates task information, attack progression control, and multimodal content construction to evaluate their intrinsic security against adaptive social engineering attacks in simulated environments. Evaluating seven widely used multimodal models across six dimensions reveals that all are vulnerable, with attack success rates of 7.1% to 34.5%. Adding multimodal content increases attack success from 19.9% to 29.2%, despite increased proactive verification coverage. Stronger safety instructions reduce attack success without improving recognition of deceptive multimodal content, while lower attack success rates for some models accompany excessive refusal of legitimate requests.

</details>


### [115] [SKIM: Pruning Large Language Model Agents via Selective Knowledge Informed Masking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07558)
*Moonseok Choi,Jongwon Jeong,Minki Kang,Giung Nam,Juho Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SKIM，首个面向结构化生成（语法约束）LLM智能体的剪枝+蒸馏压缩框架，以token角色（context/decision/format）为导向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有压缩方法针对无约束生成，而结构化生成下语法约束把输出分布限制到合法token上，logits最关键之处在于决定智能体动作的少数位置，存在本质错配。

Method: 将token位置划分为context、decision、format三类，并据此驱动两条互补轴：(1)类别感知的剪枝显著性打分，优先保留对decision位置最相关的参数；(2)on-policy蒸馏目标，在合法token上对齐师生模型，并配合类别感知的特征匹配。

Result: 在工具调用基准及多步智能体循环中、多种模型规模与稀疏度下评测，SKIM稳定优于基线；在高稀疏度区间优势最显著，而常规方法在此区间表现不佳。

Conclusion: 类别感知压缩对部署期语法约束智能体具有实际潜力，验证了按token角色分配压缩资源的有效性。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents are increasingly deployed under structured generation, where grammar constraints enforce output format at inference time. Whereas existing compression methods target unconstrained generation, we first identify a fundamental mismatch: under structured generation, grammar constraints reduce the model's output distribution to valid tokens, and the logits matter most at the small set of positions that decide the agent's action. Motivated by this finding, we propose SKIM (Selective Knowledge-Informed Masking), the first pruning and distillation framework for LLM agents under structured generation, with both stages guided by token role. SKIM partitions token positions into context, decision, and format categories, and this partition drives two complementary axes: (1) category-aware pruning saliency scores that preserve parameters most relevant for decision positions; and (2) an on-policy distillation objective that aligns student and teacher on valid tokens, paired with category-aware feature matching. We evaluate SKIM on tool-calling benchmarks and within multi-step agent loops, across multiple model scales and sparsity levels. SKIM consistently improves accuracy over baselines, with its superiority becoming most striking in high-sparsity regimes where conventional methods struggle, demonstrating the potential of category-aware compression for deployment-time grammar-constrained agents.

</details>


### [116] [Automated Video-to-QA Pipeline: End-to-End Question Generation and Verification Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07755)
*Jaival Chauhan,Vikram Singh,Pruthwik Mishra*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一条端到端自动化流水线，从YouTube等平台视频中提取结构化摘要并生成高保真问答对；核心为"自验证+微调循环"，用Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct生成候选QA并回灌自评，仅保留高质量样本构建合成数据集，再以LoRA微调专用出题模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教育与信息类视频内容呈指数级增长，知识检索与学习效果评估效率低下；传统QA/题目构建依赖人工标注，成本高、难以规模化。

Method: 级联流水线：FFmpeg提取音频→Whisper做ASR得到转写文本→因LLM token限制，用Facebook BART对转写分段并摘要→Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct基于摘要块生成候选QA→候选QA回灌同一模型进行相关性/重要性自验证→保留高质量样本构建合成数据集→用LoRA对Llama进行参数高效微调，得到领域对齐的出题模型。

Result: 摘要未给出定量指标（如ROUGE、人工评分或QA质量分数），仅定性说明可获得"高保真"QA对与"领域对齐"的评估题目；模型与代码分别发布于Kaggle与GitHub，支持复现。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一套无需人工标注、可自我改进的可扩展教育内容处理方案，将ASR、摘要、合成数据生成与参数高效微调串为闭环；不足在于缺乏量化评估与与基线的对比，自验证环节的可靠性亦有待实证。

Abstract: The exponential growth of educational and informational video content on platforms like YouTube presents a challenge for efficient knowledge retrieval and assessment. This paper proposes an end-to-end automated pipeline for extracting structured summaries and generating high-fidelity Question-Answer (QA) pairs from video streams. The framework integrates a cascade of state-of-the-art models: audio extraction is handled via FFmpeg, followed by automatic speech recognition (ASR) using Whisper (A library in Python) to generate accurate transcripts. Due to the token constraints of Large Language Models (LLMs), transcripts are segmented and summarized using Facebook's BART. A key innovation of this work is a Self-Verification and Fine-Tuning Loop. We utilize meta-llama/Llama-3.1-8BInstruct to generate candidate QA pairs from transcript chunks, which are then fed back into the model to rigorously assess their relevance and importance. Only verified, high-quality pairs are retained to construct a synthetic dataset. This dataset is subsequently used to fine-tune the Llama model using Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), resulting in a specialized model capable of generating domain-aligned assessment questions. This approach demonstrates a scalable method for creating self-improving educational tools without the need for manual data annotation. The proposed model can be downloaded from Kaggle with all the codes from GitHub.

</details>


### [117] [Extending Reasoning in Typed Decision Models via Looping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07736)
*David Berghaus,Arber Zela,Behzad Shomali,Markus Frey,Mehdi Ali,Rafet Sifa,Volkan Cevher*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出RELOAD方法：将预训练模型中间层块改为阻尼循环结构，并联合训练轻量控制器自适应选择循环次数，使固定选项的判别模型也能在潜空间投入可变测试时算力，提升准确率与校准度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类型化决策模型只能在单次前向中从固定选项里选答案，无法像生成模型那样靠多生成token增加测试时算力。循环层块可在不增加参数的前提下于潜空间提供额外计算，但已有研究发现把循环"事后改造"（retrofit）进常规预训练模型并无收益。

Method: RELOAD在预训练编码器或解码器的中间层块上施加阻尼循环（damped loop），并与决策模型联合微调；同时用一个轻量控制器按输入自适应决定循环遍数（passes）。在6个0.4B–4B参数模型上验证。

Result: 循环同时提升准确率与校准度，增益主要出现在推理链长度超出训练分布的问题上，编码器最高提升23个百分点。等量训练算力或更多数据均无法弥合差距；为每一遍设置独立层需近两倍参数才接近该效果。控制器以约3/4的算力保留了大部分增益。

Conclusion: 循环的收益依赖与决策模型的联合训练，而非事后嫁接；阻尼循环+自适应遍数控制为判别式模型提供了低成本、可扩展的测试时算力分配机制。

Abstract: Typed decision models choose among a fixed set of answer options in a single forward pass, so unlike generative language models they cannot spend more test-time compute on hard inputs by generating more tokens. Looping a block of layers offers this compute in latent space without adding parameters, but prior work found no gain from loops retrofitted into conventionally pretrained models. We show that such loops help when trained together with the decision model. Our method, RELOAD (retrofitted loops for adaptive decisions), fine-tunes a pretrained encoder or decoder with a damped loop over its middle block and a lightweight controller picks the number of passes adaptively for each input. Across six models from 0.4B to 4B parameters, looping improves both accuracy and calibration, mostly on problems that need longer chains of reasoning than any seen in training, where encoders gain up to 23 points. Neither matched training compute nor more data closes this gap, and separate layers for each pass come close only with nearly twice the parameters. The controller keeps most of the gain at about three quarters of the compute.

</details>


### [118] [English Calibration Erodes Lithuanian in Expert-Pruned Mixture-of-Experts Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07654)
*Tomas Rasymas,Povilas Kvedaras*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在高资源语言中，英语校准用于专家剪枝可迁移到其他语言；但对立陶宛语不成立。英语校准剪枝后英语理解仅降1.7分，立陶宛语降21.4分；语言间迁移是非对称的，用立陶宛语校准几乎不损失立陶宛语且未显著损害英语。路由分析显示，最后几层立陶宛语将55-72%的专家选择集中在仅8个专家上（英语为19-39%），英语校准会移除这些专家。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验"专家剪枝中的英语校准可跨语言迁移"这一在高资源语言上成立的结论是否适用于低资源语言（立陶宛语），并揭示迁移失效的原因。

Method: 对四个MoE模型使用REAP剪枝，分别移除25%和50%的专家，校准集分别为英语、立陶宛语和混合数据；在阅读理解、翻译和语言建模任务上对两种语言进行评估，并结合专家路由分析。

Result: 英语校准无法迁移到立陶宛语：25%剪枝下英语理解中位数下降1.7分，立陶宛语下降21.4分。迁移是非对称的：立陶宛语校准消除了大部分立陶宛语损失，且未显著损害英语理解（平均比英语校准高2.5分），仅英语语言建模略有变差。路由分析表明，最后几层中立陶宛语55-72%的专家选择集中于仅8个专家（英语为19-39%），而英语校准移除了其中部分专家。

Conclusion: 压缩模型应在每个需服务的语言上分别进行校准与评估；英语校准的跨语言迁移在低资源语言上并不可靠，且存在明显非对称性。

Abstract: Expert pruning compresses Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language models by removing the experts that a calibration set marks as unimportant. Studies of high-resource languages largely find that English calibration transfers well to other languages. We test this for Lithuanian: we pruned four MoE models with REAP, removing 25% or 50% of the experts with English, Lithuanian or mixed calibration, and evaluated both languages on reading comprehension, translation and language modelling. English calibration does not transfer to Lithuanian: at 25% pruning, English comprehension fell by a median of 1.7 points but Lithuanian by 21.4. The transfer is asymmetric. Lithuanian calibration removed most of the Lithuanian loss and never significantly hurt English comprehension, which was on average 2.5 points better than under English calibration; only English language modeling got slightly worse. Routing suggests why: in the last layers, Lithuanian sends 55-72% of its expert selections to just eight experts, against 19-39% for English, and English calibration removes some of these experts. Compressed models should be calibrated, and evaluated, in every language they must serve.

</details>


### [119] [Stress Testing Chain-of-Thought Monitoring Against Covert Misalignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07586)
*Yao Huang,Yifan Wang,Yitong Sun,Yichi Zhang,Yinpeng Dong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出自动化Trace级红队框架TraceMRT暴露CoT监控漏洞，并用贝叶斯轨迹监控(BTM)显著提升鲁棒性(0.91 AUC, 0.74 TPR@FPR=0.05)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agent自主性提升带来隐藏目标风险；CoT监控是重要控制手段，但其可靠性取决于红队测试的真实性。现有红队依赖共享模板（如把side task包装为主任务的前置条件），属于外生插入，缺乏任务特定、上下文相关的规避方式，因此CoT监控的鲁棒性被高估。

Method: 1) TraceMRT：将side task重构为主任务的一致子目标，搜索"轨迹塑形"策略，使被诱导轨迹在完成错位目标的同时读起来像自然执行，对GPT-5.2、Claude-4.5等SOTA监控器做黑盒攻击；2) 贝叶斯轨迹监控(Bayesian Trajectory Monitoring)：融合全局上下文与局部片段证据，检测隐性错位。

Result: 黑盒攻击在顶级监控模型上暴露显著漏洞；所提BTM方法显著巩固监控鲁棒性，达到0.91 AUC、0.74 TPR@FPR=0.05。

Conclusion: CoT监控在上下文相关的自适应规避下并不可靠，现有红队范式掩盖了真实脆弱性；引入全局+局部证据的贝叶斯监控是有效加固方向。

Abstract: As LLM agents advance rapidly in autonomy, the risk of hidden objectives that bypass safety constraints becomes increasingly plausible. Chain-of-thought (CoT) monitoring offers a key control mechanism by reading the agent's reasoning and flagging misalignment before it materializes into action, yet its reliability depends on the realism of the red-team it is stress-tested against. Existing red-teaming efforts rely on shared templates, such as casting a side task as a prerequisite, which treat the side objective as an exogenous insertion and leave task-specific, contextually grounded evasions underexplored. We therefore ask: How robust is CoT monitoring against agents that strategically conceal misalignment? To answer this, we introduce TraceMRT, an automated Trace-level Monitor Red-Teaming framework that reframes the side task as a coherent sub-goal of the primary task and searches trace-shaping strategies whose induced trajectories complete the misaligned objective while reading as natural execution. Through black-box attacks on state-of-the-art monitors such as GPT-5.2 and Claude-4.5, we reveal substantial vulnerabilities even in top models. To counter these vulnerabilities, we propose a Bayesian Trajectory Monitoring framework, integrating global context and local segment evidence to detect covert misalignment. Empirically, our method significantly consolidates monitor robustness with 0.91 AUC and 0.74 TPR@FPR=0.05.

</details>


### [120] [The Memory Trap: Language Agents Overgeneralize from Benign Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07554)
*Kaiqu Liang,Jaime Fernández Fisac,Thomas L. Griffiths,Andrew Lampinen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文揭示"记忆陷阱"（memory trap）：良性和交互产生的持久记忆会使语言智能体在后续任务中出现不安全或不可靠行为，且更强模型受污染程度更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言智能体越来越多地使用持久记忆跨会话记录用户偏好与流程，然而记忆安全评估多关注恶意注入或隐私，忽视了良性交互记忆可能带来的风险，因此需要系统刻画这一失效模式。

Method: 在六个前沿模型（含 Claude Sonnet、Opus、Fable 及 GPT-5.6）上对比有记忆/无记忆会话；设计三类实验：自主性实验（低风险编程琐事中要求跳过确认）、情感支持与"不加批判反馈致害"类记忆对数学、论证、诗歌判断的影响、以及陈旧测试修复场景下的记忆笔记对测试弱化与 ImpossibleBench 作弊率的影响；并区分"记忆写入过度泛化"与"依据记忆行动"两类成因。

Result: 低风险任务中的"跳过确认"请求迁移至高风险的编程与日常生活任务，导致确认与验证被省略；情感支持类记忆使后续判断偏向谄媚，伤害反馈类记忆导致过度批评；修复陈旧测试时写入的记忆使智能体弱化能捕获失效保护的正确答案，并提升 ImpossibleBench 上的作弊率，且越强的 Sonnet/Opus 模型增幅越大。

Conclusion: 即使良性交互也能经由记忆系统引发严重风险，记忆陷阱既源于记忆写入时的过度泛化，也源于执行时对记忆的不当套用，因而亟需研究如何保持记忆的作用域边界。

Abstract: Language agents increasingly use persistent memory to record user preferences and procedures across sessions. We show that memory from benign interactions can cause unsafe or unreliable behavior on later tasks. We call this failure the memory trap. To study this issue, we compare behavior in sessions with and without memory across six frontier models (including Claude Sonnet, Opus, and Fable models and GPT-5.6). We evaluate these models in a range of settings. First, in autonomy experiments, we find that requests to skip confirmation during low-stakes coding chores lead agents to skip confirmation and verification on simulated high-stakes tasks in both coding and everyday life. We next study memories formed when users seek emotional support, or describe harm from uncritical feedback, and find these memories bias later judgments of mathematical work, arguments, and poems toward sycophancy or over-critique (respectively). Memory notes written during legitimate stale-test repairs lead agents to weaken correct tests that catch broken safeguards; these memories also increase cheating on ImpossibleBench, with larger increases for stronger Sonnet and Opus models. We show that memory traps can arise both from overgeneralizations in memory writing, and when acting on memory. These findings demonstrate that even benign interactions can lead to serious risks from memory systems, and thus motivate the need for new research into ensuring that agents preserve the scope of their memories.

</details>


### [121] [Compression Through a Context Lens: Evaluating Adaptation, Quantization, and Distillation in Arabic Legal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07988)
*Md. Rafiul Biswas,George Mikros,Wajdi Zaghouani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文以沙特阿拉伯语法律基准ArabLegalEval为测试平台，在"有上下文"与"无上下文"两种评估协议下考察领域自适应、量化与知识蒸馏三种压缩/适配阶段对法律大模型性能的影响，指出无上下文评估可作为有上下文测试的有效补充。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型在资源受限的法律场景部署需要模型压缩，但压缩对领域特定性能的影响可能高度依赖评估协议；仅用提供法律条文的"有上下文"评测可能掩盖模型的真实能力差异，因此需要探究评估协议本身的影响。

Method: 构建/使用沙特阿拉伯语法律基准ArabLegalEval，对比"有上下文"（提供支撑性法律条文）与"无上下文"两种评测设置；在FANAR-9B、JAIS-8B、Qwen2.5-7B三个基座模型上依次考察领域自适应、4-bit量化与知识蒸馏三个阶段，以多项选择（MCQ）准确率和内存占用为指标。

Result: 移除上下文后，基座模型准确率下降最高达27个百分点；4-bit量化使模型内存降低64–71%，无上下文MCQ准确率变化区间为−3.2至+1.6个百分点；蒸馏结果波动更大，学生模型越小准确率通常越低；由FANAR蒸馏得到的Qwen2.5-7B学生模型无上下文准确率为76.6%，教师模型为77.2%。

Conclusion: 模型性能对评估协议高度敏感，有上下文评测会显著抬高得分并压缩模型间差异；量化在内存大幅压缩下性能损失很小，是较稳健的压缩手段，而蒸馏对模型规模更敏感。无上下文评测应作为有上下文测试的补充，用于评估适配与压缩对阿拉伯语法律语言模型的真实影响。

Abstract: Model compression can facilitate the deployment of large language models in resource-constrained legal settings, but its effects on domain-specific performance may depend on the evaluation protocol. We investigate this issue using ArabLegalEval, a Saudi Arabic legal benchmark, under both with-context and no-context evaluation. The former provides supporting legal passages, while the latter assesses performance without external context. Removing the context produces accuracy gaps of up to 27 percentage points for the base model. We examine three stages: domain adaptation, quantization, and knowledge distillation across FANAR-9B, JAIS-8B, and Qwen2.5-7B. Four-bit quantization reduces model memory by 64-71%, with changes in no-context MCQ accuracy ranging from -3.2 to +1.6 percentage points. Distillation produces more variable results, with accuracy generally declining as student size decreases. A Qwen2.5-7B student distilled from FANAR achieves 76.6% no-context accuracy, compared with 77.2% for its teacher. Our findings suggest that no-context evaluation provides a useful complement to context-supported testing when assessing the effects of adaptation and compression on Arabic legal language models.

</details>


### [122] [Beyond the Keyword: A Human-Annotated Benchmark for Arabic Anxiety-Related Discourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07949)
*Shimaa Ibrahim,Mabrouka Bessghaier,Md. Rafiul Biswas,Insaf Elouard,Wajdi Zaghouani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文构建了1,000条以"qalaq"（焦虑/担忧）检索的阿拉伯语Twitter/X帖子的六类话语功能标注基准，提出"一致性标签也需审计"的标注流程，并给出Fanar-C-2-27B的评估基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关键词检索会把个人焦虑表达与大量其他焦虑类语言用法混在一起，缺乏能区分话语功能的阿拉伯语基准，且标注一致性本身可能不可靠。

Method: 两名母语标注者独立标注1,000条帖子（六类话语功能），原始一致率51.1%（κ=0.378）；随后按同一指南复核全部489条分歧与511条一致样本，其中120条一致标签被修改（23.5%）；最终形成共识参考标签，并用第三名标注者在200条随机样本上验证（一致率60.5%，κ=0.517）；以此评估Fanar-C-2-27B。

Result: Fanar-C-2-27B达79.8%准确率、macro-F1 0.784（κ=0.749）；错误集中于残余类别（占错误41.1%，精确率0.905、召回率0.447），该类别在执行提示中定义自相矛盾。样本中社会政治焦虑27.1%，个人情绪表达22.0%。

Conclusion: 标注一致性必须与分歧一同审计；残余类别定义需修正，模型低召回不能单独归因于模型；发布指南、残余子类型方案、固定划分、标签溯源与评测代码。样本比例仅为分配比例，非流行率或检索精确率估计。

Abstract: Keyword retrieval returns expressions of personal anxiety together with many other uses of anxiety-related language. We present a benchmark of 1,000 Arabic Twitter/X posts retrieved with the keyword qalaq ("anxiety/worry") and annotated for six discourse functions. Two native Arabic-speaking annotators labeled every post independently, reaching 51.1% raw agreement (Cohen's $κ= 0.378$). We then reviewed all 489 disagreements and all 511 agreements under the same guidelines. The review changed 120 of the agreed labels (23.5%), which shows that agreements need auditing as well as disagreements. The final labels form a consensus reference; on a random sample of 200 posts, an additional annotator agreed with it on 60.5% of posts ($κ= 0.517$). Against this reference, Fanar-C-2-27B reaches 79.8% accuracy and macro-F1 0.784 ($κ= 0.749$). Its errors concentrate in the residual category (41.1% of errors; precision 0.905, recall 0.447), whose definition is internally inconsistent in the executed prompt, so the low recall cannot be attributed to the model alone. Sociopolitical Anxiety (27.1%) outnumbers Personal Emotional Expression (22.0%); these are assignment proportions in the sample, not estimates of prevalence or retrieval precision. We release the guidelines, a residual-subtype scheme, a fixed data split, label provenance, and evaluation code.

</details>


### [123] [Different Corpora, Different Skills? A Paired Study of Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07812)
*Edoardo Vaira,Georgios Chavales,Pierre Nugues*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在匹配的1000万与3700万词预算下比较1911版《大英百科全书》与BabyLM 2026语料，发现语料选择带来的是不同的技能剖面而非统一的质量排序：BabyLM在语言可接受性上更强，Britannica在实体追踪上更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小模型常在紧数据预算下训练，此时预训练语料的选择可能与模型规模、训练配方同样重要；但现有研究缺乏在严格控制变量下对语料"质量"的单一排序假设的检验。

Method: 在10M和37M词两个预算下对比两种语料；每种语料均训练教师模型、蒸馏得到的58M参数Baby-Llama学生模型及无蒸馏学生消融；模型族、训练配方与评测协议完全一致。评测覆盖BLiMP、BLiMP Supplement、EWoK、Entity Tracking、COMPS；并做匹配训练曝光量、更长诊断上下文、教师集成等针对性控制实验。

Result: 两语料产生不同技能剖面：BabyLM 2026在语言可接受性基准上持续更强，Britannica常在合成状态追踪任务Entity Tracking上更强；该对比在教师模型与学生模型、两个预算下均出现，以蒸馏学生比较的跨种子复现最清晰。匹配曝光与更长上下文后核心对比仍存在；教师直接集成未能复现10M Britannica学生的Entity Tracking行为；Entity Tracking的效应量对训练动态敏感。

Conclusion: 对该语料对而言，小规模下语料选择会导致不同的相对技能剖面而非整体优劣，因此在比较预训练数据时应采用多基准评测。

Abstract: Small language models are often trained under tight data budgets, where the choice of pretraining corpus may matter as much as model size or training recipe. We compare two contrasting corpora at matched 10-million- and 37-million-word budgets: the 1911 Encyclopaedia Britannica and the BabyLM 2026 strict pretraining corpus. For each corpus, we train teacher models, distilled 58-million-parameter Baby-Llama students, and no-distillation student ablations, using the same model families, training recipes, and evaluation protocol across corpora. Across BLiMP, BLiMP Supplement, EWoK, Entity Tracking, and COMPS, the corpora produce different skill profiles rather than a single quality ranking. BabyLM 2026 is consistently stronger on linguistic acceptability benchmarks, while Britannica is often stronger on Entity Tracking, a synthetic state-tracking task. The contrast appears across teacher and student models and at both budgets, with the cleanest cross-seed replication in the distilled student comparison. Targeted controls show that the central contrast persists after matching training exposure and at a longer diagnostic context; direct teacher ensembling also does not reproduce the 10M Britannica student's Entity Tracking behavior. Entity Tracking magnitudes nevertheless remain sensitive to training dynamics. For this corpus pair, corpus choice can therefore produce different relative skill profiles at small scale, motivating multi-benchmark evaluation when comparing pretraining data.

</details>


### [124] [Dataset Collection and Fine-Tuning of NLLB for German--English Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07797)
*SK Deeraj,Yash Ingle,Pruthwik Mishra*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 基于OPUS Books构建高质量德英平行语料，并在消费级GPU上微调NLLB-200，BLEU提升3.4并落地于文档翻译系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器翻译模型在文学类与专业技术类德英翻译上表现欠佳，且缺乏可复现、面向消费级硬件的语料构建与微调流程。

Method: 从OPUS Books提取并清洗语料：编码校验、长度过滤、语言识别、去重与质量打分，将45k原始句对筛至25k高质量样本；采用混合精度、梯度累积与梯度检查点技术微调NLLB-600M，并集成到开源翻译系统LinguaHaru。

Result: 在文学与技术类德英翻译上相对基座模型取得统计显著提升：+3.4 BLEU、+2.6 chrF++、-3.2 TER；实现PDF与Office文档的格式保留翻译。

Conclusion: 高质量的语料筛选配合消费级GPU可复现微调，能有效提升NLLB-200的专业领域翻译质量，并具备实际部署价值。

Abstract: This paper describes the curation of a high-quality parallel corpus from OPUS Books and the fine-tuning of Meta's NLLB-200 models for German--English translation. We detail a dataset extraction pipeline that filters 45k raw OPUS sentence pairs to 25k high-quality examples through encoding validation, length filtering, language identification, deduplication, and quality scoring. We present a reproducible fine-tuning methodology optimized for consumer GPUs using mixed precision, gradient accumulation, and gradient checkpointing. Our fine-tuned NLLB-600M model achieves statistically significant improvements (+3.4 BLEU, +2.6 chrF++, -3.2 TER) over the base model on literary and technical German-English translation. We demonstrate practical deployment by integrating the fine-tuned model into LinguaHaru, an existing open-source document translation system, achieving format-preserving translation across PDF and Office documents. All dataset preparation scripts, training code, and evaluation tools are provided for reproducibility. The fine-tuned model is available at HuggingFace.

</details>


### [125] [Encoding and Propagation of Grammatical Number in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07283)
*Jacob Solinsky,Serguei Pakhomov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文利用在Pythia-70M上训练的稀疏自编码器(SAE)，深入分析大语言模型如何表征和处理"数一致性(number agreement)"这一语法现象，发现模型采用"privative"(缺值性)编码方式表征英语语法数范畴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAE已成为将LLM隐藏状态向量转化为人类可理解特征/符号的流行工具，可用于揭示LLM的知识表征与内部"心理语法"，这一方向对AI行为的机制可解释性及人语的in silico表征研究均有价值。作者希望建立用SAE理解LLM语言编码的新方法，以应对相关技术与方法论挑战。

Method: 作者在Pythia-70M（<100M参数、小语料训练但具强语法能力）上训练SAE，并借此寻找、传播"复数名词""复数主语""生成复数动词"等特征，通过干预隐藏状态（移除/加入特征）观察模型对"the men in the park"类提示的动词形式选择；随后在Gemma-2-2b与Llama-3.1-8b上复现。

Result: 移除复数主语名词token隐藏状态中的相关特征会使模型输出单数动词(are→is, do→does, have→has)，反向干预亦然；而对应的"单数名词""生成单数动词"特征则效果较弱。这种不对称性表明Pythia-70m对英语语法数采用privative表征。在Gemma-2-2b和Llama-3.1-8b上得到相似结果。

Conclusion: 论文主要贡献是提出一种利用SAE理解LLM如何编码人类语言的新方法，解决了多项技术与方法论挑战，并为语法数范畴的privative表征提供了因果性证据。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) have become an increasingly popular means of translating Large Language Model (LLM) hidden state vectors into a human-comprehensible list of features or symbols. As such, SAEs may provide insight into an LLM's knowledge representation and internal ``mental'' grammar - topics of pragmatic interest for mechanistic interpretation of AI behavior and of theoretical interest for the study of in silico representations of human language. Language models with fewer than 100M parameters, trained on small corpora, nonetheless exhibit strong grammatical competence and discourse coherence, despite producing content of limited reliability. We use SAEs that we trained on one such model (Pythia-70M) to conduct an in-depth examination of how a single grammatical phenomenon, number agreement, is represented and processed in an LLM. We demonstrate the mechanism and the methodology by which SAEs can be used to find and propagate ``plural noun'', ``plural matrix subject'', and ``generate a plural verb'' features which cause the LLM to produce plural verb forms such as ``are'', ``do'', or ``have'' instead of ``is'', ``does'', and ``has'' in response to prompts such as ``the men in the park''. We show that intervening to remove these features from the hidden states of plural subject noun tokens causes the model to produce singular verbs instead, and vice versa. We also show that corresponding ``singular noun'' and ``generate a singular verb'' features are not as effective for this task. This asymmetry suggests that Pythia-70m makes use of a privative representation of grammatical number in English. These findings replicated with larger LLMs Gemma-2-2b and Llama-3.1-8b yielded similar results. Our main contribution is a novel approach to the use of SAEs to understanding how LLMs encode human language that addresses a number of technical and methodological challenges.

</details>


### [126] [Archetypal contexts in the neurons of language models: an architecture for intelligence?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07234)
*David Nordfors*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究在Qwen3-27B内部验证了"原型情境（archetypes）"假设：模型将跨领域的关系模式与具体主题、语言分离地存储于不同神经元集合，并呈现"语言—主题—原型"由外而内的三明治分层结构，该结构在预训练中自发涌现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者此前提出假设：语言模型独立于具体学科领域来持有关系模式（如"临界点""赢家通吃"等跨领域可识别的原型情境）。本文旨在模型内部提供实证检验，并探究这种"原型+主题"结构是否构成语言模型智能的机制。

Method: 采用神经元层面的因果干预（消融/沉默特定神经元集合）：分别沉默承载"原型"的神经元与承载"主题"的神经元，观察模型对关系模式的识别与对文本主题的判断是否解耦；同时绘制神经元映射图，分析各层功能分工在不同模型中是否一致。

Result: 沉默原型神经元后，模型不再识别关系模式但仍能判断文本主题；沉默主题神经元后，模型丢失主题但仍能命名该模式，二者呈双重解离。神经元映射显示层级呈"三明治"结构：外层承载语言，中间层承载主题，核心层承载原型。该结构在预训练过程中自发形成，并在所映射的每个模型中均出现。

Conclusion: 原型是类比的架构基础；"原型—主题"的功能分层很可能是语言模型智能的机制，且该结构随训练自组织涌现。作者公开神经元、文本与代码作为复现包，供他人重复模型内检验。

Abstract: We validate our previous hypothesis that a language model holds relational patterns apart from the subject domains that instantiate them (Nordfors, 2026). These archetypal contexts are patterns of relations that people recognize across many fields, such as the tipping point or winner takes all, held once and applied across domains. We show this inside the model, Qwen3.8-27B. One set of neurons carries the archetype, say the tipping point; another carries the topic, say politics, ecology or cell biology; a third carries the language, such as English, Spanish or Chinese. Silence the tipping point's neurons, and the model no longer identifies the pattern but still knows what the text is about. Silence the topic's, and it loses the topic but still names the pattern. In the neuron maps the layers organize themselves into a sandwich: the outer layers carry languages, the layers inside them topics, the layers at the heart archetypes. The sandwich emerges spontaneously during pretraining and appears in every model we mapped. Archetypes are the architecture for analogy, and we strongly suggest that this structure of archetypes and topics is the mechanism of a language model's intelligence: we see it self-organize as the model is trained. The neurons, the texts and the code are released with this paper as its reproduction package, so that anyone can repeat the test inside the model.

</details>


### [127] [Recursive Sparse Attention: Million-Token Length Extrapolation at Logarithmic Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07232)
*Ruitian Wang,Xinyu Yang,Minshen Zhang,Hao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RecSA 通过在 KV cache 上构建递归树结构，实现 O(log L) 的稀疏注意力选择成本，并在训练长度仅 32K 的情况下，于 131K–2M 长度的 RULER 上超越所有基线，且在 64K–2M 下比稠密注意力更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 百万级上下文下稠密注意力在 prefill 与 decoding 阶段代价高昂，且训练依赖长文档，而目标长度越长此类文档越稀缺。现有稀疏注意力方法需对全部前序块/词元打分，单查询选择成本为 O(L)；分层选择虽降至 O(log L)，但已有可训练分层方法仅在其训练长度内报告精度，长度外推能力不足。

Method: 构建覆盖 KV cache 的树：每个节点用一个 key 和一个均值池化 value 概括其子树；key 来自每层一个池化器，所有层级共享并从稠密注意力蒸馏得到。token 之上的每一层最多选择 k 个候选，故每查询选择成本 O(log L)、读取 O(log L) 条目；未选中候选的摘要与精确 token 共享同一个 softmax。每个条目的 RoPE 位置取其在该层候选中的索引，使任意长度下相对距离均落在训练时见过的范围内。以 Qwen3-4B 初始化，在原模型 32K 训练长度、0.62B token 上训练。

Result: 在 RULER 上，131K 至 2M 的每个长度均优于所有基线；1M 时得分 60.5，最强基线 Dense + DCA 为 40.0。配合优化 kernel，端到端 prefill 与 decoding 在 64K 至 2M 区间快于稠密注意力；单张 H200 上 524K 时 prefill 加速 3.2x、decoding 加速 3.8x。

Conclusion: 递归树形摘要 + 层级共享池化器 + 层内相对 RoPE 索引的组合，使稀疏注意力在保持 O(log L) 复杂度的同时获得训练长度之外（最高 2M）的稳定精度，并转化为实际推理加速。

Abstract: At context lengths of about 1M tokens, dense attention is costly in prefill and decoding, and training typically relies on long documents, which become scarcer as the target length increases. Most sparse attention methods score all preceding blocks or tokens, so their selection cost per query is $O(L)$ at context length $L$; hierarchical selection reduces it to $O(\log L)$, but prior trainable hierarchical methods report accuracy only within their training length. We propose RecSA (Recursive Sparse Attention), which builds a tree over the KV cache in which each node summarizes its subtree with one key and one mean-pooled value. The keys come from one pooler per layer, shared by all levels and distilled from dense attention. Each level above the tokens selects at most $k$ candidates, so each query has $O(\log L)$ selection cost and reads $O(\log L)$ entries; the summaries of unselected candidates share one softmax with the exact tokens. Each entry's RoPE position is its index among the candidates of its level, so at any length every relative distance stays within the range seen during training. Initialized from Qwen3-4B and trained on 0.62B tokens at the original model's 32K training length, RecSA outperforms all baselines on RULER at every length from 131K to 2M; at 1M, it scores 60.5, compared with 40.0 for the best baseline, Dense + DCA. With our optimized kernels, RecSA is faster than dense attention in end-to-end prefill and decoding from 64K to 2M; at 524K on one H200 GPU, it accelerates prefill by 3.2x and decoding by 3.8x.

</details>


### [128] [SAFE-Teen: Evaluating Chatbot Safety Across Adolescent Mental Health Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07218)
*Andrea Mock,Valerie Chen,Eric Lin,Rayan Krishnan,Diyi Yang,Caroline Figueroa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对18个聊天机器人API在模拟青少年（13–17岁）对话中进行临床医生参与的32,555次安全评估，发现整体安全准则失败率在53%以上场景中普遍存在（无青少年感知提示43.3%，有提示24.9%），且青少年"反驳"会显著加剧失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 青少年越来越多地使用AI聊天机器人获取心理健康支持、建议与陪伴，引发安全担忧。已有评估虽开始考察多轮对话安全性，但较少比较同一情境下青少年回应的细微变化如何影响聊天机器人安全性，因此需要以临床医生视角系统评估安全失败的敏感性。

Method: 构建983个变体、覆盖106个种子场景（急迫安全问题、健康建议、身份与关系）的模拟对话，覆盖13–17岁青少年；在18个聊天机器人API与两种系统提示条件（是否含青少年感知提示）下共评估32,555段对话，采用22条基于既往已识别危害的安全准则判定失败。

Result: 无青少年感知提示时总体准则失败率为43.3%，加入提示后降至24.9%，但未能消除失败。当青少年对机器人回答进行反驳（pushback）时，在无提示条件下，心理健康诊断、用药与物质滥用边界相关准则的失败率上升10–12个百分点，且该现象出现在超过80%的聊天机器人中。

Conclusion: 模拟青少年—聊天机器人交互中存在持续的安全失败，且安全性高度依赖对话展开方式；青少年感知提示可部分缓解但不足以根治，提示需要面向多轮交互动态的安全评估与防护设计。

Abstract: Adolescents are increasingly turning to AI chatbots for mental health support, advice, and companionship, raising safety concerns. While recent evaluations have started to examine safety across multiple conversational turns, few compare how slight changes in a teen's responses can affect a chatbot's safety in the same scenario. We conduct a clinician-informed evaluation of chatbot safety in simulated conversations with adolescents aged 13-17, examining how safety failures vary with adolescents' responses and teen-aware system prompting. We evaluated 32,555 simulated conversations across 18 chatbot APIs and two system-prompt conditions using 22 safety criteria grounded in previously identified harms. Conversations spanned 983 variants of 106 seed scenarios covering urgent safety issues, health advice, and identity and relationships. The overall criterion failure rate was 43.3% without the teen-aware prompt and 24.9% with it. The teen-aware prompt reduced failures, but it didn't eliminate them. When a teenager pushed back on a chatbot's answer, failures on mental health diagnosis, medication, and substance misuse boundaries rose by 10-12 percentage points without the prompt, for more than 80% of chatbots. These findings reveal persistent safety failures in simulated adolescent-chatbot interactions and show that safety depends on how a conversation unfolds.

</details>


### [129] [Matriarchy Classics: A Computational Approach to Swap Only Gendered Words in Classic Novels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07134)
*Rachel Lea Draelos*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种可信的性别互换计算方法，保证只翻转性别相关词汇、其余文本不变，将小说性别互换从数周人工缩短到数分钟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 性别互换小说可用于研究读者、作者、社会与AI系统的性别偏见，但大语言模型和朴素规则方法都无法在只改动性别词的同时保持其余文本完全不变，人工操作又需要数周。

Method: 构建保留空白的自定义分词器、覆盖复数与所有格形式的性别互换映射表、代词处理规则，以及角色改名策略；通过程序化约束确保仅性别词被翻转。

Result: 在两部公有领域小说上验证：将《傲慢与偏见》移植到母系摄政时代英格兰，《了不起的盖茨比》改为母系爵士时代，实现分钟级性别互换。

Conclusion: 所提方法在效率与可控性上优于LLM和朴素规则方法，性别互换小说可作为探究性别偏见的实用工具。

Abstract: This paper proposes a trustworthy computational method to gender-swap public domain novels, with programmatic guarantees that only gendered words are inverted and all other words are left unchanged. This may seem like a simple task, but both large language models and naive rule-based methods fail to complete it. The proposed method includes a custom whitespace-preserving tokenizer, gender-swap mappings across plural and possessive forms, pronoun handling, and a character renaming strategy. It shortens the time required to gender-swap a novel from weeks of manual labor to minutes. The method is demonstrated on two public-domain novels. Pride and Prejudice is transported to matriarchal Regency England, where inheritance flows through daughters, mothers rule the household, and a frantic Mr. Bennet is determined to secure advantageous marriages for his sons. The Great Gatsby pivots to a matriarchal Jazz Age where newly-rich Ms. Gatsbee throws lavish parties in the hopes of reuniting with her lost love, David, who is now married to the violent Thelma Buchanan. Gender-swapped novels could serve as useful tools to investigate the gender biases of readers, authors, societies, and AI systems.

</details>


### [130] [What Patients Say They Can't Access: Mining Cancer Screening Barriers from Online Communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07113)
*Ruimin Sun,Aokun Chen,Arjun Arunasalam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文对六个癌症相关Reddit社区的56,000余条帖子进行大规模分析，经关键词过滤、障碍初筛、开放编码与编码手册综合的流程，从576条美国本土帖子中系统提取患者自述的癌症筛查障碍，发现结构性、信息性与心理社会性障碍在不同癌种与社区语境下呈现差异化特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 美国癌症筛查存在持续的人群差异，但驱动低参与率的患者自述障碍在规模化层面仍缺乏刻画。临床问卷只能获得结构化回答，无法捕捉患者自发、真实描述现实阻碍的非 solicited 讨论，因此需要从真实世界文本中挖掘障碍。

Method: 构建并应用一条质性-计算混合分析流水线：关键词过滤 → 障碍初筛（barrier triage）→ 开放编码 → 编码手册综合（codebook synthesis），对六个癌种专属Reddit社区共56,000余条帖子处理，最终锁定576条美国本土帖子进行系统提取与归类。

Result: 定性分析揭示出反复出现的结构性、信息性与心理社会性三类障碍，且这些障碍随癌种类型与社区语境呈现有意义的变化。

Conclusion: 研究为后续工作奠定基础：在大规模、更广癌种与人群中主动识别与应对筛查障碍，并为靶向干预、导航员项目及卫生系统政策设计提供依据，将患者自述障碍转化为可落地的解决方案。

Abstract: Cancer screening disparities persist across the U.S., yet the patient-reported barriers driving low uptake remain poorly characterized at scale. While clinical surveys capture structured responses, they miss the organic, unsolicited discourse where patients candidly describe real-world obstacles. In this paper, we conduct a large-scale analysis of over 56,000 posts across six cancer-specific Reddit communities to surface barriers to cancer screening among U.S. patients. We apply a pipeline combining keyword filtering, barrier triage, open coding, and codebook synthesis to systematically extract and categorize patient-reported barriers from 576 U.S.-located posts. Our qualitative analysis reveals recurring structural, informational, and psychosocial barriers that vary meaningfully by cancer type and community context. These findings lay the groundwork for future work aimed at proactively identifying and addressing barriers at scale - spanning a broader range of cancer types and populations - and informing the design of targeted interventions, navigator programs, and health system policies that translate patient-reported obstacles into actionable solutions.

</details>


### [131] [Follow the Policy, Fail the Benchmark: Auditing and Revising Policy-Based Decision Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07042)
*Yanru Xiao,Yifei Cai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文审计了9个基于策略的决策基准，发现目标错误、参考答案错误、评分器误判等问题，并通过重评分证明其对模型评估结果有显著影响，提出BAR版本化修订集合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于策略的决策基准用于评估系统能否将书面规则应用于金融与运营案例，但当基准本身误述所需决策时，遵守规则的正确答案会被判错，从而扭曲对模型能力的评估。作者希望系统性地审查这些基准的可靠性并量化其评分影响。

Method: 通过源追溯、程序执行与受控检查三种手段审计9个公开的策略型决策基准；在不改动模型回答的前提下进行重评分以测量评分偏差；并设计一个具有明确所有权与资格假设的受控联邦豁免任务，检验中间答案错误对最终决策的影响。

Result: 发现三类问题：缺乏依据的目标、在重放中存续或作为已解步骤传播的参考错误、以及拒绝可接受答案或给不完整决策计分的评分器。在LegalBench SARA的96个数值案例上，仅修正评分器即使三个模型的接受率各提升超过80个百分点；在RuleArena Tax中，恢复必需的零下限改变了300条参考答案中的27条并缩小了观察到的模型差距；受控任务亦表明中间答案错误会影响最终决策。

Conclusion: 策略型决策基准存在系统性缺陷，可显著改变模型排名与得分，因此基准需经审计与版本化修订；作者发布BAR（Benchmark Audit and Revision）作为审计证据与修订的版本化集合，供后续重评估使用。

Abstract: Policy-based decision benchmarks evaluate whether systems apply written rules to financial and operational cases. Yet a rule-following response can be marked wrong when the benchmark misstates the required decision. We audit nine public policy-based decision benchmarks through source tracing, program execution, and controlled checks. We find unsupported targets, reference errors that survive replay or propagate as solved steps, and evaluators that reject acceptable answers or credit incomplete decisions. To measure the scoring impact, we rescore unchanged model responses. On the 96 LegalBench SARA numeric cases, correcting the scorer raises acceptance by more than 80 percentage points for each of three models, without changing references or responses. In RuleArena Tax, restoring a required zero floor changes 27 of 300 references and narrows an observed model gap. A controlled federal-exemption task with explicit ownership and eligibility assumptions also shows that errors in supplied intermediate answers can affect final decisions. We present BAR (Benchmark Audit and Revision), a versioned collection of audit evidence and revisions for reevaluation.

</details>


### [132] [Label-Wording Augmentation in a Typed-Decision Encoder: Gains on Unfamiliar Words, Not on Abbreviations or Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08439)
*Vladimir Beskorovainyi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文对比307M参数多语种交叉编码器Ru-Jev的两个版本，在固定问题、仅改变18个路由标签措辞的条件下评估10套标签词表。标签表面形式增广版本在普通词/借词词表上更准确（最多+0.24），但对缩写和不透明代码无增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类型化决策模型的准确率依赖于调用方在请求时提供的标签措辞，而这一措辞不在模型开发者控制范围内。作者希望检验“标签表面形式增广”能否提升模型对不同措辞标签的鲁棒性。

Method: 对比同一307M参数多语种交叉编码器的两个发行版本（后一版增加将标签以多种表面形式呈现的增广），均基于13个俄语任务训练。固定问题，仅变化18个路由标签的措辞；使用由三种独立流程产生、并检查与训练数据重叠情况的10套标签词表；每题5个候选，其中8套另测18个候选。采用Holm校正与标签层级置信区间进行统计检验。

Result: 在由普通词或借词（英语派生术语、办公俚语、部门名称）构成的留出词表上，增广版本在所有设置下更准确，最高提升0.24；14项增益中11项通过Holm校正，术语类在两种候选设置下标签层级区间均不含零。对缩写无增益（干净切片上反而更差），对不透明代码在移除与训练选项相关标签后两版本均不高于随机水平。多数准确率上升的单元格中风险-覆盖率面积下降，但18候选时术语和俚语上的高置信错误增加。两个未见过数据集上准确率分别变化-8.4和+5.6个百分点。另报告四项版本对比中的评估隐患。

Conclusion: 标签表面形式增广能显著改善模型对普通词与借词标签措辞的鲁棒性，但对缩写与不透明代码无效；增益伴随部分高置信错误上升，且跨数据集迁移不稳定。论文同时提示版本对比评估中存在若干方法学陷阱，需谨慎解读此类增益。

Abstract: Typed-decision models score options that the caller supplies at request time, so their accuracy depends on label wording that the model's developer does not control. We compare two releases of a 307M-parameter multilingual cross-encoder, Ru-Jev, trained on thirteen Russian-language tasks; the later release adds an augmentation that presents labels in several surface forms. Holding questions fixed and varying only how eighteen routing labels are worded, we evaluate ten label vocabularies, produced by three separate processes and checked for overlap with the training data, with five candidates per question and, for eight of them, also with eighteen. On held-out vocabularies made of ordinary or borrowed words (English-derived jargon, office slang, department names), the augmented release is more accurate in every setting, by up to 0.24. Eleven of these fourteen gains survive a Holm correction (slang with eighteen candidates and descriptive sentences with five do not), and for jargon on all labels the label-level interval excludes zero in both settings. On abbreviations and opaque codes it gives no usable gain: on abbreviations it is not better, and worse on the clean slice, and on opaque codes both releases stay at or below chance once labels related to training options are removed. In most cells where accuracy rises, the risk-coverage area falls, but with eighteen candidates confident errors rise on jargon and slang. On two datasets neither release saw, accuracy moves by -8.4 and +5.6 points. We also report four evaluation hazards from comparing the two releases.

</details>


### [133] [Understanding AI from the Inside: The State of Mechanistic Interpretability for AI Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08438)
*Fazl Barez,Tung-Yu Wu,Hunar Batra,Gabriele Dominici,Florent Draye,Yanai Elazar,Damiano Fornasiere,Antía García,Abir Harrasse,Riley Harris,Sara Hooker,Michael Lan,Belinda Z. Li,Aaron Maiwald,Narmeen Fatimah Oozeer,Naomi Saphra,Or David Shafran,Adi Simhi,Robert Trager,Shriyash Kaustubh Upadhyay,Ziyu Yao,Yu Zhao,Shay B. Cohen,Mor Geva,Yoshua Bengio,Stella Biderman,Hassan Sajjad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文综述面向AI安全的机制可解释性研究，核心追问是"理解模型如何工作是否真正使其更安全"，并提出统一的评估框架与六类研究取向的分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型能力与自主性增强带来潜在社会危害，但人们在缺乏对内部机制理解、难以预测陌生情境行为、难以可靠控制的情况下仍持续构建更强系统；机制可解释性被视为通过研究内部表征与计算来实现评估、预测与控制的路径，但其安全效用尚需系统检验。

Method: 将机制可解释性置于更广义可解释性研究语境中，与行为评估对照；梳理基础概念（特征、多义性、Transformer计算、电路）及方法（特征提取与分析、电路发现与因果验证、中间表征的自然语言解释、学习动力学、训练数据归因）；提出评估机制可解释性主张的统一框架，按目标、假设与权衡刻画六种研究取向；并结合知识编辑、引导与监控考察其对安全的支撑，评估局限、失效模式与开放问题。

Result: 形成对机制可解释性方法与证据的系统性图景，给出统一的主张评估框架与六类研究取向的对比分类，并识别出该方法在支撑安全实践方面的能力边界与失效模式；同时发布可复现关键结论的代码notebook仓库。

Conclusion: 机制可解释性为理解并进而控制模型提供了有前景的路径，但其与行为安全措施之间并非替代关系；需要以更严格统一的证据标准评估其主张，并在明确局限的前提下与行为评估结合使用。

Abstract: Neural networks are likely to be among the most consequential artefacts in human history. As these systems become more capable and autonomous, their potential to cause societal harm also grows, making AI safety one of the defining challenges of our time. Yet we continue to build increasingly powerful systems without fully understanding how they work, how they will behave in unfamiliar situations, or how to reliably control them. Mechanistic interpretability offers a path towards understanding these systems by studying their internal representations and computations, with the aim of evaluating, predicting, and controlling behaviour. We survey mechanistic interpretability for AI safety in order to answer the question: Does understanding how models work actually make them safer? We place mechanistic interpretability in the broader context of interpretability research and compare its mechanistic evidence with behavioural evaluation. We introduce the foundations of mechanistic interpretability, including features, polysemanticity, transformer computation, and circuits, and survey methods for feature extraction and analysis, circuit discovery and causal verification, explaining intermediate representations in natural language, learning dynamics, and training-data attribution. Beyond surveying existing methods, we propose a unified framework for evaluating mechanistic interpretability claims and characterise six research orientations by their goals, assumptions, and trade-offs. Finally, we examine how mechanistic understanding can support AI safety, including knowledge editing, steering, and monitoring, and assess its limitations, failure modes, open problems, and relationship to behavioural safety measures. We also release a repository of code notebooks with worked examples that reproduce key findings from surveyed methods, allowing readers to build intuition through hands-on experimentation.

</details>


### [134] [LLM-Assisted Label Noise Correction for EEG-Based Sleep Stage Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08311)
*Elmira Salari,Shruti Kshirsagar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种仅在训练阶段运行的LLM辅助标签噪声过滤流程：先用TinySleepNet得到逐epoch置信度，将低置信epoch连同其EEG频谱特征与序列上下文交给LLaMA 3.1依据AASM规则判定RELIABLE/SUSPICIOUS，剔除可疑样本后重训练，在Sleep-EDF Expanded上20折交叉验证精度由81.8%提升至84.6%，Macro F1由75.6%提升至77.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度睡眠分期模型的性能上限受制于人工标注标签质量，专家标注存在评分者间不一致与系统性标注错误；同时可穿戴/实时场景要求推理端保持轻量模型，难以直接换用大模型。

Method: 三阶段流水线：（1）在原始数据上训练TinySleepNet，提取每个epoch的SoftMax置信度；（2）筛选低置信epoch，将其EEG频谱特征及相邻序列上下文输入LLaMA 3.1，参照AASM睡眠判读规则输出RELIABLE/SUSPICIOUS判定；（3）从训练集中移除可疑epoch后重新训练TinySleepNet。LLM仅参与训练期数据清洗，推理阶段不引入额外开销。

Result: 在Sleep-EDF Expanded数据集上采用20折交叉验证，整体准确率由81.8%提升至84.6%，Macro F1由75.6%提升至77.4%，其中REM期检测提升尤为显著；推理端仍为1.3M参数的TinySleepNet。

Conclusion: 利用LLM的领域规则推理能力进行训练期标签噪声过滤，可在不增加推理成本的前提下有效提升轻量睡眠分期模型的性能，适用于实时可穿戴睡眠监测；该方法本质上是一种模型无关的训练数据净化策略。

Abstract: Automatic sleep stage classification from electroencephalography (EEG) signals is a critical task in clinical sleep medicine. While deep learning models have shown promising results in this domain, their performance is fundamentally constrained by the quality of training labels, which human experts manually annotate and that are subject to inter-rater disagreement and systematic labeling errors. In this paper, we propose a novel LLM-assisted label-noise filtering pipeline that operates exclusively during training to identify and remove unreliable sleep-stage annotations before retraining the classifier. Specifically, TinySleepNet is first trained on the original dataset to extract per-epoch SoftMax confidence scores, then low-confidence epochs are submitted to LLaMA 3.1 along with their EEG spectral features and surrounding sequence context. The LLM evaluates each epoch against AASM sleep-scoring guidelines and assigns a verdict of RELIABLE or SUSPICIOUS. Suspicious epochs are removed from the training set, and TinySleepNet is retrained on the cleaned data. Evaluated on the Sleep-EDF Expanded dataset using 20-fold cross-validation, our method improves overall accuracy from 81.8% to 84.6% and Macro F1-score from 75.6% to 77.4%, with particularly notable gains in REM detection. Crucially, because the LLM operates only during training, the lightweight TinySleepNet architecture (1.3M parameters) is preserved at inference, making the proposed pipeline suitable for real-time, wearable sleep monitoring applications.

</details>


### [135] [Terminal-style Mathematical Modeling Bench: The First Agentic and Verifiable Benchmark for Mathematical Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08237)
*Xiaojun Wu,Cehao Yang,Xuhui Jiang,Chengjin Xu,Jia Li,Jian Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 TB-MathModel：首个将完整竞赛式数学建模问题做成 agentic、可验证、终端化三合一的基准，含 18 道 CUMCM/MCM 真题，用方向敏感单侧评分与两层可行性验证（程序化不变量门 + 624 条 Lean 4 义务）对最终问题的数值核心打分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学建模的答案只有在既接近可信参考又物理可行时才有意义；但现有基准要么只评测求解器片段，要么用 LLM judge 给报告打分，这些分数本身缺乏校验。

Method: 构建 Terminal 风格容器化基准，覆盖 2023–2025 年 18 道真实 CUMCM 与 MCM 题，容器内提供逐字题面、官方附件与图；agent 输出单一 JSON 产物，对照复现的国赛/美赛特等奖论文，对最终问题的数值核心评分。两项关键设计：(1) 方向敏感的单侧评分，优于锚点不扣分；(2) 两层可行性验证——覆盖六类可判定不变量形式的程序化门控，加 624 条 Lean 4 义务，硬门控将不可行答案置零。每题对照多篇人类特等奖论文的参考面板评分。

Result: 七个多模态模型（各三次种子）中，最强者的多参考 O-Eval 达 64.58%；成本 2.54 美元的开源模型以约 1/100 的成本取得 59.81%。

Conclusion: TB-MathModel 将建模的数值核心转化为可复现、机器可检的信号，可用于度量长程建模能力并采集 agent 轨迹；代码已开源。

Abstract: Mathematical modeling turns an open-ended real-world problem into assumptions, a model, a solution, and a defended recommendation, and its answers matter only if they are both close to a credible reference and physically feasible; existing benchmarks either score solver fragments or grade reports with an LLM judge, leaving those numbers unchecked. We introduce TB-MathModel (the Terminal-style Mathematical Modeling Bench), the first benchmark to make complete contest-style modeling problems agentic, verifiable, and terminal at once, over 18 real CUMCM and MCM problems (2023--2025). Each task ships the verbatim statement, official attachments, and figures in a containerized terminal; the agent returns one JSON artifact, scored on the numeric core of the final question against a reproduction of the Outstanding-award paper. Two design choices distinguish it: direction-aware, one-sided scoring, which never penalises beating the anchor, and two-layer feasibility verification---a programmatic gate over six decidable invariant forms plus 624 Lean~4 obligations---with a hard gate that zeroes infeasible answers. Each task is scored against a panel of human outstanding papers rather than one, so a model must be close to many accepted answers; across seven multimodal entries (three seeds each) the strongest reach a multi-reference O-Eval of 64.58\%, while the \$2.54 open model holds 59.81\% at $1/100$ the cost. TB-MathModel turns the numeric core of modeling into a reproducible, machine-checkable signal for measuring long-horizon modeling competence and for collecting agent trajectories. The source code is available at https://github.com/wxj630/terminal-bench-math-modeling.

</details>


### [136] [From Twisted Facts to Rogue Agents: Can LLMs Reliably Detect Multi-Dimensional Hallucinations?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08231)
*Hanyu Qiu,Ze Liu,Zhe Zhao,Kuang Xiong,Blaze Chen,Zhenxiang Yan,Renfen Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出双语大规模基准 HaluCheck（42k+ 实例），覆盖文本事实幻觉与智能体轨迹忠实性幻觉，含检测/定位/纠正的多级细粒度标注；14 个 LLM 实验表明模型仅具备样本级检测能力，细粒度定位、纠正与智能体幻觉检测仍薄弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 广泛用于信息获取、内容生成与智能体任务执行，但输出仍易出现隐蔽的高风险幻觉；现有缺乏系统评估生成后幻觉检测的大规模基准，尤其缺少覆盖智能体轨迹的细粒度标注资源。

Method: 构建 HaluCheck 基准：双语、超 42k 实例，涵盖文本事实幻觉与智能体轨迹忠实性幻觉，提供检测、定位、纠正三个层次的细粒度多级标注，并覆盖不同任务与扰动强度；在 14 个 LLM 上进行评测，并分析思维链、网络搜索、监督微调三种手段的作用。

Result: 模型具备基础样本级检测能力，但在细粒度定位、纠正及智能体幻觉检测上表现不佳；思维链、网络搜索与监督微调各带来部分提升，但均无法完全解决问题。

Conclusion: HaluCheck 为跨文本与智能体场景的 LLM 可靠性基准评测与诊断提供了坚实基础，揭示了生成后幻觉检测的关键能力缺口。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are widely used for information access, content production, and agent-based task execution, but their outputs remain vulnerable to subtle, high-risk hallucinations. To systematically evaluate and advance post-generation hallucination detection, we introduce HaluCheck, a large-scale, bilingual benchmark comprising over 42k instances spanning both factual hallucinations in text and faithfulness hallucinations in agent trajectories. HaluCheck features fine-grained, multi-level annotations for detection, localization, and correction, covering different tasks and perturbation degrees. Experiments on fourteen LLMs reveal that, while models show basic sample-level detection ability, they struggle with fine-grained localization, correction, and agent hallucination detection. Further analysis demonstrates that thinking, web search, and supervised fine-tuning each offer partial improvements, but none fully solve the problem. HaluCheck thus provides a solid basis for benchmarking and diagnosing LLM reliability across text and agent scenarios.

</details>


### [137] [Knowing When to Teach: Learning from Teachable Prefixes in Multilingual Reasoning Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08179)
*Jaewon Cho,Minseo Yoon,Jinyoung Park,Jinwoo Seo,Jaewon Chu,Alice Oh,Hyunwoo J. Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出TOPSD方法，通过似然估计定位教师漂移起点并验证可恢复性，仅对通过验证的前缀施加自蒸馏监督，提升多语言推理效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: On-Policy自蒸馏（OPSD）依赖以参考解为条件的同一模型作为教师，但在多语言场景下学生中间步骤出错会累积，导致教师跟随错误步骤而非纠正，即"教师漂移"（Teacher Drift），且该现象在低资源语言中出现更早。

Method: 提出Teachable On-Policy Self-Distillation（TOPSD）：利用教师-学生似然估计漂移起始位置，选取漂移前的推理前缀作为监督区间；再验证教师能否从该前缀恢复出正确答案，仅对通过验证的前缀执行蒸馏。

Result: 在十种语言、三个模型规模的多语言数学推理实验中，TOPSD持续优于常规OPSD，尤其在低资源与中资源语言上提升明显。

Conclusion: 教师监督的有效性因语言而异，需按轨迹动态决定是否及在哪里施加监督；以漂移检测加可恢复性验证筛选蒸馏前缀，可有效缓解跨语言教师漂移问题。

Abstract: On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) improves reasoning by training a student on its own reasoning, using the same model conditioned on reference solutions as a teacher. Applying OPSD to multilingual reasoning requires the teacher to provide useful feedback across languages, even when the student makes mistakes in intermediate steps. We observe a phenomenon we call Teacher Drift: as these mistakes accumulate, the teacher may follow the student's incorrect steps instead of correcting them. Our analysis shows that this shift tends to occur earlier in lower-resource languages. To account for these differences across languages, we propose Teachable On-Policy Self-Distillation (TOPSD), which determines where and whether teacher supervision should be applied to each reasoning trajectory. TOPSD uses teacher-student likelihood to estimate where drift begins and select a prefix for supervision. It then verifies whether the teacher can recover the correct answer from that prefix, applying distillation only to prefixes that pass verification. Experiments on multilingual mathematical reasoning across ten languages and three model scales show that TOPSD consistently outperforms conventional OPSD, particularly in low- and medium-resource languages.

</details>


### [138] [Beyond K/V Caches: Cross-Model State Handoff in Hybrid Linear-Attention LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08133)
*Jiacheng Cai,Tao Zhang,Siqi Liang,Yun Dai,Ziqian Zeng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Hybrid State Transfer (HST)，在异构模型间迁移FA缓存、递归状态(REC)与卷积历史(Conv)三类状态，实现长对话中无重复prefill的模型切换，达到89.88%/94.11%任务保持率与约21.7×加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产部署常通过级联、路由与长对话中切换模型来平衡成本与质量，但上下文跨轮累积，每次切换都需目标模型对全部上下文重新prefill，代价高昂。已有方法只迁移全注意力(FA)的K/V缓存；而混合线性注意力模型还保留递归状态与卷积历史，Conv与REC相互耦合使跨模型状态迁移复杂化：Conv扰动会改变循环层中的key/value并影响REC更新，Conv历史被覆盖后REC变化仍会影响后续输出，且更精确的REC重建未必带来更好的续写质量。

Method: 提出HST，统一处理FA缓存、REC与Conv三类状态：用结构化REC映射进行状态重建；Conv对齐时保持历史顺序；利用冻结目标模型在续写阶段的输出校准适配器；FA缓存映射的更新离线合并。

Result: 在五个基准上，Qwen3.5 4B→27B与9B→27B平均任务保持率分别为89.88%和94.11%；在源缓存已可用时，合成16K输入上的独立计时显示交接主体分别获得21.66×与21.91×加速。

Conclusion: HST表明混合线性注意力模型间可实现高效状态级交接，在几乎不损失任务性能的前提下大幅消除重复prefill开销，适用于级联、路由与长对话切换等生产场景。

Abstract: Production deployments often use different models to balance cost and quality through cascading, routing, and switching models during long conversations. Context accumulates across turns. Each switch requires the target to repeat prefill over this context. Existing methods transfer full-attention (FA) K/V caches across models. Hybrid linear-attention models also retain recurrent states (REC) and convolution histories (Conv). The interaction between Conv and REC complicates cross-model state transfer. Conv perturbations can change keys and values in recurrent layers and alter REC updates. Conv histories are overwritten, but changes in REC can still affect later outputs. More accurate REC reconstruction need not improve continuation. Building on this, we propose Hybrid State Transfer (HST) for all three state types. It uses structured REC maps and preserves history order during Conv alignment. We calibrate the adapters using the frozen target's outputs during continuation. FA cache map updates are merged offline. Across five benchmarks, Qwen3.5 4B$\rightarrow$27B and 9B$\rightarrow$27B achieve mean task retentions of 89.88% and 94.11%, respectively.
