With source caches already available, separate timing on synthetic 16K inputs yields handoff body speedups of $21.66\times$ and $21.91\times$.

</details>


### [139] [ReviewMap: Mapping Evaluative Acts and Automation Boundaries in Peer-Review Comments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07489)
*Kihyun Na,Injung Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 ReviewMap 框架，将同行评审意见显式区分为"评估（assessment）"与"校准（calibration）"两类评价行为，并测量 LLM 识别这两类行为的可靠性，界定出人机协作的自动化边界，并据此提出三项应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究未区分评审意见中"论文做了什么（评估主张与学术标准）"和"为何重要（贡献价值与会议契合度校准）"这两种不同的评价行为。作者认为，显式区分二者并测量 LLM 识别的可靠性，是人类审查评审判断及其依据的前提，也为 NLP 在同行评审中确立比"全自动评审"更诚实的使命。

Method: 提出 ReviewMap 框架：第一层将每条评论分类为"评估"或"校准"行为，第二层按行为类型条件性地施加更细粒度属性。在 500 篇 ICLR 2026 论文上采用多模型 LLM 共识标注，并在 470 条开发集评论上做五人人工验证，以操作化准则（模型间 κ≥0.75）衡量可靠性。

Result: 行为层（act layer）可被可靠自动化（κ≥0.75），属性层（attribute layer）则需人工监督，由此界定出一条可测量的自动化边界。语料层面发现：17% 的审稿人-论文实例不含任何面板编码的校准；校准在 Strengths 中的占比约为 Weaknesses 中的 3 倍。

Conclusion: 区分并可靠识别评估与校准行为具有可行性，但自动化存在明确边界。该边界支撑三类应用：面向审稿人的"先评估后校准"工作流指南、面向作者的逐条 rebuttal 策略脚手架、面向领域主席的讨论阶段仪表盘。

Abstract: Peer-review comments check what a paper does and judge why it matters, but no existing scheme distinguishes these two evaluative acts. We argue that making these acts explicit and measuring how reliably LLMs identify them is a prerequisite for responsible LLM-assisted review analysis: humans need to inspect review judgments and their grounds. This defines a more honest mission for NLP in peer review than full automation. We present ReviewMap, a framework that classifies each comment as assessment of claims and scholarly standards or calibration of contribution value and venue fit, with finer-grained attributes applied conditionally by act type. Applied to 500 ICLR~2026 papers through multi-model LLM consensus, the act layer proves reliably automatable under the operational guideline (inter-model $κ\geq 0.75$), supported by five-human validation on 470 development comments, while the attribute layer requires human oversight, defining a measured automation boundary. At the corpus level, 17% of reviewer-paper instances contain no panel-coded calibration, and the panel-coded calibration share is nearly $3\times$ as high in Strengths as in Weaknesses. This boundary informs three affordances: a review guideline that operationalizes the assessment-then-calibration workflow for reviewers, a per-comment rebuttal strategy scaffold for authors, and a discussion-phase dashboard for area chairs.

</details>


### [140] [Recursive Self-Improvement for Long-Horizon Role-Playing Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07469)
*Y. L. Li,Y. H. Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一个有界递归自我改进（RSI）框架，通过版本化更新器迭代修订角色记忆、人格指令与轮级提示策略，显著降低长程角色扮演对话的不良回复率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程角色扮演对话需同时满足角色身份一致性、可靠记忆与情境敏感交互，现有方法难以在长对话中维持这些要素。

Method: 将有界RSI形式化为带显式输入、可编辑字段、验收门与停止条件的版本化策略；更新器修订三类对象：原角色记忆、稳定人格指令、轮级提示构建策略，并用修复结果反哺其诊断规则、编辑路由与候选选择。

Result: 内部评测覆盖50个原创角色×5个模型，每对2组100轮对话（每条件500组对话）；不良回复率由14.00%(6,973/49,824)降至4.94%(2,459/49,793)，绝对降9.06个百分点、相对降64.71%；600次盲测人工对比中294胜187平119负，判定性比较胜率71.19%。

Conclusion: 结果支持完整框架在内部测试分布上提升整体对话质量，但结论限于该内部分布。

Abstract: Long-horizon role-playing dialogue requires consistent character identity, reliable memory, and context-sensitive interaction. We present a bounded recursive self-improvement (RSI) framework that revises original-character memory, stable persona instructions, and a policy for constructing turn-level prompts. Repair outcomes also inform revisions to the updater's diagnostic rules, edit routing, and candidate selection. We specify the updater as a versioned policy with explicit inputs, editable fields, acceptance gates, and stopping conditions. An internal evaluation covers 50 original characters and five models, with two 100-round conversations per character-model pair: 500 conversations per condition. Across the pooled evaluated replies, the bad-response rate decreases from 14.00% (6,973/49,824) to 4.94% (2,459/49,793), a 9.06-percentage-point reduction and a 64.71% relative reduction. In 600 blinded human comparisons, RSI receives 294 wins, 187 ties, and 119 losses, yielding a 71.19% win rate among decisive judgments. The results support improved aggregate dialogue quality for the complete framework on the internal test distribution.

</details>


### [141] [Adapting LLM Judges to Observed Human Annotation Policy through Soft Prompt Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07126)
*Tamás Janusko,Sven Kleinkop,Ricardo Bochnia,Gunnar Hempel,Steffen Tomschke,Jürgen Anke,Maik Thiele*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出用混合软提示微调（文本提示+可学习连续提示嵌入）对齐开源LLM评审器与人类标注策略，在FairBotBench上训练，于偏见、毒性、个性化三维度评估，一致率稳定提升，Qwen 3.5 9B微调后在三维度全面超越托管参考评审器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专有模型作为自动评审器与人类一致率高，但成本高、难以实际部署；轻量开源评审器成本友好但与人评一致率偏低。作者希望在不改变模型权重的前提下缩小这一差距。

Method: 参数高效的软提示微调：在FairBotBench基准上训练软提示，并将文本提示（textual prompt）与学习到的连续提示嵌入（continuous prompt embeddings）组合成"混合软提示"；随后在偏见（bias）、毒性（toxicity）、个性化（personalization）三个维度上评估适配后评审器与人类标注的一致率，并与未微调基线对比。

Result: 混合软提示微调相对对应未微调基线在三个评估维度上均持续提升与人类标注的一致率；对Qwen 3.5 9B，适配后的评审器在全部三个维度上同时超过两个托管（hosted）参考评审器。

Conclusion: 软提示调优可作为低成本、可落地的开源LLM评审器对齐手段，混合式（文本+连续嵌入）设计优于纯基线；轻量开源模型经此类参数高效适配后可达到甚至超越专有评审器的一致率水平，具备实际部署价值。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly employed as automated judges for evaluating chatbot responses. While proprietary models can achieve high agreement with human annotators, they remain costly and difficult to deploy in practical settings. Lightweight open-source LLM judges provide an attractive alternative but typically exhibit lower agreement with human ratings. In this paper, we investigate whether soft prompt tuning can align open-source LLM judges more closely with human annotation policy. We train soft prompts on the FairBotBench benchmark and evaluate the adapted judges across the dimensions of bias, toxicity, and personalization. Our findings demonstrate that hybrid soft-prompt tuning, which combines textual prompts with learned continuous prompt embeddings, consistently improves agreement with human annotations over the corresponding untuned baselines. For Qwen 3.5 9B, the adapted judges surpass both hosted reference judges across all three evaluation dimensions.

</details>


### [142] [Consistent yet Contextual: Learning Author-Level Mixtures of Style for Authorship Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07111)
*Omkar Yadav,Michael Jiang,Kenan Alkiek,Jian Zhu,David Jurgens*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文提出对作者本人建模（允许一个作者拥有多种一致但不同的写作风格），显著提升作者验证/归属性能，并系统考察作者写作何时最具"个人惯用风格"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有作者验证与归属方法主要建模文档风格，判断两篇文档是否同一作者，但普遍忽略对作者本身的建模以及作者风格的多样性程度。

Method: 提出多种作者表征建模方案，核心是让作者表征捕捉多个一致但彼此可区分的写作风格（多风格作者模型）；并基于该模型在四个场景下分析作者风格的"个人惯用性"（idiolectality）：(i) 写作受众规模变化、(ii) 距模型训练时间的时间跨度、(iii) 前文语境的影响、(iv) 文类规范对风格的影响。

Result: 多风格作者表征带来显著性能提升；四项实验均显示不同作者在写作的个人惯用程度上差异显著，某些设定下文本对"谁写的"具有更强信息量。

Conclusion: 作者应以多风格方式建模，而非单一风格向量；该结论对作者验证与归属的建模方式及数据选择均有明确启示。

Abstract: Approaches to authorship verification or attribution typically model the style of a document, asking whether two documents were likely written by the same author. While recent approaches have been performant, such approaches largely ignore modeling the authors themselves and how varied their style is. Here, we introduce multiple alternatives for modeling the author and demonstrate that allowing an author's representation to capture multiple consistent but distinct writing styles yields significant performance improvements. Using our new authorship model, we then examine when an author writes in a way that is most idiomatic of their style across four scenarios: (i) variation in the size of their writing audiences, (ii) temporal distance from when the model was trained, (iii) the effect of preceding context, and (iv) the effect of genre norms on style. Across all four experiments, we demonstrate that authors vary substantially in how idiolectic their writing is. Our results have clear implications for both the modeling and data used for authorship verification and attribution, showing that authors need to be represented with their multiple styles and that some settings produce text that is much more informative about who wrote that text.

</details>


### [143] [Reclassification Is All You Missed: Surfacing Variant Reinterpretation Need in Online Health Communities at Population Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07054)
*Kamalakkannan Ravi,Mengshu Nie,Annapurna Poduri,Cong Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 构建PRISM——一个基于大语言模型的智能体流水线，在无接触的"干跑"模式下筛查在线健康社区帖子，识别与基因变异重解读相关的讨论、核查变异后续是否被重新分类，并起草带免责声明的联络消息；由此绘制出人群尺度的重解读需求图谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ClinVar中的基因变异常在被检测后发生重新分类（有时由"意义不明"变为"致病"），但患者重新联络（recontact）实施不一致，责任在实验室、临床医生与患者之间分散、缺乏明确界定。同时，许多受影响人群在公共在线健康社区讨论其检测结果，而临床重新联络机制往往覆盖不到这些人群，导致重解读信息与其临床意义之间存在可及性缺口。

Method: 提出PRISM：一个智能体式（agentic）大语言模型流水线，流程为（1）筛查在线健康社区帖子；（2）核查帖中涉及的变异此后是否在ClinVar等数据库中发生重新分类；（3）起草带免责声明的外联联络消息。研究在"无接触"（no-contact dry run）设定下运行，即不实际联系用户。评估覆盖32个社区共390万条帖子，并在30条真实帖子草稿的留出集上由临床医生进行事实准确性与可发送性的双维度评判。

Result: 经智能体判定的、与重解读相关的讨论占比很低（0.288%），但高度集中于专科遗传学社区（6.30%，而一般医疗咨询类社区为0.10%），且自2014年以来增长约五倍；PRISM共识别出123个候选分类差异，其中5个属于从"意义不明"变为"致病/可能致病"。在30条真实帖子草稿的留出集上，临床医生判定27条事实有据、15条适合发送，说明事实准确性是临床可接受外联的必要而非充分条件。

Conclusion: 公共在线健康社区中存在真实且快速增长的变异重解读信息需求，且高度集中于遗传学专科场景；大模型智能体流水线可在无接触、带免责的前提下规模化筛查并辅助起草患者外联信息，但事实准确性不足以保证临床可接受性，仍需临床审核环节。PRISM提供了人群尺度的重解读需求图谱，代码与数据将于论文发表时公开。

Abstract: Genetic variants are routinely reclassified in ClinVar after a patient is tested, sometimes from uncertain to pathogenic, yet patient recontact is inconsistent, with no clear duty and responsibility diffused among labs, providers, and patients. Many affected people discuss their results in public online health communities that clinical recontact often does not reach. We built PRISM, an agentic large-language-model pipeline that screens these posts, checks whether the variant was later reclassified, and drafts a disclaimer-bearing outreach message, in a no-contact dry run. Across 3.9 million posts from 32 communities, agent-predicted reinterpretation-relevant discussion is rare (0.288%) but concentrated in specialist genetics communities (6.30% versus 0.10% in general medical-advice communities) and growing fivefold since 2014; PRISM surfaces 123 candidate classification differences, 5 of them from an uncertain submission to pathogenic or likely pathogenic. On a held-out set of 30 real-post drafts, a clinician judged 27 factually grounded and 15 fit to send: factual accuracy is necessary but not sufficient for clinically acceptable outreach. PRISM contributes a population-scale reinterpretation-need map with code and data to be released upon publication.

</details>


### [144] [Evaluating Token Probabilities as Confidence Estimators in Human Behavior Simulations: A Case Study in Luxembourgish Next Like Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07735)
*Nils Schwager,Simon Münker,Alistair Plum,Achim Rettinger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文主张在噪声数字痕迹数据上用LLM模拟人类行为时，仅用准确率评估不够，必须同时考察校准性。作者以RTL.lu的"下一个点赞预测"为案例，发现仅用上下文学习的开源模型过度自信且不如简单统计基线；从3B扩到8B准确率上升但校准变差；任务特定监督微调可将ECE从0.2降至0.006、准确率从0.536升至0.758。结论：LLM智能体的置信估计应与准确率一并评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究评估LLM模拟人类行为的"操作效度"时普遍只看准确率，隐含假设人类行为是确定性的；但在噪声大的数字痕迹数据上，预测本身存在固有不确定性，因此模型的置信度是否可靠（校准）同样决定其效度，而这一点被忽视。

Method: 构建"下一个点赞预测"案例任务：给定用户在卢森堡新闻平台RTL.lu上的历史交互，预测其对某条评论的评分；将开源模型（Ministral 3B、8B）的token概率作为置信度估计，与简单统计基线在准确率和校准（ECE）两维度对比，并检验模型规模、行为历史长度及任务特定监督微调的影响。

Result: 仅用上下文学习时，测试模型过度自信，准确率与校准均劣于简单统计基线；准确率与校准不同步——3B→8B提升准确率但恶化校准，更长的行为历史提升准确率而置信度下降；任务特定监督微调是有效的校准干预，ECE由0.2降至0.006，准确率由0.536升至0.758。

Conclusion: 准确率与校准并非一致变动，规模扩展与更多上下文不能保证置信估计可靠，任务特定微调可同时改善二者。因此在使用LLM智能体模拟人类行为时，应将置信估计（校准）与准确率并列评估以提升操作效度。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to simulate human behavior, their operational validity is typically assessed through accuracy alone, implicitly treating human behavior as deterministic. We argue that on noisy digital trace data, a model's performance depends not only on predicting the correct outcome but on its calibration. We present a case study testing whether the token probabilities of open-weight LLMs can serve as confidence estimates in a Next Like Prediction task: predicting how a user of the Luxembourgish news platform RTL.lu rates a comment, conditioned on their prior interactions. Evaluating Ministral 3 (3B, 8B), we find that, with in-context learning only, the tested models are overconfident and are outperformed on accuracy and calibration by a simple statistical baseline. Accuracy and calibration do not move together: scaling from 3B to 8B improves accuracy while degrading calibration, and longer behavioral histories raise accuracy while confidence declines. Task-specific supervised fine-tuning acts as an effective calibration intervention, reducing Expected Calibration Error from 0.2 to 0.006 while improving accuracy from 0.536 to 0.758. We conclude that confidence estimation should be evaluated alongside accuracy to improve validity when using LLM-based agents.

</details>


### [145] [O$^2$PD: Overlap-Gated On-Policy Distillation on Reliable Prefix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07731)
*Shuaishuai Zu,Jihao Zhao,Pengfei Li,Guanghuan Fang,Ben Feng,Yuhan Liu,Biao Qin,Baochuan Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Overlap-gated On-Policy Distillation (O²PD)，通过仅在学生轨迹前20%前缀上对齐、并设计支持集策略与重叠门控 α-散度，解决 on-policy 蒸馏中"对齐哪些 token"与"如何对齐"两个问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 top-k OPD 存在两点不足：(1) 教师对学生生成的 rollout 进行打分时本质上是 off-policy 的，其下一 token 指导的可靠性存疑；(2) 仅在教师 top-k 支持集上计算 RKL 会丢弃支撑集之外的剩余概率质量。由此引出两个隐含问题：应对齐哪些 token，以及如何对齐这些 token。

Method: 1) 定量分析教师监督的可靠性，发现学生轨迹前缀上的对齐比全上下文更可靠，故 O²PD 仅作用于每条 rollout 的 20% 前缀 token。2) 从理论上刻画 top-k OPD 的两个问题：Teacher-support Uncovering 与 Dominant-Mode Mismatch。3) 提出两种支持集策略：Reset Support（为每个 token 附加 reset atom，汇聚教师 top-k 之外的 mass）与 Support Convergence（将支持集收敛到已对齐的交集）。4) 采用重叠门控的 α-散度，在 mode-covering 与 mode-seeking 之间逐 token 插值，以重叠度作为门控切换支持集并自适应调节 α。

Result: 摘要未给出具体实验数值结果；方法层面通过前缀对齐提升监督可靠性，并通过 reset atom 回收 top-k 外质量、以重叠门控 α-散度缓解 Teacher-support Uncovering 与 Dominant-Mode Mismatch。

Conclusion: O²PD 统一回答了 OPD 中"对齐哪些 token"（前缀子集）与"如何对齐"（支持集策略 + 重叠门控 α-散度）两个问题，是对 top-k OPD 在可靠性与支持集覆盖上的系统性改进。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) supplies dense token-level supervision by aligning a student to the teacher's next-token distribution on its own generated rollouts. Existing works mostly implement top-$k$ OPD, applying reverse Kullback-Leibler ($\RKL$) divergence between the teacher's and the student's renormalized distributions over teacher top-$k$ support. However, the teacher scores student-generated rollouts that are inherently off-policy for itself, leaving the reliability of its next-token guidance unclear. Restricting the divergence to the teacher top-$k$ support also discards the remaining mass outside that support. These gaps leave two questions implicit: 1) which tokens to align, and 2) how to align those tokens. To answer these questions, we propose Overlap-gated On-Policy Distillation (O$^{2}$PD). As for 1), we quantitatively analyze the reliability of teacher supervision and find that alignment on the student's trajectory prefix is more reliable than alignment on the full context. Therefore, our O$^{2}$PD is applied only to the 20\% prefix tokens of each student rollout. As for 2), we theoretically characterize two problems of top-$k$ OPD: the \emph{Teacher-support Uncovering} and the \emph{Dominant-Mode Mismatch}. Based on these analyses, we propose two support strategies: the \emph{Reset Support} strategy attaches a reset atom to every token, pooling mass outside the teacher top-$k$, and the \emph{Support Convergence} strategy converges the support to the already aligned intersection. Our O$^{2}$PD employs the designed overlap-gated $α$-divergence to interpolate between mode-covering and mode-seeking for per-token alignment, where the overlap is used as a gate to switch the support and adaptively modulate $α$.

</details>


### [146] [Enhancing Repository-Level Code Completion with Reinforcement Learning in Real-World Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07510)
*Zhenlong Dai,Zhuoluo Zhao,Xiu Tang,Sai Wu,Chang Yao,Zhipeng Gao,Jingyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种面向仓库级代码补全的强化学习框架，通过标识符驱动的意图识别、奖励驱动的补全学习和选择性探索策略，提升Code LLM对文件内外上下文及跨文件依赖的理解与推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Code LLM在仓库级代码补全中难以从仓库中聚焦合适上下文，且缺乏对跨文件依赖的深度推理能力。

Method: 构建强化学习框架：(1) 标识符驱动的意图识别，捕获补全意图以理解文件内与跨文件上下文；(2) 奖励驱动的补全学习，在复杂仓库场景中有效施加奖励以增强跨文件推理；(3) 选择性探索策略，引导模型聚焦低置信度、高奖励的token，探索有价值但未被充分探索的补全模式。

Result: 实验结果表明该方法显著提升了Code LLM的性能，代码与数据集已开源。

Conclusion: 该强化学习框架能有效改善仓库级代码补全中上下文聚焦和跨文件依赖推理的问题，为真实软件开发场景下的Code LLM应用提供可行方案。

Abstract: The rapid development of large language models (LLMs) for code, which closely aligns with the practical needs of real-world software development, has sparked growing attention in repository-level code completion. However, existing Code LLMs present challenges in focusing on suitable contexts from the repository and in deeply reasoning about cross-file dependencies. To address these challenges, we propose a novel reinforcement learning framework for repository-level code completion. To better understand both in-file and cross-file contexts, we employ identifier-driven intent recognition to capture completion intent, thereby improving the model's performance in real-world scenarios. To enhance cross-file reasoning, we propose a reward-driven completion learning to effectively reward in complex repository completion scenarios. To guide LLMs in completing code that aligns with intended functionality and repository context, we introduce a selective-exploration strategy that directs the model to focus on low-confidence, high-reward tokens, promoting the exploration of valuable, underexplored completion patterns. Experimental results show that our approach significantly improves the performance of Code LLMs. The code and the dataset are available at https://github.com/zhenlongDai/RepoCompletion.

</details>


### [147] [How Do AI Agents Waste Your Money?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07238)
*Huimin Zeng,Yuxuan Lu,Jesse Cai,Eric Hanchen Jiang,Di Wu,Po-Nien Kung,Wenbo Hu,Ziyi Wang,Dakuo Wang,Jiaxin Pei,Kai-Wei Chang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出统一框架测量异构智能体系统中的token浪费：记录并归一化完整轨迹，用LLM-as-a-Judge识别可移除片段并映射回轨迹做token级量化，再通过直接干预（删除片段后恢复执行）验证浪费的有效性。在线上剪枝实验中移除41–46%的推理内容而基本保持任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作显示智能体token成本异常高，但缺乏系统化识别与度量token浪费的方法，无法区分"用量大"与"真正冗余"。

Method: 统一框架三步：1）记录并归一化完整智能体轨迹；2）用LLM-as-a-Judge识别可移除文本片段；3）将片段映射回原轨迹进行token级测量。并通过删除标记片段、恢复执行、观察任务结果与后续动作变化来做干预式验证。在此基础上对多种模型、任务与harness配置进行对比分析。

Result: 在线剪枝实验中，在两个不同智能体基准上移除41–46%的模型推理内容，任务性能基本保持。分析发现：(1)浪费在任务与智能体设置间的差异大于模型间差异；(2)显式推理与可见执行呈现不同浪费模式；(3)相同模型与任务下，不同harness引入不同低效来源。浪费具有聚合token用量无法揭示的系统级结构。

Conclusion: 该框架把token浪费转化为可操作的优化信号，可用于训练更高效模型、设计更优推理策略与优化智能体harness，目标从"降低token用量"转向"减少不必要的计算"。局限在于LLMaJ判定的可靠性依赖验证干预，且浪费结构随任务/harness变化，需要逐场景分析。

Abstract: AI agents may consume massive tokens across a wide range of tasks, but how many of those tokens are really necessary? Although prior work has shown that agents can incur unexpectedly high token costs, we still lack a systematic way to identify and measure token waste. In this work, we introduce a unified framework for measuring token waste across heterogeneous agent systems. Our framework records and normalizes complete agent trajectories, identifies removable spans using an LLM-as-a-Judge (LLMaJ), and maps the identified text spans back to the original trajectory for token-level measurement. We validate the identified waste through direct interventions by removing marked spans, resuming agent execution, and measuring changes in task outcomes and subsequent actions. In online pruning experiments, we remove 41-46% of the model's reasoning while largely preserving task performance across two distinct agent benchmarks. Using this framework, we analyze agents across multiple settings. Our analysis shows that token waste has a system-level structure that aggregate token usage alone cannot reveal. First, waste varies more across tasks and agent settings than across models. Second, reasoning and visible execution can exhibit different waste patterns. Third, different harnesses can introduce distinct sources of inefficiency even under the same model and task. Overall, our framework transforms token waste into an actionable optimization signal, enabling researchers and practitioners to train more efficient models, design better reasoning strategies, and optimize agent harnesses toward reducing unnecessary computation rather than merely reducing token usage.

</details>


### [148] [Linguistic boundaries emerge from pressure-constrained processing dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08085)
*Rui He,Philipp Homan,Wolfram Hinzen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出语言边界（如句界）源于三种加工需求的交互：维持未解决的依存关系、整合已完成的语义关系、更新语义语境；结构上的完句允许但不确定边界关闭。生成式模拟、人类听者边界判断、自然阅读实验与大语言模型表征分析共同支持这一连续加工动力学观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类认知把连续经验切分为有界单元又保持其连续性，语言同样被组织为词、短语等有界结构单元，句子是语法构造过程的最终边界。作者追问这类边界如何涌现，并希望超越纯句法/韵律解释，从实时加工动力学角度给出统一说明。

Method: (1) 生成式模拟：不预设边界位置与成分标签，由"未解决依存负荷、整合成本、语义语境更新"三种需求驱动，检验能否产生类句子的段长分布与特征时间尺度；(2) 人类听者边界标记任务：考察各加工指标能否在句法与韵律线索之外预测边界选择；(3) 自然自定步速阅读：检验句末减速与加工动力学变化的一致性；(4) 用大语言模型处理同一叙事文本，分析句末隐藏状态几何（局部内在维度、轨迹平直度、表征更新幅度）及与阅读时间、下一词惊讶度的关系。

Result: 模拟产生了类句子的段长分布与不同特征时间尺度；边界处未解决依存负荷下降、整合成本出现峰值、累积压力重置。听者更倾向在未解决依存更少、整合成本与语义失配更高、且前段记忆负荷升高处标记边界，且该效应超出句法与韵律线索。自然阅读中句末减速与记忆负荷下降、整合成本与语义失配上升相吻合。大语言模型在句末表现出局部内在维度升高、隐藏状态轨迹更平直，随后表征更新更大；边界对阅读时间与隐藏状态几何的解释力在加工需求之外仍有增量，但对下一词惊讶度解释力很有限。

Conclusion: 语言边界可理解为连续加工动力学中状态转换的离散显现：结构完句只是允许而非决定边界关闭，边界由依存维持、关系整合与语境更新三类需求的交互所涌现；跨模拟、行为与模型表征的多层证据支持这一统一解释。

Abstract: Human cognition organizes continuous experience into bounded units while maintaining continuity across them. Linguistic elements are also organized into bounded structural units such as words and phrases, with sentence marking the final bound of a grammatical construction process. How do such boundaries emerge? We propose boundaries emerge from interacting demands to maintain unresolved dependencies, integrate completed relations, and update semantic context, with structural completion allowing but not determining closure. First, in a generative simulation without predefined boundary positions or constituent labels, these demands produced sentence-like segment-length distributions and distinct characteristic timescales, with structural completion allowing but not determining closure. Near boundaries, unresolved dependency load declined, integration cost peaked, and accumulated pressure reset. Then, we tested whether same demands predicted human listeners' boundary choices during comprehension. Listeners were more likely to mark boundaries with fewer unresolved dependencies, higher integration cost and semantic mismatch, and elevated preceding memory load, beyond syntactic and prosodic cues. Third, in naturalistic self-paced reading, sentence-final slowing coincided with declining memory load and increasing integration cost and semantic mismatch, broadly consistent with the simulation. Processing same narratives with a large language model revealed increased local intrinsic dimensionality and straighter hidden-state trajectories at sentence endings, followed by larger representational updates. Boundary explained additional variation in reading time and hidden-state geometry beyond processing demands, but considerably less in next-token surprisal. Together, these findings suggest linguistic boundaries emerge as discrete manifestations of transitions in continuous processing dynamics.

</details>


### [149] [AraEngage: Interpretable Linguistic Correlates of Engagement in Arabic Facebook News](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07926)
*Shimaa Ibrahim,Nour Elhouda Dalloua,Md. Rafiul Biswas,Wajdi Zaghouani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建阿拉伯语社交媒体互动数据集 AraEngage（10,000 条 Facebook 新闻帖，2,451 个页面，57 国，2023–2024），结合可解释语言学特征分析粉丝归一化互动量的相关因素，并做语言学审计揭示无标音阿拉伯语中词典特征的测量误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 英文互动特征的直接移植忽略了无标音（unvocalized）阿拉伯语的词形歧义，使词汇特征所测量的构念发生偏移；需检验哪些可解释阿拉伯语指标与互动相关，以及这些关联在发布者语境、互动定义与词汇歧义下是否可信。

Method: 构建查询定义的抽样数据集 AraEngage，配对互动计数与可解释语言学特征；以随机森林（帖子级划分，R²=0.38）建模，用 SHAP 解释；并进行语言学审计，检查命令式列表同形异义（"express!"/"via"）、模糊限制语列表（qad+未完成体"may" vs qad+完成体"has indeed"）与宗教词表（虔敬套语与政治新闻地名混用）。

Result: 宗教表达、消息长度、句长与词汇密度为主要相关因素；与英文新闻不同，正面情感在本样本中与更高互动相关；存在"长度悖论"：未调整的原始互动对长帖最高，而模型对粉丝归一化互动的归因偏向短帖；命令式与模糊限制语关联接近零，可能因测量误差而被衰减。

Conclusion: 所有结论均为观察性关联而非因果；标准英文词典特征在无标音阿拉伯语中脆弱，需针对同形异义与构式歧义精化词表。作者公开派生特征、互动计数、精化词表与分析代码，依 CrowdTangle 条款不再分发原始帖子。

Abstract: Engagement features developed for English are routinely applied to Arabic, but unvocalized Arabic changes what a lexical feature measures. We introduce AraEngage, a query-defined sample of 10,000 public Arabic Facebook news posts from 2,451 pages in 57 countries (2023-2024) that pairs engagement counts with interpretable linguistic features. We use it to examine which interpretable Arabic indicators are associated with follower-normalized engagement, and how far those associations can be trusted given publisher context, the definition of engagement, and lexical ambiguity. Religious expressions, message length, sentence length, and lexical density are the leading correlates in a Random Forest model ($R^2$ = 0.38 on a post-level split) interpreted with SHAP. Unlike English news, positive sentiment is associated with higher engagement in this Arabic news sample. Message length shows a length paradox: unadjusted raw engagement is highest for long posts, whereas the model's attributions for follower-normalized engagement favor short posts. A linguistic audit identifies why standard lexicon features are fragile in unvocalized Arabic: imperative lists contain homographs such as the form read as "express!" that is also the frequent preposition "via", hedge lists conflate the possibility marker qad + imperfect ("may") with the emphatic qad + perfect ("has indeed"), and religious lists mix devotional formulas with place names in political news. These are plausible sources of measurement error that may attenuate the near-zero associations of imperatives and hedges. All findings are observational associations with operationalized indicators. We release the derived linguistic features, engagement counts, refined lexicons, and analysis code; raw posts are not redistributed, in line with CrowdTangle terms.

</details>


### [150] [Towards Foundation Decision Models: Drex](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07854)
*Nischay Dhankhar,Alisher Amantay,Zhanibek Datbayev,Darkhan Akhmetov,Yash Shah,Nurmukhamed Ubaidullayev,Raman Saparkhan,Dos Baha,Abulhair Saparov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Drex 是一族开源决策模型（8B、9B、35B-MoE/3B激活、27B Max），输入状态与选项问题、直接输出选项概率分布，无需自回归解码；训练用SFT+RLAF（含视觉微调），在 Decision Index 0.4.1 上达 67.52 的 SOTA，延迟 183 ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归大模型（长链思维推理）虽准确率高，但生产中存在答案不一致、需解析输出、成本与延迟高等问题，作者希望用非生成式的决策模型替代。

Method: 定义“决策模型”范式：给定状态（文档/记录/智能体上下文，可选图像）与若干选项式问题（多选、是/否、有序打分），一次前向返回选项上的概率分布；训练结合监督微调与基于调整反馈的强化学习（RLAF），Max 额外做视觉微调；并用一致性损失提升对选项顺序的鲁棒性，通过消融验证。

Result: Decision Index 0.4.1 公开套件 67.52（对比 Jev 1.14.0 的 60.70）；Decision Bench v4 上与 d3-turbo、GPT-6 Sol 统计持平，落后 Claude Opus 5.5 约 1.6 分；DecideBench 上为决策模型中最高。典型请求 183 ms，远低于 GPT-6 Sol ~2.0 s 与 Claude Opus 5.5 ~3.5 s，且选项重排下输出稳定。

Conclusion: 决策模型范式可在保持竞争力的准确率的同时显著降低延迟与输出不确定性，四款模型全部开源。

Abstract: Autoregressive approaches with large language models, such as long chain-of-thought reasoning, have become the default for new AI applications because they reach higher accuracy on a wide range of tasks. In production, however, they give inconsistent answers, require parsing of outputs, and add cost and latency. Decision models address this: given a question and a set of options supplied by the user, they return a probability distribution over the options without autoregressive decoding. We present Drex, a family of four open decision models with 8B (DLM), 9B (v1.5), 35B with 3B active (v2.0), and 27B (Max) parameters. Each request consists of a state (a document, record, or agent context, optionally with images) and questions about it, each asking for a choice among options, a yes/no judgment, or a score on an ordered scale. Training combines supervised fine-tuning with reinforcement learning from adjusted feedback (RLAF), plus vision tuning for Max. Ablations show that RLAF improves accuracy and that consistency losses improve robustness to option order. On Decision Index 0.4.1, Max sets a new state of the art among the decision models we compare (67.52 on the public suite vs. 60.70 for Jev 1.14.0). On Decision Bench v4, it is statistically tied with d3-turbo and GPT-6 Sol and trails Claude Opus 5.5 by 1.6 points, and it has the highest accuracy among decision models on DecideBench. Max answers a typical request in 183 ms, against about 2.0 s for GPT-6 Sol and 3.5 s for Claude Opus 5.5, and its outputs are stable under option reordering. All four models are open source: https://github.com/nace-ai/drex-decision-models

</details>


### [151] [The Last Mile to the Real World: Agent-Native Primitives as Reusable Building Blocks for Solving Real-World Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07461)
*Haibo Jin,Peng Kuang,Xiaopeng Yuan,Lucheng Fu,Jerry Wang,Xucheng Yu,Yaoning Yu,Dehao Wu,Runguo Li,Colin Su,Jingxuan Sun,Xuyang Liu,Haojing Luo,Haohan Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 agent-native primitives（内嵌LLM的可复用资源单元）与 HEELO 框架，通过组合、验证、修正、入库的闭环按任务自动构建 harness，在二十余个基准与46个真实任务上显著提升完成率并降低时间与成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前沿模型及其自带 harness 在真实世界基准上仍表现不佳（Terminal-Bench 4.0 上 Codex 37.3%、Claude Code 51.8%）。作者将失败定位在"从触达资源到应用资源"这一步，即 last mile to the real world：现有方法只把资源放到手边，把应用能力留给调用方，导致每个 harness 都要重复重建这种能力。

Method: 核心抽象是 agent-native primitive：把资源与常驻 LLM 绑定，使其能在上下文中应用该资源并与其他 primitive 通信。框架 HEELO 从 primitive 库出发，为任务组合出特定图结构，依据环境证据验证结果，针对失败修订相关 primitive，并在验证通过后接纳新/改进的 primitive。实例化八大 primitive 家族，每类对应一种任务类型，并通过 MCP server 接入外部 agent。

Result: Dev-Primitives 将 Terminal-Bench 4.0 的解决率从 Codex 的 37.3% 提升至 55.5%（同一 backbone）；Harness Primitives 使 Terminal-Bench 2.0 Pass@1 从 60.0% 升至 72.2%；Tool Primitives 使 ToolBench 通过率从 55.8% 升至 75.1%；Navigation Primitives 使 GAIA-Text-103 准确率从 45.6% 升至 58.3%。heelo.dev 的 46 个真实任务上，两个 agent 独立完成各 9 个，接入 HEELO 后分别为 39 和 35 个，每完成任务时间减少 1.9×/2.3×，成本降低 1.55×。

Conclusion: 把"应用能力"封装进资源本身、并以可组合/可验证/可演进的 primitive 库自动构建 harness，是缩小 last mile 差距的有效路径；能力可跨模型与 harness 复用，并同时带来成功率、时延与成本收益。

Abstract: How can an agent handle a real-world task end to end? Frontier models with their own harnesses still fall short on real-world benchmarks: on Terminal-Bench 4.0, GPT-5.6 Sol with Codex resolves 37.3% of the tasks and Claude Opus 5 with Claude Code 51.8%. These tasks fail at the step from reaching a resource to applying it, which we call the last mile to the real world. Existing approaches put the resource within reach but leave the competence to apply it with the caller, so each harness rebuilds that competence. We introduce agent-native primitives, reusable units that equip a resource with a resident LLM that applies it in context and communicates with other primitives, and HEELO (Harness Engineering around Evolving Agent-Native Primitives for the Last Mile to Real-WOrld Tasks), a harness engineering framework that builds each task's harness from a library of such primitives. HEELO composes the primitives a task needs into a task-specific graph, verifies the result against evidence from the environment, revises the primitives a failure implicates, and admits new or improved primitives after validation. We instantiate HEELO with eight primitive families, each built for one kind of task, and evaluate them on more than twenty benchmarks. Dev-Primitives raise Terminal-Bench 4.0 resolution from 37.3% under Codex to 55.5% on the same backbone, Harness Primitives composed per task raise Terminal-Bench 2.0 Pass@1 from 60.0% for the seed harness to 72.2%, Tool Primitives raise ToolBench pass rate from 55.8% to 75.1%, and Navigation Primitives raise GAIA-Text-103 accuracy from 45.6% to 58.3%. On the 46 real-world tasks of our heelo.dev platform, Claude Opus 5.5 with Claude Code and GPT-6.1 Sol with Codex complete 9 each on their own and 39 and 35 when connected to HEELO through our MCP server, with 1.9$\times$ and 2.3$\times$ less time and 1.55$\times$ lower cost per completed task.

</details>


### [152] [Agent-R$^2$: Scaling Agents for Realistic Productivity Work via Real-Source Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08219)
*Teng Pan,Zixuan Wang,Yuchen Yan,Qifei Wu,Yao Chen,Yingqi Qu,Ruiqing Zhang,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 Agent-R² 真实来源数据合成框架，从 1 万条网页语料候选中蒸馏出 4k 个配对真实多格式附件与细粒度交付级评分标准的任务，训练 Agent-R²-4B，在 GDPval 与 OfficeQA 上超越 Qwen3-235B-A22B-Thinking-2507。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体难以完成真实生产力任务：需遵循长时程专业工作流、理解异构文件上下文、在细粒度要求下产出可用交付物。关键瓶颈是缺乏可扩展的后训练数据——缺少真实工作意图、真实文件环境与交付物级别的评测。

Method: Agent-R² 框架全程以真实职业种子、语料抽取的工作流证据、真实网页来源文件为落地基础（而非人工指定流程或 LLM 生成文件）；从 10k 网页语料候选中蒸馏 4k 任务实例，配真实多格式附件与细粒度交付级 rubric；用监督微调 + 基于 rubric 的强化学习训练 4B 模型。

Result: 仅用 4k 实例训练，Agent-R²-4B 在 GDPval（51.0% vs. 48.0%）与 OfficeQA（14.8% vs. 13.8%）上均超过 Qwen3-235B-A22B-Thinking-2507；同时提升端到端生产力表现与文件落地推理能力。

Conclusion: Agent-R² 缓解了智能体在充分落地、构建或验证前就产出表面合理交付物的“捷径行为”，促进持续探索；表明真实来源、可扩展的数据合成是小模型在生产力任务上超越大模型的有效路径。

Abstract: Large language models have made rapid progress in agentic reasoning and tool use, raising expectations for agents that can take over realistic productivity work from humans. However, current agents still struggle to finish such tasks, where success requires following long-horizon professional workflows, understanding heterogeneous file contexts, and producing usable deliverables under fine-grained requirements. A key bottleneck is the lack of scalable post-training data that reflects real work intent, authentic file environments, and deliverable-level evaluation. To close this gap, we introduce Agent-R$^2$, a $\textbf{r}$eal-source data synthesis framework that scales training data for $\textbf{r}$ealistic productivity work. Instead of relying on manually specified workflows or LLM-generated files, Agent-R$^2$ grounds synthesis throughout the pipeline in real occupational seeds, corpus-derived workflow evidence, and authentic web-sourced files. From 10k web-corpus candidates, Agent-R$^2$ distills 4k task instances that pair real multi-format attachments with fine-grained, deliverable-level rubrics. On this data, we train Agent-R$^2$-4B via supervised fine-tuning and rubric-based reinforcement learning, improving both end-to-end productivity performance and file-grounded reasoning. Trained on only 4k instances, Agent-R$^2$-4B surpasses Qwen3-235B-A22B-Thinking-2507 on both GDPval (51.0$\%$ vs. 48.0$\%$) and OfficeQA (14.8$\%$ vs. 13.8$\%$). Further analysis shows that Agent-R$^2$ mitigates shortcut behavior, where agents produce superficially plausible deliverables before sufficiently grounding, constructing, or verifying them, while promoting sustained exploration.

</details>


### [153] [Cultural Adaptation within Repeated Social Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08334)
*Chen Cecilia Liu,Roi Reichart,Anna Korhonen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将LLM文化适应重构为通过社交互动进行规范同化的连续过程，并在沙盒环境中验证记忆机制能支撑跨重复交互的纵向学习、跨社交场景迁移与五语种规范同化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类文化大量是默会的，通常通过情境化社交互动习得；现有评估多基于问答任务，只能测"知道什么"，无法测"如何适应"。因此需从知识考察转向适应能力考察，探索LLM能否在交互中持续进行文化适应。

Method: 构建沙盒环境，让LLM作为交互智能体在重复社交互动中学习默会文化规范；将文化适应重新定义为规范同化的连续过程，并引入/考察记忆机制对纵向学习的作用，同时测试其向不同社交场景的迁移能力及五种语言下的多语规范同化。

Result: 记忆机制可促进跨重复交互的纵向学习，支持向不同社交场景的迁移，并实现五种语言的多语规范同化。

Conclusion: 研究为LLM的持续、交互驱动的文化适应奠定基础，表明评估应从静态文化知识转向动态适应能力。

Abstract: Much of human culture is tacit and is typically learned through situated social interaction. As LLMs are deployed as interactive agents across diverse communities, assessing their cultural competence demands moving beyond existing question-answering approaches, shifting from what they know to how they adapt. In this work, we broaden the scope of cultural adaptation in NLP by investigating whether LLMs can adapt through repeated interactions via a sandbox environment. By reframing cultural adaptation as a continuous process of norm assimilation through social interaction, our work demonstrates that memory mechanisms foster longitudinal learning across repeated interactions, facilitate transfer to different social settings, and enable multilingual norm assimilation across five languages. Together, our findings lay the groundwork for continuous, interaction-driven cultural adaptation in LLMs.

</details>


### [154] [Polarity Classification with Multitask Learning Syntax Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08323)
*Muhammad Imran,Carlos Gómez-Rodríguez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文在统一多任务学习框架下比较两种将句法知识注入神经情感分析模型的策略（Standard-MTL 与 Contrastive-MTL），基于四种序列标注编码在7种语言11个数据集上验证：相对编码下的 Contrastive-MTL 在11个中的10个数据集优于无句法基线，且全面优于推理时解析的 SELSP 并快1.5–9倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 词典方法透明但难以处理否定与强化等语言现象；将句法解析引入SA流程可缓解该问题，但传统解析器带来显著计算开销。既有工作表明把依存句法分析重构为序列标注（SEL）可提升效率，因此需要探索在统一MTL下高效注入句法知识的策略。

Method: 提出并对比两种策略：Standard-MTL——共享编码器加两个任务特定头，联合学习依存句法分析（以SEL形式）与情感极性分类；Contrastive-MTL——通过对比辅助目标与句法偏置的注意力池化提供隐式句法引导，无需显式标签预测。两者训练时均依赖句法标注，推理时无需手工组合规则或外部解析器；实验采用四种SEL编码。

Result: 评测11个数据集、7种语言（MARC的英/西/法/德，及SemEval-2022 Task 10的英/西/挪/巴斯克/加泰罗尼亚7个数据集）。相对编码下，Contrastive-MTL在11个中的10个数据集上优于无句法基线，并在SELSP适用的全部6个数据集上超过该推理时解析的规则流水线，同时评估速度提升1.5–9倍；Standard-MTL表现更依赖数据集与编码方式。

Conclusion: 训练阶段的隐式句法监督可达到甚至超越推理阶段显式解析的效果，且不引入额外计算开销，是一种更高效的句法知识融合范式。

Abstract: Sentiment Analysis (SA) is a fundamental NLP task concerned with the identification and categorization of subjective information in text. While lexicon-based methods offer transparency, they often struggle with linguistic phenomena such as negation and intensification. Syntactic parsing has been integrated into SA pipelines to address this, but conventional parsers introduce significant computational overhead. Recent work shows that reformulating dependency parsing as a Sequence Labeling (SEL) task can improve efficiency. We propose and compare two strategies for integrating syntactic knowledge into a neural sentiment analysis model under unified Multi-Task Learning (MTL). The first, Standard-MTL, jointly learns dependency parsing (via sequence labeling) and sentiment polarity classification using a shared encoder with two task-specific heads. The second, Contrastive-MTL, provides implicit syntactic guidance through a contrastive auxiliary objective and a syntax-biased attention pooling mechanism, without requiring explicit label prediction. Both rely on syntactic annotations during training and eliminate need for hand-crafted compositional rules or external parsing at inference. We evaluate eleven datasets spanning seven languages---the MARC corpus (English, Spanish, French, German) and seven datasets from SemEval-2022 Task 10 (English, Spanish, Norwegian, Basque, Catalan)---using four established SEL encodings. With the relative encoding, Contrastive-MTL outperforms a syntax-naive baseline on 10 of 11 datasets and surpasses the external rule-based pipeline (SELSP) that parses at inference on all six datasets where the latter applies, while being 1.5--9$\times$ faster at evaluation. Standard-MTL is more dataset- and encoding-dependent. These results show implicit syntactic supervision during training can match or exceed explicit parsing at inference without computational overhead.

</details>


### [155] [Measured Coupling for Parallel Decoding in Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08307)
*Tal Oved,Lior Wolf*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出用真实耦合（提交一个 token 对另一位置置信度的影响）替代均值场解码中的相似度代理，通过主前向传播内的廉价探针测得，无需训练或额外模型，显著减少扩散语言模型的解码步数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型每步可生成多 token，但需判断哪些 token 可安全同时提交；均值场解码用"预测相似度"作为代理来惩罚位置对，该代理并不精确反映真实耦合。

Method: 将均值场解码的相似度代理替换为真实耦合度量——即提交某 token 会如何改变模型在另一位置的置信度；利用主前向传播内的廉价探针测量该耦合，无需训练或额外模型，并直接嵌入原有均值场并行提交规则。

Result: 在 3 个模型、4 个基准上，方法所需解码步数远少于均值场解码，多数设置下更快（含使用 KV cache 的场景），平均精度代价为 1.1 个点；打乱耦合或替换为均匀奖励时增益消失（消融验证耦合信息有效）。

Conclusion: 真实耦合比相似度代理更适合作为并行提交的冲突判据，可在几乎不损精度的前提下大幅提升扩散语言模型解码效率，且实现开销低、易集成。

Abstract: Diffusion language models can write many tokens per step, but must choose which tokens are safe to commit together. Mean-field decoding penalizes pairs of positions whose predictions look alike. We replace this proxy with the coupling itself: how much committing one token changes the model's confidence at another. Cheap probes inside the main forward pass measure it, with no training or extra model, and it plugs into the same mean-field rule. On three models and four benchmarks, our method needs far fewer steps than mean-field decoding and is faster in most settings, also with a key-value cache, at an average accuracy cost of 1.1 points. Shuffling the couplings or replacing them with a uniform bonus loses this gain.

</details>


### [156] [τ-bench-LILT: Evaluating Conversational Agents on Realistic Multilingual Tool-Use Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07203)
*Kaden Uhlig,Kiarash Mokhtari,Anıl Arslan,Yunsu Kim,Joern Wuebker*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 τ-bench-LILT，将 τ³-bench 扩展为覆盖德语、韩语、土耳其语和波斯语的多语言工具调用对话智能体基准，全部层（数据库、用户模拟器、任务）原生本地化，评测显示非英语下性能显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿的工具使用对话智能体评测几乎全部以英语进行，导致在其他语言和地区部署智能体的机构存在关键评测空白。

Method: 构建 τ-bench-LILT：在航空、零售、电信、银行四个领域对德语、韩语、土耳其语、波斯语进行原生本地化——客户数据库填充符合当地文化的记录，用户模拟器提示由母语专家通过对比标注迭代精炼，任务依据本地化数据库更新并贴合目标地区的真实场景。

Result: 对八种智能体模型的评测显示，与英语相比韩语和波斯语的零售领域性能下降最大，pass^4 最高下降 61 分。

Conclusion: 英语单语评测会高估工具调用对话智能体的实际能力，多语言原生本地化基准对真实部署评估是必要的；代码与数据已开源。

Abstract: Frontier evaluations of tool-using conversational agents are conducted almost exclusively in English, leaving a critical gap for organizations deploying agents in other languages and regions. We present $τ$-bench-LILT, a multilingual extension of ${τ^3}$-bench covering German, Korean, Turkish, and Farsi in the airline, retail, telecom, and banking domains. We natively localize every layer of the benchmark: customer databases are populated with culturally appropriate records, user-simulator prompts are refined iteratively through contrastive annotation by native-language experts, and tasks are updated according to the localized database and to reflect realistic tasks in the target region. Evaluation across eight agent models shows the largest drops compared to English in the Korean and Farsi retail domains, by up to 61 points in pass^4. Code and data released at https://github.com/lilt/tau-bench-lilt.

</details>


### [157] [CardioLM: A multilingual suite of small language models for the cardiology domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07940)
*Manuela-Daniela Danu,Miguel Rodríguez-Ortega,Alberto Becerra-Tome,Pablo Arancibia,Fernando Gallego-Donoso,Lorenzo Pratesi,Xi Bai,Andrei-Damian Dinescu,Salvador Lima-López,Andrea Guala,Saskia Haitjema,Jonas Mack,Michele Miranda,Wesam Al Nabki,Jan Rodríguez-Miret,Vasile George Marica,Vladimír Kincl,Kateřina Dostálová,Michael Andrej,Alicia Ujil,Corinne Allaart,Machteld Boonstra,Malin Overmars,Anoop Shah,Giovanna Ceroni,Jonathan Kossoff,Domenico D'Amario,Renzo Laborante,Antonio Iaconelli,Isabella Fumarulo,Jacopo Lenkowicz,Livia Lilli,Emma Weiss,Alex Scafa,Felix Lindberg,Milou Gamage,Eulàlia Farré-Maduell,Sébastien Bratières,Martin Krallinger,Bram van Es*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文提出CardioCCC（508篇西班牙语心脏病病例报告，经机器翻译、标注投影与临床医生审核扩展到捷克语、荷兰语、英语、意大利语、罗马尼亚语和瑞典语，覆盖疾病、症状、操作、药物四类实体）与CardioLM（含语言特定编码器CardioBERTa及基于CardioCCC微调的CardioNER），实验表明专科预训练对下游NER增益有限，而多语言联合训练在几乎所有语言与实体类型上取得最佳严格F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心血管诊疗产生大量本地语言自由文本记录，但临床NLP资源集中于英语，且缺乏同时具备心脏病学特异性、多语言性与广泛实体覆盖的基准数据集，限制了多国临床信息抽取的开展。

Method: 构建CardioCCC多语言心脏病学语料：以西班牙语原始病例报告为源，通过机器翻译、标注投影和临床医生审核扩展至六种目标语言，标注疾病、症状、操作、药物；构建CardioLM：语言特定编码器CardioBERTa在心脏病学与生物医学语料上继续预训练，CardioNER在CardioCCC上微调NER模型；与单语系统、规则词典基线对比，并采用严格/宽松span匹配评估及临床医生人工评分。

Result: 心脏病学预训练使伪困惑度最多降低8倍，但严格span级F1变化不超过0.04，收益集中于骨干模型缺乏生物医学覆盖的语言；联合训练的多语言XLM-RoBERTa-large在几乎所有语言与实体类型上严格F1最佳，较最强单语系统高至多0.13；残余错误多为边界检测问题，宽松匹配使疾病、操作、症状F1提升0.10-0.23，而药物仅提升0.01-0.07；临床医生对CardioNER评分显著高于规则词典基线，各实体类型平均评分由26%升至73%。

Conclusion: 专科领域预训练并非下游实体识别的决定性因素；多语言联合训练在几乎所有设置下效果最佳，并为多国临床信息抽取提供更简便的部署路径。

Abstract: Background: Cardiovascular care generates large volumes of clinical free-text documentation in local languages. However, clinical natural language processing (NLP) resources remain concentrated in English, and no existing benchmark is cardiology-specific, multilingual, and broad in entity coverage.
  Methods: We present two contributions. The first is CardioCCC, a corpus of 508 cardiology case reports originally published in Spanish and extended to Czech, Dutch, English, Italian, Romanian, and Swedish through machine translation, annotation projection, and clinician review, with annotations covering diseases, symptoms, procedures, and medications. The second is CardioLM, comprising CardioBERTa, a set of language-specific encoders obtained by continued pretraining on cardiology and biomedical corpora, and CardioNER, a set of named entity recognition (NER) models fine-tuned on CardioCCC.
  Results: Cardiology-specific pretraining reduced pseudo-perplexity by up to a factor of eight, yet changed strict span-level F1 by at most 0.04, with benefits concentrated in languages whose backbones lacked biomedical coverage. The jointly trained multilingual XLM-RoBERTa-large models achieved the best strict F1 in nearly every language and entity type, exceeding the strongest monolingual system by up to 0.13 F1. A large share of residual errors concerned boundary detection: relaxed matching recovered 0.10-0.23 F1 for disease, procedure, and symptom, but only 0.01-0.07 for medication. Clinicians rated CardioNER well above a rule- and dictionary-based baseline, with the mean rating across entity types rising from 26% to 73%.
  Conclusions: Specialty-specific pretraining is not the decisive factor for downstream entity recognition, whereas multilingual joint training yields the strongest results in nearly all settings and a simpler deployment path for multi-country clinical information extraction.

</details>


### [158] [Do Recommenders Need to Think in Words? Latent Reasoning for Efficient LLM-Based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07866)
*Hongyang Su,Beibei Kong,Lei Cheng,Chengxiang Zhuo,Zang Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LatentRec 用训练时的隐式推理模块与逐步头部监督，替代推理时的 CoT 文本生成，在保持/提升准确率的同时大幅降低推理延迟，且模块可在部署时丢弃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于 LLM 的推荐系统普遍在每次物品预测前生成 CoT 文本以激活推理能力，但每条推荐需多消耗数十到数百 token，推理成本高。作者试图验证：推理时推理产物（文本或隐式）能否仅靠训练时监督来替代，而不损失准确率。

Method: 提出 LatentRec 框架，含三部分：(1) 2.5M 参数的 Continuous Thought Module，在训练时对 LLM 隐状态迭代 K 步潜在推理；(2) Soft Token Selection，弥合隐状态与输入嵌入间约 14 倍的范数差距；(3) Per-step Reasoning Loss（PRL），通过 LLM 预测头对每个中间状态以目标 Semantic ID 首 token 为监督。推理阶段可丢弃该模块。

Result: 在三个 Amazon 基准上，相较 CoT 蒸馏基线，HR@10 相对提升 4.0–5.0%，batch=1 时延迟降低 18.3 倍；逐步监督在三个数据集上均一致提升准确率。

Conclusion: 推理能力可通过一次性训练时监督获得，而非每次推荐时重复付出；逐步头部监督是可替代推理时 CoT 的有效路径，部署时无需保留额外模块。

Abstract: LLM-based recommender systems increasingly generate chain-of-thought (CoT) text before each item prediction to activate the LLM's reasoning capacity, costing tens to hundreds of extra tokens per recommendation at inference. We ask whether inference-time reasoning artifacts (textual or latent) can be replaced by training-time supervision alone, without loss of accuracy. We propose LatentRec, a framework for LLM-based generative recommendation that replaces inference-time reasoning artifacts with training-time per-step head supervision. LatentRec has three components. A 2.5M-parameter Continuous Thought Module iterates K latent reasoning steps over the LLM's hidden state during training. Soft Token Selection bridges the ~14x norm gap between hidden states and input embeddings. A Per-step Reasoning Loss (PRL) supervises every intermediate state through the LLM's prediction head against the first token of the target Semantic ID. The trained LLM retains this accuracy without the module at inference, so the module can be discarded at deployment. On three Amazon benchmarks, LatentRec improves HR@10 by 4.0-5.0% (relative) over a CoT-distilled baseline at 18.3x lower latency (batch=1). Per-step training supervision consistently improves accuracy across three datasets, paid once at training rather than at every recommendation.

</details>


### [159] [Decodability Is Not Validity: Evaluating Nuisance Sensitivity in Language Model Concept Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07805)
*Ayal Klein*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出概念探针的"可解码性"(AUROC)不能可靠反映测量纯度：统计等价的探针在干扰属性敏感性上可相差数倍，概念测量应做"验证"而非仅看"解码"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作普遍以预测准确率(AUROC)评价概念提取器，隐含假设"能解码=测得准"。但探针分数可能混入与目标概念相关的干扰属性(nuisance)成分，导致概念测量失真，且缺乏对这一风险的可靠诊断手段。

Method: 在四个目标–干扰设置（CEBaB两方面情感；BiasBios职业与性别；Amazon评论情感×否定；情感×第一人称框架）下，比较两种探针、两个LLM与一组概念提取器，用"判别证据"进行对照：(1)固定目标时分数对干扰的依赖；(2)对"仅改干扰"的输入编辑的响应，经两个LLM评判者过滤，并与安慰剂编辑对比；同时对比相关训练与去相关训练。

Result: 相关训练数据下，留出AUROC统计等价的提取器在四设置中三个里干扰敏感性相差数倍；可解码性与干扰敏感性无稳定关系，其秩相关跨设置变号。效应由探针训练数据决定而非干扰的自然关联：与情感无关的第一人称框架被吸收程度不低于与负面情感共现的否定；去相关训练可消除系统性效应。采用需干扰标签但无需反事实的"干扰感知"开发期诊断来挑选提取器，可在默认选择敏感处降低反事实干扰敏感性，代价是少量准确率损失。

Conclusion: 可解码性是干扰敏感性的不可靠代理指标。概念测量应引入基于干扰标签的验证式诊断来筛选提取器，而不是仅以预测精度评价。

Abstract: Two concept probes can be equally accurate at predicting a concept from a language model's hidden states while their scores contain different mixtures of the target concept and a correlated nuisance attribute. In four target--nuisance settings (two aspect sentiments in CEBaB; occupation and gender in BiasBios; Amazon review sentiment with negation, and with first-person framing), two LLMs, and a population of concept extractors, we compare decodability with discriminant evidence: the score's dependence on the nuisance at fixed target, and its response to input edits intended to change only the nuisance, filtered by two LLM judges and compared against placebo edits. Under correlated training data, extractors that are statistically equivalent in held-out AUROC differ up to several-fold in nuisance sensitivity in three of the four settings, and decodability is not a reliable proxy for it: their rank correlation changes sign across settings. The effect follows the probe's training data, not the nuisance's natural association with the target: first-person framing, unrelated to sentiment in natural reviews, is absorbed at least as much as negation, which co-occurs with negative sentiment, and decorrelated training removes the systematic effect. Selecting extractors with nuisance-aware development diagnostics, which need nuisance labels but no counterfactuals, lowers counterfactual nuisance sensitivity wherever the default choice is sensitive, at a small accuracy cost. Concept measurements should be validated, not just decoded.

</details>


### [160] [Bridging Structure and Semantics: Knowledge Graph Verification via Triple-to-Text Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07780)
*Ziang Cui*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GenVer：用序列化三元组与生成的自然语言表示（NLR）联合打分的生成式三元组验证器，在四个FactFB变体上取得88.56%–91.52%的最高分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义准确性是知识图谱质量的重要维度。现有方法中，基于图的打分利用关系结构，文本表示则让分类器借助预训练语言模型（PLM）编码的知识，两类信息尚未在同一验证框架中充分结合。

Method: 提出GenVer生成式验证器：先通过三元组—文本对齐过程为序列到序列生成器筛选训练对，生成三元组的自然语言表示（NLR）；随后由BERT分类器融合三元组本身及其生成NLR的特征进行打分。

Result: 在基于FB15k、以扰动负三元组构造的四个FactFB变体上，GenVer的分类准确率在被评估的基线中最高，范围为88.56%–91.52%。

Conclusion: 实验表明生成的表示有助于三元组分类；案例分析同时暴露了语义漂移与高置信度错误两类局限。

Abstract: Semantic accuracy is an important dimension of knowledge graph quality. Graph-based scoring methods exploit relational structure, while textual representations allow classifiers to draw on knowledge encoded by pre-trained language models (PLMs). We present GenVer, a generative verifier that scores triples using both their serialized form and generated natural language representations (NLRs). A triple-text alignment procedure selects training pairs for a sequence-to-sequence generator; a BERT-based classifier then combines features from each triple and its generated NLR. On four FactFB variants, constructed from FB15k using perturbed negative triples, GenVer obtains the highest classification accuracy among the evaluated baselines, ranging from 88.56% to 91.52%. The experiments suggest that generated representations can help triple classification, while the case analysis also exposes semantic drift and high-confidence errors.

</details>


### [161] [Interactive Backdoor Attacks in LLM-Based Proactive Dialogue Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06969)
*Zhiyang Xia,Yuming Shang,Xi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种交互式后门攻击框架，通过训练数据投毒使基于LLM的主动式对话系统在触发后多轮交互中被引导至攻击者指定目标，并在心理咨询场景下验证三类攻击的有效性与现有防御的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM主动式对话系统需用外部对话数据微调，存在后门风险；但现有LLM后门研究多局限于"触发即输出指定回复"的单步设定，缺乏对触发后多轮交互持续引导（交互式后门）的刻画。

Method: 采用训练数据投毒：在微调对话中植入"触发条件相关的引导策略偏置"，由普通用户话语即可激活，无需部署时改prompt或攻击者介入。设计三种递进设置：(1) Direct Attack，触发后即时响应偏离；(2) Constrained Progressive Attack，沿预定义多轮轨迹逐步推进目标；(3) Unconstrained Progressive Attack，面向开放式交互与多样用户延续的目标引导。以心理咨询为代表性场景，在两种LLM骨干与两个对话数据集上实验，并评估训练数据过滤与三种准入后防御基线。

Result: 三种攻击在各自交互设置下均有效达成目标，同时基本保持正常效用（utility）；防御评估显示被测训练数据过滤器几乎保留全部投毒对话，三种准入后防御基线仍留有大量攻击特定成功率。

Conclusion: 后门风险应从单步回复扩展到多轮目标引导维度；现有数据过滤与后置防御难以应对此类交互式后门，需发展面向多轮轨迹一致性的检测与缓解方法。

Abstract: LLM-based proactive dialogue systems provide goal-directed support through sustained interaction. Fine-tuning their underlying models on externally supplied dialogue data introduces backdoor risks. Existing LLM backdoor studies primarily focus on eliciting an attacker-specified response upon trigger activation. We present an interactive backdoor attack framework that uses training-data poisoning to steer post-trigger, multi-turn interactions toward an attacker-specified objective. Poisoned fine-tuning dialogues implant a trigger-conditioned guidance-policy bias that ordinary user utterances can activate, without deployment-time prompt modification or further attacker intervention. Specifically, we instantiate three complementary attacks under progressively expanded post-trigger interaction settings: (1) Direct Attack, which targets an immediate response deviation upon trigger activation, retaining the conventional trigger--response setting; (2) Constrained Progressive Attack, which progressively advances the attack objective along a predefined multi-turn trajectory; and (3) Unconstrained Progressive Attack, which extends target-directed steering to open-ended interactions with varied user continuations. Using psychological counseling as a representative setting, experiments on two LLM backbones and two dialogue datasets show that all three attacks effectively achieve their objectives under their respective interaction settings while largely preserving normal utility. Separate defense evaluations show that the tested training-data filters retain nearly all poisoned dialogues, while three post-admission baselines leave substantial residual attack-specific success.

</details>


### [162] [Latency-Aware Adaptive Best-of-$N$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07453)
*Alon Jacobson,Qian Xie,Peter I. Frazier*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出两阶段自适应Best-of-N推理算法，利用首批奖励与延迟决定是否及如何采样第二批，以延迟感知方式最小化生成等效成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线服务中延迟也是成本，且随服务器负载变化；固定阈值的自适应TTC方法在生成快时算力不足、慢时算力过量，无法兼顾延迟成本。

Method: 针对Best-of-N采样，先抽取初始批次，再将奖励映射到其提示奖励分布CDF值以跨任务复用权衡参数；离线回归预测首批最佳奖励CDF值的分布，闭式给出期望改进，并与预测成本比较决定是否抽取第二批及规模；阈值随首批延迟下降，无需显式测量服务器负载。

Result: 在偏好对齐基准上以模拟方式评估，成本以生成等效成本（生成数加换算后的延迟）计：方法在更低生成等效成本下达到Best-of-N的奖励水平；基于首批延迟的条件判断至少与固定阈值（延迟无关）基线节省同等成本，且在延迟高波动、延迟随批量增大、第二批延迟可由首批高度预测时节省更多。

Conclusion: 将实测延迟纳入停止决策可有效降低在线服务中的推理成本，为自适应TTC提供延迟感知的实用方案。

Abstract: Test-time compute (TTC) improves language model responses by spending additional computation at inference time, and the right amount of compute depends on the prompt. Adaptive TTC methods allocate compute based on signals of response quality such as observed rewards, often by stopping once the signal exceeds a fixed threshold, and generally measure cost by the number of generations or tokens. But in online serving, latency is also a cost: it grows with server load, so a method using a fixed threshold spends too little compute when generation is fast and too much when it is slow. For Best-of-$N$ sampling, a widely used TTC method, we propose a two-stage adaptive algorithm that draws an initial batch, then uses the observed rewards and the batch's latency to decide whether to draw a second batch and at what size by comparing expected improvement against predicted cost: it draws one when the first batch's best reward is below a threshold that decreases with the first batch's latency, without explicitly measuring server load. We map each reward to its value under the cumulative distribution function (CDF) of the prompt's reward distribution, so the same tradeoff parameters can be reused across tasks; a regression fitted offline on other prompts predicts a distribution for the CDF value of the first batch's best reward, giving the expected improvement in closed form. We evaluate the method by simulation on a preference-based alignment benchmark, measuring generations-equivalent cost: generations plus latency, converted to generations. The method reaches Best-of-$N$'s reward at lower generations-equivalent cost, and conditioning on the measured first-batch latency saves at least as much of that cost as fixed-threshold (latency-unaware) baselines, and more when latency is highly variable, when it grows with batch size, and when the second batch's latency is highly predictable from the first's.

</details>


### [163] [MedGraph: Maintaining Patient State with Graph Memory for Long-Term Medical Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08433)
*Donghua Zhang,Zizheng Wang,Ze Dong,Yiding Feng,Siyu Qian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出MedGraph图记忆系统，针对纵向医疗对话中记忆系统的三类错误（证据过时、混入他人健康事实、缺失安全事实），通过带时间戳的事实抽取、事实归属、链条化存储与按提问时间读取的检索，在MedMemoryBench上取得最高总体准确率，较最强基线提升10.5/10.4分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跟随患者多次就诊的LLM医疗助手需要随临床状态变化而保持正确的记忆。作者考察五个记忆系统在纵向医疗对话上的错误，归纳出三类反复出现的原因：检索到的证据是过时状态、混入他人的健康事实、缺少答案所依赖的安全事实。

Method: MedGraph为图结构记忆，构建阶段：从每次会话抽取带日期的事实，将每个事实归属到其所描述的人，并把用药、诊断与测量记录链接成保留早期状态的链条；检索阶段：以患者链条为单位排序，按问题所问时点读取每条链，并附上原始对话文本；同时将患者画像中的全部已记录安全事实随每个问题一并提供。

Result: 在MedMemoryBench上，MedGraph在GPT与Qwen两种骨干模型下均取得最高总体准确率，分别比五个基线中最强者高10.5和10.4个点；在GPT设置下记忆构建与回答均高效。

Conclusion: 针对纵向医疗对话中过时证据、跨人事实混淆与安全事实缺失三类问题，按时间建模、按人归属的图记忆是有效方案，可显著提升LLM医疗助手的记忆准确性。

Abstract: LLM-based medical assistants that follow a patient across visits need a memory that keeps the patient's clinical state correct as it changes. We examine the errors of five memory systems on longitudinal medical dialogue and find three recurring causes: the retrieved evidence gives an outdated state, includes another person's health facts, or lacks a safety fact the answer depends on. We present MedGraph, a graph memory built for these three cases. Construction extracts dated facts from each session, attributes each fact to the person it describes, and links medication, diagnosis, and measurement records into chains that keep earlier states. Retrieval ranks the patient's chains as units, reads each chain at the time the question asks about, and adds the original dialogue text; a patient profile lists every recorded safety fact with every question. On MedMemoryBench, MedGraph has the highest overall accuracy with both a GPT and a Qwen backbone, 10.5 and 10.4 points above the strongest of five baselines, with efficient memory construction and answering in the GPT setting.

</details>


### [164] [SpecCTMC: Exact Speculative Simulation of Diffusion Language Model Continuous-Time Markov Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08362)
*Siva Rajesh Kasa,Yasong Dai,Sumit Negi,Hongdong Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SpecCTMC：一种无需训练、无需独立草稿模型的投机采样器，用于加速掩码扩散语言模型的精确连续时间采样，在保持联合分布不变的前提下减少顺序模型评估次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码扩散语言模型支持并行文本生成，但独立采样多个token会破坏token间依赖并改变输出分布；而精确的连续时间采样虽避免该近似误差，却通常需要每个token进行一次顺序模型评估，形成顺序瓶颈。

Method: 提出SpecCTMC投机采样器：利用此前已计算的模型预测提出连续的token更新，并行评估这些中间状态，并施加精确的接受-校正（acceptance-and-correction）程序；无需单独草稿模型，也无需训练。理论上证明其保持底层连续时间马尔可夫链关于事件时间、生成顺序与token取值的联合分布。

Result: 在LLaDA-8B-Instruct与Dream-v0-Instruct-7B上、五个基准测试中，相比直接连续时间采样减少1.73–4.38倍的顺序模型评估次数，同时保持参考采样分布；既不引入时间离散化误差，也不引入并行解码带来的额外因子分解误差。

Conclusion: SpecCTMC在严格保持参考分布的前提下有效缓解了精确连续时间采样的顺序瓶颈，为扩散语言模型提供了高效且无偏的加速采样方案。

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models offer parallel text generation, but independently sampling multiple tokens can disrupt their dependencies and change the output distribution. Exact continuous-time sampling avoids this approximation, yet typically requires one sequential model evaluation per token. We introduce SpecCTMC, a training-free speculative sampler that reduces this sequential bottleneck without a separate draft model. SpecCTMC proposes successive token updates using previously computed model predictions, evaluates their intermediate states in parallel, and applies an exact acceptance-and-correction procedure. We prove that SpecCTMC preserves the joint distribution of event times, generation order, and token values of the underlying continuous-time Markov chain. It therefore introduces neither time-discretization error nor additional factorization error from parallel decoding. Experiments on LLaDA-8B-Instruct and Dream-v0-Instruct-7B across five benchmarks demonstrate 1.73-4.38x fewer sequential model evaluations than direct continuous-time sampling, while preserving the reference sampling distribution. Our code is available at: https://github.com/kasakh/specctmc

</details>


### [165] [Axis-LoRA: Probing and Adapting Embedding Models for Personal-Memory Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07820)
*Dohoon Kim,Junho Park,Hyunwoong Bae,Seyoon Kim,Taesup Moon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Axis-LoRA：在嵌入模型内部为时间、地点、人物、事件四个维度各设一个LoRA分支，并用逐查询门控组合，显著提升个人记忆检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM被越来越频繁地问及用户个人经历，检索需按时间、地点、人物、事件区分记忆。但现有个人记忆流程依赖冻结的稠密嵌入模型，作者发现其难以刻画这四个维度：改换记忆的日期或地点几乎不改变嵌入，且在冻结嵌入之上训练投影也无法恢复缺失信号。问题根源在模型内部——每个维度由不同的注意力头模式编码。

Method: 基于"各轴由不同注意力头模式编码"的发现，提出Axis-LoRA：按轴设置的适配器，在嵌入模型内部放置四个LoRA分支，并通过逐查询门控（per-query gate）进行组合；仅用小型轴反事实训练集训练极小比例的基础参数。

Result: 在三个标准嵌入模型上，于LongMemEval、ATM-Bench、LoCoMo评测，Axis-LoRA使四基准平均R@10提升+1.28至+1.56个百分点，且仅训练少量参数。

Conclusion: 个人记忆检索的维度信息位于嵌入模型内部而非可事后投影恢复；通过按轴LoRA分支+门控的轻量内部适配可有效增强四维区分能力，兼顾性能与训练开销。代码已开源。

Abstract: As LLMs are increasingly asked questions about a user's personal experiences, retrieval must distinguish memories by time, place, person, and event. Current personal-memory pipelines rely on frozen dense embedding models to embed memories for retrieval, but we find that these models do not capture the four axes well. Swapping a memory's date or place barely shifts its embedding, and a trained projection on top of the frozen embedding does not recover the missing signal. The problem lives inside the model, where each axis is encoded by a distinct attention-head pattern. Guided by these findings, we propose Axis-LoRA, a per-axis adapter that places four LoRA branches inside the embedding model and combines them via a per-query gate. Across three standard embedding models evaluated on LongMemEval, ATM-Bench, and LoCoMo, Axis-LoRA improves 4-benchmark mean R@10 by $+1.28$ to $+1.56$~pp while training only a small fraction of the base parameters on a small axis-counterfactual training set. Code is available at https://github.com/dohoonkim-ai/axis-lora.

</details>


### [166] [HyFlash: A Prefix-Memory Prior for On-Device Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07263)
*Jongwoo Ko,Pashmina Cameron*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HyFlash：紧凑型并行草稿器，用"前缀记忆先验"将长程上下文建模与草稿位置细粒度交互解耦，在有限草稿侧内存下提升投机解码接受率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并行块草稿通过单次主干前向预测多个未来位置以加速投机解码，但端侧部署受限于草稿器容量与上下文存储。内存紧张时，草稿器既要建模长程依赖，又要建模细粒度草稿位置交互，而全上下文注意力的 KV-cache 开销随上下文增长，难以承受。

Method: 提出前缀记忆先验（prefix-memory prior），分离两类建模职责：局部注意力负责近期上下文与草稿位置间交互；循环记忆将历史目标特征压缩为固定大小状态，承担长程建模。由此形成有界草稿侧内存下的归纳偏置，兼顾局部与长程建模。

Result: 实验显示 HyFlash 的接受率高于全上下文注意力，同时 KV 存储显著减少；在更浅的草稿器与更长上下文下增益更大。

Conclusion: 通过结构化解耦长程与局部建模，HyFlash 在受限内存条件下优于全上下文注意力草稿器，为端侧投机解码提供了更优的容量—内存—接受率权衡。

Abstract: Parallel block drafting accelerates speculative decoding by predicting multiple future positions in a single backbone pass, but on-device deployment constrains both drafter capacity and context storage. Under tight memory budgets, drafters must model long-range dependencies and fine-grained draft-position interactions without the growing KV-cache overhead of full-context attention. We propose HyFlash, a compact parallel drafter that introduces a prefix-memory prior to separate long-range context modeling from fine-grained draft-position interactions with limited capacity. Local attention captures recent context and draft-position interactions, while recurrent memory compresses historical target features into fixed-size states. This inductive bias enables effective local and long-range modeling with bounded draft-side memory. Experiments show that HyFlash achieves higher acceptance than full-context attention with substantially less KV storage and with larger gains for shallow drafters and long contexts.

</details>


### [167] [Source-balanced Capped Fisher: Bounded Evidence Aggregation for User Inference in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07228)
*Yang Liu,Ran Zou,Wanrong Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 source-balanced capped Fisher（SCF）聚合方法，通过已知未参与源评估文档分数的异常性，结合校准型决策规则实现源级参与推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文档级分数虽能提供参与证据，但难以有效聚合：文档在基线可预测性、以及对同源其他文档所学信息的反映强度上存在差异，导致直接合并证据受限。

Method: 将文档分数基于已知未参与源转化为参照尾频率，再转为负对数证据；对单文档贡献设上限（capping），并在源内取平均以平衡；最后用针对另一组已知未参与源校准的 p 值进行源参与假设检验。

Result: 在源可交换性下提供有限样本 Type-I 错误控制；四个真实数据集/四个领域的实验中，SCF 的 AUROC 在所有评估聚合方法中最高，且在多数设置下检测功效最高，实证假阳性率接近或低于名义水平。

Conclusion: SCF 通过尾部校准、贡献截断与源内平均，兼顾稳健性与检出能力，配合校准决策规则可获得可靠且竞争性强的源级参与推断。

Abstract: User inference asks whether a source contributed documents to a language-model update, even when the auditor observes only fresh documents from that source. Document-level scores can provide evidence of participation, but combining this evidence is challenging: documents differ in baseline predictability and in how strongly they reflect information learned from other documents of the same source. We propose source-balanced capped Fisher (SCF), an aggregation method that uses known nonparticipating sources to assess how unusual each document score is. It transforms the resulting reference-tail frequencies into negative-log evidence, caps individual contributions, and averages them within each source. This limits the influence of isolated extreme scores while retaining evidence across multiple documents. We complete the inference procedure with a decision rule that tests source participation using a p-value calibrated against a separate set of known nonparticipating sources. The rule provides finite-sample Type-I error control under source exchangeability. Experiments across four real-world datasets show that SCF achieves the highest AUROC among the evaluated aggregation methods in four domains. With the calibration-based decision rule, SCF delivers consistently competitive detection power across the tested settings, achieving the highest power in many cases, while empirical false-positive rates remain generally close to or below the nominal levels.

</details>


### [168] [Fine-Tuning a Transducer for Impaired Speech: Where the Gains Appear and What They Mean for Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07587)
*Kien Nguyen,Tuan Dinh,Bao Phan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文报告团队参加 Speech Accessibility Project Challenge 2 (SAPC2) 的方案：基于公开语音识别器在挑战数据上微调，Test1 最好成绩 7.81% CER（官方基线 15.26%），CPU 实时系统延迟中位数约 0.9 秒、错误率 7.96–8.07%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音识别对 ALS、帕金森病、脑瘫、唐氏综合征、中风等导致的非典型语音识别效果差，需提升此类人群的识别可及性与公平性。

Method: 参加 SAPC2 挑战赛，采用公开预训练语音识别模型在挑战数据上微调；线上系统部署于 CPU，并对比熟悉/新说话人、已见/未见句子的错误率差异。

Result: Test1 最佳 7.81% CER（基线 15.26%）；线上 CPU 系统合并延迟中位数约 0.9 秒，错误率 7.96–8.07%；微调对新说话人中原本最难识别者收益最大；训练中已见句子错误率显著低于新句子（2.58% vs 6.42%），且该差异大于说话人熟悉度的影响。

Conclusion: 微调可显著改善非典型语音识别且可满足 CPU 低延迟部署；评测需区分句子记忆与说话人泛化，作者据此总结此类系统的评测经验。

Abstract: Speech recognition still works poorly for many people whose speech is affected by conditions such as ALS, Parkinson's disease, cerebral palsy, Down syndrome or stroke. We took part in the Speech Accessibility Project Challenge 2 (SAPC2) with a public speech recognizer that we fine-tuned on the challenge data. Our best entry scored 7.81% character error rate on Test1, the first of two hidden test sets (official baseline 15.26%). Our live entries ran on CPUs with a combined median latency of about 0.9 seconds at 7.96-8.07% error. On new speakers, fine-tuning reduced errors most for the speakers that an off-the-shelf recognizer finds hardest to understand. Sentences already seen in training had far lower error rates than new ones (2.58% vs 6.42%), a larger difference than being a familiar speaker made. We close with lessons for evaluating such systems.

</details>


### [169] [Continued Training of Continuous Language Flows with Token Identity Squared Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07721)
*Jiawei Jiang,Zhaoxiang Zang,Zhengtao Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Token Identity Brier Flow（TIBF），将平方监督直接施加于token身份，在冻结预训练嵌入的前提下改进预训练语言流模型的连续生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续MSE目标对预测概率分布不可区分：不同概率分布经嵌入加权可得到相同向量，导致监督信号与连续生成之间缺乏恰当连接，限制有限额外训练下的生成质量。

Method: TIBF在高斯观测模型下将网络得分与token匹配得分结合，用Brier损失监督token概率，并以概率加权嵌入进行连续生成；理论上证明概率后验平方误差界定了连续去噪预测与流速度的平方误差，并给出风险分解（观测固有不确定性与后验拟合误差），以及理想预测下收敛到token嵌入、含预测误差时局部收敛的充分条件。

Result: 在相同续训预算与常见预训练初始化下，冻结嵌入的TIBF在OWT上Gen.PPL为33.96、LM1B上为81.17，优于连续MSE与CE续训基线；在两个数据集的所有测试采样预算下均取得最低Gen.PPL，并在LM1B上取得最低重复率。

Conclusion: 以token身份为监督对象的Brier式流训练可有效衔接监督与连续生成，配合冻结预训练嵌入与聚焦概率预测的续训策略，显著提升生成困惑度与多样性。

Abstract: Diffusion and flow models have recently been extended to language modeling, generating text through continuous denoising in token embedding spaces. Pretrained models provide token representations and contextual prediction capabilities, but improving generation with limited additional training requires an appropriate connection between supervision and continuous generation. Continuous MSE evaluates a predicted vector obtained by weighting token embeddings with predicted probabilities; different probability distributions can yield the same vector and are therefore indistinguishable under this objective. We propose Token Identity Brier Flow (TIBF), which applies squared supervision directly to token identities when continuing pretrained language flows. TIBF combines network scores with token-matching scores under a Gaussian observation model, supervises token probabilities using the Brier loss, and uses probability-weighted embeddings for continuous generation. With fixed token embeddings, we show that the squared error between predicted token probabilities and the true posterior bounds the squared errors of continuous denoising predictions and flow velocities. A risk decomposition separates uncertainty inherent in the observation from posterior fitting error, motivating continued training that preserves pretrained embeddings and focuses on probability prediction. We also establish convergence to token embeddings under ideal predictions and sufficient conditions for local convergence with prediction error. From common pretrained initializations and under matched continuation budgets, frozen-embedding TIBF achieves Gen.PPL values of 33.96 on OWT and 81.17 on LM1B, outperforming continuous-MSE and CE continuation controls. It attains the lowest Gen.PPL at every tested sampling budget on both datasets, together with the lowest repetition rate on LM1B.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [170] [The Geometry of Simulated Perspectives: Dissociating Representation and Behaviour in a Multimodal Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08262)
*Mathis Immertreu,Fitim Abdullahu,Thomas Kinfe,Claus Metzner,Helmut Grabner,Patrick Krauss,Achim Schilling*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究系统刻画了Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct中"人格条件化视觉趣味性"判断的表征几何，发现提示框架比人格内容更能重塑内部表征，而人格内容对行为判断影响更大，揭示表征距离不等于行为相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态语言模型能模拟不同观察者视角，但被赋予的视角如何重塑其内部表征、以及这些表征变化是否驱动其判断，尚不清楚。

Method: 以Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct为对象，在100张图像与53种提示条件下系统变化人格内容及引入人格的语言框架，记录行为评分与逐层隐状态，分析表征几何随深度的重构。

Result: 表征几何随处理深度系统性重组：浅层主要编码图像身份，人格特异结构在较深层出现；提示框架对内部表征的塑造强于人格身份，而人格内容对行为判断影响更大；即便是无响应变化的最小化身份陈述也能改变表征。

Conclusion: 表征距离未必反映行为相关性；模拟视角在人工认知系统表征空间中表现为独立结构，内部表征几何与其功能后果存在根本性区分。

Abstract: Multimodal language models can simulate different observers, yet it remains unclear how assigned perspectives reshape their internal representations and whether these changes govern their judgements. Here, we investigate the representational geometry of persona-conditioned visual interestingness in Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct. Across 100 images and 53 prompting conditions, we systematically vary persona content and the linguistic framing used to introduce it, while recording behavioural ratings and layer-wise hidden states. We observe a systematic reorganisation of representational geometry across processing depth: early layers primarily encode image identity, whereas persona-specific structures emerge in later layers. Strikingly, prompt framing reshapes internal representations more strongly than persona identity, whereas persona content has a greater impact on behavioural judgements. This dissociation is further illustrated by minimal identity statements that alter representations even without changing responses. These findings demonstrate that representational distance does not necessarily reflect behavioural relevance. More broadly, they reveal how simulated perspectives emerge as distinct structures within the representational space of artificial cognitive systems, highlighting a fundamental distinction between the geometry of internal representations and their functional consequences.

</details>


### [171] [Who Should Know What? Empirical Information Design for Multi-Agent LLM Economies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07667)
*Mete Erdogan,Mirac Suzgun,Aneesh Pappu,Guowei Xu,Batu El,Andrew A. Schoen,Furong Huang,John P. Dickerson,Mert Pilanci,James Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"实证信息设计"框架，在多智能体LLM经济市场中系统比较信息披露策略，发现更细粒度信息的效果高度依赖具体模型，信息设计与模型选择需协同优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统表现不仅取决于底层模型能力，还取决于信息共享政策（各智能体观察到什么信息及细节程度）。核心问题：给AI智能体更详细信息是否总能改善集体结果，还是透明度反而会降低效率？

Method: 提出实证信息设计（empirical information design）实验框架，在固定交互规则与模型的前提下，跨LLM智能体参与的经济市场系统比较披露政策。评估十款前沿LLM，涵盖多资源交易、双边谈判、双重拍卖三类场景，并利用真实效用（ground-truth utilities）将可识别机会与已实现结果解耦。

Result: 结果高度依赖模型：交易中，披露精确估值（而非序数偏好排序）使GPT-6 Sol实现收益从最大值98%降至91%，却使Claude Sonnet 5从72%升至86%；谈判中，精确信息使Grok 4.7达成更不均衡的协议，而Claude Opus 5.5对称分配收益；双重拍卖中，完全披露抬高报价需求、引发市场崩溃并降低福利——Gemini 3.1 Pro（97%→78%）、Claude Opus 5.5（94%→64%）、Grok 4.7（97%→62%）。

Conclusion: 模型选择与信息设计必须协同优化，因为不同LLM对信息的策略性反应对系统目标的影响存在显著差异；提高透明度并非普遍有益。

Abstract: The performance of multi-agent LLM systems depends not only on the capabilities of the underlying models but also on information-sharing policies, which govern what information each agent observes and at what level of detail. But does providing AI agents with more detailed information always improve collective outcomes, or can transparency paradoxically degrade efficiency? To investigate this, we introduce empirical information design, an experimental framework that systematically compares disclosure policies across economic markets populated by LLM agents, while holding interaction rules and models fixed. We evaluate ten frontier LLMs in multi-resource trade, bilateral bargaining, and double auctions, using ground-truth utilities to decouple identified opportunities from realized outcomes. We find that finer information disclosure yields highly model-dependent results. In trade, revealing exact valuations rather than ordinal preference rankings reduces realized gains for GPT-6 Sol (from 98% to 91% of maximum) yet increases them for Claude Sonnet 5 (from 72% to 86%). In bargaining, exact information leads Grok 4.7 to produce less balanced agreements, whereas Claude Opus 5.5 divides gains symmetrically. In double auctions, full disclosure inflates price demands, triggering market breakdown and lowering welfare for Gemini 3.1 Pro (97% to 78%), Claude Opus 5.5 (94% to 64%), and Grok 4.7 (97% to 62%). Overall, these results suggest that model selection and information design must be co-optimized, as LLMs vary in how their strategic responses to information impact system objectives.

</details>


### [172] [BlockLock: Reproducible LLM Inference with Fixed Batch Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06977)
*Xiuyuan Qi,Pedro Reviriego,Javier Conde,Shanshan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BlockLock 通过固定受保护请求所见的计算几何（而非约束全部算子）来实现批量无关的可复现推理：BlockLock-Rows 用固定宽度行并保留批量解码，BlockLock-Pack 用末端锚定打包并隔离 dummy 段后提取 KV 做仅目标解码。在5个稠密模型、3种单卡与双卡初步实验中，两种模式均保持目标序列与 Top20 logit 位级一致；在同等交付工作量下，相比 vLLM Batch-Invariant，Rows 模式延迟降低53.5–55.0%，吞吐提升115.2–122.2%，且无需自定义算子、仅约束受保护请求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可复现的 LLM 推理要求同一请求无论同批次中其他请求如何变化都产生相同输出。现有方案代价各异：batch-invariant kernels 需约束每个算子（且需自定义核实现）；以固定形状解码隔离 prefill（CoRun 类基线）则放弃共享 prefill 计算并对 decode 进行 padding，牺牲效率。因此需要一种既保留共享批量 prefill 又能低成本实现位级可复现的方案。

Method: BlockLock 保留共享批量 prefill，仅钉住受保护请求的数值路径：固定该请求所见的计算几何。BlockLock-Rows 采用固定宽度行，同时保留所有请求的批量解码；BlockLock-Pack 采用末端锚定打包并加入隔离 dummy 段，随后提取受保护请求的 KV 状态进行仅目标解码。两种模式在给定的按部署契约下，当同伴请求变化时保持目标输出位级不变，且无需自定义 kernel，仅约束受保护请求。

Result: 在五个稠密模型（Qwen3-8B/14B、Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct、Gemma-3-4B/12B-IT）、三种单卡部署（NVIDIA A6000、A100、H100）以及两张 A6000 的初步张量并行实验上，于 tinyBenchmarks 与 BFCL 评测：两种模式在报告测试中均保持目标序列与 Top20 logit 位级一致。同等工作量下，相比 vLLM Batch-Invariant，BlockLock-Rows 延迟降低 53.5–55.0%，吞吐提升 115.2–122.2%。

Conclusion: 通过只固定受保护请求的数值几何，BlockLock 在保留共享批量 prefill 的同时实现了位级可复现推理，显著优于全算子约束的 batch-invariant kernels（延迟与吞吐均大幅改善），并优于放弃共享 prefill 的隔离式基线；其优势在于实现简单（无需自定义算子）、开销局限于受保护请求，并已在多模型、多硬件与初步多卡设置中验证。

Abstract: Reproducible LLM inference requires that a request produces the same output regardless of the other requests in the batch. Existing solutions pay for this in different ways: batch-invariant kernels constrain every operator, and isolating prefill with fixed-shape decode ({our CoRun-style baseline}) gives up shared prefill compute and pads decode. We present BlockLock, which keeps shared batched prefill and pins only the numerical path of the protected request: it fixes the geometry that the request sees, with {two modes: BlockLock-Rows uses fixed-width rows and retains batched decode of all requests; BlockLock-Pack uses terminal-anchored packing with an isolated dummy segment, then extracts the protected request's KV state for target-only decode. Under a stated per-deployment contract, both modes preserve the target's output bitwise when its companions change.} Across five {dense} models (Qwen3-8B/14B, Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, and Gemma-3-4B/12B-IT) and three single-GPU deployments (NVIDIA A6000, A100, and H100), we evaluate our approach on both tinyBenchmarks and BFCL{; we also include initial tensor-parallel experiments on two NVIDIA A6000 GPUs. Both modes preserve target sequences and Top20-logit bits in the reported tests.} At equal delivered work, {BlockLock-Rows} reduces latency by 53.5--55.0\% and improves throughput by 115.2--122.2\% relative to vLLM Batch-Invariant; compared with batch-invariant kernels, it needs no custom kernel implementations and constrains only the protected requests.

</details>


### [173] [TCSExam: Evaluating Frontier LLMs on the Full Range of TCS Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07962)
*Yifan Luo,Zhengying Liu,Yang Yuan,Zhilin Yang,Andrew Chi-Chih Yao*

Main category: cs.AI
