Method: 在四种方法、多种harness、通用与编程任务上评估；采用重复评测、受控的agent扩展、组件级干预与admission replay（准入重放）等手段，诊断进化过程中的模式。

Result: 16个进化agent均出现"获得新任务但丢失已覆盖任务"的现象；某通用任务案例中可靠性提升5.2分却丢失10个已覆盖任务；多数可靠性增益伴随部署成本上升；具体改动通常优于通用提示，但组合后收益会变化；最高分agent在四个池中均未带来最大平均提升；更严格准入会丢弃有用替代方案；迁移时通用任务目标覆盖率通常下降，而外层harness下编程目标覆盖率上升。

Conclusion: 基准分数不足以反映可靠、高效、可迁移的改进，需采用保留感知（preservation-aware）评测、经检验的决策规则与明确适用条件；据此提出的EvoAudit将诊断转化为对select/analyze/mutate/gate的修订，取得最佳性能。

Abstract: Agent harness evolution enables recursive self-improvement (RSI) without updating model weights. We examine whether higher benchmark scores yield reliable, efficient, and transferable improvement. We evaluate four methods across harnesses, general and coding tasks. Repeated evaluations, controlled agent expansion, component interventions, and admission replays reveal recurring patterns. All sixteen evolved agents gain previously uncovered tasks while losing previously covered ones. In one general-task case, reliability rises by 5.2 points despite losing ten previously covered tasks. Most reliability gains also increase deployment cost. Specific changes usually outperform generic reminders, but their benefits can change when combined. The highest-scoring agent does not yield the largest mean improvement in any of our four pools, and stricter admission can discard useful alternatives. Transfer selectively preserves gains: coverage usually falls on the general-task target across both harness types and paradigms, but rises on the coding target with the outer-layer-only harness. These findings motivate preservation-aware evaluation, tested decision rules, and explicit applicability conditions. As a constructive follow-up, EvoAudit turns these diagnostics into revisions of select, analyze, mutate, and gate, achieving the best performance.

</details>


### [242] [Align Before Aggregation: Basis-Consistent Federated LoRA under Heterogeneous Ranks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07598)
*Pengpeng Qiao,Yang Cao,Lingling Zhang,Guo Cheng,Junwei Chen,Manjiang Yu,Wei Yang Bryan Lim,Masatoshi Yoshikawa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 FedAbA，一种基一致的异构秩联邦 LoRA 聚合框架，通过局部基对齐与一致性感知加权解决服务器端重分解后秩前缀下发不稳定的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构客户端容量下联邦微调 LLM 日益重要，异构秩 LoRA 使其可行。现有服务器端聚合先由客户端特定 LoRA 因子 (A_i,B_i) 合成 ΔW_i 再平均。虽然这些矩阵更新同处 ℝ^{m×n} 权重空间可直接平均，但生成它们的 LoRA 因子可能表达在客户端特定的局部基上。在"合成—聚合—分解—下发"循环中，这种基不一致会导致服务器重分解后下发给低秩客户端的秩-r_i 参考前缀不稳定。

Method: FedAbA 首先提取每个客户端的局部基，将其对齐到全局参考基的对应前缀，在聚合前构建每个局部矩阵更新的基一致重构；随后以结合客户端数据规模与一致性得分的权重聚合这些重构，再进行精确的系数空间 SVD 重分解，并在秩前缀下发前做规范固定（gauge fixing）。理论上阐明了基一致性的作用并支撑所提一致性感知聚合机制。

Result: 大量实验表明 FedAbA 优于代表性联邦 LoRA 基线，且在秩异质性最强时增益最大。

Conclusion: 基一致性是异构秩联邦 LoRA 聚合中的关键因素；通过基对齐、一致性感知加权与系数空间精确重分解，可显著提升异构秩场景下的聚合质量与稳定性。

Abstract: Federated fine-tuning of large language models (LLMs) under heterogeneous client capacities is increasingly important, and low-rank adaptation (LoRA) with heterogeneous ranks makes it practical. Existing server-side aggregators compose local matrix updates $ΔW_i$ from client-specific LoRA factors $(A_i,B_i)$ before aggregating them. Although these matrix updates lie in the same weight space $\mathbb{R}^{m\times n}$ and can be averaged directly, the LoRA factors that generate them may expressed client-specific local basis. In the typical compose--aggregate--factorize--dispatch loop, this basis inconsistency can make the rank-$r_i$ reference prefix dispatched to lower-rank clients unstable after server-side refactorization. In this work, we propose \textbf{FedAbA}, a basis-consistent federated LoRA aggregation framework for heterogeneous ranks. FedAbA first extracts each client's local basis, aligns it to the corresponding prefix of the global reference basis, and constructs a basis-consistent reconstruction of each local matrix update before aggregation. It then aggregates these reconstructions with weights that combine client data size and consistency score, followed by exact coefficient-space SVD refactorization and gauge fixing before rank-prefix dispatch. Our theoretical analysis clarifies the role of basis consistency and supports the proposed consistency-aware aggregation mechanism. Extensive experiments show that FedAbA outperforms representative federated LoRA baselines, with the largest gains under the strongest rank heterogeneity.

</details>


### [243] [CST-Bench: Benchmarking Counterfactual Spatial Transition Reasoning with Causal Transition On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07541)
*Ying Li,Yongle Cheng,Jing Li,Xingquan Cai,Yawei Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出反事实空间推理基准 CST-Bench（7,082 例、6 类干预、12 种变换算子，含密集因果标注），揭示普通空间推理有效方法在反事实干预下仍会失效，并提出 CT-OPSD 将因果状态转移链蒸馏进仅依赖真实观测的学生模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间推理基准主要聚焦静态场景理解，未考察物体被移动或替换后场景状态如何改变；模型不仅要推断空间关系，还需推断干预带来的状态转移与关系变化，这一能力缺乏评测与监督资源。

Method: 构建 CST-Bench 基准，覆盖 6 种干预类型与 12 种变换算子，并提供密集因果标注（干预源、受影响关系、预期效应、不变关系、度量变化）；在此基准上评测通用 MLLM 并比较后训练策略；进一步将反事实空间推理形式化为因果状态转移建模，提出 CT-OPSD：利用标注引导教师模型学习反事实状态转移与因果推理链，再将推理链蒸馏到仅从真实观测推理的学生模型。

Result: 在普通空间推理上有效的方法在反事实干预下仍可能失败，说明该任务具有独立难度；CT-OPSD 验证了标注可用于指导模型改进，使模型在仅依赖真实观测的条件下获得反事实推理能力。

Conclusion: CST-Bench 是具挑战性的反事实空间推理基准与可复用的监督资源；CT-OPSD 初步展示了如何利用其标注提升模型能力，为 MLLM 的因果空间推理评测与训练提供统一框架。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning is a fundamental capability of multimodal large language models (MLLMs), yet existing benchmarks mainly focus on static scene understanding. When objects are moved or swapped, models must infer not only spatial relations but also how interventions change the scene state. To address this problem, we introduce CST-Bench, a benchmark for counterfactual spatial reasoning. CST-Bench contains 7,082 examples covering 6 intervention types and 12 transformation operators, with dense causal annotations including intervention sources, affected relations, expected effects, invariant relations, and metric changes. We benchmark general-purpose MLLMs on CST-Bench and further compare post-training strategies on CST-Bench and standard spatial benchmarks. Results show that methods effective for ordinary spatial reasoning can still fail under counterfactual interventions. We further formulate counterfactual spatial reasoning as causal state-transition modeling and propose CT-OPSD, which uses CST-Bench annotations to guide a teacher model in learning counterfactual state transitions and causal reasoning chains. These chains are then distilled into a student model that reasons only from real observations. Overall, experiment results establish CST-Bench as a challenging benchmark and reusable supervision resource for counterfactual spatial reasoning. CT-OPSD further provides an initial demonstration of how its annotations can support model improvement.

</details>


### [244] [FINCOSMOS: An Open-World Evaluation Paradigm for Agents in Finance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07464)
*Yupeng Cao,Xueqing Peng,Lingfei Qian,Haohang Li,Yangyang Yu,Anke Xu,Wenbo Cao,Yan Wang,Yuechen Jiang,Yuyan Wang,Zhuohan Xie,Yankai Chen,Zehan Li,Jimin Huang,Xue Liu,Jian-Yun Nie,Sophia Ananiadou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出FINCOSMOS——面向金融智能体的开放世界评测范式：模拟用户仅在被询问时披露隐藏策略，智能体需自行从Web获取数据、在数据截止约束下构建回测并交付可验证结果；评分无需参考答案，由程序校验时点正确性、重算全部指标并核对策略规格，须同时满足用户满意与15项否决检查才被接受。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言智能体在开放Web上为用户工作，金融是其信任最难建立的场景：数据须来自Web、策略仅存在于用户脑中、正确性依赖时间。因此智能体可能"静默失败"（使用未获授权的策略、未经验证的数据、含未来信息的回测），却仍产出与正确结果同样有说服力的报告。现有基准通过固定任务、数据或时间线来封闭世界，实质上是消除了这些失败模式而非度量它们。

Method: 构建开放世界评测：模拟用户持有由组合语法（compositional grammar）采样的隐藏策略，仅在智能体主动询问时披露；智能体初始无数据，须从Web采集数据、在数据截止点下自建回测并交付可验证结果。评分机制免参考解：程序验证point-in-time正确性、重算所有报告指标、将交付策略与隐藏规格比对，且需通过15项veto检查并由用户确认满意。实验覆盖4个编码智能体与1个无工具模型，共150次会话；另有真人专家归因分析与带持久记忆的反馈学习实验。

Result: 150次会话中无一被接受：凡模拟用户签字认可的会话均至少违反一项程序检查；智能体仅主动提出54–64%的未被披露细节。人类专家将88.33%的失败归因于智能体。引入FINCOSMOS反馈后，具备持久记忆的智能体学会了全部10项任务。

Conclusion: 金融智能体的主要风险在于静默失败而非表面质量，封闭世界基准会掩盖此类问题；FINCOSMOS以开放式、免参考答案、多点否决的评测方式揭示了当前智能体在需求澄清、时点正确性与数据验证上的系统性缺陷，同时表明基于该反馈的持续记忆学习可显著改善表现。

Abstract: Language agents increasingly work for users on the open Web, and finance is where trusting them is hardest. The data must come from the Web, the strategy lives in the user's head, and correctness depends on time, so an agent can fail silently: a strategy the user never asked for, unverified data, or a backtest that uses the future yields a report as convincing as a correct one. Existing benchmarks close the world somewhere, fixing the task, the data, or the timeline, and thereby remove these failures instead of measuring them. We introduce FINCOSMOS, an open-world evaluation paradigm for agents doing financial work. A simulated user holds a hidden strategy sampled from a compositional grammar and reveals it only when asked. The agent receives no data, so it must acquire data from the Web, build its own backtest under a data cutoff, and deliver verifiable results. Grading needs no reference answer. Programs verify point-in-time correctness and recompute every reported metric, the delivered strategy is checked against the hidden specification, and a run is accepted only when the user is satisfied and none of 15 veto checks fails. Across four coding agents and a model without tools in 150 sessions, no session is accepted: every session that the simulated user signed off fails a program check, and agents raise only 54--64\% of the details they were not told. Human experts attribute 88.33\% of failures to the agents. With FINCOSMOS feedback, an agent with persistent memory learned all ten tasks.

</details>


### [245] [StepBack: Step-Level Error-Localized Resampling for Efficient Test-Time Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08403)
*Yang Ouyang,Jung-Eun Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出StepBack：一种步骤级错误定位重采样方法，用轻量信号定位首错位置并只重采样该步及其后缀，在保持SC精度同时降低token开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自洽性（SC）解码需按预设预算采样大量完整推理轨迹，成本高昂；现有高效变体主要靠提前停止整条轨迹采样或在题目间分配样本数，未利用推理过程的步骤级结构信息。

Method: 先以小规模草稿轨迹集为输入，利用轻量信号定位可能的首个错误位置；随后仅对该检测到的错误步骤及其后缀进行重采样，最后对答案进行多数投票。

Result: 在七个数学推理与多跳问答基准上平均达到与SC相当的准确率，token使用量最多减少41.26%；且可与Adaptive Consistency等自适应推理方法结合，进一步提升推理时效率。

Conclusion: 将效率优化从轨迹级/题目级下移到步骤级错误定位重采样，是一种正交且可组合的测试时推理加速策略。

Abstract: Self-consistency (SC) is a widely used decoding strategy for improving chain-of-thought reasoning, but it requires sampling many complete reasoning trajectories with a preset budget. Its efficient variants reduce the cost mainly by stopping full-trajectory sampling early or distributing the number of samples across questions. In this paper, we propose StepBack, a step-level error-localized resampling method for efficient test-time reasoning. Given a small set of draft trajectories, StepBack localizes likely first error positions using lightweight signals and then resamples the detected error step and its suffix, followed by majority answer voting. Empirically, StepBack matches the accuracy of SC on average across seven mathematical reasoning and multi-hop QA benchmarks, while reducing token usage by up to 41.26%. In addition, by nature, StepBack can be combined with adaptive inference methods such as Adaptive Consistency, further improving their inference-time efficiency.

</details>
